## Felix / 菲利克斯

===Original===
Felix sobbed in the taxi: “Can’t you see we are all damned.” And that love and death were one.

===Chinese===
菲利克斯在出租汽车里呜咽着："难道你们看不见吗，我们全都要下地狱。"还哭诉说，爱与死原是一体。

===Original===
They had considerably enlivened the cabaret, a sentimental infamy, its men and girls drunker than the clients. Among their slobbering, rapacious familiarity, the three appeared like drunk young gods. And Felix, a young king receiving his subjects, was courtly to the fawning, swarming band of both sexes in changed clothes. Proud of his companions, unconscious that he was paying for the party, he did not know that Boris owed money there, how he balanced the chance of being dunned with his worth as conductor of a rich client, and hoped that his debt would be put on to Felix’s bill. A thing he would not arrange. He had not yet come to that.

===Chinese===
他们把那家卡巴莱夜总会（cabaret）搅得热闹非凡——那是个滥情败德的所在，店里的小伙子和姑娘们喝得比客人还凶。在他们那涎漉漉、贪馋馋的厮缠亲热中间，这三位活像喝醉了的年轻神祇。而菲利克斯，好比一位接见臣民的少年君王，对那群换了行头、蜂拥献媚的男女臣仆彬彬有礼。他以自己的同伴为荣，浑然不觉这场欢宴的账单正落在他头上；他不知道鲍里斯在那儿欠着债，不知道他怎样掂量着被讨债的风险与自己作为阔客引荐人的身价，指望把那笔债记到菲利克斯的账上。这种事，他是不会点头安排的。他还没有堕到那个地步。

===Original===
Round their table moved the herd of painted animals, Felix’s subjects, their tongues parting their lips for what they might get out of the flower-skinned, sapphire-eyed boy, who looked at nothing but Boris. Black briar-rose, he called him, who saw Felix an absurd young splendour. Felix noticed him strange, observant, a moon-baby. Not how the infant was putting two and two together. Nor would he have cared, lifted above any complex of the shopkeeper to be paid in any kind of thanks.

===Chinese===
围着他们那张桌子打转的，是一群涂了油彩的走兽——菲利克斯的臣民；他们舌头舔开嘴唇，惦着能从这个花般肌肤、蓝宝石眼睛的男孩身上榨出点什么，而这男孩的眼睛里只看得见鲍里斯。菲利克斯唤他"黑刺野蔷薇"；在这位的眼里，菲利克斯不过是一场可笑的年少荣华。菲利克斯觉出他古怪、有眼力，是个月亮婴孩；却没觉出这婴孩正把桩桩件件串到一处，渐渐看出端倪。他也不会在乎——他早已凌驾于小店主那种非得收进某种谢报才安生的心病之上。

===Original===
It was the American, later, who developed an intoxicated conscience about Felix when, in the taxi, romantic metaphysics and song gave way to hysteria. He cuffed him roughly into place. Unfortunately, Felix’s head broke a window, and all he did was lean out and cry: “I want to suffer as you have suffered, Boris﻿—Police! police!”

===Chinese===
后来，在出租汽车里，当浪漫的形而上学和歌声让位于歇斯底里，对菲利克斯生出一份醉醺醺良心的，正是那位美国人。他抡起拳头，把他狠狠搡回原位。不巧，菲利克斯的头撞破了一扇车窗，而他做的唯一一件事，就是探出身子喊道："我要像你受过的苦那样受苦，鲍里斯——警察！警察！"

===Original===
The taxi slowed round a corner. He was bleeding from a cut. Boris alone kept his wits. Withdrew Felix’s head delicately from the hole in the glass, shouted in Russian at the chauffeur he had picked. Felix had become merely a thing to get home. They pulled up at the Foyot and got him out. Out but not up. The American retrieved the silk hat rolling on the stones: while Boris extracted three hundred francs, taxi, window money and useful change for the next day.

===Chinese===
出租汽车拐过街角，放慢了车速。他一道口子正在淌血。只有鲍里斯还守得住神志。他把菲利克斯的头从玻璃窟窿里轻轻巧巧地抽回来，又用俄语冲自己挑定的司机大声吆喝。菲利克斯这时已经沦为一件只消弄回家去的物件。车在弗瓦约（Foyot）餐馆门口停了下来，他们把他弄下车。人是下来了，却没能站起来。美国人拾起那顶在石子路上滚动的大礼帽；鲍里斯则取出了三百法郎——车钱、赔窗子的钱，外加转天派得上用场的零头。

===Original===
Then came the pilgrimage to which they were hardly equal, in the liftless, ancient barrack, their support to each other physical not moral, an age of social hatred lived through as they hoisted Felix upstairs and round corners, indifferent to his cries that he must *faire pipi*, followed by the concierge jangling the key. Ages of dissimilarity between the American, sudden flower of strength and looks, and the Russian-Tartar brat of family, to whom neurasthenia had become a habit.

===Chinese===
接下来便是一段他们几乎担当不起的朝圣之旅——在那座没有电梯的古老公寓楼里，两人彼此撑持，凭的是力气而非道义；他们把菲利克斯扛上楼、扛过一个又一个拐角，恍如一同熬过整整一个社会仇恨的世纪，全不理会他哭喊着非去 *faire pipi*（撒尿）不可，身后还跟着那位把钥匙摇得叮当乱响的门房。美国人与那俄裔鞑靼小子之间，隔着亘古的悬殊——前者是力气与相貌骤然绽放的花朵，后者是世家娇养出的娃娃，神经衰弱早成了他的老习惯。

===Original===
They dumped Felix on the bed. Boris sponged away the blood, and got off the clothes that would hurt. The blood sickened the other and made him fretful. He gave an odd exhibition of nerves. Boris soothed him and they stumbled downstairs. In the street he said: “I have a little money. Let’s go on.” The American refused. Boris circled a little on the pavement, and, finding himself alone, drifted off to his little hotel, and slept face-downwards in his only convincing suit.

===Chinese===
他们把菲利克斯撂倒在床上。鲍里斯用海绵揩去血污，脱下那些会磨疼他的衣裳。血腥气让另一个恶心难受、焦躁起来，发作了一通古怪的神经质。鲍里斯安抚住他，两人跌跌撞撞下了楼。到了街上，他说："我还有一点钱。咱们接着玩去。"美国人拒绝了。鲍里斯在人行道上兜了一小圈，发觉只剩自己孤零零一个，便飘回他那家小旅馆，穿着他唯一一套像样的衣服，脸朝下睡着了。

## Carston and Clarence / 卡斯顿与克拉伦斯

===Original===
Carston’s day had been a penance. A train had landed him some time in midmorning at a place called Chard. Picus had said that it was nearer Tollerdown than Starn, but no one there had heard of the place. The station lay in no immediate relation to the village. The inn was fusty and unsympathetic. The heat atrocious. A day for no sane man to tramp while the sun was high. Miles across another bend of the heath where Picus had lost him, the down-banks rose, aery turf walls, solid as flesh and blood. One of them was Tollerdown. He held up a passing motorist, who was kind. He gave him a lift down a white road sprung like an arrow across the moor that filled the lowlands like a dark dragon’s wing.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿这一天形同苦修。一列火车在上午十来点钟把他撂在一个叫查德（Chard）的地方。皮库斯（Picus）原说这里离托勒当（Tollerdown）比离斯塔恩（Starn）近，可那儿谁都没听说过这地方。车站跟村子之间隔着一段不即不离的无关。客栈又闷又不可亲。热得怕人。这种日子，日头正毒的时候，没有哪个神志正常的人会去徒步赶路。数英里开外，越过荒原又一道弯——皮库斯指的路就是在这一带把他弄丢的——丘坡耸了起来，一道道透空的草皮城墙，结实得如同血肉之躯。其中一座就是托勒当。他拦下一辆路过的汽车，开车人心肠好。车子载着他顺一条白路飞驰，白路像一支离弦的箭射过荒原，荒原漫满洼地，像一条黑龙的翅膀。

===Original===
At the foot of the turf, he set Carston down. “Go up the track,” he said, “and make towards the sea. If it’s not this shoulder, it’s the one that follows it.” Carston mounted, into silence, on to height. He had never been so well in his life, could not have stood that if he had not been so well. Never had his heart been so touched. Could he stand that?

===Chinese===
在草坡脚下，他把卡斯顿放下。「沿这条小径上去，」他说，「朝着海走。要不是这道山肩，就是紧挨着它的那下一道。」卡斯顿往上爬，爬进寂静，爬上高处。他这辈子从没这么硬朗过；若不是这么硬朗，他也扛不住眼前这件事。他的心也从没这样被触动过。他扛得住吗？

===Original===
He mounted, past the trees, the copses, the gorse patches, on to the last crest of raw grass. The earth and the sea extended in a perfect circle round him. He had only to follow the hill’s spine, and drop halfway into the valley to strike the cottage before he walked over into the sea. Like a man who has been given a heavy treasure that he has not looked at and must carry home, he walked on.

===Chinese===
他往上爬，走过树木、杂树林、一丛丛荆豆，登上最后一道生草的山脊。大地和海洋在他四周铺展成一个浑圆的圆。他只消沿着山的脊线走，到半坡折进山谷，就能寻到那座村舍，省得一路走进海里。像一个接过一桩沉甸甸的宝物、还没看过一眼、却必须背回家去的人，他继续走着。

===Original===
Bear your burden in the heat of the day, he sang, who had been in great request at parties for his bawdy repertory. His track ran through five barrows. By one was a crook-backed angry thorn. A bad patch. He passed it, glad to have left them behind, keeping his face towards the sea. Interlude this day alone, in a train. On a hill. Find a cottage on a shelf. Console its inhabitant. Bring him a cup to pop down a well. Fetch him away. You could make a ballad about it. About a mile more to go. *One more river to cross.* The turf turned over in what was almost a cliff. He was not on Tollerdown. Picus and the man in the car had said two hills. This one dropped into a narrow neck. The great bank he could now see rising on the other side, that was Tollerdown. He cursed, slid down the breakneck path, over a wall of unmortared stone bound with bramble that ripped his clothes; across a field, ploughed and deserted, its furrows baked to iron. Over a gate crested with barbed wire, whose rusty thorns drew blood from his knee. Sprinted down a sparkling grit road, met cattle and an angry dog. Hurry, hurry, he did not know why. Get this over. Hanging about an eternity he’d been, up in the air. Now for people and the end of the cup. A baker’s cart passed him on the road, directed him, and he found himself mounting again by the way Scylla had come. Stopped at the open cottage door, knocked, waited, went in. He saw the bird. The torn papers. He went through.

===Chinese===
白日炎炎，背起你的重担——他唱道；此人素来在宴会上大受欢迎，凭的是一套荤歌艳曲的保留节目。小径穿过五座古冢。一座旁边立着一棵驼背的、怒气冲冲的山楂树。不是善地。他走了过去，很高兴把它们甩在身后，脸始终朝着海。今日插曲，独自一人：先在火车里，后在山丘上。到山腰台地上找一座村舍。安抚它的住户。给他带一只杯子来，好丢进井里。再把他接走。这都能编成一支歌谣了。还有约一英里路。*还有一条河要渡。*草皮在一处近于悬崖的地方翻卷下去。他脚下这座不是托勒当。皮库斯和车里那人都说的是两座山。这一座垂进一道窄窄的山颈。他现在望得见的、在另一侧耸起的大丘，那才是托勒当。他骂了一句，溜下那条险路，翻过一道不施灰浆、缠满荆棘的干石墙，荆棘撕破了他的衣裳；又穿过一片犁过又抛荒的田，犁沟烤成了铁。再翻过一道顶着铁丝网的栅门，锈刺扎得他膝盖见了血。他顺着一条砂砾闪闪的路一路疾奔，迎面遇上牛群和一条恶狗。快些，快些，他不知道为什么。把这桩事了结了吧。他已经吊在半空，晃荡了一个永恒。现在该见人了，该让这杯子有个收梢。路上一辆面包房的货车超过他，给他指了路，他发觉自己又沿着斯库拉（Scylla）来时的那条道往上爬了。他在敞开的村舍门前停住，敲了敲，等了等，走了进去。他看见了那只鸟。撕碎的纸片。他径直穿屋而过。

===Original===
He saw Clarence, slowly and awkwardly trying to restring the bow, and the lovely nightmare, Scylla hanging bound to the stake of her love. His reason had vanished. Returned, abnormally clear. A madman and the girl probably dead. No gun. Behind them a gulf to the sea. Was I made a man for this? *Lighten our darkness.*

===Chinese===
他看见了克拉伦斯（Clarence），正迟缓而笨拙地试着给那张弓重新上弦；还有那可爱的噩梦——斯库拉，被捆着，悬吊在爱的刑桩上。他的理性曾一度消失。又回来了，清明得反常。一个疯子，姑娘多半已经死了。没有枪。他们身后，一道深渊直落到海。我生而为男人，就是为了这个吗？*求主照亮我们的黑暗。*

===Original===
Playact. He pulled out the cup. It had kept its jade-coolness. He showed it to Clarence.

===Chinese===
做戏。他掏出那只杯。它还存着一股玉的凉。他把它拿给克拉伦斯看。

===Original===
“Just got here. Picus wants it put back in the well, and you to come to Tambourne. See? Sent me.” He took his arm: “Put it in yourself. He said you were to. Drop it in. Feel how cool it is. Wants to get back to where it came﻿—into water. He’ll be wretched if you don’t.”

===Chinese===
「刚到。皮库斯要把它放回井里，还要你去坦伯恩（Tambourne）。明白？是他派我来的。」他挽住他的胳膊：「你自己来放。他吩咐的该是你。扔下去就是。摸摸它多凉。它想回到它来的地方——回到水里。你不放，他会难过的。」

===Original===
Clarence staggered a little, moving towards the well.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯朝井边挪去，身子晃了一下。

===Original===
“My head’s not cool,” he said. “Hurts like hell. The boy wants it dropped in. I can’t see why I should attend to all his fancies.”

===Chinese===
「我的脑子不清凉，」他说。「疼得要命。这孩子非要把它扔进去。我不明白，凭什么他一时一个念头都得由我来伺候。」

===Original===
Carston tried not to look at Scylla, not let him turn. Clarence’s step shambled a little, his head dropped.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿竭力不去看斯库拉，也不让他转身。克拉伦斯的脚步有些拖沓，头垂了下去。

===Original===
“I’m not to do it. Only you.”

===Chinese===
「不该我来做。只能是你。」

===Original===
“All right. Here goes.” Plop went a noise a very long way below them. Clarence covered his eyes with his hands.

===Chinese===
「好吧。这就来。」噗通一声，响在他们底下极深极远的地方。克拉伦斯双手捂住了眼睛。

===Original===
“Dear man, it was decent of you to come. Such a way and the country strange to you. Hope you had a car. D’you mind if I go in for a bit and fix you up some tea?” Carston guided him carefully, back turned from what was out there in the sun, into the house-shadow, into the studio.

===Chinese===
「亲爱的，你能来真是太够意思了。这么远的路，这地方你又人生地不熟。但愿你坐了车。我进去一小会儿，给你弄点茶，行吗？」卡斯顿小心地引着他，背朝太阳底下那些东西，走进屋影里，走进画室。

===Original===
“I’ll make tea. You lie down a bit.” He was thinking how to lock him in, when the young man dropped, moaning that his head hurt, and that something was trying to get out through his eyes. Carston hoped it might be the tears he’d cry when he knew what he’d done. He had always liked Clarence, disliked that his affection should have turned to horror. He even put a cushion under his head. Then snatched up a knife in the kitchen, rushed out and cut Scylla free, and carried her on to the sitting-room couch. Then followed a time when time indefinitely suspended and extended itself. Attempts to withdraw the wood that pierced her, to stop the blood, to revive her, sustain her, dreading her consciousness and her unconsciousness alike. Listen to Clarence moaning, listen for him moving. He had not found a key to lock him in. Try to find a revolver without leaving Scylla, and later not to fall over the gun he had laid across the table at full cock. At one time he wondered if he should pitch Clarence over the cliff while he went for a doctor, and went nearly mad as the light failed, for he saw her coming back to her right mind alone, and the ghost of the man who had injured her crawling up the cliff-face to go on with his dream out of the flesh, and two ghosts, not one, would carry on, the torturing and the tortured.

===Chinese===
「我去沏茶。你躺一会儿。」他正盘算怎么把他锁在屋里，年轻人就瘫倒了，呻吟着说头疼，说有什么东西正想从他的眼睛里钻出去。卡斯顿希望那会是眼泪——等他知道了自己干下的事之后，会淌下的眼泪。他一向喜欢克拉伦斯，不甘心这份好感竟化作了恐怖。他甚至还往他头底下垫了一只靠垫。随后他从厨房抄起一把刀，冲出去割断绳索放下斯库拉，把她抱到起居室的长沙发上。接下来是一段时光自己无限悬停又无限延展的时光。把穿透她的木杆拔出来，止血，唤她醒转，把她撑住，她昏着怕、她醒了也怕。听克拉伦斯呻吟，留神听他有没有起身。他没找着锁门的钥匙。想找一把左轮枪，又不肯离开斯库拉；过后又得小心，别绊着他自己横在桌上、顶上膛的那把枪。有一阵他甚至盘算，要不要趁去请医生的工夫把克拉伦斯推下悬崖；天光将尽时他几乎发了疯，因为他眼见她独自一个醒转过来，而那个伤了她的人的鬼魂正爬上崖壁，要撇下肉身继续做它的梦——那样一来，作祟的就不是一条鬼，而是两条，一个施刑，一个受刑，没完没了地演下去。

---

===Original===
An immensely long shadow flung back was travelling the hills. As the sun slipped incandescent into a crescent of far cliff, Carston heard outside whistling, liquid notes of everything that has wings. He remembered, Like Mozart. Thought it might be death, coming sweetly for Scylla, as Picus walked into the house.

===Chinese===
一道甩得老长老长的影子，正掠过群山。太阳炽亮地滑进远崖的一道弯口，卡斯顿听见外面有哨音，是天下一切生翅之物婉转如流泉的鸣声。他想起来：像莫扎特（Mozart）。他想，那也许是死神，来温柔地接斯库拉——这时皮库斯走进了屋子。

===Original===
He saw Carston glaring, feeling for the gun, heard him say:

===Chinese===
他看见卡斯顿目光凶狠，正伸手摸枪，听见他说：

===Original===
“You sent her to this. You laid this trap for her. You drove him mad﻿—”

===Chinese===
「是你把她送进这一步的。是你给她设下了这个套。是你把他逼疯的——」

===Original===
He answered:

===Chinese===
他答道：

===Original===
“If that were so, should I have sent you? Should I have come myself? Whisht man, let’s look.” Passing, he put the gun at safe, and Carston saw him lay Scylla’s body across his knees, open the chemise he had slit up and retied with a scarf.

===Chinese===
「果真如此，我还会派你来？还会亲自来？嘘，伙计，来看看。」走过时他把枪扳到保险上。卡斯顿看见他把斯库拉的身体横放到自己膝上，解开那件贴身衬衣——他先前剪开、又用一条围巾重新系上的。

===Original===
“Scylla, you silly bitch, wake up. Man, I know all about wounds. Side glanced off a rib, the rest’s nothing.”

===Chinese===
「斯库拉，你个小傻瓜，醒醒。老兄，伤口的事我门儿清。肋上擦过一道，其余都不算什么。」

===Original===
“All but our cruelty to her. I’ve not been that.”

===Chinese===
「只除开我们对她的狠心。我倒没狠过心。」

===Original===
“No, you’ve not. Less than us. Yes, call it my fault. I can be sane. Where’s Clarence?”

===Chinese===
「是啊，你没有。比我们谁都轻。行，就算我的错。我还清醒得了。克拉伦斯在哪儿？」

===Original===
“In the studio, not quite conscious. I can’t find the key.”

===Chinese===
「在画室里，神志不清。钥匙我找不着。」

===Original===
“He’ll do. Scylla’s quite comfy here. Go and make her some tea. Stiff whisky for us. Clarence had a bad head wound. With that and the sun, and my bitchery﻿—Where’s the cup?”

===Chinese===
「他不碍事。斯库拉在这儿挺安适。去给她沏点茶。给咱们俩来烈的威士忌。克拉伦斯头上伤得不轻。加上那伤、加上这太阳、再加上我干的混账事——杯子呢？」

===Original===
“In the well. I made him put it down. Said it was your orders. Then he collapsed.”

===Chinese===
「在井里。我让他放下去的。说是你的吩咐。跟着他就垮了。」

===Original===
“Where did this happen?”

===Chinese===
「这是在哪儿出的事？」

===Original===
“At the back. Go and look.”

===Chinese===
「在屋后。去看看吧。」

===Original===
Picus went out into the quarry and looked at the statue of himself. Spots of Scylla’s blood, blackening in the dying light. None of his own. He took an axe from the woodpile and knocked the image of himself to a stump. Carston heard the dry pieces falling, the patter of dust.

===Chinese===
皮库斯走进屋后的采石场，看着他自己那尊雕像。斯库拉的血点，在渐熄的天光里发黑。没有一滴是他自己的。他从柴堆上取下一柄斧头，把自我的像砍成一截短桩。卡斯顿听见干燥的碎块纷纷坠落，尘土簌簌作响。

---

===Original===
Scylla stirred and sat up. Two cups of tea pressed to her lips met and clicked together.

===Chinese===
斯库拉动了动，坐起身来。两只茶杯同时送到她唇边，碰在一处，叮的一响。

===Original===
“I can’t drink out of two cups at once.” Carston withdrew his. She drank.

===Chinese===
「我不能一口气用两只杯子喝茶。」卡斯顿收回了他的那只。她喝了。

===Original===
“Is it Picus?” she said, feeling for their hands.

===Chinese===
「是皮库斯吗？」她说着，伸手去摸他们的手。

===Original===
Carston said:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿说：

===Original===
“Is there nowhere in this hell-forsaken country where we can get a doctor?”

===Chinese===
「这该遭天谴的鬼地方，就没有一处请得到医生？」

===Original===
“She doesn’t need one,” said Picus﻿—“only us. No, love, I won’t go away. We’re going to sleep here. Hush, love. I’ve got to do magic and make you well. Better magic than at Gault.”

===Chinese===
「她用不着医生，」皮库斯说——「有咱们就够了。不，亲爱的，我不走。咱们就在这儿睡。嘘，亲爱的。我得施一回魔法，把你治好。比在高尔特（Gault）施的魔法更高明。」

===Original===
“What’s happened to Clarence?”

===Chinese===
「克拉伦斯怎么了？」

===Original===
“We’ve put him in the studio. His head’s all wrong. Tomorrow he won’t remember about this.”

===Chinese===
「我们把他安置进画室了。他脑子全乱了。到明天，他就记不得这一出了。」

===Original===
“He isn’t coming back? Picus, I don’t know how. Lydia wrote an idiot letter. I just came in. Not to be beastly, but to try﻿—” She began to cry a great deal. Carston stayed with her. Picus went to the studio alone.

===Chinese===
「他不回来了？皮库斯，我不知道事情怎么就闹成了这样。莉迪亚（Lydia）写了封蠢透的信。我进来……不是存心使坏，只是想——」她放声大哭起来。卡斯顿留下来陪她。皮库斯独自去了画室。

===Original===
Soon he came back with an armful of bedding. He laid it on the floor.

===Chinese===
不多时他抱着满怀铺盖回来了，把它铺在地上。

===Original===
“He’s asleep. He will sleep.”

===Chinese===
「他睡着了。他会一直睡下去。」

===Original===
“What are we going to do?” cried Carston, at exhaustion’s breaking point.

===Chinese===
「我们到底该怎么办？」卡斯顿喊道，人已到了精疲力竭崩断的边缘。

===Original===
“Sleep. We shall all sleep. Where we are, round her. Cover her over. Put a drink for her in the night. Finish the whisky. So. We shall all sleep. You at the foot. I at the side. On this side, love, or you’ll lie on the cut in your hair.

===Chinese===
「睡觉。我们都睡。就在这儿，围着她睡。给她盖严实。夜里给她搁一份喝的。把威士忌喝完。就这样。我们都睡。你睡脚那头。我睡旁边。睡这一边，亲爱的，不然要压着你头发里的伤口了。

===Original===
“Shoes off, Carston. We shall need our feet tomorrow.

===Chinese===
「鞋脱了，卡斯顿。明天咱们用得着脚。

===Original===
“Door open, and perhaps a rabbit will come in.

===Chinese===
「门开着，说不定会进来一只兔子。

===Original===
“Sleep, man, sleep. We must. Scylla, that’s a fat star winking. Clarence is locked in. Had a turn like this before, and thought he was a nun.”

===Chinese===
「睡吧，老兄，睡吧。非睡不可。斯库拉，那是一颗胖星星在眨眼。克拉伦斯锁在里头了。他从前也这么发作过一回，还只当自己是修女。」

===Original===
Carston heard a giggle.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿听见一声嗤笑。

===Original===
Of course, if Picus said a rabbit would come in. If Scylla wanted a rabbit to come in.﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
当然，只要皮库斯说兔子会进来。只要斯库拉想要兔子进来。……

---

===Original===
The shepherd’s wife sat up on a heap of rag quilts. The thatch bore down over a window sunk in the rubble wall, the panes wood-squared, double-fastened with paint, the ledge filled by a tropical green geranium, flowerless, filtering the light. The shepherd snored.

===Chinese===
牧羊人的妻子从一堆布头拼被里坐起身。茅草屋檐低低压着一扇嵌进碎石墙的窗，窗上的玻璃用木条格成方块，又叫层层叠叠的厚漆封了两道，窗台上挤着一盆热带的绿天竺葵，不开花，滤着光。牧羊人打着呼噜。

===Original===
“Get up!” she squealed, and kicked him. “I be going across to the house.”

===Chinese===
「起来！」她尖声嚷道，踹了他一脚。「俺这就过宅子那边去。」

===Original===
A little later the old trollop left the rustic slum, and was crossing the hill’s dewy shoulder in the delicate light. The day before she had been afraid to go; but in the night, encouraged by a bottle of whisky, she had seen Mr. Ross in a dream.

===Chinese===
过了一会儿，这邋遢的老婆子离开了那座乡下破窝，趁着柔和的天光，穿过山丘露水莹莹的肩头。头一天她还不敢去；可到了夜里，仗着一瓶威士忌壮胆，她梦见了罗斯先生（Mr. Ross）。

===Original===
Clarence had built the studio out into the quarry at the back. She looked in first at its window and saw him sleeping there. Always out and about early he was. Picus had left the key outside in the lock. She went in. Clarence woke. There was a pain in his brain that felt like a nut. Before there had been a worm in the nut, but that had gone to sleep. He had felt the nut before. In a day or so there would not even be a nut, certainly not what went before the nut, the worm boring and making a wild pain that made a wild dream, on the edge of whose memory he was living.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯把画室搭了出去，伸进屋后的采石场里。她先凑到画室窗前往里张望，看见他睡在里面。他这人向来起得早、闲不住。皮库斯把钥匙留在外头的锁眼里。她走了进去。克拉伦斯醒了。他脑子里有一处疼，摸上去像一枚坚果。先前坚果里是有条虫的，如今虫睡去了。这枚坚果他从前也觉出来过。再过一天半天，连坚果也不会有了，更别说坚果之前的东西——那条虫钻啊钻，钻出一种狂野的疼，疼出一个狂野的梦；他如今就活在这个梦的记忆边上。

===Original===
“You’re early,” he said﻿—“Get some tea.” And I’m in my clothes. “And mind you put it in with the teaspoon.”

===Chinese===
「你来早了，」他说——「去沏点茶。」好在我还和衣躺着。「记住，要用茶匙量着放。」

===Original===
He went to the well to sluice himself and saw his statue in bits. Looked up for a rockfall from the quarry, which was impossible. Found bits of wood and feathers sticking in the clay and strode back to the kitchen. Heard her clacking that indeed she didn’t know, and that the living-room door was locked.

===Chinese===
他走到井边想冲把脸，却看见自己的雕像碎成一地。抬头去找采石场的落石，可落石根本不可能。又发现黏土里扎着碎木和羽毛，便大步走回厨房。听见她正呱啦呱啦地说她实在什么也不知道，还说起居室的门锁上了。

===Original===
He went round to the front, to the open door, saw where a hare had made her form. Looked in and saw, still sleeping, Picus, Carston, Scylla. He shook his friend’s shoulder gently:

===Chinese===
他绕到前头来，走到敞开的门前，看见一处野兔伏卧出来的窝。往里一望，看见皮库斯、卡斯顿、斯库拉还睡着。他轻轻摇了摇他朋友的肩膀：

===Original===
“Hi, boy, what’s happened?” Picus woke at a touch, pointed outside and rose silently.

===Chinese===
「喂，老弟，出什么事了？」皮库斯一碰就醒，朝外面指了指，悄没声地起了身。

===Original===
“Who’s taken my statue outside and smashed it?”

===Chinese===
「谁把我的雕像搬出去砸了？」

===Original===
“Come out with me. Out and down a bit. A boat’s in. Down at the Lobster Pot they’ll fry us fish.”

===Chinese===
「跟我出来。出去往下走一截。有船进港了。到龙虾锅（Lobster Pot）去，他们会给我们炸鱼吃。」

===Original===
“But I’m not shaved. Can Carston fix up some breakfast for Scylla? Does he know the old woman can’t? What’s happened, lad? You look like a wet Sunday. Headache again?”

===Chinese===
「可我还没刮脸呢。卡斯顿能不能给斯库拉弄顿早饭？他知道那老太婆弄不来吗？到底出什么事了，小伙子？你蔫得像下雨天的礼拜日。又头疼了？」

===Original===
“No. Only you must come on.”

===Chinese===
「没有。只是你必须跟我来。」

===Original===
He dropped sharply down the hill, Clarence behind him. He felt his mouth twist into a sneer. Clarence the kindly host, the country-gentleman making the best of a cottage and lack of retainers. Then that contempt was unjust. The unfamiliar concept of justice and injustice stuck and was accepted.

===Chinese===
他顺着山势陡然往下走，克拉伦斯跟在后头。他觉出自己的嘴扭出一个讥诮。克拉伦斯，那位殷勤的东道，那位把一座村舍和手下无人将就到最好处的乡绅。随即又觉出这蔑视不公道。公正与不公道——这套生疏的概念扎了进来，被接受了。

===Original===
Then that punishment was on him. He had to operate on Clarence, not prick and bewilder. Had to undo his arts, his graces, his wit. Clarence’s first protection would be to turn on him. A man of perverse and subtle mind, he would be quick to distort to save himself. Making me think.

===Chinese===
接着，那惩罚落到了他自己头上。他必须给克拉伦斯动手术，不能只是刺一刺、搅一搅。必须拆掉他的技艺、他的风致、他的才思。克拉伦斯头一重自卫，必是反咬他一口。这样一个人，心思乖张而精细，为求自保，歪曲起来是很快的。让我想想。

===Original===
Then at the Lobster Pot he acquired immediately tea, butter, bread, jam, and the first batch of the landlady’s personal fish.

===Chinese===
于是在龙虾锅，他立刻置办到了茶、黄油、面包、果酱，还有女房东亲手炸的头一拨鱼。

===Original===
“Damned hungry,” said Clarence, helping him to the one small real sole.

===Chinese===
「饿死了。」克拉伦斯说着，把那份唯一货真价实的小鳎鱼往他盘里让。

===Original===
Ouf. Why did Clarence look so lovingly at him, when for the first time on record he threw it back on to his plate? He did not like being hurt. The others were more used to being hurt. Now that he had to hurt, he did not want to. (A reaction impossible to Carston, for whose race sadism is not fun but a serious expression.)

===Chinese===
呼。克拉伦斯为什么那样一往情深地望着他，偏偏在他有生头一回把鱼扔回盘子里的当口？他不喜欢受伤害。旁人受伤害受得更惯。如今轮到他必须伤人，他倒不情愿了。（这种反应卡斯顿是断不可能有的；对他的种族来说，施虐从来不是玩笑，而是一种郑重其事的表达。）

===Original===
Ow. How much he cared for Clarence, for sport and adventure and work shared. More than them all. Except Scylla. Because that love was shot through with something like an arrow and the feather of a bird. The blood on her white shoulder, the rose feet and feather of a bird.

===Chinese===
哎。他有多在乎克拉伦斯啊，在乎那份共享的游戏、冒险和劳作。胜过所有人。除了斯库拉。因为那份爱被什么东西射穿了，像一支箭带着一茎鸟翎。她白肩上的血，玫瑰色的脚，和一茎鸟翎。

===Original===
*Ai.* His breath came on different sighs. One more river to cross. To be sure that he did not act, in this, in any way like his father. That understood, he left his desperate network of light and dark and gave himself up: neither to Clarence, nor to fear: but to a space full of clear forms and veritable issues. What he must do in order not to be any way like his father. Was that to give himself to Scylla? He had met her on his path. So. The bird’s thought darted into a song:

===Chinese===
*唉。*他的气息落在一声声不同的叹息上。还有一条河要渡。要拿准自己在这件事上，行事没有一丝一毫像他父亲。想明白了这一层，他便离开那张光与暗织成的绝望罗网，把自己交了出去：不交给克拉伦斯，也不交给恐惧：而是交给一片盈满清明形体与真实题旨的空阔。为了不像他父亲，他必须去做的是什么。是把自己交给斯库拉吗？他在自己的路上遇见了她。就这样。鸟的念头一闪，化作一支歌：

===Original===
> So every way the wind blows this sweetie goes in the South.

===Chinese===
> 就这样，风往哪边吹，这小亲亲就往哪边去，在南方。

===Original===
While Clarence saw an assurance like maturity drawing itself in the set of the head and the subtle mouth.

===Chinese===
而克拉伦斯看见，一种近乎成熟的笃定，正在那头颅的姿势和精微的嘴角里显出形来。

===Original===
Picus looked for a moment out to sea, and began:

===Chinese===
皮库斯朝海面望了片刻，开口道：

===Original===
“What have you been doing the last three days, since I went off to Tambourne?”

===Chinese===
「我去了坦伯恩之后，这三天你都做了些什么？」

===Original===
“Stayed on with Ross a bit. Walked over. Got the place shined up.”

===Chinese===
「陪罗斯多待了几天。一路走了过来。把屋子拾掇得亮亮堂堂。」

===Original===
“It must have been pretty hot?”

===Chinese===
「一定够热的吧？」

===Original===
“The sun bored like worms into your head.”

===Chinese===
「太阳跟虫子似的往你脑袋里钻。」

===Original===
“What happened then?”

===Chinese===
「后来呢？」

===Original===
“There was lots to do, but I found the nights, short as they are, damned long. When it isn’t dark and it’s going to get dark and you listen out. You know. But in places like this you can never tell what day which happened.”

===Chinese===
「事情多的是可做，可我发现这些夜晚，短归短，却长得要命。天还没黑、眼见又要黑下去的时候，你竖起耳朵听着动静。你懂的。可在这种地方，你永远说不清哪件事是哪一天出的。」

===Original===
“What was yesterday like?”

===Chinese===
「昨天是什么光景？」

===Original===
Clarence screwed round, ever so little equivocally.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯拧过身子来，带出一丝极细微的支吾。

===Original===
“I sort of remember that something rather miserable happened in the morning. Might have been a letter.” And quickly﻿—“But I can’t account at all for the statue being in bits. I know you’ll say it doesn’t matter what happens to my work, but Ross liked it. You said you liked it yourself﻿—”

===Chinese===
「我影影绰绰记得，早上出了件挺凄惨的事。兴许是一封信。」随即又急急地——「可雕像碎成这样，我怎么也解释不通。我知道你会说我的作品落个什么下场无所谓，可罗斯喜欢它。你自己也说过喜欢——」

===Original===
“Looksey,” said Picus, tenderly﻿—“you’ve got to know, you know. You went off the deep-end again.”

===Chinese===
「听我说，」皮库斯温声道——「你得知道，你是知道的。你又发作了一回。」

===Original===
“You mean I smashed it myself?”

===Chinese===
「你是说，是我自个儿砸了它？」

===Original===
“No. I did a bit. I mean I broke it. I felt I had to.”

===Chinese===
「不。我砸了一小半。我是说，是我砸的。我觉得非砸不可。」

===Original===
Clarence listened gravely, his eyes still altering their angle.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯肃然听着，眼珠还在改换角度。

===Original===
“Well, if you thought it bad, that’s that. But you’ve taken so much of my life, do you think you should do in my work, also?”

===Chinese===
「好吧，你既然觉得它坏，那就这样吧。可你已经拿走了我那么多生命，你觉得，还该连我的作品也结果掉吗？」

===Original===
“It wasn’t for that. You’ve forgotten about yesterday. You said something miserable happened, and it did.” (Now are his eyes shifting memory or madness?) “Remember when you thought you were a nun? This time you must have thought you were Apollo, or a Roman official with an early Christian. There was some story in town, and Lydia sent you a letter. And Scylla came down here on purpose to clear it up and fetch you along. She found you shooting at me, and you tied her up and shot her. Carston came over and probably saved her life. I followed, and by then you’d got through your fit and were asleep. She isn’t badly hurt. That’s what happened. Why I brought you down here. And you can kick me for my fantasies and tempers. Half the blame’s on me.” Is this going to release me? Have I been looking for that? *This sweetie goes?*

===Chinese===
「不是为了那个。你把昨天忘了。你说出了件凄惨的事，也真出了。」（此刻他眼里转的是记忆还是疯癫？）「记得你当自己是修女的那回吗？这一回，你准是把自己当成阿波罗了，或者一个看守早期基督徒的罗马官员。城里传了桩闲话，莉迪亚给你来了封信。斯库拉特意下来把话说清，接你回去。她撞见你在朝我放箭，你就把她捆起来，射她。卡斯顿赶了过来，多半救了她一条命。我随后到，那时你已发作完毕，睡熟了。她伤得不重。事情就是这样。这也是我把你带下这儿来的缘故。你尽管冲我的幻想和脾气踢我一脚。一半的错在我。」这样能放得过我吗？我一直找的，就是这个吗？*这小亲亲这就去了？*

===Original===
“It’s another of your stories,” Clarence said.

===Chinese===
「这又是你编的一套故事。」克拉伦斯说。

===Original===
“Go up and see.”

===Chinese===
「上去看看。」

===Original===
“Excuse to put me in an asylum. I get you.”

===Chinese===
「找由头把我送进疯人院。我明白你。」

===Original===
“Balls, man. The old man at Tambourne, the vicar, I mean c’d explain. Tell us what we could do.”

===Chinese===
「胡扯，伙计。坦伯恩那位老人家，我是说牧师，他能说明白。会告诉咱们该怎么办。」

===Original===
“His orders aren’t even valid.”

===Chinese===
「他下的训谕根本就不作数。」

===Original===
“Don’t know what you mean. Go and see. You tied her with the lariat. You shot a gull to wing your arrows. There’s one struck her shoulder and her side. After Carston had cut her down, I smashed myself up with the axe. Sort of apology.”

===Chinese===
「不知道你什么意思。去看看。你用套索捆的她。你射下一只海鸥给箭装翎。有一支中了她的肩和肋。卡斯顿把她解下来之后，我拿斧头把我自己的像砸了。算是赔个不是。」

===Original===
“Did I really shoot a gull?”

===Chinese===
「我真射了一只海鸥？」

===Original===
“You shot her till she fainted.”

===Chinese===
「你射她，一直把她射得晕死过去。」

===Original===
“Did I drag your statue out and shoot you?”

===Chinese===
「我把你的雕像拖出去，还射了你？」

===Original===
“Picked me out carefully.”

===Chinese===
「还细细地挑中了我。」

===Original===
“It was the best thing I’ve done, but I haven’t hurt you, boy?”

===Chinese===
「那是我干过的最漂亮的一件事，可我没伤着你吧，老弟？」

===Original===
“You threw Scylla. She cut her head on a stone. Carston took an arrow out below her left breast. She was pinned to me by her shoulder﻿—”

===Chinese===
「你把斯库拉掼了出去。她的头磕在石头上，破了。卡斯顿从她左乳底下取出一支箭。她让肩上那支钉在了我身上——」

===Original===
“Getting kick out of it, aren’t you?”

===Chinese===
「你从这上头找着乐子了，是不是？」

===Original===
“Go and see.”

===Chinese===
「去看看。」

===Original===
“And face that dumb fool Carston.”

===Chinese===
「还得去对着那个闷葫芦卡斯顿。」

===Original===
“Look at Scylla.”

===Chinese===
「去看看斯库拉。」

===Original===
“Where’s the cup?”

===Chinese===
「杯子在哪儿？」

===Original===
“You put it in the well.”

===Chinese===
「你把它放进井里了。」

===Original===
Silence, while Picus watched the bright, brown close-set eyes turn this way and that. Never into his eyes. Never out to sea. Over his shoulder, at the fish-bones, into his cup.

===Chinese===
沉默。皮库斯看着那双亮亮的、生得凑近的棕色眼睛滴溜溜转来转去。从不落进他的眼睛。从不望向海。只越过他的肩头，落在鱼骨上、杯子里。

===Original===
“It’s a clever way of breaking things up. You say that you came later?”

===Chinese===
「这拆散一局的好手段。你说你是后来才到的？」

===Original===
“An hour. You were lying in the studio. You were saying something about worms and time and cups. I think you know, that you actually *did* a dream.”

===Chinese===
「晚了一个钟头。你正躺在画室里。嘴里念叨着什么虫子啦、时间啦、杯子啦。我想你是知道的——你是真的*做*成了一场梦。」

===Original===
His simplicity amazed Clarence: made him thoughtful.

===Chinese===
他这份率真叫克拉伦斯吃惊：也叫他沉思。

===Original===
“I am sure that you’re letting Carston take you in. You’re simple sometimes, bless you. You weren’t there. He found me a bit off my head and I went in and fell asleep. As a matter of fact, I don’t remember yesterday.

===Chinese===
「我敢担保，是你让卡斯顿给蒙了。你有时候真是单纯，老天保佑。你当时不在场。他发现我有点神志失常，我就进了屋，睡着了。说实在的，昨天我一点不记得。

===Original===
“I’m going up. You might stay and see if that net we broke is mended and follow. I even think you believe this, but it is more likely to be some revenge of Carston’s﻿—”

===Chinese===
「我这就上去。你不妨留下，看看咱们弄破的那张网补好了没有，再跟上来。我甚至觉得连你自己都信了；可这多半是卡斯顿的什么报复——」

===Original===
It was suggested to him, fretfully and quite unjustly, that Carston could neither improvise a bow and arrows, throw a leaded cord, or hit a sitting haystack. And it was painful on their present undertaking to see Clarence stride off to clear up the affair. Picus fidgeted about the beach and threw unsuccessful ducks and drakes. One suddenly skimmed out. So much for that.

===Chinese===
有人焦躁地、而且全然不公地向他点明：卡斯顿既不会现做一张弓、一副箭，掷不出一根坠铅的绳套，也射不中一座立在原处的干草垛。看克拉伦斯大步流星走开去把这件事弄个水落石出——就眼下这桩共同的事务而言——实在叫人不好受。皮库斯在滩上转来转去不安生，打着一个个不成样子的水漂。忽然有一枚擦着水面飞了出去。就这样吧。

===Original===
> And every way the wind blows this sweetie goes in the South.

===Chinese===
> 而无论风往哪边吹，这小亲亲都往那边去，在南方。

---

===Original===
Clarence followed him full of anger, full of breakfast up the hill. Then, as he climbed and felt the strengthening sun, of a kind of catchy fear. The nut was shrinking. How was he to persuade Carston that they had not been entertained by a sadist? The business faintly excited him. With each step he felt the sun’s menace. He wanted to be alone under the cool thatch and whittle at a mazer he was making to hold punch at parties. A present for Scylla.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯跟着他往山上走，满腔的怒火，满肚子的早饭。随后，越往上爬，越觉出太阳渐渐发了力，心里换成了一种黏人的恐惧。坚果在缩小。他要怎样才能说服卡斯顿，让他相信招待他们的并不是一个施虐狂？这件事又隐隐让他兴奋。每迈一步，他都觉出太阳的威吓。他想独自待在阴凉的茅草顶下，削他手里那只木碗——宴会上盛潘趣酒用的。一件给斯库拉的礼物。

===Original===
The night before Carston had thrown out the half-plucked gull over the cliff. It had caught on a bush, and almost at the door Clarence saw the torn white rags. He stood a long time while the dew dried.

===Chinese===
头天夜里，卡斯顿把那只拔了半边毛的海鸥抛下了悬崖。它挂在一丛灌木上，克拉伦斯在离门几乎一步之遥的地方看见了那些撕烂的白布条。他站了很久，一直站到露水收干。

===Original===
“I suppose I thought she was the bird.” The whole memory came back. The nut in his head dissolved like a drop of wax. His skull filled with pure memory.

===Chinese===
「我大概是把她当成那只鸟了。」全部的记忆涌了回来。他脑子里的那枚坚果像一滴蜡似的消融了。颅骨里灌满了纯粹的回忆。

===Original===
The figure he had cut with his excuses. How save his reputation for sanity? With Picus. With all of them?

===Chinese===
他已用自己的遁辞雕成了眼前这副人形。他还怎么保得住神志清明的名声？在皮库斯跟前。在他们所有人跟前？

===Original===
What does one do when one has done a thing like that?

===Chinese===
一个人做下了那样的事，之后该做什么？

===Original===
How act a repentance unfelt as yet, only betrayal by time, chance, magic, interfering friends, offending gods?

===Chinese===
一场悔意至今未曾真感到的忏悔，要怎么演出来？——事情败露，不过是败在时间、机缘、魔法、爱管闲事的朋友、被触犯的众神手里。

===Original===
The gull, held on a twig by a pinion-feather, loosened, balanced a second, and vanished over the cliff.

===Chinese===
那只海鸥让一根拨风羽挂在细枝上，松了脱，悬空稳了一瞬，消失在悬崖外头。

===Original===
“I must follow,” he said, “now.”

===Chinese===
「我必须跟着去，」他说，「现在就去。」

===Original===
The sun had thrown his shadow to the threshold. Carston saw it and said nothing, afraid, helping Scylla to splash in water smoky with most of her host’s scents, combing the blood out of her hair. Sweet to have her safe and look after her. Then he heard her say, “There’s Clarence.” She had seen him at the cliffs edge. Carston held that he waited to be seen, but in truth he had forgotten Carston and Scylla. Carefully looking not down but out to sea. Taking a last pull at memories there.

===Chinese===
太阳已把他的影子掷到门槛上。卡斯顿看见了，一言不发，心里害怕，正扶着斯库拉在水里扑腾——那水里雾蒙蒙溶着她男主人多半瓶的香水——替她把发里的血一梳一梳清出来。她平安了，由他照看，这滋味是甜的。随后他听见她说：「克拉伦斯来了。」她已在崖边望见了他。卡斯顿认定他是存心等人瞧见，其实他早把卡斯顿和斯库拉忘在了脑后。他小心地不看脚下，只望着海。在那儿对种种记忆做最后一番啜饮。

===Original===
Of Picus. Of the band he had grown up with. Of war, whose issues he had found too simple. Of their spiritual adventure he had not been equal to. Of the fool he had made of himself. The revenge his death would be. Not stay to be called Judas. *And bring our souls to His high city.*

===Chinese===
想皮库斯。想同他一起长大的那伙人。想那场战争——他早觉得它的是非题未免太简单。想那场他力有未逮的精神冒险。想他把自己弄成了怎样一个傻瓜。想他的死将成全怎样一场复仇。绝不住下来，等着被人喊作犹大（Judas）。*并将我们的灵魂带往他的高城。*

===Original===
He took a step to the edge. Scylla jumped off the divan, and with her hand at her side, ran out to him.

===Chinese===
他朝崖缘迈了一步。斯库拉从长榻上跳下来，一只手按在肋侧，向他跑了过去。

===Original===
“Clarence, come in.”

===Chinese===
「克拉伦斯，进来。」

===Original===
She had hold of him as he had held her.

===Chinese===
她攥住了他，像他当日攥住她那样。

===Original===
“What’r’you doing out in your chemise?”

===Chinese===
「你穿着贴身衬衣出来做什么？」

===Original===
“You know. Come in.”

===Chinese===
「你知道的。进来。」

===Original===
“Get me,” she said to Carston, “a wooden bowl in the studio, and a green baize roll of tools.” She lay down again. Clarence paced about once or twice, and sat down beside her.

===Chinese===
「给我拿来，」她对卡斯顿说，「画室里那只木碗，还有那卷绿呢裹着的工具。」她又躺下了。克拉伦斯踱了一两个来回，在她身边坐下。

===Original===
“There’s going to be an awful party over at the house. Felix is bringing home a Russian.”

===Chinese===
「宅子里就要办一场了不得的宴会了。菲利克斯（Felix）要带一个俄国人回家来。」

===Original===
He said:

===Chinese===
他说：

===Original===
“I’m not mad. No need to go on like that. I remember. The bird made me.”

===Chinese===
「我没有疯。用不着这样没完没了。我记得的。是那只鸟支使我干的。」

===Original===
“Did you think I was it?”

===Chinese===
「你当时以为我就是它？」

===Original===
“No. There was a letter, and the sun and you know my head.”

===Chinese===
「不是。有那么一封信，有太阳，再说你也知道我这脑袋。」

===Original===
“Look,” she said, and pulled off the handkerchief that tied her shoulder﻿—“and my head is cut and my side. It was partly my fault that Lydia wrote to you. Go on carving while we talk.”

===Chinese===
「看，」她说着，扯下扎在肩上的那方手帕——「我的头破了，肋也破了。莉迪亚给你写信，一半怪我。咱们说话，你只管刻你的。」

===Original===
He did as she told him. Carston watched them. Like an idyll: a young lover making a present for his sweetheart, sitting on her bed. A harrow of wild geese with their necks out at flight. A border of fish.

===Chinese===
他依着她的话做了。卡斯顿望着他们两个。像一幅田园小景：年轻的恋人坐在她床边，给心上人做一件礼物。一队列阵而飞的野雁，只只引着颈。一道鱼纹的边饰。

===Original===
“It ought to be set. Can you work in silver, Clarence? We might melt down that atrocious salver﻿—”

===Chinese===
「该给它镶一道口。你做得了银活吗，克拉伦斯？咱们可以把那只难看透顶的大银盘熔了——」

===Original===
Insufferable to be hushed like this. He preferred Carston glaring at him, wondering if he should get the gun. Picus came in.

===Chinese===
被这样哄抚过去，实在忍无可忍。他倒宁愿卡斯顿虎视眈眈瞪着他，盘算着要不要去取枪。皮库斯进来了。

===Original===
“D’you know now?”

===Chinese===
「现在你知道了？」

===Original===
“Yes. And I’m not fool enough to imagine that there’s any apology or excuse. Or forgiveness that isn’t from duty or impulse. You can have Scylla.”

===Chinese===
「知道了。我也还没傻到指望什么道歉或者开脱。也没傻到指望那不出于责任或冲动的宽恕。斯库拉归你。」

===Original===
“I knew you’d take it wrong,” said Picus. “We’re not talking about beds and we know who we’ll sleep with. What you ought to know is﻿—”

===Chinese===
「我就知道你会把话听拧，」皮库斯说。「我们谈的不是床笫之事，各人跟谁睡，大家心里都有数。你该知道的是——」

===Original===
“Look here,” said Carston. “You’ve had a touch of the sun. We’ll grant that. Scylla has a fool female friend in London, fool enough to be in love with you. Wrote you a spiteful letter you lap up. Scylla comes down to explain it and comfort your feelings, and you try to kill her by torture. I know you were mad. If you don’t pull yourself together and try and face it, everyone will know you were mad; for you’ll do it again outside your home circle. The world won’t make delicate excuses for you, you spoilt, hysterical, self-pitying, self-centred, uninventive, incompetent son of a bitch.”

===Chinese===
「你听着，」卡斯顿说。「你是中了暑。这一点我们认。斯库拉在伦敦有个犯傻的闺中朋友，傻到爱上了你。给你写了封恶毒的信，你还受用得了不得。斯库拉下来解释这事，安慰你的情绪，你倒好，用酷刑想弄死她。我知道你当时疯了。你要不振作起来，试着去面对它，人人都会知道你疯了；因为出了自家这个小圈子，你还会再来一回。这世界不会细声细气替你找台阶的，你这个惯坏的、歇斯底里的、自怜自艾的、自我中心的、没点子、没本事的狗娘养的。」

===Original===
“Not uninventive,” said Scylla, “but you’d better try something else.”

===Chinese===
「不是没点子，」斯库拉说，「不过你最好换个法子试试。」

===Original===
“I’m taking you over to Tambourne right away. We’ll start now, and you can wait at the inn while I get a car. The old parson there is the company you need. You can come back to Gault, if they want you, when you’ve got your senses back.”

===Chinese===
「我这就送你去坦伯恩。现在就动身，我去张罗车的时候，你在客栈里等着。那边那位老牧师，正是你需要做伴的人。等你神志恢复了，他们若还肯要你，你再回高尔特来。」

===Original===
Picus nodded. “We are all for you, Carston.”

===Chinese===
皮库斯点了点头。「我们都向着你，卡斯顿。」

===Original===
“All of us,” said Scylla.

===Chinese===
「我们都是。」斯库拉说。

===Original===
“Don’t say,” he answered, “that if I stay here much longer, I shall be one of you. Because I never shall, and I don’t want to be.”

===Chinese===
「可别说，」他答道，「我再在这儿待久些，就会变成你们一路的人。因为我永远不会，我也不想。」

===Original===
“Our house is your house,” said Scylla.

===Chinese===
「我们的家就是你的家。」斯库拉说。

===Original===
“Besides,” said Picus, “did you ever enjoy a summer more?”

===Chinese===
「再说，」皮库斯道，「你还更痛快地享受过哪个夏天吗？」

===Original===
“Hasn’t it been better than a movie? Leave Clarence at Tambourne and come over and look at Felix’s find.”

===Chinese===
「这不比电影还好看？把克拉伦斯撂在坦伯恩，过来瞧瞧菲利克斯寻来的宝贝。」

===Original===
In his heart he knew he would not. Though there was continuity in this adventure, a circle like the design on Clarence’s mazer, a ring near to a magic ring, he knew that nothing would induce him to go back to that poverty and pride, cant and candour, raw flesh and velvet; into that dateless, shiftless, shifting, stable and unstable Heartbreak House. Not for a bit. Off to Paris on his own folk-adventure. In his last moments with them, looking at Clarence’s bowl, he saw the changes in things.

===Chinese===
他心里明白自己不会去。这场冒险自有它的绵延，自成一环，像克拉伦斯木碗上的纹样，一枚近似魔法之环的环；可他也明白，无论什么都不能诱他回到那种贫穷与骄傲、伪善与坦白、生肉与天鹅绒里去；回到那座无年无月、无着无落、迁流不定、又稳又不稳的伤心之家（Heartbreak House）里去。一时一刻也不。他要独自上巴黎去，走他一场自己的民间冒险。同他们在一起的最后时刻，望着克拉伦斯那只碗，他看见了事物里的种种变化。

===Original===
There had been an apple once. There had been an apple tree. When it gave no more apples, it had made fire, and a slice of its trunk had become a bowl cut out into birds. The bowl unless it was turned into fire again, would stop growing and last forever. Things that came out of time, and were stopped; could be made over into another sort of time.

===Chinese===
从前有过一只苹果。从前有过一棵苹果树。到它再也结不出苹果的时候，它化成了火，树干上截下的一片成了一只碗，碗沿雕出一只只鸟。这碗除非再化成火，便会停止生长，存留到永远。那些从时间里长出、又被止住的东西；可以改铸成另一种时间。

===Original===
Clarence sat silent, a tear or so falling, shame and anger mounting. Once away, he would leave Carston; would not go to Tambourne. He would go to Tambourne because he must have somewhere to hide. The old parson might have comfort for him while Picus was with Scylla, and she enjoyed the reward of warriors. She and Picus alone together, playing at happy warriors.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯默默坐着，落下一两滴泪，羞耻和怒气一阵阵往上涌。一旦脱开身，他就会撇下卡斯顿；本不愿去坦伯恩。可他还是会去坦伯恩，因为他总得有个地方藏身。皮库斯伴着斯库拉的时候，那位老牧师或许能有几句安慰给他，而她呢，正享用战士的犒赏。她和皮库斯单独在一处，扮演一对快活的战士。

===Original===
If there was nothing for him at Tambourne, there would still be Picus’s father, a fine story to pick over together.

===Chinese===
要是坦伯恩那边没有他的份儿，至少还有皮库斯的父亲在，两人可以一同把一个好听的故事翻来覆去地咀嚼。

===Original===
He said:

===Chinese===
他说：

===Original===
“Perhaps you’ll send my clothes. We must go before midday. I shouldn’t like Carston to have a repetition before he gets me to Tambourne.”

===Chinese===
「或许你们会把我的衣裳捎来。我们必须在正午前动身。我可不想让卡斯顿在把我送到坦伯恩之前，再经一遍重演。」

===Original===
*And bring our souls to His high city.*

===Chinese===
*并将我们的灵魂带往他的高城。*

===Original===
He took Scylla’s hand, remained a moment in her embrace. Carston followed him down the hill.

===Chinese===
他握了斯库拉的手，在她怀里停了片刻。卡斯顿跟着他下了山。

---

===Original===
That afternoon cloud flecks flew over and the wind freshened. Ross and Nanna left the house scented with boiling sugar and took a walk down to the sea. He listened to a comparative history of her jam-making and a sketch of her intentions about the vegetable marrows with the interest he gave to each man on his subject alike. From the cliff above the fisherman’s hut they saw a ketch running before the south wind, straight for the bay.

===Chinese===
那天下午，云斑飞掠而过，风势转劲。罗斯和娜娜（Nanna）离开满屋子熬糖的甜香，散步下到海边。他听她历数历年制果酱的比较史，又听她略陈对西葫芦的种种打算，那份兴致，与他听任何人谈起本行时不相上下。从渔人小屋上方的崖头，他们望见一艘双桅帆船乘着南风疾驶，直奔海湾。

===Original===
“A French boat,” he said, “they’re running her in too close to the reef.” The old nurse shaded her eyes.

===Chinese===
「一条法国船，」他说，「他们抢进得太贴礁石了。」老保姆抬手遮住眼睛。

===Original===
“It’s Mr. Felix steering. In a hurry he is as usual. It’s a nice way to bring his friend home.” Silence. A Russian brought over in a fishing-smack: in a hurry. The ketch made the channel (where the bluff hid it from the coastguards’ telescopes and the sooner the better), and Ross saw Felix and a sailor drop into a dinghy and pull fast for shore. In the stern sat another. They went down to the water’s edge to meet them. A few strokes out, Felix sprang thigh deep in the weed and dragged up the boat till her keel scraped the rocks. He embraced Ross, turned and gave the boy a hand to spring to shore.

===Chinese===
「是菲利克斯先生在掌舵。还是那样急慌慌的。这可真是个把朋友接回家的好法子。」一阵沉默。用一条捕鱼帆船捎带过来的一个俄国人：急急忙忙。双桅船进了水道（海岬在那儿正好挡住缉私队的望远镜，越快越好），罗斯看见菲利克斯和一个水手跳进小艇，飞快朝岸上划来。艇尾还坐着一个人。他们走到水边去迎接。离岸还有几桨远，菲利克斯跳进齐腿深的海草里，把小艇拽上来，直拽到龙骨刮上礁石。他拥抱了罗斯，又转身伸手，扶那年轻人跃上岸来。

===Original===
“Ross, this is Boris.”

===Chinese===
「罗斯，这位是鲍里斯（Boris）。」

===Original===
“But what made you come this way?”

===Chinese===
「可你们怎么走了这一条道？」

===Original===
He thought that he was looking at something at the same time old and young. A youth he understood. An age he did not. Also that it was worn and tired and sick. And that Felix’s eyes were like dark-blue coals, his step certain, his voice without petulance.

===Chinese===
他觉得自己正看着一件又老又年轻的东西。那份年轻他懂得。那份苍老他不懂。还觉出它磨损了、疲了、病了。还觉出菲利克斯的眼睛像深蓝色的炭，步子笃定，嗓音里没有一丝焦躁。

===Original===
“I had no papers,” said Boris.

===Chinese===
「我没有证件。」鲍里斯说。

===Original===
“We got into a row,” said Felix, “the police raided a café, and we did a bolt. We ran straight into two men up a back street and sent them spinning. One was hurt. Then I saw that it was no good, especially for Boris, and got a car to the coast. Paid up those chaps to bring us and cut back. There’s a third man below to replace Boris in case we were seen from the shore.”

===Chinese===
「我们惹出了一场乱子，」菲利克斯说，「警察突袭了一家咖啡馆，我们撒腿就跑。在后街迎面撞上两个人，把他们打得团团转。有一个挂了彩。随后我看出这样不行，尤其对鲍里斯，就雇了辆汽车到海边。付钱给那几位送我们过来，再折回去。舱里还藏着第三个人，万一从岸上叫人望见，好顶替鲍里斯。」

===Original===
Not bad for Felix. Ross looked again at what he had brought, standing on the tidemark, his back to the water, the ooze soaking his poor shoes. The sailors landed two suitcases.

===Chinese===
对菲利克斯来说，干得不坏。罗斯又打量了一遍他带回来的这件东西：他站在潮痕上，背朝着海，淤泥正浸透他那双寒酸的鞋。水手们卸下两只手提箱。

===Original===
“See here,” he said, “and excuse us. D’you mean you have no papers and no papers you can show﻿—?”

===Chinese===
「是这样，」他说，「恕我们直言。你是说，你没有证件，也拿不出任何可以出示的证件——？」

===Original===
“He’s a White,” cried Felix, “and he lost them.”

===Chinese===
「他是白俄，」菲利克斯嚷道，「证件丢了。」

===Original===
Boris said: “That is exactly so.”

===Chinese===
鲍里斯说：「正是如此。」

===Original===
“And you’re not running dope, or away from any crime worth mentioning?”

===Chinese===
「你也不贩毒，也没有犯下什么值得一提的罪在逃？」

===Original===
“On my honour, no﻿—I need a holiday and your cousin was good enough﻿—”

===Chinese===
「以我的名誉担保，没有——我需要休养一阵，承蒙令表弟好意——」

===Original===
Ross saw that, so far as it went, this was true. The vistas opening were more oblique. He had only to look at that head in its sea-wide aureole, the high forehead and temple-thinned black hair, the slanted cheekbones, and observant green eyes. From the remote east. Out of the sea. Lovely, ugly, helpless, highborn thing. Whipcord and ice and worn out. Wangle him papers in London.

===Chinese===
罗斯看出，就这句话而言，是真话。只是往后续开的前景更为曲折。他只消看看那颗头——一圈让海风吹散的光环罩着，高额头，鬓边见稀的黑发，斜出的颧骨，一双审视的绿眼睛。来自遥远的东方。从海里出来。可爱的、丑陋的、无依无靠的高门第之物。鞭绳、冰，加一身耗损。得在伦敦给他设法弄一纸证件。

===Original===
“Boris, our stranger,” he said. “Our nurse.”

===Chinese===
「鲍里斯，我们的远客。」他说。「我们的保姆。」

===Original===
Boris kissed her hand. They climbed the little cliff path. At the top he began to look around him.

===Chinese===
鲍里斯吻了她的手。他们登上小小的崖径。到了顶上，他开始环顾四周。

===Original===
Out at sea, it had been land, earth under his feet after a night and day’s pitching. Land: an interesting new place. Another people who might have no use for him. Why should they? No longer in doubt, soon there would be food and a bath and fresh linen and bed, he took a look at England. He saw a line of treeless hills, a puzzle of fields; under his feet a pattern of sweet herbs. An arrow of wood they entered, into a tunnel of light where birds broke cover, green even under the feet. A house where the windows were doors and stood open, in front of which a yucca, taller than a man, had opened its single flower-spike. Over the house, a hill turned wall. Into a room where air and bees whispered, honey smelt and the sea. And something he remembered: the smell of fruit bubbling in copper pans, in a kitchen﻿—a child with his nurse﻿—in a country-house, in Russia, in a pine forest.

===Chinese===
在海上时，它一直是陆地；颠簸了一夜一天之后，此刻泥土就在他脚下。陆地：一个有趣的新地方。又一个也许用不着他的民族。凭什么用得着他呢？疑虑既去，很快就有饭吃、有澡洗、有干净衬衣和床铺了，他便放眼看了看英格兰。他看见一线不长树的丘山，一片谜阵似的田畴；脚底下是一幅香草的图纹。他们走进一支木头箭，走进一条光的隧道，鸟儿从掩蔽处惊起，连脚底下都是绿的。一座窗即是门、门洞大开的房子，房前一株高过人的丝兰，绽开了它独一枝花穗。房子上方，一座山化成了墙。走进一个房间，那里空气与蜜蜂低语，闻得见蜂蜜和海。还有一样他记得的东西：果子在铜锅里咕嘟冒泡的气味，在一间厨房里——一个孩子和他的保姆——在一座乡间宅邸里，在俄国，在一片松林里。

## Carston / 卡斯顿

===Original===
He was struggling with the branch line of a remote English railway. He got to a place where people changed, and was in the mood to bear with the proceedings of another century.

===Chinese===
他正跟英国一条偏远铁路的支线较着劲。他到了一处人人换乘的地方，心境恰好，受得住那另一个世纪的一套流程。

===Original===
He had plenty of them before he reached the village called Tambourne. Plenty of fine old women in black-beaded bodices, one button always missing where the strain came over the breasts. Plenty of young livestock being shifted up and down the line. Plenty of the porters’ family party. Plenty of a plate of macaroons locked alone in a glass box in a deserted refreshment room.

===Chinese===
在抵达那个名叫坦伯恩的村子之前，这些他都领教了个够。有的是穿缀黑珠紧身衣的体面老妇人，胸口吃紧处总缺一颗扣子。有的是沿线来回倒腾的幼畜。有的是搬运工拖家带口的一大家子。有的是孤零零一盘蛋白杏仁饼，锁在一间冷清茶点室的玻璃柜里。

===Original===
Plenty of superb trees, and white nettle-scented dust. At the inn called the Star at Tambourne, plenty of regret for Nanna’s fine darned linen and China tea. A night of stars and bats came very slowly. Once out of the wood and away from his relations he asked himself why in Christ’s name he had come to see old Mr. Tracy. An ancient of days was living a stroll away from him at Tambourne House. He fetched the cup from his suitcase and put it on the red baize parlour table, a dumb circle of pale green. Why couldn’t the thing speak? Just once. Dumb was the word for it. He got rather tight all by himself, but without inspiration. He would have to go and call, have to go call. All up that yellow drive by himself.

===Chinese===
见够了气派的大树，闻够了带荨麻气息的白尘。在坦伯恩的星宿旅馆里，他又生出够多的懊悔——懊恼娜娜那些织补得精细的亚麻布不在手边，中国茶也不在。一个星斗与蝙蝠之夜，姗姗来迟。一旦出了树林、离开了亲戚们，他便自问：究竟是出于什么鬼原因，他要跑来看老特雷西先生？一位亘古常在者就住在坦伯恩宅里，离他不过散步一程。他从手提箱里取出那只杯，放在红呢台面的客厅桌上——一圈哑然的淡绿。这东西怎么就不能开口？哪怕就一回。哑然，用它形容正合适。他独自一人喝得有几分醉，却毫无灵感。他总得去登门拜会，非去不可。独自一人，把那条黄色车道一路走到头。

## Clarence and Scylla / 克拉伦斯与斯库拉

===Original===
Scylla slept at Starn. She overslept. A terrific heat had sprung up, and made her feel that there was danger in approaching the hills.

===Chinese===
斯库拉睡在斯塔恩。她睡过了头。腾腾的酷热起来了，让她觉得朝山丘那边去有危险。

===Original===
Neither Ross nor Clarence had been seen at Starn, only Nanna had driven in on the baker’s cart to conduct her favourite campaign about the quality of preserving sugar.

===Chinese===
在斯塔恩，罗斯和克拉伦斯一个也没露面，只有娜娜坐着面包房的货车进城，去进行她最钟爱的一场运动——为果酱糖的质量讨个说法。

===Original===
“She just wouldn’t listen to me, ma’am. All she ’ud say was that she wouldn’t have you or your raspberries poisoned by what I’d sent.” The grocer’s wife told her.

===Chinese===
“她就是听不进我的话，太太。她翻来覆去只一句：她可不能让我送去的糖，害了您，害了您的木莓。”杂货店老板娘这样告诉她。

===Original===
So Nanna was making jam. Felix was partial, especially to raspberry jam. Russians put it in their tea. It was after lunch that she discovered that there was not a car to be had, and also took a lift off the baker down the valley to Tollerdown. She bumped and swayed over the flint-dressed road, the white dust powdering her, the overwhelming sun bearing her down, until the driver pulled up at the valley’s end, an earshot from the sea, under the hill.

===Chinese===
于是娜娜做起了果酱。菲利克斯偏爱这一口，尤其偏爱木莓酱。俄国人还往茶里搁它。过了午饭，她才发现一辆汽车也雇不着，便又搭了面包房的便车，顺山谷而下到托勒当去。她在铺燧石的路上一路颠簸摇晃，白尘扑了满身，烈日当头压下来，直到赶车的在谷底停车——山丘底下，离海只隔一段听得见涛声的路。

===Original===
Vast its burnt gold desert shoulder rose beside her, the ribbon path bleached and crumbling. She went up. Struggle with fire and earth and steepness upset her physically: her arms were red, her neck beaded with sweat, her chemise stuck to her skin. Poor nymphs of Artemis. What complexion could stand it? That was why they were painted hunting in woods. Halfway up she sank on a stone and fanned herself with her hat. Remembered another walk, to Starn. She feared that Clarence had seen her, was sulking inside instead of coming to meet her. That was sad. She remembered how once in London she had come to him straight back from Spain, and he had lifted her up and carried her over the threshold, so glad he had been to see her.

===Chinese===
焦金色的、荒漠似的巨大山肩在她身旁拔起，带状的小径晒得发白、酥裂剥落。她往上爬。同烈火、同泥土、同陡峭的搏斗搅坏了她的身子：两条胳膊晒得通红，脖颈缀满汗珠，无袖衬衣贴在皮肤上。可怜的阿尔忒弥斯的女仙们。什么样的容颜经得起这个？难怪人家总画她们在林中行猎。爬到半山，她瘫坐在一块石头上，摘下帽子扇风。想起另一次走路，去斯塔恩的那次。她怕克拉伦斯已经望见她了，正在屋里怄气，不肯出来迎她。那真叫人难过。她想起有一回在伦敦，她刚从西班牙归来就径直去找他，他把她抱起来，抱过了门槛——他见到她，曾那样欢喜。

===Original===
Clarence had not seen her. Unshaved, half-dressed, he was trying to torture the body of Picus, the statue he had done of him in clay. He had dragged it out against the quarry wall and pierced it with arrows of sharpened wood, feathered from a gull he had shot overnight.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯没有望见她。他不曾修面，半披着衣裳，正忙着折磨皮库斯的身体——他用黏土塑的那尊皮库斯像。他把塑像拖出来抵在采石壁上，用削尖的木箭穿透它，箭羽取自他头天夜里打的一只海鸥。

===Original===
Scylla found the door open and went softly in.

===Chinese===
斯库拉见门开着，便轻轻走了进去。

===Original===
“Clarence, I’ve come all this way. Can I have tea?”

===Chinese===
“克拉伦斯，我大老远来的。能喝口茶吗？”

===Original===
He heard the low voice, thought of the gull crying. She saw the bird’s half-plucked body, bloody on the floor, and that there were papers torn in strips and little darts. She turned over a fold and saw her own body, and her cry was more like the gull. Bird-alone in the lonely room. Except for a ghost called Clarence, everything was empty. She thought:

===Chinese===
他听见那低低的嗓音，想起海鸥的啼叫。她看见那只拔了一半毛的鸟，血淋淋地躺在地板上，还看见撕成长条的纸和一支支小飞镖。她掀开一层纸，看见了自己的身体，她的一声惊叫，比海鸥更像海鸥。孤鸟困在孤室里。除了一缕名叫克拉伦斯的幽魂，一切都空空荡荡。她想：

===Original===
Run away: Can’t: Where to? It’s all empty, and my knees shake. And I’m curious. Curious and furious and only my body is afraid.

===Chinese===
逃：逃不掉：往哪儿逃？一切皆空，我双膝打颤。可我又好奇。又好奇又狂怒，只有我的身体在害怕。

===Original===
Clarence wanted to be sure about the bird. He came in slowly, dazed with violence and grief. Bad conscience and fear of making a fool of himself nagged his blazing obsession. He saw Scylla at the door in silhouette, her scarf fluttering off the back of her neck, sweat-darkened curls appliquéd on her forehead, her hat thrown familiarly on a chair, her mouth open.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯要把那只鸟的事弄个明白。他慢慢走进来，被暴戾和悲恸冲得神志发懵。良心的不安、又怕当众出丑，一阵阵啃噬他那团烈焰般的执念。他看见斯库拉立在门口的逆影里：围巾从颈后飘飞，汗浸黑的鬈发贴饰在额前，帽子熟稔地甩在一把椅子上，嘴张着。

===Original===
“Come and look,” he said, and with the fingers of one hand dug into her collarbone, led her through the kitchen into the half-circle of quarry behind.

===Chinese===
“来看看。”他说，一只手的指头抠进她的锁骨，领她穿过厨房，走进屋后采石场的半环之中。

===Original===
She saw Picus in greenish clay, pricked with white feathers. Clarence had made him exactly as he was, a body she had known, for which hers ached.

===Chinese===
她看见皮库斯立在泛绿的黏土里，缀满白色羽翎。克拉伦斯把他塑得分毫不差——一具她熟识的身体，她自己的身体曾为它作痛。

===Original===
“You see,” he said, “I only had what I’d made of him to do it to.”

===Chinese===
“你瞧，”他说，“我手上只有我亲手塑出的他，只好拿它来下手。”

===Original===
There was an arrow through his throat, and his head had not fallen forward.

===Chinese===
一支箭穿过他的喉咙，而他的头没有向前垂落。

===Original===
“You’re going down the well, where the cup came﻿—”

===Chinese===
“你要下井去，那只杯就是从井里上来的——”

===Original===
“Why, Clarence?”

===Chinese===
“为什么，克拉伦斯？”

===Original===
“Best place for you, my fancy-girl. If there’s enough water, you’ll drown. If there isn’t, and I don’t think there is, you’ll break every bone in your body.”

===Chinese===
“再合适不过的去处了，我的美人儿。水够深，你就淹死。水不够——我看是不够——你就摔断浑身的骨头。”

===Original===
She could run like a lapwing, but he could run fast. She was strong as a tree-cat, but he could tear her in two.

===Chinese===
她能跑得像田凫，可他也跑得快。她壮得像只野猫，可他能把她撕成两半。

===Original===
“I came to bring you to Picus. He does not want you to be alone on Tollerdown. He is at Tambourne. Lydia sent you a silly letter because” (get his vanity if you can) “she is so in love with you that she’s mad.”

===Chinese===
“我是来领你去见皮库斯的。他不愿你独自留在托勒当。他在坦伯恩。莉迪亚给你写那封蠢信，是因为”（能拿住他的虚荣就拿住）“她爱你爱得发了疯。”

===Original===
“And so are you, it seems. Gods! I’m a lucky chap. Unfortunately, Picus doesn’t join the harem. He doesn’t like me any more.

===Chinese===
“看来你也是。诸神啊！我真是个走运的家伙。可惜皮库斯不肯进这后宫。他再也不喜欢我了。

===Original===
“Going to marry me, are you? You shall in a way. I mean to follow you down the well.”

===Chinese===
“你要嫁给我，是吗？从某种意义上说，你会如愿的。我打算跟着你一块儿下井。”

===Original===
“Picus is at Tambourne, waiting for you.”

===Chinese===
“皮库斯在坦伯恩，等着你。”

===Original===
“In time he will be here again. My body will fetch him.”

===Chinese===
“早晚他还会回到这儿来。我的身体会把他引回来。”

===Original===
“You are the most beautiful man in the world, but you won’t be when they get you up out of the well.”

===Chinese===
“你是世上最美的男人，可等他们把你从井里捞上来，你就不是了。”

===Original===
He took her other shoulder in his fingers, thrusting them into the muscle-hollow under her neck, hurting her. She forgot him exacting, petulant; remembered him long before, beautiful, merry, inventive, good. And cruel now. Stupid cruelty. Cruelty frightened her. She lied:

===Chinese===
他用指头扣住她另一边肩膀，直捣进她颈下的肌窝，弄疼了她。她忘了此刻的他——苛索的、乖戾的；记起很久以前的他——俊美、快活、妙想迭出、心肠好。而如今残忍。愚蠢的残忍。残忍吓坏了她。她撒谎了：

===Original===
“Clarence, I am going to marry Carston﻿—I teased Lydia﻿—” He turned her towards the well.

===Chinese===
“克拉伦斯，我要嫁给卡斯顿了——我只是逗逗莉迪亚——”他把她的身子转向井口。

===Original===
“There will be one less of you bitches to come into our lives.”

===Chinese===
“闯进我们生活的你们这号贱货，就要少一个了。”

===Original===
“We bear you, and I am no stronger in your hands than that bird. Why did you shoot a gull? It isn’t done.” Time seemed very precious. Only a thimbleful left. The well very near. The sun turning a little away from them.

===Chinese===
“我们受着你们，而我落在你手里，不比那只鸟多一分气力。你为什么打死一只海鸥？这种事没人做得出来。”时间显得万分金贵。只剩一顶针的量了。井那么近。太阳微微侧过身去。

===Original===
“Woodpecker,” she shrieked, and flung Clarence off, and ran to the statue. She had been so careful not to say that name, and now saw Clarence hurrying to her, the mournful crazy mask splitting, the mouth turning up, the eyes shooting death at her. And Picus, pierced with arrows, smiled down his sweet equivocation. She heard: “That’ll do better.” He had a cord round his waist. He had cattle-ranched once: that was his lariat. She ran once round the statue. A second later he had thrown her, picked her up half-stunned and tied her against Picus. A black flint had cut her head, a patch of blood began to soak through the moon-fair hair.

===Chinese===
“啄木鸟。”她尖叫一声，甩开克拉伦斯，朝塑像奔去。她一直那样小心，从不说破那个名字，此刻却眼见克拉伦斯向她扑来——那张凄惶疯狂的假面绽裂了，嘴角挑起，双眼向她射出死亡。而皮库斯，身中数箭，朝下绽开他甜美的两可之笑。她听得一句：“这个更管用。”他腰间缠着一根绳。他从前在牧牛场上干过活：那便是他的套索。她绕着塑像跑了一周。一秒钟后，他已把她掼倒，拾起打得半晕的她，把她捆在皮库斯身上。一块黑燧石割破了她的头，一片血渍开始渗进那月光般莹白的头发。

===Original===
Clarence walked back and stood by the kitchen door, fitting an arrow to the string. It ripped the skin on her shoulder and entered the clay. She saw another fly towards her and notch her forearm. Another, and there was a tearing pain below her left breast.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯踱回去，站在厨房门口，往弦上搭箭。箭撕开她肩头的皮肉，扎进黏土。她看见另一支朝她飞来，咬进她的前臂。又一支，左乳之下便是一阵撕裂般的痛。

===Original===
Three instants of pain, set in one of fear. Like a great jewel. Clarence stood by the kitchen door, sharpening an indifferent arrow. She made a supreme effort: not to scream much: not to betray herself. Then a moment of absolute contempt of Clarence. Then of pain. Then, as if she were looking out of a window, into a state, a *clarté* the other side of forgiveness. Not by that route. She fainted.

===Chinese===
三瞬疼痛，嵌在一瞬恐惧里。像一颗巨大的宝石。克拉伦斯立在厨房门口，磨着一支漠然的箭。她拼尽了全力：不大声尖叫：不暴露自己。而后，是对克拉伦斯彻底轻蔑的一瞬。而后，是疼痛的一瞬。而后，仿佛凭窗望出去，望入一种境地，一种宽恕彼岸的澄明（*clarté*）。不走那条路。她晕了过去。

## Clarence / 克拉伦斯

===Original===
At the cottage on Tollerdown, Clarence began a call to order. He had stayed on a night with Ross, the last of them to scatter.

===Chinese===
在托勒当的小屋里，克拉伦斯开始着手重整秩序。他同罗斯又多住了一夜，他们俩是众人里最后散伙的。

===Original===
The cottage was indistinguishable from the white, flint-casing chalk rock out of which it was made. God knows when built, its walls sagged in a broken angle with the down-slope. Placed at the mouth of a quarry, he lived naked as could be after his late smothering in trees. A single mountain-ash at the quarry-mouth raised its scarlet against the hot white cutting and the burnt gold grass. From the door a path of glassy flints ran to the cliff’s edge, and joined the valley track. At the angle the cliff broke sheer. Four hundred feet below the sea murmured and tumbled on a beach of round yellow stones. Clarence had set the flint path, chipped and cemented them for a touch of construction in an air-haunted land. A place where no sane man would live. But there was generally water in the well-shaft, and just then a blood-mist of poppies on the stony earth, cultivated to just that level. With an ache that he did not understand was for Versailles, Clarence had swung in.

===Chinese===
这座小屋与砌造它的白垩岩浑然一体、无从分辨——那是一种裹着燧石外壳的白岩。天知道何年所建，屋墙顺着坡势，以一个断裂的角度倾侧着。小屋立在采石场口，他在这里住得再赤裸不过——此前他刚在树丛里闷了个透。采石场口一株独生花楸，把满树猩红举向灼热的白色切壁和烧成金色的草。打门口起，一条燧石小径莹莹生光，直铺到崖边，接上谷中的小路。就在那道折角处，悬崖陡然断落。四百英尺之下，海在滚圆的黄石滩上低语、翻滚。那条燧石小径是克拉伦斯亲手铺的，石头经他凿削、抹浆，给这片空冥中有魂灵出没的土地，添上一丝人造的痕迹。一个神志清明的人绝不肯住的地方。可井筒里多半有水，而恰在那时，多石的地面上腾起一层血雾似的罂粟——耕作恰好到那个份上。怀着一种他并不明白是对凡尔赛的隐痛，克拉伦斯荡进了这里。

===Original===
Inside there was not the mess men are expected to make for themselves. A little art and craft, a little cubism, a little chic, made interesting by one of Picus’s amusements, models of all sorts of ships.

===Chinese===
屋里没有男人独居时该有的那种狼藉。一点美术与手工，一点立体主义，一点雅致，再点缀上皮库斯的一件消遣玩意儿——各式船舶的模型——就趣味盎然了。

===Original===
A viking-boat was a dragon on the sail, a shield-wall along the sides. An Armada-ship, the Virgin all aboard. A lovely proa. A greek galley, and, the first thing Clarence saw, Picus’s card at the mast with “A present for Scylla” on it.

===Chinese===
一艘维京船，帆上一条龙，两舷一道盾墙。一艘无敌舰队的大船，满船皆是圣母。一艘漂亮的马来帆船。一艘希腊桨舰——而克拉伦斯头一眼看见的，是桅杆上皮库斯的卡片，上书“赠斯库拉的礼物”。

===Original===
Now where did Picus find out how to do them? A family mystery. History did not exist for him. He hated the sea. And that black open boat might have come out of an egg hatched at Salamis.

===Chinese===
可皮库斯是从哪儿学来这一手的？一桩家族之谜。历史于他并不存在。他恨海。而那条黑色的敞船，简直像从一枚萨拉米斯孵出的蛋里钻出来的。

===Original===
In the living-room, panelled, and painted a flat jade green, Clarence plumped the scarlet cushions, making everything gay; while in the kitchen, the shepherd’s wife set the water running in the cobble channels, skinned rabbits, polished the blue plates. The particular master was back. Long ago, before she’d married the shepherd, and had ten children and lost but three and taken up with the soldiers before he died and his brother had come along, ’twas the same name, and they’d a disease in common, and she’d still be walking over to the camp at Chard, and though she’d lost her teeth, and the better part of her speech, the lads would be over themselves with beer in their pockets, she’d been kitchenmaid at one of the country houses.

===Chinese===
在嵌着壁板、漆成一色平玉绿的客厅里，克拉伦斯把猩红坐垫拍得蓬松，教满屋都亮堂欢快起来；厨房里，牧羊人的妻子放水淌过鹅卵石槽，剥兔皮，把蓝盘子擦得锃亮。她那位独特的主人回来了。很久以前——在她嫁给牧羊人、生了十个孩子只夭折了三个之前；再往前，在她跟上那些当兵的、一个没等死、他兄弟又续上来之前（还是同一个姓，害的同一种病）——她如今还常走去查德的兵营；虽说牙掉光了，话也丢了泰半，那些小子还是会揣着啤酒，殷勤得不知如何是好——她从前在一家乡间大宅里当过厨房女仆。

===Original===
She could not read or write, and her time must have been different from ours. Mr. Picus gave her port of an evening, but the one she felt about was Ross. This singular *fille de joie*, over fifty, toothless, palateless, type of disreputable peasant hag, when she knew he was in the land, would stand out on the turf and watch for him. Gobble at Clarence, cooking meticulously the food he would not let her touch: ask if Mr. Ross would be coming over. Scylla terrified her. Nanna laughed at her and was called ma’am.

===Chinese===
她不识字，不会写，她的时代想必与我们的不同。皮库斯先生傍晚会赏她一杯波特酒，可她心里牵记的是罗斯。这位奇特的风尘女（*fille de joie*），年过五十，没了牙齿，没了上颚，一副不体面的乡下老妪的样子——只要知道他还在人世，便站到草坡上张望他来。对着克拉伦斯咕咕哝哝，一面一丝不苟地烹煮他不许她沾手的饭菜：一面打听罗斯先生会不会过来。斯库拉吓得她魂飞魄散。娜娜笑话她，倒落得一声“太太”。

===Original===
Clarence strode out, collarless, in riding breeches, to draw water. He looked down into the well, dark fern-ringed tranquillity, round which had happened such a singular little event.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯大步跨出屋去，没戴领子，穿一条马裤，去打水。他俯望井底——一圈暗蕨环拥的静谧，就在那一圈静谧边上，发生过那样一桩小小的奇事。

===Original===
He drew one bucket after another, and sluiced them over his body, branded with shrapnel and bullet and bayonet thrust.

===Chinese===
他打上一桶又一桶，兜头浇遍全身——那身体上烙着弹片、枪弹和刺刀的印记。

===Original===
A vast, delicate strength, not used, not properly understood, piteously alone against the white rock and wash of the blue-wrinkled sea. A scarlet coat in a palace and some gold lace on his shoulders would have fitted him better, watched only as he was by a gasping, furtive old woman behind the kitchen panes. If the other had been there, he would have shown affectation, talked about his nakedness and her. While Ross and Picus would have skipped through the house and chaffed her if they had noticed her at all. Such was Clarence’s audience, with a scattering of poppies, a house huddled against the ground, and below the aphrodite sea.

===Chinese===
一副阔大而精微的力气，无人使唤，也无人真正懂得，凄凄然独自撑在白岩与蓝皱之海的冲刷之间。其实，一件宫里的猩红外套、肩头几道金绦，更配他——毕竟窥看他的，只有厨房窗后一个气喘吁吁、贼头贼脑的老妇人。换了另一位在场，他就要摆起做作，谈他的赤身，谈她了。而罗斯和皮库斯会蹦跳着穿屋而过，若是留意到她，还要打趣她几句。克拉伦斯的观众不过如此——外加几星散落的罂粟，一座蜷伏在地的小屋，和小屋底下那片阿佛洛狄忒之海。

===Original===
Indoors he preened and poked everywhere, exceedingly afraid of the coming night. Then he would be alone, the shepherd’s wife off at her mincing trudge to her hovel, where occasionally was heard the roar of a carouse. Ross might have joined it and been the life and soul of the party, or lain out sea and star sailing.

===Chinese===
回到屋里，他东理理、西翻翻，对即将到来的夜怕得厉害。到那时他就只身一人了，牧羊人的妻子迈着细碎沉重的步子，回她的窝棚去了——那边偶尔传出一场酒宴的喧嚷。罗斯说不定正凑在其中，做全场的性命与灵魂，要不然就仰面躺倒，航行于海与星辰之间。

===Original===
Clarence, by himself, was simply and terribly afraid. Not of individuals, but of a menace that walked hand in hand with night, joined with the fear natural in remote places to a man not intuitively tuned. First he told himself that everything would not be ready for Picus, and he would scold him: then that Picus’s scenes were a disgrace. Then that Picus was never coming back. Then that it was Scylla who would not let him come back. A rage got him by the throat, shook him, crawled over him. By the time it was night, he was incoherent, and half a dozen times started over the hill to walk seventy miles inland to Tambourne where Picus might be. It was not his humour that checked him, but fear of the vast spaces under the star-blazing sky. The stars were not his friends. The Pleiades may have been weeping uselessly for him. When he lifted his eyes up the hills, he averted them. Rough, barrow-haunted places. He shuddered and turned back.

===Chinese===
独处的克拉伦斯，怕得简单而可怕。不是怕某个人，而是怕一种与黑夜携手同行的威胁，这威胁又与荒僻之地天然加在一个直觉不曾调准的人身上的恐惧合流。起初他告诉自己：一切都还没替皮库斯备好，他要数落他；继而：皮库斯搞出的那些场面丢人现眼；再继而：皮库斯再也不回来了；末了：是斯库拉不放他回来。一股狂怒掐住他的喉咙，摇他，在他周身爬。及至入夜，他已经语无伦次，五六次动身翻山，要徒步七十英里进内陆，去皮库斯可能在的坦伯恩。拦住他的不是他的心绪，而是对烈烈繁星下那浩茫空阔的恐惧。星辰不是他的朋友。昴星团或许正为他徒然垂泪。他抬眼望向群山，旋即挪开。粗粝的、荒冢出没的地方。他一个寒噤，转身折返。

===Original===
Only candlelight in the cottage, in the silvered sconces on the jade-blue walls. Casket he had made for Picus, hung with brilliant 18th century paintings of birds. That woman had done it, the slender, cousinly bitch. Once he had thought of dropping the handkerchief at her, and few he’d ever done it to had said no. His extreme vanity had never surmounted the transition from his boy’s beauty, which Ross had taken as a matter of course.

===Chinese===
小屋里只余烛光，在玉蓝墙上的银烛台里。他给皮库斯做的那只匣子，挂满明艳的十八世纪禽鸟画。干出这事的，是那个女人——那个苗条的、表亲似的贱人。他一度想朝她丢下手帕，而被他丢过手帕的人，鲜少有说“不”的。他那极度的虚荣，始终没能熬过从少年之美脱身的这一变——那少年之美，罗斯当年是视作理所当然的。

===Original===
Introvert, introvert said his mind, full of fashionable fads. Then his torture came on him again as the huge night swept on, and even his fear of it was forgotten in the grinding and tearing of his frustration and desolation and rage against Scylla, until for all human purposes he was mad. In other surroundings it would have been a bad breakdown, needing work, praise, new loves, above all admiration. Here, a pebble-throw from a gulf of air, it was ruin for one who in camps and cities and a classic personal relation had been heroic.

===Chinese===
“内向，内向。”他的头脑说着，满脑子都是时髦的字眼。随后，随着浩大的夜滚滚推进，他的酷刑又上了身，连对黑夜本身的恐惧，也淹没在挫败、凄凉与对斯库拉的狂怒的碾磨撕扯里——就人类的一切凭准而论，他已经疯了。换个环境，这不过是一场糟糕的崩溃，需要工作、褒奖、新的恋爱，尤其是仰慕。而在这里，离一道空气的深渊只一石之遥，这对一个在军营里、城市里、在一段古典式的私情里做过英雄的人，便是毁灭。

===Original===
The story of the cup, now become a horror, came in. That his reason was not overset was because he took the hollow greek ship with “A present for Scylla” on it, and broke it to splinters.

===Chinese===
那只杯的故事——如今已成恐怖——也趁虚而入。他的理智之所以没有倾覆，是因为他抓起那条空心的希腊船——上书“赠斯库拉的礼物”的那条——把它摔成了齑粉。

===Original===
Next morning he had not slept and sat staring when Lydia’s letter came. A horrible fit of laughing frightened the shepherd’s wife. She ran home like a half-plucked hen, while Clarence with affected deliberation for some unknown frightful audience took pen and paper and wrote in his exquisite hand.

===Chinese===
翌晨，他一夜未睡，正枯坐呆望，莉迪亚的信到了。一阵可怕的狂笑惊跑了牧羊人的妻子。她像只拔了一半毛的母鸡，逃回家去；克拉伦斯却装出一副从容的做派——给某个无名的、可怖的观众看——提笔铺纸，用他一手精雅的字迹写信。

===Original===
He told Lydia that it was not so, and in a few lines conveyed such a loathing of Scylla that Lydia half saw the truth, and nearly went to find her. But Philip found the letter amusing, and she did not go.

===Chinese===
他告诉莉迪亚事情并非如此，寥寥数行里，灌注了对斯库拉那样的憎恶，莉迪亚隐约看出了几分真相，险些动身去找她。可是菲利普觉得这信有趣，她便没有去。

===Original===
The levelling afternoon sun that came in through the cottage door found Clarence drawing Scylla, on huge sheets of paper pinned to the walls. In charcoal, obscenely and savagely contorted, and with little darts made of fine nibs and empty cartridge-cases he pierced the bodies of his paper martyrs. Then he tore them down, finding no content in it, so that ragged strips of paper covered the floor, the silver divan, and the cushions bright as fresh blood.

===Chinese===
从小屋门口斜照进来、把万物拉平的午后阳光，照见克拉伦斯正在画斯库拉——画在钉满四壁的巨幅纸上。炭笔勾成，扭曲得淫恶而狂暴；他又用细笔尖和空弹壳做成的小镖，扎穿他那些纸上殉道者的身体。随后他把它们尽数撕下，从中榨不出半点满足，于是残破的纸条铺满了地板、银灰的躺榻，和鲜亮如新血的坐垫。

===Original===
Perhaps he was the man who had suffered most from the disbelief and disuse of all forms of religion. Bred a Catholic, he had left the church and the question superciliously, uneasily. Incapable of Ross’s and Scylla’s faith that there was a faith, with all its pains and invisibility, unquestioned as air. A religion externalised by a powerful discipline might have upheld him, but all that he had then was a suspicion that this was the punishment of a neglected set of gods.

===Chinese===
或许，他是受苦最深的那个人——受着一切宗教形式遭人不信、遭人废置之苦。他自幼受天主教教养，却以一种倨傲而不安的姿态，离开了教会，也搁下了那个问题。他无能于罗斯和斯库拉的那种信——信世上终究有一种信，连同它全部的疼痛与无形，像空气一样不容置疑。一种由森严教仪外化出来的宗教，本可以支撑住他；而他当时仅有的，是一种疑心：这是被怠慢的一班神明所降的惩罚。

===Original===
The hour came when the light began to show up the earth in relief, with a distinctiveness almost monstrous, like a drug reverie. A little freshness blew in off the water, a cloud or so travelled, teaspoonfuls of fire-dipped cream. Spent with pain, his fear of the night returned.

===Chinese===
时辰到了，天光开始把大地凸雕出来，分明得近乎怪异，像一场药力的迷梦。水面吹来一丝清新的风，一两朵云游过，一茶匙一茶匙蘸过火的奶油。他心力交瘁，对黑夜的恐惧又回来了。

===Original===
To 

 Peter and Paul

===Chinese===
献给 

 彼得与保罗

===Original===

> Armed with madness,
 

 I go on a long voyage

===Chinese===

> 以疯狂为武装，
 

 我踏上漫长的航程

## Felix / 菲利克斯

===Original===
Felix bolted black and stormy to his hotel and emerged again into the gold light, fresh as roses. He crossed the river, and on the brink of the Champs-Élysées felt the rhythm of Paris begin to stir him and caress. A movement of tireless youth, each instant crystallized a century, illustrated by details small and intimate, grandiose or chic. His lost goodness returned, recomposed out of adoring attention, until like a polished bay, the Place de la Concorde opened before him. There the fragile Paris façades grouped themselves round the bronze ladies washing their faces, round a boy wandering out among the skimming taxis, in love.

===Chinese===
菲利克斯黑着一张风暴脸，一口气奔回旅馆，再出来时已步入金色天光，鲜润如玫瑰。他渡过河去，一到香榭丽舍大街边上，便觉出巴黎的节奏开始撩动他、抚爱他。一种不知疲倦的青春的涌动，每个瞬间都凝出一整个世纪，由细小而贴身、宏大或时髦的种种细节来图解。他失落的良善回来了，由倾慕的专注重新缀合，直到协和广场像一面磨亮的水湾，在他面前豁然张开。脆弱的巴黎立面在那里围拢成群，围着以水洗面的青铜仕女，围着一个神不守舍的少年——他踱到轻掠而过的出租车之间，恋爱着。

===Original===
He walked some way before he remembered that he had a rendezvous with a person as well as Paris, and turned back along the Rue de Rivoli, drowned in the evening sky. He had forgotten what he had left behind, novice at his first ceremony of mystery, he turned up the Rue Boissy d’Anglas and found himself indoors again.

===Chinese===
走出去好一程，他才想起除了巴黎，他还同一个人有约，于是折回里沃利街，一路沉在傍晚的天色里。他把抛在身后的一切忘了个干净——像初涉奥秘仪式的新入门者——拐上布瓦西·当格拉街，发觉自己又回到了屋子里面。

===Original===
His friend was with a band. Felix hated bands. No setting for him when he felt that he was not really there. He knew he had an inferiority complex, but there were too many draped legs and wrapped coats, and he might not have heard the last story, would have to pretend it had arrived stale from London. He would be found out, and no one would care how he loved Paris, or how much he knew about art. And with what was left of his generous simplicity, he did not count at all on his clothes which were the original of many replicas, or on his money, which was not borrowed.

===Chinese===
他的朋友同一个乐队圈子坐在一处。菲利克斯讨厌乐队圈子。他自觉魂不守舍的时候，这种场合便没有他的座。他知道自己有自卑情结，可眼前叠着的玉腿太多、裹紧的大衣太多，最新的笑话他又多半没听过，只好装作它从伦敦捎来时已经不新鲜了。他会露馅，而谁也不会在乎他多么爱巴黎，不在乎他对艺术懂得多少。凭他那份慷慨与单纯剩下的余沥，他也全不指望自己的衣裳——那是许多仿品的原样——更不指望他的钱——那可不是借来的。

===Original===
Here was a different kind of loneliness. With his own generation, not as at home with the half-generation ahead. Loneliness all the same, self-imposed. In the French-American group he was a distinguishable figure. Boys fresh as roses in a shopwindow, as picked and perfect. Only a close observer might have said that Felix was still on his bush. Or having left it, that his stalk was not down in the water.

===Chinese===
这是一种别样的孤独。是同他自己这一代人的孤独，不像在国内时，陪着长半辈的那一代。孤独终究是孤独，还是自找的。在这个法美混合的圈子里，他算个一眼可辨的人物。那些少年像橱窗里的玫瑰一样新鲜，一样经过挑拣、完美无瑕。唯有观察入微的人才会说：菲利克斯还在枝头上。或者说，纵然下了枝，他的花茎也不曾浸到水里。

===Original===
How to pretend to be the devil you are afraid to be.

===Chinese===
怎样装成那个你不敢做的魔鬼。

===Original===
How to be a grand seigneur on nothing a year.

===Chinese===
怎样分文不进，还做一位大贵人。

===Original===
How to be yourself when you do not know that self, and are afraid to find out.

===Chinese===
怎样做你自己，当你并不识得那个自己，又怕把它查个明白。

===Original===
How to get tight when you don’t do it regularly.

===Chinese===
怎样喝醉，当你平日并不常喝。

===Original===
The café walls were black, filled with mirror panels squared with small red and gold lights. Like an old mirror that has a circle of miniature mirrors inlaid in its glass, the place reflected and repeated a great deal of what is going on in the world. And Felix, with his letter of introduction, could not pull it out, with his pass behind the scene over his heart, could not present it. He forgot the walk he had taken, fell back into the easy trick of disapproval, mask for longing to be a little king in that bright crowd. King over a French boy, pencilled like a persian miniature, discreet and gentle as a cat. Shut absolutely in his race, yet escaping it by an indescribable sweetness, a perfume of goodness uncorrupted by intelligence which would last﻿—how long? Felix told himself that his complexion could not last long anyhow, and retired on a loud excuse to dust a little powder on his own fair nose and chin. Never be able to impress that boy, who was neither in authority or out, who wore his youth for a fairy-cloak which for Felix was a naked skin.

===Chinese===
咖啡馆的墙是黑的，嵌满一方一方镜板，间以细小的红金灯盏。像一面老镜子、玻璃里嵌着一圈小镜子，这地方把世上正闹腾的许多事映了又映。而菲利克斯揣着引荐信，却抽不出来；揣着一张通往幕后的通行证，贴在心口，却呈递不上去。他忘了方才走的那一路，缩回不以为然的老花样里——那是面具，掩着他想做那明亮人群中一个小小国王的渴望。做那个法国少年的王——那少年像一幅波斯细密画描出来的，矜持温软如猫。全然封在自己的种族里，却又凭一种说不出的甜美逸出其外，一缕良善的香气，未被智识败坏——这香气撑得了多久？菲利克斯对自己说，横竖他这副颜色撑不了多久，便高声寻了个借口退席，往自己白皙的鼻子和下巴上扑了点粉。他永远无法折服那个少年——那少年既不在权势里，也不在权势外，把青春当一件仙人斗篷披着，而对菲利克斯，那分明是一层赤裸的皮肤。

===Original===
Nor the buddha-shaped musician from the Midi, loved for his wit, serving his turn with each of them in turn as though they were not there. Felix turned to the boy he had come to see, the adorable American who knew everybody. He wondered if he was after all another Carston. (Felix had underrated Carston, having no experience between the man from Boston and his friend from the Middle West.) He was smarter than any Carston, brimmed up with sap and impudence, a boy who, if he had come from the moon, would have made his friends dream until he taught them not to. On whom, if Felix had known it, Scylla reckoned for vengeance. He was glad enough to see Felix, wear him for a night or so in his cap. Remember him as a spoiled, sweet girl remembers. Felix would have the money for several parties and was an authentic specimen. He would give him his turn.

===Chinese===
也折服不了那位米迪来的、佛陀身形的音乐家——众人爱他的机智，他挨个儿周旋这一圈人，仿佛他们全不在场。菲利克斯转向他来见的那位少年——那个谁都认识的可人美国青年。他寻思，这家伙到底会不会是又一个卡斯顿。（菲利克斯低估了卡斯顿，因为他毫无经验，分不清波士顿人和他这位中西部朋友的差别。）他比随便哪个卡斯顿都精乖，满得往外冒浆汁和痞气；这样的少年，哪怕打月亮上来，也能叫朋友们做梦，直到他教会他们别做。而斯库拉，菲利克斯若是知道，正指着他算复仇的账呢。他见到菲利克斯够高兴的，会把他当根羽毛，在帽檐上别个一两夜。往后记起他来，就像一个娇养甜美的姑娘记旧情人那样记着。菲利克斯出得起好几场派对的酒钱，又是一件地道的标本。他会给他轮上一轮的。

===Original===
They all spoke French better than he, who always wanted to do it too well, whose ear was not in tune. His next brandy went down, and as his brain quickened he heard a party being discussed for late that night. Would they ask him? How could he bear it if they didn’t? In reality it did not occur to the Frenchmen that he would not want to go to Montmartre, and did not know how to take care of himself. Not that they were interested﻿—the brandy mounted darkly﻿—nobody was interested. Their pure speech hummed like a dynamo. He stretched himself insolently and spoke to a bulb-cheeked yellow-haired child there pursuing the career, and impressed by Felix’s obvious need to do nothing of the sort.

===Chinese===
他们个个法语都说得比他强——他总想把它说得太好，耳朵又调不准弦。又一杯白兰地落了肚，脑子转快了，他听见众人正商量当夜深夜的一场聚会。他们会不会请他？若不请，他怎么受得了？其实那些法国人压根儿没想过他会有不想去蒙马特的道理，也没想过他不会照管自己。倒不是他们上心——白兰地黑沉沉往头顶涨——谁也不上心。他们纯正的言语嗡嗡如一台发电机。他放肆地伸了个懒腰，同一个脸颊鼓鼓、黄头发的少年搭起话来——那孩子在那边经营前程，见菲利克斯分明毫无此志，倒对他肃然起敬。

===Original===
The party began to discuss a well-known eccentric who had just left.

===Chinese===
席间开始议论一位刚走的有名怪人。

===Original===
“Oh, that man﻿—” said Felix, gladly. But his comments in pretentious French were too severe, and left the others in doubt whether he really knew him. Soon they were not listening, and he saw the Frenchwoman catch his friend’s eye. Only the bulb-cheeked child was still attentive. Little *faux monnayeur*, fresh from his lycée, still rather ingenuous, he helped Felix along his transitions of envy, wonder, and fear.

===Chinese===
“哦，那个人啊——”菲利克斯欣然接口。可他用夸饰法语下的断语太严苛，弄得旁人吃不准他到底认不认得那人。不多时他们便不听了，他看见那位法国女人同他朋友递了个眼色。只有那个脸颊鼓鼓的孩子还在听。小伪币犯（*faux monnayeur*）——刚出中学校门，还相当天真——他一路扶着菲利克斯，走过嫉妒、惊叹与恐惧的一段段过渡。

===Original===
A Russian boy came in, tight in a merry circle of private intoxication, small, black, Asiatic head in air. He sang:

===Chinese===
一个俄国少年走了进来，被自斟自饮的一圈欢喜箍得紧紧的，小小的黑发亚洲脑袋昂在半空。他唱道：

===Original===
> “Si par hazard tu vois ma tante,
 

 Compliments de ma part.”

===Chinese===
> “倘若你碰巧见到我姑母，
 

 请代我问候。”

===Original===
A baby-faced negro rolled his drum. The saxophone began to cough out variations, apparently played backwards, on “Dinah Lee.” His memory of the sweet tune pricked a bubble in the boy’s petulance. He asked the Frenchwoman to dance, and moving easily with her, a little drunk, he began to bubble praise of Paris. When they got back, the little bar off the dancing-room was roaring with the love of life. In a frieze along the bar, in squares and fives and sixes round the scarlet tables were all the right people to play with. People of the world and the half-world, people who found the arts useful, and a fair number of people who were found useful by the arts. Eminent eccentrics, the very poor, the very rich, capital in wits or youth or looks or wit; diversity of creatures, young society in action, the motif of the time and the place repeated in the exit and entry of the pick of Paris’ basketful of boys. It was Felix’s party, too, if he could have forgotten himself, let himself go, torn up his silly little mask. Instead, he allowed himself to feel homesick, looked timidly at his friend who had caught the eye of a princess, and was making her sure she was pleased to have caught his.

===Chinese===
一个娃娃脸的黑人滚动着他的鼓。萨克斯管开始呛咳出《黛娜·李》的变奏，听上去像是倒着吹的。那支甜曲子勾起的记忆，在少年的躁郁里刺破了一只泡。他请那位法国女人跳舞，带着几分酒意同她款款而舞，开始咕嘟咕嘟冒出一串对巴黎的称颂。等他们跳完回来，舞厅旁边的小条吧正为生之爱闹腾得震天响。沿吧台一长溜如一条浮雕带，围着猩红圆桌两人一方、五人一堆、六人一伙的，全是正对路数的玩伴。上流社会的人，半上流社会的人，觉得艺术有用处的人，还有相当一批被艺术觉得有用处的人。声名显赫的怪人，赤贫的人，巨富的人，在机智、青春、容貌或才情上握有本钱的人；众生百态，年轻的社会正在运转，时代与场所的母题，随着巴黎满篮少年中的尖子们进进出出而反复奏鸣。这一场本来也有菲利克斯的份——只要他能忘掉自己，放得开，把那可笑的小面具撕了。他却任由自己害起乡愁，怯怯地望着他的朋友——那位已勾住了一位公主的目光，正让她确信：被他勾住，是她的乐意。

===Original===
