“Let’s have a day in the country tomorrow. We might go to Versailles.”

===Chinese===
“明天到乡下去过一天吧。咱们可以去凡尔赛。”

===Original===
“All right, if we’re up.” The voice was soft and virile, touched with a brogue. No getting him to himself where everyone was out to be seen. In desperate need of a focus he gave up trying to understand French, and began to describe the people at his table to himself. He would never get to know them. Who ever gets to know the French? He might as well have something to remember. When he was a famous man, they’d wish they’d been nicer to him. There was the Frenchwoman in her man’s coat, without makeup, half-boy, half great lady, level-eyed and low-voiced, incapable of pretension or any false gesture. Then the musician’s Chinese rotundity, the pouting lips, flat and amiable white hands, contradicted by a chin cleft like a Caesar’s, and terribly intelligent eyes. Felix respected those eyes. They had looked at him kindly, and he suspected pity, found himself caught with an emotion that was like pity when he looked at the third, the young Frenchman, because his beauty was “no stronger than a flower.” Scented like one, too, out of a bottle Felix wanted the name of.﻿—“But he’s no brains,” he said, “and they say he takes drugs. It’s a pity.” He nursed the idea that he could be happy nowhere. Was there anyone else in the room who was alone? He looked around. Even two boys having a row at the bar were in contact. A swing door burst open. It was the Russian back again, if anything, tighter. Felix saw a glass of champagne beginning to slide on the top of the downy black head. Skull of a tibetan idol: mouth of a wicked baby. In the middle of the floor he lowered the glass, and began an on-the-spot Charleston. “Hey, hey,” he shouted. Then a rune:

===Chinese===
“行啊，只要起得来。”那嗓音柔和而雄浑，带一点土腔。人人都在争着露脸的地方，休想把他单独拉出来。他急需要个焦点，索性死了听懂法语的这条心，转而在心里给同桌的人一一画像。他永远别想真正认识他们。又有谁真正认识过法国人？不如记下点什么，留作日后的念想。等他成了名人，他们会后悔当初没待他更好些。那位法国女人，穿一件男式外套，不施脂粉，半个假小子，半个贵妇人，眼神平正，嗓音低沉，做不出虚骄，也做不出任何假手势。再就是音乐家那佛陀式的圆浑——嘟起的嘴唇，白皙、平板而和善的双手——却被一副凯撒般开裂的下巴和一双聪明得吓人的眼睛拆了台。菲利克斯敬重那双眼睛。它们曾和善地看过他，而他疑心那和善是怜悯；待到望向第三位、那个年轻的法国人，他发觉自己被一种近于怜悯的情绪攫住了——因为那人的美“还不及一朵花结实”。香气也像花，打一个菲利克斯想知道名字的瓶子里来。——“可他没有脑子，”他说，“人家还说他吸毒。可惜了。”他抱着一个念头不放：他到哪儿也快活不起来。这屋里还有谁形单影只？他环顾四周。连吧台边吵翻了脸的两个少年，也是彼此连通的。一扇弹簧门砰地撞开。还是那个俄国人，又来了——若是还能更醉，这回更醉了。菲利克斯看见一杯香槟正开始顺着那绒毛似的黑头顶往下滑。一副西藏偶像的骷髅：一张恶婴的嘴。他到舞池正中放平了杯子，当场跳起一支查尔斯顿。“嘿，嘿，”他喊道。接着是一段咒歌：

===Original===
> “O qu’il est beau, mon village,
 

 Mon Paris,
 

 Mon Paris.”

===Chinese===
> “啊，我的村庄多美，
 

 我的巴黎，
 

 我的巴黎。”

===Original===
He shot through to the dancing-room. He was ugly. He had exquisite hands and feet. Probably a shop-assistant pretending to be a prince. Felix’s demon was on the spot. He came back again, carrying the glass now like a chalice, empty, and looking crossly into it. Felix burst out laughing.

===Chinese===
他一溜烟穿进舞厅。他生得丑。却有一双极精致的手脚。多半是个店员，冒充着王子。菲利克斯的魔星恰好在场。他又回来了，这回把杯子端得像一只圣杯，空的，还气呼呼地朝里面张望。菲利克斯放声大笑。

===Original===
“Excuse me,” he said, “have another drink,” and went with him to the bar.

===Chinese===
“失陪一下，”他说，“请再喝一杯。”便同他往吧台去了。

===Original===
“*Enfin il s’amuse, le petit anglais*,” said the musician. Felix’s friend frowned. Money spent on that demon would not last long. But Felix had sensed that here was another isolation, but not its quality, its brutal and innocent acceptance of things. But he was talkable-to, with his unmodulated French, his trick of sprawling sideways at the bar, his head pitched forward, laughing down at his feet.

===Chinese===
“这小英国人总算乐起来了（*Enfin il s’amuse, le petit anglais*）。”音乐家说。菲利克斯的朋友皱起眉。花在那魔星身上的钱，撑不了多久。可菲利克斯已经觉出，这里头又是另一种孤独——只是还没触到它的质地：那种对万事既残忍又天真的领受。不过这人还聊得来——用他那不加修饰的法语，用他那在吧台边侧身摊坐的习惯，用那颗前倾的、低头冲着自己双脚发笑的脑袋。

## I / 一、林中静宅

===Original===
In the house, in which they could not afford to live, it was unpleasantly quiet. Marvellously noisy, but the noises let through silence. The noises were jays, bustling and screeching in the wood, a hay-cutter, clattering and sending up waves of scent, substantial as sea-waves, filling the long rooms as the tide fills a blowhole, but without roar or release. The third noise was the light wind, rising off the diamond-blue sea. The sea lay three parts round the house, invisible because of the wood. The wood rose from its cliff-point in a single tree, and spread out inland, in a fan to enclose the house. Outside the verandah, a small lawn had been hollowed, from which the wood could be seen as it swept up, hurrying with squirrels, into a group of immense ilex, beech and oak. The lawn was stuck with yuccas and tree-fuchsias, dripping season in, season out, with bells the colour of blood.

===Chinese===
在这座他们住不起的宅子里，安静得叫人不舒服。说吵也真是吵极了，可那些声响偏偏放寂静穿了过去。声响是松鸦，在林子里蹿动尖叫；是割草机，咔哒咔哒，掀起一阵阵香浪，扎实得如同海浪，像潮水灌满喷水洞那样灌满一间间深长的屋子，却不闻轰鸣，也不见喷发。第三种声响是微风，从钻石蓝的海面上起来。海从三面围着宅子，叫树林挡住，看不见。树林从崖尖上起于一棵树，向内陆铺展开去，张成一柄扇子，把宅子围在当中。游廊外面，挖出一小片草坪，从那里望得见树林怎样涌上来，带着松鼠一路匆匆，汇入一丛高大的圣栎、山毛榉和橡树。草坪上插满丝兰和倒挂金钟树，季复一季、年复一年地垂滴着血色的钟形花。

===Original===
Once the house was passed, the wood gave it up, enclosed it decently, fenced a paddock, and the slip of dark life melted into the endless turf-miles which ran up a great down into the sky.

===Chinese===
宅子一过，树林便把它撇下，端端正正把它围拢，圈起一块围场，这一缕暗色的生意便化进无穷无尽的草野里去——草野漫上一道大丘原，一直漫进天里。

===Original===
The silence let through by the jays, the hay-cutter, and the breeze, was a complicated production of stone rooms, the natural silence of empty grass, and the equivocal, personal silence of the wood. Not many nerves could stand it. People who had come for a week had been known to leave next day. The people who had the house were interested in the wood and its silence. When it got worse, after dark or at midday, they said it was tuning-up. When a gale came up-Channel shrieking like a mad harp, they said they were watching a visible fight with the silence in the wood.

===Chinese===
松鸦、割草机和微风放过来的那份寂静，是件复杂的合成品：石屋的寂静，空旷草野的天生寂静，还有树林那种含糊其辞、别有心事的寂静。没几副神经受得住它。来住一礼拜的人，有过第二天就动身的。这座宅子的主人一家，偏偏对树林和它的寂静心有兴趣。等它闹得更凶的时分——天黑以后或正午——他们说那是乐队在调音。等狂风顺海峡而上、像疯了的竖琴一样尖啸起来，他们说，那是自己在看一场同林中寂静的搏斗，一场看得见的搏斗。

===Original===
A large gramophone stood with its mouth open on the verandah flags. They had been playing to the wood after lunch, to appease it and to keep their dancing in hand. The house was empty. Their servants had gone over to a distant farm. The wood had it all its own way. They were out.

===Chinese===
一台大留声机张着口，立在游廊的石板上。午饭过后，他们一直奏乐给树林听，既为安抚它，也为看住自己的舞兴。宅子空着。仆人们到远处一座农庄去了。树林称心如意，独断独行。他们都出门了。

===Original===
There were two paths through the wood to the sea. A beeline through the high trees, of fine grass, pebble scattered, springing and wet. Then, across the wet ditch that was sometimes a stream, a path through the copse in figures of eight, whose turns startled people. As the wood narrowed, this way ended in a gate on to the grass, the nearest way to an attractive rabbit-warren. These were the only two paths in that country, except a green road which led from the house over the down to the white road and from thence on to the beginnings of the world, ten miles away.

===Chinese===
穿过树林下海，有两条路。一条是直线，穿行高树之间，细草铺路，缀着卵石，弹性十足而潮湿。另一条要跨过那条时而成溪的湿沟，在矮林里拐出一个又一个 8 字，急转弯处常叫人吃一惊。到树林收窄的地方，这条路到一扇门前为止，出门便是草野，是到一片讨人喜欢的兔子窝最近的路。这地方就只有这两条路，外加一条绿车道，从宅子翻过丘原通到白公路，再从那里通往十英里外世界的起点。

===Original===
There was only one house except a shepherd’s cottage, and a little fancy lodge, the wood had swallowed, which they let to a fisherman in exchange for fish. The fisherman was a gentleman, and a fine carver in wood. The shepherd was a troglodyte. He came home drunk in the moonlight spinning round and yelling obscene words to the tune of old hymns. They were equally friends with both. They belonged to the house and the wood and the turf and the sea; had no money and the instincts of hospitality; wanted everything and nothing, and were at that moment lying out naked on a rock-spit which terminated their piece of land.

===Chinese===
方圆之内只有一座房子，外加一间牧羊人的小屋，还有一座花哨的小门房——树林把它吞了进去——他们把它租给一个渔夫，换鱼。渔夫是位绅士，也是木雕的好手。牧羊人是个穴居野人般的家伙。他会醉醺醺地在月光里打着旋回家，就着老赞美诗的调门嚎脏话。他们同这二位交情不相上下。他们属于这宅子、这树林、这草野和这海；没有钱，却有好客的本能；什么都想要，又什么都不想要；而此刻，正一丝不挂地躺在一条石岬上——他们那块地皮，到那里便到了头。

===Original===
The cliffs there were low and soft, rounded with a black snout, but based on a wedge of orange stone, smooth and running out square under the sea.

===Chinese===
那里的崖又低又软，隆起一只黑色的吻部，底下却垫着一楔橙色的石头，光溜溜的，方方正正地伸到海底下去。

===Original===
Up and down the channel, high cliffs rose, airy, glittering, but some way off. Their headland was low, their valley shallow and open, spiked only with undersea reefs, no less lovely and disastrous than the famous precipices which made their coast their pride.

===Chinese===
海峡上下，处处耸起高崖，空灵，闪亮，只是隔着一截路。他们这处岬地低矮，山谷浅而开敞，只在水下戳着暗礁——比起那些叫这段海岸引以为豪的著名峭壁来，可爱不遑多让，为祸也不遑多让。

===Original===
“*Mare Nostrum*,” they said, in Paris or in London, at the sea’s winter takings there. An outlet for a natural ferocity they were too proud to exercise, too indifferent to examine. Also a kind of ritual, a sacrifice, willing but impersonal to their gods.

===Chinese===
“*Mare Nostrum*（我们的海）”——在巴黎也好，在伦敦也好，一提起这片海冬日里的斩获，他们便这样说。那是天生凶性的一条出口，他们太骄傲，不屑施展；太冷漠，不屑省察。也是一种仪式，一场献祭，对他们的诸神心甘情愿，却不带一点私情。

===Original===
Meanwhile the weather was good. One of them sat up, and rolled off the reef’s edge into the sea.

===Chinese===
这当口天气正好。其中一个坐起身，从礁沿上滚进海里。

===Original===
A brother and sister to whom the house belonged, and a young man they had known a long time. They called her Scylla from her name Drusilla, altering it because they said she was sometimes a witch and sometimes a bitch. They were handsome and young, always together, and often visited by their friends. It was Felix, the brother, who had swum out. His sister sat up and watched him with the touch of anxiety common to females, however disciplined. “Be careful,” she called, “the tide’s turning.” He wallowed under the sea.

===Chinese===
宅子归一对兄妹所有，同他们在一起的，还有一个相识已久的年轻人。她本名德鲁西拉（Drusilla），他们却把这名字叫成了斯库拉（Scylla）——改名，是因为他们说，她有时是个女巫，有时是条母狗。他们都俊美，都年轻，总在一处，朋友们也常来探访。游出去的，是当哥哥的菲利克斯（Felix）。妹妹坐起身来望着他，带着再怎么训练有素也免不了的那一点女性的焦心。“当心，”她喊道，“转潮了。”他在海面底下打着滚。

===Original===
“Leave him alone,” said the other man, “it’s the last day’s peace,” and rolled over on his face and ate pink seaweed.

===Chinese===
“别管他，”另一个男人说，“这是最后一天的清净。”说着翻身趴下，吃起粉红色的海藻来。

===Original===
She approved because it was good for his complexion, wood-brown as they were fair, but she stood up and watched the boy’s head popping in and out of the crisp water. Naked, the enormous space, the rough earth dressed her. The sparkling sea did not. But the sea at the moment was something for the men to swim in, an enormous toy. She thought again: He won’t drown. Besides, why worry? Lay down again, and fed an anemone with a prawn.

===Chinese===
她赞成，因为这于他的肤色有益——他们兄妹白皙，他却是林子般的棕色——可她还是站起身，望着那少年的脑袋在脆亮的水波里一冒一冒。赤裸着，这浩大的空间、这粗粝的大地给她披上了衣裳。波光闪闪的海却没有。不过眼下的海，是给男人们游泳用的，一件巨大的玩具。她又想：他淹不死。再说，操什么心呢？便重新躺下，拿一只虾喂海葵。

===Original===
“Ross, why do you say ‘the last day’s peace’? You like people when they come.”

===Chinese===
“罗斯（Ross），你为什么说‘最后一天的清净’？人家来做客，你是喜欢他们的。”

===Original===
He answered:

===Chinese===
他答道：

===Original===
“One always enjoys something. But this one’s an American.”

===Chinese===
“人做什么总能得点乐子。可这一位，是个美国人。”

===Original===
“No, we’ve never had one before.”

===Chinese===
“不，咱们以前从没接待过美国人。”

===Original===
“I don’t mind ’em. I always like their women. But take it from me, all we shall get out of this one is some fun. He won’t like the wood. The wood will giggle at him.”

===Chinese===
“我不计较这个。他们的女人我一向喜欢。可是听我的，咱们从这一位身上能得到的，只有一点乐子。他不会喜欢这树林的。树林会冲他咯咯地笑。”

===Original===
“It laughs at us.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“它也笑话咱们。……”

===Original===
“We don’t mind﻿—it’s our joke.” He laughed, sitting upright staring down-Channel, his head pitched back on an immensely long neck, his mouth like a wild animal’s, only objectively preoccupied with the world. She thought: Grin like a dog, and run about the rocks, accepting him as she accepted everything there. She said: “Give him a good time and see what happens.” That was her part of their hospitality, whose rewards were varied and irregular. None of them, with perhaps the exception of Felix, could understand a good time that was not based on flashes of illumination, exercises of the senses, dancing, and stretches of very insular behaviour.

===Chinese===
“咱们不在乎——这是咱们自己的笑话。”他笑了，坐直身子眺望海峡下游，脑袋后仰在一条极长的脖颈上，嘴像一头野兽的嘴，对这个世界只抱着一种客观的专注。她想：像狗那样龇牙而笑，在礁石间跑来跑去。她便这样接纳了他，如同接纳那里的一切。她说：“让他玩个痛快，看看会怎样。”这便是她在这家的待客之道里分到的那一份，报酬五花八门，没个定准。他们几个——也许菲利克斯除外——都理解不了一种痛快，倘若它不奠基于灵光的闪现、感官的操练、舞蹈，以及大段大段的孤岛做派。

===Original===
Something long and white came up behind them out of the sea. An extra wave washed Felix a ledge higher. “Thank you,” he said and skipped across. “Oh, my dear, I’m sure an octopus caught my leg.”

===Chinese===
一个又长又白的家伙，从他们身后的海里升了起来。一个额外的浪头把菲利克斯托上高一级的石台。“多谢。”他说着蹦跳过去。“哦，亲爱的，准是只章鱼抓了我的腿。”

===Original===
“D’you remember,” said Ross, “the chap last winter who killed them with his teeth and fainted at the sight of white of egg?”

===Chinese===
“你还记得吗，”罗斯说，“去年冬天那个家伙——用牙齿咬死章鱼，一看见蛋清就晕倒？”

===Original===
The pleasant memory united them; they became a triple figure, like Hecate the witch, amused, imaginative. They put on their things: Felix’s pretty clothes, Ross’s rough ones, the girl, her delicate strong dress. With their arms round her shoulders, they crossed the rocks and went up the cliff-path, and through the wood to the house.

===Chinese===
这段愉快的回忆把他们连成一体；他们化作一个三身的形象，像三形女巫赫卡忒（Hecate），觉得有趣，浮想联翩。他们穿上各自的衣物：菲利克斯那身漂亮的衣裳，罗斯那身粗陋的衣裳，姑娘那条精致而结实的裙子。两个男人用手臂搂住她的肩膀，跨过礁石，登上崖间小路，穿过树林，往宅子里去了。

## II / 二、独赴斯塔恩

===Original===
They woke to a clean superb day. The high trees broke the sun, and Scylla admired the form of them, standing straight to the east in the natural shape of trees, their tops curled to the west, tightened and distorted against the ocean wind.

===Chinese===
他们醒来，迎来一个澄澈明净的极好天。高树筛碎了太阳，斯库拉（Scylla）欣赏它们的形态：向东笔直挺立，是树的自然形状；树冠却向西蜷曲，顶着海上的风，被勒得紧绷绷，扭得变了形。

===Original===
Below them the copse was a knit bundle, almost as firm as stone. Still there were long shadows as she dressed, still dance and flash of birds. The wood was innocent, the house fresh and serene. At breakfast, in a quicksilver mirror, she saw the men come in. The eagle over it had a sock-suspender in its beak, but between the straps and the distortion she did not like the look of them.

===Chinese===
在高树底下，矮林是捆紧的一束，几乎同石头一样结实。她穿衣时还有长长的影子，还有鸟雀的舞蹈与闪烁。树林是无辜的，宅子清新而安详。早饭时，她在一面水银镜里看见男人们进来。镜子上方那只鹰，喙里挂着一根吊袜带；可隔着那些带子和镜面的变形，她不喜欢他们那副模样。

===Original===
They meant not to help her with the American, whom they had ordered like a new record from town. Who would have to be met.

===Chinese===
他们打定主意，不帮她招待那个美国人——那人是他们像从城里订一张新唱片似的订来的。总得有人去接站。

===Original===
They were like that. She was like that herself, but did not manage to give in to it. Which made her despise herself, think herself too female.

===Chinese===
他们就是这种脾气。她自己也是这种脾气，却始终拗着不肯就范。这就叫她看不起自己，觉得自己女人气太重。

===Original===
All three had work to do. She got hers done, and at the same time believed herself the sole stay of her men. Separated and bound to them because of her service they seemed unable to do without.

===Chinese===
三个人都有活儿要干。她把自己的干完了，同时又相信，自己是她那两个男人唯一的依靠。因为这份伺候，她同他们既分开又拴在一起，而他们似乎离了它就不行。

===Original===
Ross was saying, cautiously: “That man’s coming today.” She wondered what he was thinking about in the train. Staring about and trying to remember the name of the station. A name as familiar as their own. Nothing to him. Fun to make it part of his consciousness? Fun for them. The men were saying: “We really ought to go over to Tollerdown and see if the others have turned up.”

===Chinese===
罗斯正出言谨慎：“那人今天到。”她暗想，他此刻在火车里正想些什么。四下张望，使劲回想站名。一个同他们自己的名字一样熟悉的站名。于他，却什么也不是。把它纳进自己的意识，算一桩乐事？于他们，才是一桩乐事。男人们说：“我们真该去托勒当（Tollerdown）跑一趟，看看其他人到了没有。”

===Original===
Ross’s hair curled like black gorse, Felix’s spread like burnt turf. “And not to Starn with me?” she said. A long day off they would have on the turf together; Ross poking about for rare plants, Felix making up a tolerable poem. Ten miles she would walk, also alone in the hills. At the end there would be a point of human life, a station shed a stone’s throw from a crazy square, old houses tilted together; the gaping tourists, the market-day beasts; the train poking its head suddenly round an angle in the hills. There she would be eventually accompanied by a stranger, neat, interested, polite.

===Chinese===
罗斯的头发卷得像黑金雀花，菲利克斯的头发铺散开来，像烧过的草皮。“就不陪我上斯塔恩（Starn）去？”她说。本来，他们可以在草野上一起歇个长长的一天：罗斯四处翻找稀有植物，菲利克斯凑出一首过得去的诗。她却要走上十英里，同样独自一个，在山里。走到尽头，会有那么一点人间烟火：一座车站棚屋，离一个歪歪扭扭的广场一箭之地，老房子倾着身子挤作一堆；张口呆看的游客，集市日的牲口；火车猛地从山坳一角探出头来。到了那里，最后会有一个陌生人来陪伴她：整洁，饶有兴致，彬彬有礼。

===Original===
Then back in a car, flight after the steady walk﻿—which would end where she was now, in a place like a sea-pool, on the lawn grass, in the cool rooms, under the trees, in the wood.

===Chinese===
然后乘车回来，从容步行之后的一程疾驰——而这疾驰的尽头，正是她此刻所在的地方：一处宛若海边石潭的所在，在草坪上，在凉爽的房间里，在树底下，在树林中。

===Original===
“All right,” she said, “I’ll go﻿—if you order six lettuces and four lobsters, a basket of currants; and Felix does the flowers.” There would be six lobsters and four lettuces. She needed to be alone as much as they.

===Chinese===
“行，”她说，“我去——只要你们订六棵莴苣、四只龙虾、一篮醋栗；插花归菲利克斯。”到时候送来的，准是六只龙虾、四棵莴苣。她同他们一样，也需要独处。

===Original===
She took her hat, and ashplant, and left them.

===Chinese===
她拿起帽子，拄上一根白蜡木手杖，离开他们走了。

===Original===
For a while she climbed the green road, worn down in places to its flints, black glass set in white porcelain rings. Below her the field-chequered sea-valley collected a haze. The sea was a hardly visible brilliance. On the top of the down, she looked inland, across another valley to another range, and far inland to Starn on its hill, the hub of the down-wheel, set in its cup of smoke and stone. A very long way over the grass, a very long way down a chalk-road. A longer way through a valley track, called Seven Fields into Starn. Seven Fields, because Felix said there were seven different kinds of enclosures, all unpleasant. A yellow field, a dirty field, a too-wet field, a field where you stubbed your feet. A field with a savage cow, a field with a wicked horse. Always something wrong, whichever way you walked it, except the fourth, which was not a field but an open copse, treed and banked and prettified. *Midsummer Night’s Dream*, true greenwood. And hope, one way, of Starn in a mile and a half. She needed it by the time she reached the copse, in spite of her light stride and airy dress. The boys were off by now, somewhere on Gault cliffs, which was not a nice place, but a wonder and a horror, overhanging a gulf over a wood full of foxes the surf lapped, where even she had never been. The boys would be sitting there, dangling their legs, the gulls fanning them, an unsailable bay under them, transparent, peacock-coloured, where under the water the reefs wound like snakes.

===Chinese===
有一程她沿着绿车道往上爬，路面有几处磨得露出燧石——黑玻璃嵌在白瓷圈里。她脚下，田垄如棋盘的海谷积起一层薄雾。海是一片几乎看不见的亮。上了丘原顶，她朝内陆望去：越过又一道山谷，望见又一道山梁；再往腹地深处，是山头上的斯塔恩——丘原这只车轮的毂，嵌在它那只烟与石的杯里。走草地是长长的路，顺白垩路下去也是长长的路。更长的路，是穿过一条谷间小道，名叫“七片田”（Seven Fields），通进斯塔恩。叫它七片田，是因为菲利克斯说，那里有七种不同的圈地，个个不讨人喜欢。一片黄田，一片脏田，一片太湿的田，一片尽硌脚的田。一片有恶牛的田，一片有烈马的田。不管你打哪一头走，总有一片要出岔子，只有第四片例外——那不算田，是一片敞开的矮林，有树有埂，拾掇得漂漂亮亮。《仲夏夜之梦》，真正的绿林。往一头走，还有一英里半便到斯塔恩的指望。等走到那片矮林时，她正需要这份指望，尽管她步子轻捷，裙衫也薄透。两个小伙子这会儿已经出门，在高尔特崖（Gault cliffs）上的什么地方。那不是个好去处，却是一桩奇观、一桩可怖：悬在一道深渊之上，渊底一片满是狐狸的树林，浪头舔着它——连她也从没去过。小伙子们这会儿该坐在那儿，晃着两条腿，海鸥给他们扇着风，脚底下是一弯行不了船的海湾，透明，孔雀色，水面底下暗礁蜿蜒如蛇。

===Original===
It was all very well. She had told Felix to collect mushrooms and not allow Ross to experiment. He could get them in Ogham meads﻿—What was she worried about? Money, of course, and love affairs; the important, unimportant things. Hitherto God had fed his sparrows, and as good fish had come out of the sea. But everywhere there was a sense of broken continuity, a dis‑ease. The end of an age, the beginning of another. Revaluation of values. Phrases that meant something if you could mean them. The meaning of meaning? Discovery of a new value, a different way of apprehending everything. She wished the earth would not suddenly look fragile, as if it was going to start shifting about. Every single piece of appearance. She knew it was only the sun, polishing what it had dried. Including her face, her makeup had made pasty with sweat. There was something wrong with all of them, or with their world. A moment missed, a moment to come. Or not coming. Or either or both. Shove it off on the war; but that did not help.

===Chinese===
这一切倒也罢了。她已经吩咐菲利克斯去采蘑菇，不许罗斯做实验。他在欧甘草甸（Ogham meads）就能采到——她还在操什么心呢？自然是钱，还有情事；那些要紧的、不要紧的东西。迄今为止，上帝一直养活着他的麻雀，海里也捕出过一样好的鱼。可是四面八方都有一种接不上茬的感觉，一种不安（dis-ease）。一个纪元的末尾，另一个纪元的开头。一切价值的重估。这些话，若是你当得起真，倒也自有分量。意义的意义？一种新价值的发现，一种领会万物的不同方式。她但愿大地别忽然显得脆弱，仿佛就要挪动起来。每一件表象都是如此。她知道那不过是太阳，在给它晒干的东西上光。连她的脸也算在内——脂粉叫汗水沤得发了腻。他们全体总有点不对，或是他们的世界有点不对。一个错过的时刻，一个将至的时刻。或者不来。或者二者居一，或者兼而有之。把这推给战争吧；可那也无济于事。

===Original===
Only Ross was all right﻿—He never wanted anything that he did not get. Life had given it up and paid over Ross’s stakes, because once his strong appetites were satisfied, he did not want anything in human life at all. It was something to eat and drink, to embrace and paint. Apart from that, he knew something that she was only growing conscious of. And wouldn’t tell. Not he﻿—laughed at her for not knowing, and for wanting to know.

===Chinese===
只有罗斯安然无恙——他从不想要一样他得不到的东西。生活认了输，把罗斯的赌注如数付清，因为他那些强烈的胃口一旦餍足，人间的生活，他便什么都不想要了。生活于他，不过是拿来吃喝、拥抱和描绘的东西。撇开这个不谈，他还知道一样她才刚刚开始意识到的东西。却不肯说。他才不说——倒要笑话她不知道，笑话她想知道。

===Original===
Felix was quite different. Felix was scared. Fear made him brittle and angry and unjust. Without faith.

===Chinese===
菲利克斯全然不同。菲利克斯心里害怕。恐惧叫他易碎、易怒、不公正。失了信仰。

===Original===
Faith was necessary for the knowledge of God. Only, there were fifty good reasons for supporting the nonexistence of God. Besides, no one wanted to believe that any more. That was the point. And it was a shame for those two men to make her go all that way through a valley, while they were grubbing about in the wind. The next stile was a beast. She crossed it heavily. A long corner to turn, and there would be Starn to look at. There was that horse again, knotted and stiff and staring at her. It was too far to come. Miles behind her, a white road stood on its head over the hill that led to the green road that led to their house. She had come down that road, a long time ago, turning her back on the sea, to get to Starn, to meet an American, who would like her, not for long, and no one else﻿—Someone had barbed-wired the gap, damn him! She flung herself on her back and wriggled under, jumping up with too great an effort. What was she really doing, out in this burning valley at midday? They force me with more virtue than is convenient to me. Not innocently﻿—How can we be innocent? I am going to let things go. A witch and a bitch they call me. They shall see. She flung into the inn at Starn, ashamed of her appearance, red and dusty, and ordered a long drink.

===Chinese===
信仰是认识上帝所必需的。只不过，支持上帝不存在的理由，足足有五十条。再说，如今也没有谁愿意再信那个了。症结就在这里。可那两个男人也真不害臊，叫她穿谷越岭走那么远的路，自己却在风里扒来扒去。下一道栅栏坎真是个畜生。她沉甸甸地跨了过去。再拐过一道长弯，就望得见斯塔恩了。那匹马又在那里了，筋节虬结，僵直地瞪着她。这一路委实太远了。在她身后好几英里，一条白色公路倒竖在山坡上——那条路通向绿车道，绿车道通向他们的宅子。很久以前，她就是顺着那条路下来的，背对着海，为的是到斯塔恩来接一个美国人——那人会喜欢她，喜欢不了多久，旁的谁也不会——有人拿铁丝网把缺口封上了，该死的！她仰面往地上一摔，扭着身子从网底下钻过去，跳起来时用力过猛。晌午时分，独自一个，在这火烧火燎的山谷里，她究竟在干什么？他们拿多于我消受得起的德行逼迫我。并非天真无邪——我们怎么可能天真？我要撒手不管了。女巫，母狗，他们这样叫我。叫他们瞧吧。她一头冲进斯塔恩的客栈，为自己的模样害臊——又红又土——要了一大杯解渴的冷饮。

## III / 三、井底得杯

===Original===
Cool, rested, made up, she went to the station. It is always pleasant to collect someone expected out of a train. She wished it had been someone she wanted, someone known or necessary to be known. Michael, who went with the house﻿—Tony, she wished to know better﻿—Vincent, she might get off with﻿—the peacocks of her world. Then she reminded herself of the pleasure it would be to show a stranger their land, as they knew it, equivocal, exquisite. From what she had observed of Americans, almost certain to be new.

===Chinese===
凉爽，歇足了精神，妆也化好了，她动身去车站。把一个预期中的人从火车上接出来，总归是桩愉快的事。她但愿接的是她想要的人，一个早已相识、或不结识不可的人。迈克尔（Michael）——随宅子一并附带的那位；托尼（Tony），她倒想多结识几分；文森特（Vincent），她没准能跟他厮混出个名堂——她那个世界里的孔雀们。随即她提醒自己：领一个陌生人去看他们的这片土地，按他们自己认得的样子去看——暧昧幽微，精美绝伦——这也是一桩乐事。照她对美国人的观察，这东西几乎必然是新鲜的。

===Original===
Then she was flying through lanes, an attentive, intelligent old-young man beside her.

===Chinese===
随后她驾着车在乡巷间飞驰，身边坐着一个殷勤、聪明、少年老成的男人。

===Original===
“God! What a beautiful place,” he said. When “beautiful” is said, exactly and honestly, there is contact, or there should be. Then, “This is the England we think of. Hardy’s country, isn’t it?”

===Chinese===
“天哪！多美的地方，”他说。当“美”这个字说得又准确又诚恳的时候，人与人之间就有了接触——或者说，理应如此。接着他又说：“这就是我们心目中的英格兰。哈代（Hardy）笔下的乡野，对吧？”

===Original===
“Yes, don’t rely too much on the weather and the food.”

===Chinese===
“是啊，只是别太指望这里的天气和吃食。”

===Original===
“Don’t worry about me. I’ve done some camping.”

===Chinese===
“不用替我操心。我露营是露过的。”

===Original===
Nice man. But when he stood in the verandah and looked about him, he said: “I couldn’t have imagined it.”

===Chinese===
人不错。可是等他站到游廊上、四下里张望的时候，他却说：“这是我想都想象不出来的。”

===Original===
At tea, he said: “It seems to me that you have everything. No luxury, but all the beauty there is.”

===Chinese===
喝茶的时候，他说：“依我看，你们什么都不缺。没有奢华，可天下的美是一样不缺的。”

===Original===
Slightly overdoing the beauty business﻿—Beauty is a too concentrated food. And what did he mean about luxury? There was a sort of lean splendour about their things, anyhow. Still, his repose and his careful manners flattered her. She wondered when the men would be back, smelling of turf and thyme, and settle him in. Not a sign of them, and she’d told him they’d gone out to get mushrooms, usually picked at dawn. She took him up to his room and left him. Alone, he sat down on the bed, pensive. “Lord!” he said, “how did I get here?” The properties of the room included a bidet, a chart of the coast, and a still-life of poisonous-looking wood flowers, Felix’s work. He thought that the berries were deadly nightshade, which they were. He looked out of the window, the verandah roof sloping beneath him, of slate flags, patched and bound with lichens and ferns, and wondered when and how it had all been put together. His eyes travelled to a yucca, bent like an old man, and opening in a single three-foot spike. Then the wood. He had come out of simple curiosity, and to see something in England off the regulation road. So that was what this Paris bunch did when they got home? What did they do? What was there to do? Where were the men who had asked him? Some kind of trick to leave him alone with the girl?

===Chinese===
美丽这桩生意，他做得略微过了火——美是一种太浓烈的食品。至于他说的奢华是什么意思？反正他们的东西自有一种清瘦的光彩。不过，他那份安详、那副小心周到的举止，倒叫她受用。她纳闷男人们几时回来，身上带着草皮和百里香的气味，好把他安顿妥当。可连个人影也没有，而她已经告诉他，他们出去采蘑菇了——蘑菇通常天一亮就采。她领他上楼到他的房间里，便离开了。独自一人，他坐到床上，出了神。“老天爷！”他说，“我这是怎么跑到这儿来的？”这间房的物件里有一只坐浴盆、一幅海岸海图，还有一幅画着形迹可疑的有毒林花的静物，出自菲利克斯（Felix）之手。他以为那些浆果是颠茄，它们也确实是颠茄。他望向窗外，游廊的屋顶在脚下倾斜着，石板铺就，补丁累累，边缘又让地衣和蕨草箍住，他纳闷这一切是几时、又是怎么拼凑到一处的。他的目光移到一株丝兰上，它弯得像个老人，正抽出一支三英尺高的独穗来开花。再往前，就是树林了。他此行原是出于单纯的好奇，也想看看英国偏离常规路线的东西。原来这帮巴黎客回了家，过的就是这种日子？他们都干些什么？又有什么可干的？邀他来的那几个男人上哪儿去了？把他一个人撂在姑娘身边，算是什么圈套？

===Original===
Getting mushrooms? He decided, for the first time, that mushrooms grew. And that he must carry on, attentively. With immense deliberation the sun was moving west. He stretched his neck out of the window, and saw the crest of the down turn black, and draw up like a tower. He drew in his head. He did not want to see that hill with the stars trembling over it. How did they light the place? *I know moonlight, I know starlight.* Very sensitive to the arts, he admitted that he soon might be justified in singing that. *Lay this body down.* What an idea, but he might soon have to do it. To him, straight from London, Paris, and New York, the silence was intolerable.

===Chinese===
采蘑菇去了？他有生以来头一回认定：蘑菇是长出来的。并且认定，自己必须打点精神，悉力周旋下去。太阳正以极从容的步子向西行。他把脖子伸出窗外，看见丘冈的脊线转黑，像一座塔似的往上拔。他把头缩了回来。他不想看见那座山——星星在山顶上颤动。他们拿什么照亮这个地方？*我知道月光，我知道星光。*他自认对各门艺术极为敏感，于是承认：用不了多久，他唱这句话就算师出有名了。*把这身皮囊放倒。*好一个念头，可他说不定很快就得照办。对他这么一个从伦敦（London）、巴黎（Paris）、纽约（New York）直接过来的人来说，这份寂静无法忍受。

===Original===
The wood sighed at him. Just like that. Two kinds of life he did not want. The ash-fair tree-tall young woman downstairs, and the elaborate piece of leaf and wood, that was one thing and many. The wood and the woman might be interchangeable, and it wasn’t the sort of thing you wanted on a visit. He had nerves, too, a great sensibility to take impressions. Always in relation to people. Life to him was an elaborate theatre, without scenery. Here the scenery seemed to be the play.

===Chinese===
树林朝他叹息。就这么直截了当。两种他不想沾的生命。楼下那个白蜡木般白皙、树一般高挑的年轻女人，和那件叶子与木头的精工造物——那是一件东西，也是许多东西。树林和这女人说不定能互换，而做客的时候，谁也不想碰上这种事。他也是有神经的人，一副感受印象的敏锐官能。只是永远系在人与人的关系上头。对他来说，生活是一出精工细作的戏，不带布景。在这儿，布景倒似乎成了戏本身。

===Original===
He got as far as that when he remembered that downstairs there would be certainly something to drink, and began to change, beautifying himself, scrupulously and elaborately as a cat.

===Chinese===
他想到这一步，忽然记起楼下肯定有喝的东西，便着手更衣，像猫一样一丝不苟、精雕细琢地装扮起自己来。

===Original===
He had a cocktail; he had two. A woman came in. Scylla told him she was her old nurse. Was it truth, or a comedy, when she said:

===Chinese===
他喝了一杯鸡尾酒；喝了两杯。一个女人走了进来。斯库拉（Scylla）告诉他，这是她的老保姆。她说下面这句话的时候，是实情，还是一出喜剧：

===Original===
“I found the lobster and the fish Mr. Felix got in the ditch.”

===Chinese===
“菲利克斯先生从沟里弄来的那只龙虾和鱼，是我找回来的。”

===Original===
And Scylla answered:

===Chinese===
斯库拉答道：

===Original===
“Where are Mr. Felix and Mr. Ross?”

===Chinese===
“菲利克斯先生和罗斯（Ross）先生在哪儿？”

===Original===
And the nurse had said:

===Chinese===
保姆便说：

===Original===
“You never know. I’ll bring in dinner.”

===Chinese===
“这谁说得准。我这就端晚饭进来。”

===Original===
So they ate together; an eatable meal, fresh-tasting wine, and the inevitable whisky after. A rabbit crossed the lawn. A rat came under the verandah and stole a piece of bread. Two bats flew in. Scylla said:

===Chinese===
于是他们一起吃了饭；一顿还吃得下去的饭，酒入口清新，饭后照例是免不了的威士忌。一只兔子穿过草坪。一只老鼠钻到游廊底下，偷走一块面包。两只蝙蝠飞了进来。斯库拉说：

===Original===
“They’re full of lice, worse luck.”

===Chinese===
“它们满身都是虱子，真倒霉。”

===Original===
Nothing went on happening: the delicate quiet waited on them.

===Chinese===
再没有事情发生：那精微的寂静在一旁伺候着他们。

===Original===
“I expect,” said Scylla, “that they went over to Tollerdown, and found our friends had come to the cottage.”

===Chinese===
“我猜呀，”斯库拉说，“他们到托勒当（Tollerdown）去了，发现咱们的朋友住进了村舍。”

===Original===
He thought: The mushrooms are wearing thin. “Where is Tollerdown?”

===Chinese===
他心想：蘑菇这套说辞快穿帮了。“托勒当在哪儿？”

===Original===
“One of the hills in this part of the world. You know this country was given its first human character in the late stone age. That’s all the earthworks and barrows you see. Two of our friends have a cottage there. They dress up like the Prince of Wales, and quarrel like dogs. It will be fun if they are there.”

===Chinese===
“这一带山冈里的一座。你要知道，这个国家最初的人性，是石器时代晚期赋予的。你看见的那些土木工事和坟冢，全打那儿来。咱们的朋友里头，有两个在那儿有座村舍。他们打扮得像威尔士亲王，吵起架来像狗。他们要是在那儿，可有热闹瞧了。”

===Original===
Well, it might be. Anything which would give Dudley Carston a human scene. And if there was one thing in history one could hope was over, it was the stone age. But the young woman’s mind was distracted by the thought of it. She was laughing to herself. Laughing at the stone age. Real, abstract laughter. She had forgotten he was there. That her brother and her friend had disappeared. She might be mad, but she was good-looking. Women lovely and mad, or only lovely and only mad, should not be left alone in woods. Literature did not help him. He could only think of La Belle Dame sans merci, and she wasn’t that kind. She should think of him as a real man, not one of her flighty shadows too careless to be there to receive the stranger they had invited to follow them some hundred miles.

===Chinese===
唔，也许会有吧。随便什么，只要能给达德利·卡斯顿（Dudley Carston）一个有人气的场面就行。要说历史上有一桩事人们还能指望它翻篇，那就是石器时代。可一想到这个，年轻女人的心思就飘走了。她在自顾自地笑。笑那石器时代。实实在在的、抽象的笑。她忘了他在跟前。忘了她弟弟和她那位朋友都不见了。她兴许是疯了，可她好看。又美又疯的女人——或者只美不疯、只疯不美的——都不该被单独留在树林里。文学帮不上他的忙。他只想得起《无情的妖女》（La Belle Dame sans merci），而她又不是那一路。她该把他当作一个真实的男人，而不是她那些轻浮的影子中的一个——那些影子请了客人跟着他们跑上一百来英里，自己却粗心得连出面接待都不肯。

===Original===
“Shall I go and look for them?” he said.

===Chinese===
“要不要我去找找他们？”他说。

===Original===
“Where?” she said﻿—That brought it back﻿—“The openness here is deceptive, and they might come a hundred ways. I’m ashamed of their manners.”

===Chinese===
“上哪儿找？”她说——这话把事情又拉了回来——“这地方看着四通八达，其实靠不住，他们随便哪条道都回得来。他们那副做派，我都替他们害臊。”

===Original===
She was telling herself: Something has happened, I think. I told myself this morning I’d watch the scene and not try to make it right. My boy friends can go hang.

===Chinese===
她在心里对自己说：出事了，我想。今天早上我对自己说过，这出戏我只看，不去动手收拾。我那些男朋友，爱吊死去吧。

===Original===
The silence went away, and left nothing.

===Chinese===
寂静走开了，什么也没有留下。

===Original===
There was an iron clang. Carston sat tight.

===Chinese===
铁器哐啷一响。卡斯顿（Carston）端坐不动。

===Original===
“It’s the gate on to the grass,” she said, “here they are.”

===Chinese===
“是通往草场的那扇门，”她说，“他们回来了。”

===Original===
Two heavy men syncopating their walk. Must be a march of trolls in the night through the wood. Nothing natural was coming. Four tall young men crowded into the room.

===Chinese===
两个沉重的男人，把脚步走出了切分音。准是巨魔夜里穿林行军的队伍。走进来的没有一样是自然的。四个高大的青年挤进了房间。

===Original===
“So you’ve collected Carston?”

===Chinese===
“这么说，你把卡斯顿接到手啦？”

===Original===
The men from Tollerdown, of course.

===Chinese===
托勒当来的人，还用说。

===Original===
He saw three men about thirty years old. One tall and black, with close-set eyes and a walk affected to hide his strength, called Clarence. One rougher, shorter, fairer, better bred, called Ross. Then a boy, Scylla’s brother Felix Taverner, the English peach in flower, lapis-eyes, the gold hair already thinning where the temples should have been thatched. Then, last, the tallest they called Picus, grave as a marsh-bird dancing and as liable to agitation, his colour drawn from the moon’s palette, steel gilt and pale, the skin warmed to gold by the weather, cooled to winter in the dark crystal eyes.

===Chinese===
他看见三个三十岁上下的男人。一个高个黑发，两眼挤得很近，走起路来刻意做作，好掩住自己那一身力气，叫克拉伦斯（Clarence）。一个更粗豪、更矮、发色更浅、家教更好，叫罗斯。再是一个少年，斯库拉的弟弟菲利克斯·塔弗纳（Felix Taverner），一朵开到盛处的英国蜜桃，一双青金石的眼睛，鬓角本该让金发苫得厚厚的地方，头发已经稀疏了。最后是最高的那个，他们管他叫皮库斯（Picus），庄重得像一只跳着舞的沼泽鸟，也一样说激动就激动，他的颜色取自月亮的调色板，钢上镀金，泛着苍白，皮肤叫天气烘成金色，在那双暗色水晶的眼睛里，又冷成了冬天。

===Original===
Clarence and Picus crowding off to eat in the kitchen. Scylla followed them, but came back.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯和皮库斯挤到厨房吃东西去了。斯库拉跟了过去，又折了回来。

===Original===
“Something has happened, I think. If it’s what I think it is, it will be a diversion for you.”

===Chinese===
“出事了，我想。要真是我猜的那件事，倒够给你解个闷。”

===Original===
Not so sure, he waited. They came back. Their fatigue was different from his, an affair of the muscles. They seemed drunk on fresh air. He found himself faced with his usual problem, how to make a fresh event serve his turn, relate it strictly to personalities, especially his own.

===Chinese===
他可没这么大把握，便等着。他们回来了。他们的疲乏跟他的不是一路，是肌肉上的事。他们像是灌饱了新鲜空气，醉了。他发现自己又面对着那道老题目：怎么让一桩新鲜事变成为己所用，把它严丝合缝地挂到各个人物身上，尤其是他自己身上。

===Original===
That was the situation for him, as he listened, translating, to the story Felix had to tell. Felix said that Ross and he had been to a place called Gault, and he’d sung to it. Presumably a dangerous place. They had then decided to call on distant friends, who might or might not be inhabiting a cottage on a place called Tollerdown. Anyhow, supposing they were not there, a rare species of hawk known as a honey-buzzard might be observed in the vicinity. On arriving they had found their friends (Scylla seemed to be the only woman in the group, a point for reflection) in difficulties owing to their well, shrunk by the drought, yielding nothing but dead hedgehogs. A digression on the use of soda-water to make tea. An excursion down the well to clean out the hedgehogs had led to a discovery. An odd cup of some greenish stone had been found, rather like pea-soup carnelian. The state of the well had necessitated the transfer of Picus and Clarence for an indefinite stay. “You’re done in this country if your well gives out. Wait till ours does.” Carston was not interested. This might interfere with his making love to Scylla, which he had decided was to be his expression of a successful visit. Unless he found out how to use it.

===Chinese===
这就是他的处境——他一面听、一面在心里翻译，听菲利克斯讲那段他非讲不可的故事。菲利克斯说，罗斯和他去了一个叫高尔特（Gault）的地方，他还对着它唱了歌。多半是个凶险的地方。随后他们决定去拜访远处的朋友，那些人也许住、也许没住在托勒当的一座村舍里。反正，就算扑了空，附近兴许还能观察到一种珍稀的鹰，名叫蜂鹰（honey-buzzard）。到了那儿，他们发现朋友们（这一伙里，斯库拉似乎是唯一的女人，此点可供玩味）正陷在困境里，因为他们的井遭了旱、缩了水，打上来的除了死刺猬什么都没有。中间岔开一笔，谈了用苏打水沏茶的用法。一趟下井清刺猬的探险，引出了一项发现：一只某种泛绿石头做的古怪的杯被找到了，颇像豌豆汤色的玛瑙。井的状况迫使皮库斯和克拉伦斯搬了过去长住，住到几时没个准话。“在这地界儿，井要是断了水，你就完了。等着咱这口井断水吧。”卡斯顿不感兴趣。这兴许会碍着他向斯库拉求爱——那件事他已认定，是他此行圆满的表达。除非他能想出怎么利用这件事。

===Original===
Then Ross produced the cup suddenly, out of his pocket, and handed it round. Carston said:

===Chinese===
接着罗斯忽然把那只杯从口袋里掏了出来，递给大家传看。卡斯顿说：

===Original===
“That means nothing to me.”

===Chinese===
“这玩意儿对我说来毫无意义。”

===Original===
“Been cut by hand,” said Felix. “Is there a kind of opaque flint glass? Celtic twiddles, I think, very worn round the rim.”

===Chinese===
“是手工琢出来的，”菲利克斯说。“有没有一种不透明的燧石玻璃？我看是凯尔特式的旋纹花样，杯口那一圈磨得厉害。”

===Original===
A good deal was told Carston, casually, about Celts and Saxons and Romans and early Christianity; things completely over so far as he knew﻿—Not that they talked about what he hadn’t heard. Only they talked as if there was no time, no progress, no morality. He knew, of course, that there was no progress, and no morality.

===Chinese===
大家有一搭没一搭地给卡斯顿讲了不少凯尔特人、撒克逊人、罗马人和早期基督教的事；就他所知，这些都是早已彻底了结的东西——倒不是他们谈了什么他没听说过的。只是他们谈起来，仿佛没有时间，没有进步，没有道德。他当然知道，本来就没有进步，也没有道德。

===Original===
Then Ross said, roughly and softly, as though he was loving something:

===Chinese===
随后罗斯开了口，嗓音粗粝而温柔，仿佛正爱着什么东西：

===Original===
“The thing was that we fished it out with a spear.”

===Chinese===
“要紧的是，我们是用一杆矛把它捞上来的。”

===Original===
Scylla said, “Ross, that’s odd.”

===Chinese===
斯库拉说：“罗斯，这就怪了。”

===Original===
Clarence fidgeted attentively. Felix stared, and Carston saw the boy’s tricky brilliant eyes light up. Picus was grave, a man so tall and thin he seemed to go on forever. Unnaturally supple, he had seen him pick up something behind him as if it had been in front. He tried to think what a spear had to do with it.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯专心致志地坐立不安起来。菲利克斯直勾勾地盯着看，卡斯顿看见这少年那双狡黠明亮的眼睛点起了光。皮库斯神情肃穆，一个高瘦得仿佛没有尽头的男人。他柔韧得反乎自然——卡斯顿亲眼见过他弯身拾起背后的东西，轻松得仿佛那东西就在身前。他试图想明白，一杆矛跟这件事有什么相干。

===Original===
Felix said, sharply:

===Chinese===
菲利克斯尖刻地说：

===Original===
“Good old Freud.”

===Chinese===
“好一个老弗洛伊德（Freud）。”

===Original===
“Idiot!” said Ross, and turned away furious and contemptuous.

===Chinese===
“白痴！”罗斯说着，又怒又鄙地扭过头去。

===Original===
“It seems to me,” said Scylla, “that people had to start some way of thinking of things. What they saw once they’d learned to think might be quite different from the things they’d learned on.”

===Chinese===
“照我想，”斯库拉说，“人们当初总得先有个起头，好去想事情。等学会了想，他们看见的东西，跟他们学着去想时所依凭的东西，可能是全然不同的两回事。”

===Original===
Then, to Carston, she said that odd things were always happening, and old patterns repeated themselves. That it was sometimes alarming when they did, and Freud very useful in the case of irrational fear. Very true, too, when there had been a row, and no one could feel what was just and what was not. Always look out for the suppressed wish that’s taken a wrong turning. But that what had happened today was objective and odd.

===Chinese===
接着，她对卡斯顿说，怪事总在发生，老花样会自己重演。重演起来有时吓人，碰上无端的恐惧，弗洛伊德十分有用。吵过一场架、谁也说不清什么是公正什么不是的时候，这话也同样对。永远要提防那条拐错了弯的、被压抑的愿望。只是今天发生的这件事，是客观的，也是古怪的。

===Original===
Carston said:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿说：

===Original===
“I think I’ll have to ask you to explain a little more than that.”

===Chinese===
“我想，只好请你解释得再多一点了。”

===Original===
But Ross had turned round again. “I’m awfully sorry,” he said. The insolent insincerity was not meant to be lost on Carston, but it was. “Put it down to the solstice or the heat.”

===Chinese===
可罗斯又转回来了。“实在对不住，”他说。那股傲慢的虚情假意，本是存心要卡斯顿领会的，可惜他没有领会。“就记在夏至头上吧，要么记在天热头上。”

===Original===
“Tell us the news,” said Felix. “We couldn’t get back without our tea. Ross believes in perspiration. I don’t.”

===Chinese===
“给我们讲讲新闻，”菲利克斯说。“不喝上茶，我们是回不来的。罗斯信奉流汗。我不信。”

===Original===
Carston had come with elaborations of the best gossip. They listened to him﻿—rather too attentively, he thought. At the same time there was something that spoiled his effects. It was the place, the faintly lit room mixing with the starlight outside. A shallow little green dish was lying among the glasses. Might have been made out of star-material. The woman had called it a diversion, but they weren’t going to let him play. He began suddenly to dislike them, wish to humiliate them. Far too troubled to think how to do it.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿来时是备足了上好闲话的。他们听他讲——他觉得，未免听得也太用心了些。但同时，有什么东西在毁他的效果。是这个地方，是这间微光里的屋子同外头的星光交融难分。一只浅浅的绿盘子正躺在酒杯中间。简直像拿星星的材料做的。那女人方才管它叫解闷，可他们并不打算放他进场玩。他忽然开始不喜欢他们了，想羞辱他们。心里又乱得厉害，想不出怎么个羞辱法。

===Original===
Even Ross saw there was something wrong when he left them and went up to bed.

===Chinese===
等他撇下他们、上楼睡觉去的时候，连罗斯都看出有哪儿不对劲了。

===Original===
But this Carston had seen. Four ways of saying the woman good night. Ross nodded to her. Felix embraced her. Clarence kissed her gallantly, with a flourish indicating affectionate indifference to their difference of sex. Picus, busy with a syphon, crooked his forefinger at her across the room.

===Chinese===
但这一层，卡斯顿看在眼里了。向那女人道晚安，有四种道法。罗斯朝她点了点头。菲利克斯拥抱了她。克拉伦斯潇洒地吻了她，手势一挥，表明他对两人性别之别怀着深情厚谊的满不在乎。皮库斯正忙着摆弄苏打水瓶，隔着屋子朝她勾了勾食指。

## In and Out / 里里外外

===Original===
On his way down the drive Carston knew what you did. In Trollope, in cases of spiritual difficulty, you consulted the vicar. Whether it turned out well depended on whether you found a good vicar or a bad. The landlord directed him. His way led through the churchyard. He noticed a staring white monument, and read on it the name of Picus’s mother.

===Chinese===
顺着车道往下走的当口，卡斯顿知道这种事该怎么办。在特罗洛普（Trollope）笔下，遇上心灵上的难处，就去请教牧师。结果好坏，全看你遇上的是位好牧师还是坏牧师。客栈老板给他指了路。这条路要穿过教堂墓地。他注意到一座白得扎眼的纪念碑，碑上刻着皮库斯母亲的名字。

===Original===
“Old devil to bury her like that and keep it clean.” In the flagged hall, he walked up a ribbon of green matting, and saw at the end Picus playing with a blind cord.

===Chinese===
“老魔鬼，倒把她这样葬了，还把碑拾掇得干干净净。”在石板铺地的门厅里，他沿着一长条绿色地垫走过去，望见尽头处皮库斯正在摆弄一根百叶帘拉绳。

===Original===
“Tracy,” he said, “I’ve been trying to clear this up.”

===Chinese===
“特雷西，”他说，“我一直在设法把这件事弄个水落石出。”

===Original===
“Any luck?”

===Chinese===
“有眉目吗？”

===Original===
“None. Have you a good vicar?”

===Chinese===
“没有。你们这儿有位好牧师吗？”

===Original===
“I left the churchyard at two in the morning, and said ‘It’s me,’ and I’ve been here since. Sick of night and mist walking. I’ve told him. Come in.”

===Chinese===
“我凌晨两点才离开墓地，说了声‘是我’，打那以后就一直待在这儿。夜里顶着雾走路，走得腻透了。我什么都告诉他了。进来吧。”

---

===Original===
Felix woke, rolled over in a flood of gold-spangled dust to find himself lapped in faultless health and spirits. Paris’ morning surprise for her children, last night’s debauch innocent as a game of kiss-in-the-ring. Last night’s resolution clarified and unimpaired. He had Boris to find and explain to him what he had meant. Claim his own. He shaved his hardly perceptible beard, whistling an air from *Louise*. Paris was waiting for him, had given him the day, now in midmorning, which would only be begun by night.

===Chinese===
菲利克斯醒来，在一泓金屑闪烁的浮尘里翻了个身，发觉自己周身浸润在无懈可击的康健与兴致之中。这是巴黎清晨给她孩子们的意外之喜：昨夜的纵饮胡闹，天真得像一场转圈接吻的游戏。昨夜定下的决心，愈发澄明，分毫无损。他还有鲍里斯要找，要向他解释自己那些话的意思。认领回他自己的人。他刮着那圈几乎看不出的髭须，口哨吹着《露易丝》（*Louise*）里的一支曲调。巴黎正等着他，已经把这一天——此刻才过半上午——交给了他，而这一天，非得到夜里才算真正开场。

===Original===
He ran through his pockets. Boris’s address was lost. There were names of unknowns, scrawled on the cabaret cards, not the shred off a bill he remembered, the splendid name scrawled in sucked purple pencil﻿—He rushed out to find his earlier boy friend.

===Chinese===
他把口袋搜了一遍。鲍里斯的地址丢了。只剩些不相识者的名字，潦草涂在卡巴莱的卡片上，偏偏没有他记得的那片账单残角，没有那个用吮咬过的紫铅笔涂下的漂亮名字——他冲出门去，找他先前那位少年朋友。

===Original===
“My dear Felix, how should I know? Those boys sleep anywhere. You might try the quays. You’ll see him about again some night.”

===Chinese===
“亲爱的菲利克斯，我怎么会知道？那种孩子随便哪儿都睡。你不妨去码头碰碰运气。哪天夜里，你准会再撞见他。”

===Original===
“I don’t mean to wait﻿—I’ve got to find him, if I go to the police.”

===Chinese===
“我不打算等——我非找到他不可，哪怕去报警。”

===Original===
“Well, I shouldn’t do that﻿—They probably know too much about him.” Then, incautiously﻿—“He’s probably pretty sick after last night. They say his lungs are going.”

===Chinese===
“要我说，你可别报警——警察对他的底细，只怕知道得太多。”接着，一句失言——“昨晚过后，他八成病得不轻。都说他的肺快垮了。”

===Original===
Curiously, that fanned Felix. The older boy for the first time liked him well. Wondered if by any chance he saw in his eyes what “one would fain call master.” It was odd.

===Chinese===
说来奇怪，这话倒把菲利克斯心头的火扇旺了。那位年长的少年头一回真心喜欢上了他，暗自纳闷：自己方才在那双眼睛里瞥见的，会不会正是那种“人人甘愿称之为主人”的东西。真是奇怪。

===Original===
“He used to live somewhere in the nest of hotels round the Rue Bonaparte.”

===Chinese===
“他从前住在波拿巴街（Rue Bonaparte）一带那窝小旅馆里的什么地方。”

===Original===
“Good,” said Felix. “I’ll try them, one by one.”

===Chinese===
“好，”菲利克斯说，“我就一家一家地找。”

---

===Original===
Boris woke up. The young head, a little brutal and afraid in sleep, on waking lost those expressions. “*Comme j’ai fait la bombe hier*,” rubbing his eyes like a baby. A sixth-floor room in a cheap hotel in old Paris has no romantic quality. It was as much as Boris could do to rise nightly like some sort of phoenix out of the ashes of old clothes, torn socks, and Russian newspapers. A miserably recognisable room, in a bitter world now burning under the sun-cracked roof, with no room in it for penniless, palace-reared brats. No excuse for the room either. A shameless, shameful pity of disorder, and want not above the trickery. Only on the lavabo shelf there was some sort of order, and a glass full of brushes for the bright white teeth.

===Chinese===
鲍里斯醒了。那颗年轻的脑袋，睡着时带着几分凶横与惶恐，一醒来，这些神色便都褪尽了。“瞧我昨晚闹腾的那一场”（*Comme j’ai fait la bombe hier*），他像婴孩似的揉着眼睛。老巴黎一家廉价旅馆的六楼房间，毫无浪漫可言。鲍里斯夜夜从旧衣裳、破袜子和俄文报纸的灰烬里，像某种凤凰似的振身而起，已是竭尽全力。一间一看就知道有多惨的屋子，坐落在一个刻薄的世界里，此刻正在晒裂的屋顶下蒸烤，容不下身无分文、宫殿里养大的娃娃。这屋子自己也没个辩白。一副不要脸的、丢人现眼的狼藉，穷到不惮耍些手腕。只有盥洗盆的搁板上还有几分秩序，一只玻璃杯里插满刷子，伺候那口雪白发亮的牙。

===Original===
What sort of boy was the English boy? Half Boris’s nature was curious, the other﻿—it was a nature profoundly divided﻿—had no interest. The indifference lay beneath, the under-waters of the stream that ran in and out, up so many curious creeks, round islands not fixed yet on any map.

===Chinese===
那个英国少年是种什么样的人？鲍里斯的天性一半好奇，另一半——那是一种深刻分裂的天性——毫无兴趣。冷淡伏在底下，是那条进进出出的河的水下潜流，溯上一条条古怪的港汊，绕过一座座还没标进任何地图的岛。

===Original===
His interest was chiefly whether the boy would be good for a few nights of Boris’s necessities; some food, unlimited drink, no sympathy, but a kind of companionship. Charm was what he liked, who had it for sole asset. Someone to laugh with. He would have laughed if he could have seen Felix, followed by the taxi he was too impatient to jump in and out of, entering and leaving shabby door after shabby door. He lay on his back and dodged the flies. He was very tired after four years of Paris. Four actual years, but he had never been able to calculate more than a day ahead, which must set ordinary time going differently. Also, what time had he in the sense of future, a bloody curtain between him and his land, his torn roots not fed by the transplanting? Over two hundred francs (the compatriot chauffeur had been merciful) of the English boy’s money stuffed away. It might also have startled him had he seen Felix a door nearer, a door further off. One hundred and fifty francs to pacify the old *vache* in the bureau below. If misery had turned the key on him, what of it? Get the second best shoes out of the cobblers. Forget till the money was gone, and in the next spell of rain take cold and go about sore-throated and aching. Till it was time to drink again. Drink to forget. Forget what did not matter. Yet if a fly brushed his caste dignity, he would rage childishly, and laugh and forgive and not forget.

===Chinese===
他感兴趣的，主要是这少年能不能供他几夜之需：一点吃食，不限量的酒，不要同情，但要一种作伴。他喜欢的是有魅力的人，而他自己全部的家当，也只有魅力二字。得有个能一同笑的人。他若能瞧见菲利克斯那副模样，准会笑出声来：一辆出租车跟在那孩子身后，他却急得懒得跳上跳下，只顾一扇接一扇破旧的门进进出出。他仰面躺着，躲苍蝇。在巴黎熬满四年，他累极了。货真价实的四年，可他从没能盘算到一天以后的事，这想必叫寻常时间换了种走法。再说，就“将来”的意义而言，他又能有什么时间？一面血淋淋的幕隔在他和故土之间，他被撕断的根，移了栽也没得着滋养。那英国少年的钱，两百多法郎（开车的同乡总算发过慈悲）已经塞好了。他若瞧见菲利克斯离他近了一扇门、又远了一扇门，怕也会吓一跳。还有一百五十法郎，是安抚楼下账房那头老*母牛*（*vache*）的。就算穷苦已经拿钥匙把他锁死，那又如何？把那双次好的鞋从鞋匠那儿取出来。钱花完之前只管忘，等下一场雨来，就害场感冒，喉咙肿痛、浑身酸痛地四处走。直到又到了该喝的时候。喝酒是为了忘。忘掉无关紧要的事。可只要有只苍蝇拂着他的种姓尊严，他又会孩子气地暴跳如雷，接着大笑、原谅，却决不忘掉。

===Original===
Felix pitched into his room, his heart almost preceding him. Drew himself up and said languidly: “All right after last night?” Poverty that amazed him and he pitied, for a moment dismissing romance for sense.

===Chinese===
菲利克斯一头闯进他的房间，心几乎抢在人前头。他挺直身子，懒洋洋地问：“昨晚过后，还好么？”这份穷困令他吃惊，也令他怜悯，一时间，浪漫让位给了理智。

===Original===
“Get up and we’ll lunch. I’m not in Paris for long. I want you to come away with me.”

===Chinese===
“起来，咱们吃午饭去。我在巴黎待不长。我要你跟我一起走。”

===Original===
“Where?” said Boris.

===Chinese===
“去哪儿？”鲍里斯说。

===Original===
“To my people in England.”

===Chinese===
“去英格兰，我家里人那儿。”

===Original===
“I’ve always wanted to see England,” he said﻿—“we like the English best of all races.”

===Chinese===
“我一直想看看英格兰，”他说——“各民族里头，我们最喜欢英国人。”

===Original===
Russians do not gush, and he was arranging his shame about the room; the shirt he had worn last night all he had on him, and his feet grimy and no slippers, and how to turn himself into a desirable object with Felix there; and how to get his clothes out with the bill not paid if he was really going away; and how they couldn’t make Felix a cocktail, and would probably refuse to send up so much as a bottle of Vichy; and how soon or if it would be safe to tell Felix about it. And if Felix was really a bore or not. And, suddenly, how bad it was to have to think like that.

===Chinese===
俄国人不作兴满口甜言，他正忙着安顿自己为这间屋子起的羞惭：身上只有昨晚穿的那件衬衫，两脚污黑，连双拖鞋也没有；当着菲利克斯，怎样把自己拾掇成个叫人看得上眼的物件；倘若真要走，账没结，衣物怎么运出店门；这店里调不出一杯敬菲利克斯的鸡尾酒，恐怕连一瓶维希（Vichy）水都不肯往楼上送；这些事多早晚讲、讲不讲、几时讲了才稳当。还有菲利克斯到底算不算个乏味的人。还有——猛然间想到——非得这样想事情，有多糟。

===Original===
And Felix, brimmed with grace, said:

===Chinese===
而菲利克斯，雍容满身，说道：

===Original===
“This was just to tell you. Meet me at the Foyot in an hour.”

===Chinese===
“我这趟专为告诉你一声。一个钟头后，弗瓦约碰头。”

---

===Original===
In another room, in another hotel, in a chaos of elegant poverty, Scylla answered the telephone bell. Philip’s voice said:

===Chinese===
在另一家旅馆的另一间房里，在一派雅致的穷愁潦倒之中，斯库拉接起了电话。是菲利普的声音：

===Original===
“What made you let on you were engaged to Clarence the other night? He doesn’t seem to think so. Like to hear his letter?”

===Chinese===
“前几天晚上，你凭什么放出话说跟克拉伦斯订了婚？他本人好像不这么想。想不想听听他那封信？”

===Original===
She was ready for that, through the occasional sense that one has lived through an event before it arrives.

===Chinese===
这一着她早有防备，凭的是人偶尔会有的那种感觉：事情还没来，自己已经先经历过一遍。

===Original===
“What’s that? I said nothing of the sort. If Lydia likes to think such rubbish. She must have written him one of her letters.”

===Chinese===
“这话怎么说？我从没讲过这种话。莉迪亚爱信这种胡说，随她去。准是她又给他写了一封她那种信。”

===Original===
The voice changed:

===Chinese===
电话那头的声音变了：

===Original===
“I wish you hadn’t told her whatever you meant like that. She’s been upset.”

===Chinese===
“我但愿你不曾那样把不知什么意思的话说给她听。她心里一直不好受。”

===Original===
“Well, she might have married him herself once,” and rang off and sat still. This would not do. If their alliance was not to break up, she must get the idea out of Clarence’s head. Played into Clarence’s hands, she had: to finish her with Picus: give Clarence the game. Subtle-minded, he would know how to bitch things more than they were already bitched. All to bitch Lydia for bitching her. God, what a world! So much for Sanc-Grail cups and maidens. She felt positively superstitious over her own experiences. Then suffered under what seemed, after all, an unfair lack of grace. Then saw the cottage on Tollerdown, a desolation with something in it that raved. Translated it into possibility of annoyance, petty insult, even tragedy. Playing the Freud game, the name rose “Philoctetes.” Just the sort of role Clarence would pick and play badly. Did Philoctetes play well before he found his Sophocles? Oh! damn analogies. Better go down at once, make at worst an armed peace, and give him something else to think about.

===Chinese===
“哼，她自己当年差一点儿就嫁了他。”她挂上电话，坐着不动。这样可不行。她们这个同盟若不想散伙，就得把那个念头从克拉伦斯脑子里赶出去。她倒好，正把牌送进克拉伦斯手里：好断了她和皮库斯：把这一局让给克拉伦斯。他心思阴细，自然懂得怎么把已经搅坏的事搅得更坏。全为了作践莉迪亚，谁叫莉迪亚先作践她。天哪，这是个什么世道！什么圣杯呀少女呀，也就不过如此。她对自己的亲身经历，简直要迷信起来。接着，又为一桩终究似欠公道的恩宠之缺受着苦。随后，看见托勒当上的小屋，一片荒凉，屋里有什么东西在嘶号。把它译成种种可能：搅扰、小小的难堪，甚至悲剧。玩起弗洛伊德那套游戏，一个名字浮上来：“菲罗克忒忒斯”（Philoctetes）。正是克拉伦斯会挑中、又定会演砸的那种角色。菲罗克忒忒斯没遇上他的索福克勒斯（Sophocles）之前，戏演得好吗？哦！见鬼的类比。还是立刻南下，往最坏处说也结成个武装和平，再塞点别的事给他想。

===Original===
A telegram:

===Chinese===
一封电报：

===Original===
Arriving shortly with Russian ill to live with us.

===Chinese===
顷至，偕病俄人来家住。

===Original===
Nothing like Felix for letting you know. And Carston had gone to Tambourne with a plan. Picus might be there. Felix was coming back. All roads lay south again. Sleep at Starn that night and go over to Tollerdown in he morning. Have it out with Clarence. Might seduce Clarence and shut his mouth that way. Then to Tambourne and Carston’s news.

===Chinese===
论通风报信，谁也比不上菲利克斯。再说，卡斯顿已经揣着一个主意去了坦伯恩。皮库斯兴许也在那里。菲利克斯要回来了。条条大路又都朝南。当夜宿斯塔恩，明早过托勒当去。跟克拉伦斯把话挑明。兴许还能勾引克拉伦斯一场，好就此封住他的嘴。然后去坦伯恩，听卡斯顿的消息。

===Original===
With serene courage, for she was uneasy, she made her preparations.

===Chinese===
怀着安详的勇气——因为她心里其实不安——她收拾起行装。

---

===Original===
Picus introduced Carston to English ecclesiastical life. At last there would be something that he had been led to expect. The old man in his library looked and spoke right.

===Chinese===
皮库斯领卡斯顿见识了英国的教会生活。总算有一件他早被引得指望上的事了。老人坐在书房里，仪表谈吐，都对路。

===Original===
Picus said:

===Chinese===
皮库斯说：

===Original===
“I’ve told him all about it. He’s had the devil in his parish.”

===Chinese===
“我把事情原原本本告诉他了。他这教区里可是闹过魔鬼的。”

===Original===
“Well,” said the vicar, “I’ll go so far as to say that during the course of our long association, your father has illustrated my picture of hell. And, as usual, any heavenly landscape has been all around and so unobserved.” He examined the cup.

===Chinese===
“好吧，”牧师说，“我不妨把话说到这一步：在我们多年交往的整个过程中，令尊为我那幅地狱图景作了图解。而一如既往，天堂的景致四下环绕，也就无人看见。”他察看那只杯子。

===Original===
“I cannot tell you anything. A piece of worn jade, this time, for the question mark to the question we can none of us answer.”

===Chinese===
“我什么也告诉不了你们。这一回是块磨旧的玉，权当一个问号，扣在我们谁也答不上来的那个问题上。”

===Original===
“What is the question?” said Carston.

===Chinese===
“那问题是什么？”卡斯顿说。

===Original===
“Our old friend. Whether a true picture of the real is shown by our senses alone.”

===Chinese===
“我们的老朋友：单凭感官，究竟能不能显出一幅真实的实在之像。”

===Original===
“Can’t we leave it that we don’t know?”

===Chinese===
“咱们就不能安于不知、随它去吗？”

===Original===
“Then the picture we have becomes more and more unintelligible.”

===Chinese===
“那么我们手里这幅图，就越来越没法读懂了。”

===Original===
“I don’t know. All I can say is that I’ve never been so bothered, never behaved so like a skunk, never so nearly fell dead in my tracks till I got down here and began to think about such things. It’s unfashionable now, you know﻿—”

===Chinese===
“我不知道。我只能说，我从没这样烦恼过，从没把事情做得这么下作，也从没这么险些当场倒毙——直到我下到这地方，开始琢磨这类事情。要知道，如今这可不时兴了——”

===Original===
“Naturally,” said the second old man, so peaceful, so cordially, with such disinterestedness, with such interest. It was going to be a singular ecclesiastic this time. Old Mr. Tracy turned saint. Carston gave up trying to cut providence.

===Chinese===
“自然喽，”第二位老人说，那样平和，那样恳切，那样无所为，而又那样兴致盎然。这一回，要出一位非同寻常的教士了。老特雷西先生竟修成了圣徒。卡斯顿死了心，不再想跟天意耍老千。

===Original===
“Can you give us your professional view?” he said.

===Chinese===
“您能给我们一点行家之见吗？”他说。

===Original===
“My dear man, Picus comes here to be consoled for a grievance because he has given his heart away twice, and doesn’t know from which victim to ask it back. You ask my professional advice about this business of the cup; not only for its history, but on the spiritual upsets following its arrival. Here it is: say the seven penitential psalms: go carefully through your failings before man and God: communicate tomorrow at eight: come to matins and sing: attend to my sermon. In the evening sing the Magnificat and remember that when I dismiss you with the prayer Lighten our Darkness, I am saying the last word I know. (I suggest a day’s devotions because I am sure you have not done any for a long time.) Add to them the lovely sobriety of our church and our liturgy, the splendour of midsummer filtered through old glass on cold stone. That is as far as I can go in my profession, which, like the ancient mysteries, depends largely on what you bring to it. My hope is that some day somebody will bring something. In your case, Mr. Carston, clean hands and a pure heart I’ll be bound. I administer formulæ and recall memories﻿—that work and still live. In what lies the scientific triumph but that its formulæ work?”

===Chinese===
“亲爱的，皮库斯到这儿来，是为一桩委屈求安慰，因为他把心送出去过两回，又不知道该向哪位受害者讨回来。你们就杯子这桩事征求我的行家意见；不光为它的来历，还为它到来之后接连而起的心灵震荡。方子在这儿：诵那七篇忏悔诗篇：在人与上帝面前，把你们的过失细细省察：明晨八点领圣餐：来赴晨祷，同唱：细听我的讲道。傍晚唱《尊主颂》（Magnificat），并记住，当我用《求主照亮我们的黑暗》（Lighten our Darkness）那篇祷文放你们散去时，我说的已是我所知道的最后一句话。（我提议做一整天的灵修，因为我敢断定，你们很久没做过了。）在这些之外，再添上我们教会和我们礼仪那可爱的一本正经，添上仲夏的华彩透过老旧玻璃、滤落在冷石上的样子。在我的行当里，我能走到的就到这里；这行当，一如古代的密仪，大半要看你们往里带什么。我的指望是，有朝一日有谁带来点什么。至于您，卡斯顿先生，我敢保，是一双干净的手、一颗纯洁的心。我施行仪轨，唤回记忆——它们灵验，而且仍旧活着。科学的凯旋又何尝不在此——不就在它的公式灵验么？”

===Original===
No one on earth before had told Carston that he had a pure heart and clean hands. He was startled, touched, nearly cried, and said:

===Chinese===
世上还从没有一个人对卡斯顿说过，他有一颗纯洁的心、一双干净的手。他一惊，一动，几乎落下泪来，说道：

===Original===
“But we’re both in love with Scylla Taverner.”

===Chinese===
“可我们俩都爱着斯库拉·塔弗纳。”

===Original===
The second old man said:

===Chinese===
第二位老人说：

===Original===
“Well, I dare say she can do with two fine young men in love with her. She’s had no soft life, with her batch of demons.”

===Chinese===
“哦，我想两位出色的青年同时爱她，她受得住。她的日子从来不软和，还拖着她那一窝魔鬼。”

===Original===
“He means,” said Picus, “that I got off with her and he didn’t.”

===Chinese===
“他这是说，”皮库斯道，“我同她好过，他没有。”

===Original===
“I hope,” said the second old man, “that I’m being asked for my unprofessional opinion.”

===Chinese===
“我希望，”第二位老人说，“人家问我的，是行外的意见。”

===Original===
“We’re telling you,” said Picus. Carston’s courage jumped. He’d been told he had clean hands and a pure heart. Now that it had been pointed out, he saw that it was true.

===Chinese===
“我们什么话都告诉你。”皮库斯说。卡斯顿的胆气一跳。人家告诉了他，他有干净的手、纯洁的心。一经点明，他便看出这是真的。

===Original===
“Why are you so spiteful about me, Tracy? How do you think you’ll get the best out of a man if you fool him, and show you despise him and give your sweetheart away before him?”

===Chinese===
“你干吗对我这么刻毒，特雷西？你要是捉弄一个人，又让他看出你瞧不起他，还当着他的面把你心上人的事抖搂出去，你还指望从这人身上得到什么好处？”

===Original===
“Everyone goes to bed with me,” said Picus﻿—“always.”

===Chinese===
“谁个不跟我上床，”皮库斯说——“向来都是。”

===Original===
“Now that’s a new thing to sulk about,” said the vicar. “I am very useless. I cannot tell you about the cup. I cannot judge which of you does or should or could love Picus best. Or Clarence. Or you. His father will probably tell us as much truth as he finds convenient. But when I think of that sensitive, frustrated, pain-racked man who has given his life for you, Picus, alone on Tollerdown, in the fairy-house he made for you﻿—I judge no man. And I do not think it just for you, with your temperament to have the responsibility.”

===Chinese===
“这倒是个新鲜的可赌气的事由。”牧师说。“我这个人非常没用。关于杯子，我告诉不了你们。你们当中谁爱皮库斯爱得最深、该爱得最深、能爱得最深，我判断不了。克拉伦斯也好，你也好。他的父亲多半会告诉我们实话，多少全看他方便。可一想到那个敏感的、处处受挫的、被痛楚咬噬的人，他把一生都搭给了你，皮库斯，独自守在托勒当，守着他为你造的那座仙宅——我就谁也不评判了。我也不认为，以你的性子，由你来担这份责任是公道的。”

===Original===
“Nor,” said Picus, passionately, “do I.”

===Chinese===
“我也不认为。”皮库斯激愤地说。

===Original===
“Nor,” said the second old man, “why Scylla should be your leader and your neglected toy. Nor why you who all wish Felix well should have become his poison. In this business there are no easy answers, and we are left with our honour to lighten us.”

===Chinese===
“也看不出，”第二位老人说，“为什么斯库拉就该做你们的头领、做你们受冷落的玩物。看不出为什么你们这些个个盼菲利克斯好的人，反倒成了他的毒。这桩事里没有轻松的答案，我们只剩下各自的体面，来给自己照明。”

===Original===
Picus said: “Lighten what?”

===Chinese===
皮库斯说：“照亮什么？”

===Original===
“This story as I see it,” said the second old man, “is true Sanc-Grail. The cup may have been an ashtray in a Cairo club. But it seems to me that you are having something like a ritual. A find, illumination, doubt, and division, collective and then dispersed. A land enchanted and disenchanted with the rapidity of a cinema. Adventures. *Danger and awe and love.* What has Felix found in Paris that brings him home so quick? Our virtues we keep to serve these emergencies. Our virtue to induce them.”

===Chinese===
“依我看，这个故事是真正的圣杯故事。那只杯子，兴许只是开罗哪家俱乐部的一只烟灰缸。可在我看来，你们正在经历一场近乎仪式的东西。一次发现，一线启明，随之是怀疑与分裂，先是同聚，继而离散。一片土地着了魔又破了魔，快得像银幕换景。冒险。*危险、敬畏与爱。*菲利克斯在巴黎找到了什么，叫他这么快回家？我们的德行留着，正为应付这类变故；我们的德行，也正为招来这类变故。”

===Original===
“M’yes,” said Picus﻿—“but there ought to be sharper detail. It was Clarence’s spear that started me.”

===Chinese===
“唔，是啊——可总该有更分明的细节。当初挑起我来的，是克拉伦斯那支矛。”

===Original===
Carston said: “It is true. It has happened like that.” He was in a state of consciousness unique to him. Not vision, but wonder become a state, an impregnation of being: that excited and held him in absolute rest. An expectancy more real than the old furniture, the two men with him, the shallow stream that tore past the window, water whistling to itself, a running trap for light.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿说：“是真的。事情就是这样发生的。”他进入了一种对他而言独一无二的意识状态。不是幻象，而是惊奇成了一种常态、对存在的一次浸染：它激发着他，又把他定在绝对的静息之中。一种期待，比屋里那些旧家具、比同他在一起的两个人、比窗外夺路而过的浅溪更真实——溪水自顾自呼啸，是一道流动的、捕光的陷阱。

===Original===
More than an approach to wonder. Wonder was the answer, and familiar objects out of their categories. He also saw Picus without prejudice, and loved him.

===Chinese===
这不止是逼近惊奇。惊奇本身就是答案，是熟悉的事物各各走出了自己的门类。他还毫无成见地看清了皮库斯，并且爱他。

===Original===
A flock of telegrams was brought in. Carston opened his, brought in from the Star.

===Chinese===
一批电报递了进来。卡斯顿拆开他那封，是从星宿旅馆转来的。

===Original===
Going to Tollerdown then home come along Felix arriving sick Russian live with us.

===Chinese===
去托勒当随后回家来吧菲利克斯将带生病的俄国人来家住。

===Original===
“Whoops, my dear,” said Picus.

===Chinese===
“哎哟，亲爱的。”皮库斯说。

===Original===
The vicar opened two:

===Chinese===
牧师拆开两封：

===Original===
Coming south take care of us Scylla.

===Chinese===
即南下请照应我们斯库拉。

===Original===
Is Picus with you? Clarence.

===Chinese===
皮库斯在你处吗克拉伦斯。

===Original===
“The grail knights are gathering, it seems. This only I see clearly. Either this is a curiously coincidental hash, or we are taking part in events, only part of which are happening on the earth we see. Meanwhile, I approve the spacious dustbin into which you throw most things, and have seen everything thrown.”

===Chinese===
“看来，圣杯骑士们正在聚齐。我只看清这一件事：要么这是一锅蹊跷的巧合乱炖，要么我们正亲身参与一些事件，而这些事件只有一部分，发生在我们肉眼看得见的尘世上。至于其余，我赞许你们那只宽敞的垃圾箱，你们把大半事情都往里扔；我也见过样样都被扔进去。”

===Original===
“Then you believe there is a moral search?” said Carston, ignoring what paralleled with his wonder.

===Chinese===
“那么您相信，这是一场道德上的求索？”卡斯顿说，对那与自己惊奇之感并行的话充耳不闻。

===Original===
“I do. Even unprofessionally. As valid and as open to revision as research in the electromagnetic field. Practically I advise you to stick to your tastes as gentlemen and your love of art. You’re so damnably proud and fastidious you’ll do that anyhow.”

===Chinese===
“我相信。哪怕以行外的身份。它同电磁场里的研究一样站得住，也一样容得修订。落到实处，我劝你们守住身为绅士的趣味和对艺术的爱好。你们骄傲得要命、挑剔得要命，横竖也会守住的。”

===Original===
“Felix,” said Carston, eloquently, “I really couldn’t do justice to the way that boy behaved. The way he treated his sister; has and will again.”

===Chinese===
“菲利克斯，”卡斯顿说，说得言辞滔滔，“那孩子的做派，我实在没法秉公形容。他对待他姐姐的那一套；从前干过，往后还会再干。”

===Original===
“He seems to be arriving from Paris on an orgy of tending the sick.”

===Chinese===
“他这趟从巴黎回来，看来要办一场看护病人的狂欢。”

===Original===
“Feeding the hungry,” said Picus. “I know Russians. I wonder what we’re in for?”

===Chinese===
“还有喂饱饿汉。”皮库斯说。“我了解俄国人。咱们这回要领教什么？”

===Original===
The second old man said:

===Chinese===
第二位老人说：

===Original===
“I’ll take him off your hands if he is any good. The young are getting worth watching again.”

===Chinese===
“他若真有些可取，我可以把你们手里这差事接过来。年轻人又渐渐值得一看的了。”

===Original===
Carston said:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿说：

===Original===
“I wish the cup could be disposed of.”

===Chinese===
“我但愿这杯子能有个处置。”

===Original===
“I’ll go over to the Star,” said Picus, “and wait for my father’s idea of convincing you. I’ve a lech on the boots.”

===Chinese===
“我这就去星宿旅馆，”皮库斯说，“等我父亲大人拿出叫你们信服的高见来。我惦记那儿的擦靴小厮着呢。”

===Original===
When they were alone, Carston said with an effort:

===Chinese===
剩下他们独处时，卡斯顿费了点劲，开口道：

===Original===
“My intentions are very sincere towards Scylla Taverner.”

===Chinese===
“我对斯库拉·塔弗纳的心意，是非常诚挚的。”

===Original===
“I think they are. So are his. I’ll marry her to either of you with a psalm of joy if it works out that way. But you do realise that your relation with her will not be the same as hers with Picus? Young men think sex is all the same, or at best a sacred or profane love, when it’s as varied as art.”

===Chinese===
“我看是诚挚的。他的也诚挚。事情若走到那一步，我乐意唱着欢欣的诗篇，把她许给你们两位中随便哪一位。可你分明知道，你与她的关系，不会跟她与皮库斯的关系一样吧？年轻人总以为性事千篇一律，至多分个神圣与亵渎的爱，其实它跟艺术一样，千变万化。”

===Original===
They chatted. Picus brought back a letter with a black seal.

===Chinese===
两人闲谈了一阵。皮库斯带回一封黑火漆封口的信。

===Original===
> Cup found in the vestry in the church of St. Hilary-under-Llyn sometime in July 1881. Given to me by the rector, the rev. John Norris, as it could not be identified as church property. Believed by me, on the authority of *(a string of names followed)*, to be a cup of the rare but occasionally found chalices of the Celtic church.

===Chinese===
> 此杯于1881年7月间的某日，发现于勒林畔圣希拉里教堂（St. Hilary-under-Llyn）的法衣室。因无法认定属教会财产，由教区长约翰·诺里斯牧师（the rev. John Norris）让与本人。本人凭*（以下列有一串名字）*之权威，认定此杯属于凯尔特教会圣杯一类——此式罕见，而偶有发现。

===Original===
“The Llyn is on the Welsh marches,” said the vicar, “and the man’s dead.”

===Chinese===
“勒林在威尔士边地，”牧师说，“那人也已经死了。”

===Original===
Carston said:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿说：

===Original===
“Then we get nowhere.”

===Chinese===
“那咱们哪儿也到不了。”

===Original===
“Nowhere. Only in ghost-stories, and those not the best, do you get anywhere that way.”

===Chinese===
“哪儿也到不了。只有鬼故事里——还不是上乘的鬼故事——这么走才走得通。”

===Original===
“But what are we going to do with the damned thing? It can’t lie about the house like a green eye that doesn’t wink. The man’s dead. Suppose the authorities stick by Mr. Tracy. Or don’t? This has been a fool’s errand﻿—”

===Chinese===
“可这该死的东西，咱们拿它怎么办？总不能让它躺在宅子里，像一只不眨的绿眼睛。那人已经死了。万一官家一口咬定特雷西先生的鉴定。又或不咬定呢？这趟差事，本来就是白跑——”

===Original===
“I have an idea,” said the vicar. “Take it back to Tollerdown and replace it where you found it. If the next drought sends it up in a suspicious manner, well and good. It seems to like wells. And truth, if she prefers not to talk, can return to one.”

===Chinese===
“我有个主意，”牧师说。“把它带回托勒当，放回你们当初找到它的地方。下一场大旱若叫它蹊跷地浮上来，那也很好。它像是喜欢井。而真理，她若宁肯不说话，也可以退回一口井里去。”

===Original===
Carston said: “I like that.”

===Chinese===
卡斯顿说：“这主意我喜欢。”

===Original===
“Good,” said Picus, “learn it to be a toad.” Both prayed he would add﻿—“I’ll be off with it and look up Clarence.”

===Chinese===
“好，”皮库斯说，“把它调教成一只癞蛤蟆。”两人都暗暗盼着他补上一句——“我这就带了它动身，去看看克拉伦斯。”

===Original===
Not at all.

===Chinese===
他根本没有。

===Original===
“I’m not ready yet. Someone had better take it and fetch him. And Scylla. He gets ideas in his head when he’s alone there. Carston, you started travelling about with the thing. Go and drop it and bring them back. There’s a train tomorrow that starts at six.”

===Chinese===
“我还没预备好动身呢。最好有谁带着它去接他回来。还有斯库拉。他一个人在那儿，脑子里会生出种种念头。卡斯顿，当初带着这东西四处跑的就是你。你去，把它安放下去，把他们俩带回来。明天有一班六点开的车。”

## IV / 四、杯与矛

===Original===
The morning restored Carston to kinder thoughts. Last night might have been spent under the sea. If they had drawn him down, it was possible that they had not done it intentionally. His room was comfortable, if mad, full of little bits out of the sea. A ship in a bottle pleased him. On a hook was an old cap with an anchor. A ship was painted inside his morning cup of tea.

===Chinese===
早晨把卡斯顿（Carston）还给了温和些的念头。昨晚那觉，简直像是在海底底下睡的。倘若他们把他拽了下去，那也未必是有意为之。他的房间是舒适的，只是疯疯癫癫，满屋子都是海里来的小零碎。瓶子里的一艘船叫他喜欢。挂钩上挂着一顶缀着铁锚的旧帽子。他早晨喝茶那只杯的内壁上，画着一艘船。

===Original===
After breakfast he had the sea full; bathing with Felix who treated the sea like a living animal. Carston was content to show how well he swam. Very content, that he swam so well, better than the boy. Looking from the rocks inland, he thought it might really be quite all right if there was not too much scenery that called for a too high quality of attention. At least he could not go back next day. Pride forbade it. He must stay until he had some power over them. That would be his compensation for a week’s boredom and acquit him.

===Chinese===
早饭过后，他把海受用了个足；同菲利克斯（Felix）一道洗了海水浴——菲利克斯待海，像待一头活兽。卡斯顿只管亮一手自己游得多么好。非常满意——他游得这么好，比那少年还好。从礁石上望向内陆，他想，只要没有太多那种要求太高档次专注的风景，这地方说不定还真不赖。至少，第二天他是回不去的。自尊心不许可。他必须待到对他们握有几分权柄。那将是他一星期无聊的补偿，也将证明他不虚此行。

===Original===
Scylla he was reconsidering. To make love to her would be too hard work, too easy. The sort of woman who forgot all about you next day.

===Chinese===
至于斯库拉（Scylla），他正在重新掂量。向她求爱，会是桩太费力的苦差，又太容易到手。她是那种第二天就把你忘个干净的女人。

===Original===
To have power at a moment’s notice, it is as well to begin by knowing a secret. He remembered the cup still on the table at breakfast, and used by Ross as an ashtray. Then, that Felix had not used it.

===Chinese===
想要一有需要便拿得出权柄，最稳妥是先从掌握一桩秘密入手。他想起早饭时那只杯还在桌上，被罗斯（Ross）拿作烟灰缸使。又想起，菲利克斯没有用它。

===Original===
“You know,” he said to the boy, “that I was very interested in what you were saying last night. But I didn’t quite catch on.”

===Chinese===
“你是知道的，”他对那少年说，“昨晚你们讲的那些，我很感兴趣。只是我没大听明白。”

===Original===
Felix was thinking that here was something nice and new, who did the things they did, a little differently. With the fundamental error that being an American, simplicity and kindness would be his chief characteristic. He surprised Carston with his quickness to explain.﻿—“We got over there, and found Picus saying he was ill and Clarence doing all the work. As usual. So, after tea with soda water, I went down in the bucket. The others hung on to the windlass and Picus strolled out. Got a fishing spear with him, because he said high hedgehogs aren’t things to handle (they smell water and fall in, poor brutes). I raised that cup along with the corpses. We were looking at it, and Picus began to whistle. You must hear him whistle; it’s like Mozart. Said he was perfectly well again. He and Ross are mighty queer birds.”

===Chinese===
菲利克斯心里想的却是：来了个新鲜可爱的人物，干着他们干的事，只是路数略有不同。还带着一个根本性的错误认识：以为他既是美国人，头脑简单、心肠和善就该是他的头号品性。他解释得那样爽快，倒叫卡斯顿吃了一惊。——“我们到了那边，发现皮库斯（Picus）说自己病了，克拉伦斯（Clarence）一个人包揽了全部活计。跟平常一样。于是，喝过掺苏打水的茶，我就坐着吊桶下了井。其他人抓着绞盘，皮库斯溜达出去了。随身带了杆鱼矛，因为他说高处的刺猬不是能上手摆弄的东西（它们闻见水就掉进去，可怜的畜生）。我把那只杯连同那些尸体一并捞了上来。我们正端详着，皮库斯吹起口哨来了。你一定得听听他吹口哨；那像莫扎特（Mozart）。他说自己已经全好了。他和罗斯是两只了不得的怪鸟。”

===Original===
“Tell me more about your friends.”

===Chinese===
“再给我讲讲你这些朋友。”

===Original===
“Picus is Clarence’s ‘old man of the sea’ only he’s young. Clarence doesn’t know it. Scylla says I’m hers. He only does one or two small things like whistling, but he does them perfectly. Riding and blowing birds’ eggs. You saw how powerful his body is, but he’s like a bird. Off in a flash. Hence the name. Picus was the Woodpecker.

===Chinese===
“皮库斯是克拉伦斯的‘海上老人’，只不过他是年轻的。克拉伦斯自己不知道。斯库拉说我是她的。他只做一两样小事，比如吹口哨，可他做得十全十美。骑马，还有吹鸟蛋。你也看见他身子多有力了，可他像只鸟。一闪就没影。名字就是这么来的。皮库斯本是啄木鸟。

===Original===
“Clarence fights for him and with him. What he fights for, I don’t know. Clarence is quite all right. A bit insincere, because he’s afraid. And what he’s afraid of, I don’t know.”

===Chinese===
“克拉伦斯为他打架，也跟他打架。他为什么而打，我不知道。克拉伦斯人满不错。有点不真诚，因为他害怕。至于怕的是什么，我也不知道。”

===Original===
Carston could only say: “Tell me more.”

===Chinese===
卡斯顿只得说：“再讲。”

===Original===
“Scylla’s a different egg. If there is anything wrong about my sister, it’s everything. I’ve said the word ‘fear’ at least ten times lately. This time it’s my own.” He horrified Carston﻿—he was like a desperate butterfly, angry, petulant and white.﻿—“It’s she at one end, and Picus at the other, who get me going. It’s because she wants everything to happen to its last possibility. That’s how she gets kick out of life. Once a thing’s got going, she’ll understand it and manage it. And enjoy it. She’ll never tone it down. Sort of woman who’d have mothered the house of Atreus, and though I owe her everything, it’s wasted on me. She’ll enjoy﻿—”

===Chinese===
“斯库拉是另一路下的蛋。要说我姐姐有什么毛病，那就是样样都是毛病。‘害怕’这个词，我最近起码说了十遍。这一回，说的是我自己。”他叫卡斯顿骇然——他像一只绝望的蝴蝶，气恼、暴躁、煞白。——“一头是她，一头是皮库斯，把我撩拨得团团转。因为她要让每件事都发生到它最后的可能。她就是这么从生活里榨取刺激的。一件事一旦转起来，她就会懂得它、掌住它。还要享受它。她绝不会把它调得温吞。她是那种女人——搁在阿特柔斯（Atreus）一家，都能当一家之母的那种。虽说我的样样都欠她，可这些心思砸在我身上是白砸。她会享受——”

===Original===
“What will she enjoy?”

===Chinese===
“她会享受什么？”

===Original===
“What will happen out of what happened yesterday. Don’t you see. That infernal Picus is a psychic if there ever was one. Or if there is such a thing.”

===Chinese===
“享受昨天那件事里长出来的事。你还不明白。那个邪门的皮库斯，天生就是个通灵的胚子——要是世上真有通灵的人的话。要不，得先看世上有没有通灵这回事。”

===Original===
“Does she believe in that?”

===Chinese===
“她信这一套吗？”

===Original===
“Believing doesn’t trouble her. Only what is going to happen. She doesn’t create situations. She broods them and they hatch. And the birds come home to roost. Some mighty queer birds. Truth isn’t everyone’s breakfast egg. She isn’t happy till it’s hatched. Calls it knowing where you are. I wish I knew where I was﻿—”

===Chinese===
“信不信的，不劳她操心。她只操心将要发生的事。她不制造局面。她伏在上面孵，局面自己破壳。然后鸟儿一只只归巢栖宿。好一群了不得的怪鸟。真理可不是人人早饭那一颗蛋。不孵出壳来她不痛快。她管这个叫‘知道自己身在何处’。我倒巴望知道自己身在何处——”

===Original===
Carston revised his ideas again about Scylla as a lover. He could only say: “But what can she and your friend Picus make out of what happened yesterday, anyhow?”

===Chinese===
卡斯顿把斯库拉做情人这件事的想法又修订了一遍。他只得说：“可她和你那位朋友皮库斯，到底能从昨天的事里做出什么来呢？”

===Original===
“Don’t you see? It was fishing it out of the well with that old spear﻿—they always went together.”

===Chinese===
“你还不明白？要紧的是用那杆旧矛把它从井里捞了出来——它们向来是结伴同行的。”

===Original===
“What went with what?”

===Chinese===
“什么跟什么结伴？”

===Original===
“The cup of the Sanc-Grail, of course. It and the spear, they always hunted in couples. You’ve heard of it. All sexual symbolism. I wish I hadn’t.”

===Chinese===
“当然是圣所圣杯（Sanc-Grail）的那只杯。它和那杆矛，向来是成双狩猎的。这个你听说过。全是性的象征。可惜我听说了。”

===Original===
“Does sexual symbolism get you?” It would be news if it did.

===Chinese===
“性的象征那一套，拿得住你吗？”要是真拿得住，那可就是新闻了。

===Original===
“I should worry. But the Sanc-Grail was a very funny thing. People used to think it was a shallow greenish dish. And the cup’s a shallow, greenish dish. Those well-shafts on the downs might be any age. So might it. Tollerdown had a bad reputation, and I never heard of the Sanc-Grail doing anyone any good. With that moron Picus behind it, and that demon, my sister, in front of it.”

===Chinese===
“我犯不上操心。不过圣所圣杯本来就是件很逗的东西。人们从前以为它是只浅浅的泛绿盘子。而这只杯，恰是只浅浅的泛绿盘子。丘原上那些井筒，什么年代的都可能。它也一样。托勒当（Tollerdown）名声不佳，我也从没听说圣所圣杯给谁行过什么好。它后面站着那个低能儿皮库斯，前头站着那个魔鬼——我姐姐。”

===Original===
Carston took stock of several things: what he remembered of the Grail story, the possibility of anyone behaving as if it had happened, and what that implied in human character. Felix’s youth.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿盘点了几样东西：他记得的圣杯故事；有人当真拿它当发生过的事来行事的可能；还有这在人性上意味着什么。还有菲利克斯的年轻。

===Original===
He said at last:

===Chinese===
他终于说：

===Original===
“Don’t tell me your sister is superstitious.”

===Chinese===
“别告诉我，你姐姐是迷信的。”

===Original===
“Not she. Better if she was. She’d read it up and do processions and things. It might be like that. But with her it won’t get its home comforts. It will get vision.”

===Chinese===
“她才不。她要是迷信倒好了。她会去翻书查考，操办列队游行那一套。事情也许是那个样子。可到了她手里，它得不到家常的安逸。它得到的是异象。”

===Original===
On the last four words he changed, and Carston saw the sister in the brother, in the elegant, frightened boy now explaining that what he wanted was not vision, but fashionable routine.

===Chinese===
说到最后四个字，他变了脸色，卡斯顿在弟弟身上看见了姐姐——这个优雅的、受了惊的少年，此刻正解释说，他要的不是异象，而是时髦的例行公事。

===Original===
“Of course, there is nothing in it. I only meant that the find’s a reminder.”

===Chinese===
“当然，这里头什么也没有。我只是说，这桩发现是个提醒。”

===Original===
“Reminder of what?”

===Chinese===
“提醒什么？”

===Original===
“Of what it would remind you, of course. Oh! I see you don’t know. Never mind, it’s a long story.”

===Chinese===
“当然是提醒它碰到你也会提醒的那个东西。哦！我看你不知道。不碍事，说来话长。”

===Original===
Carston gave out. He was not pleased. He had been atrociously taken into confidence, and he had not understood. His earlier dislike of them returned, with an uncomfortable respect. The Sanc-Grail did not call on everybody. The boy was a young thing, telling him how much he hated poverty and dreams. What a snob he was. All about the things you could not do. Felix remembered that Ross had prepared them to be misunderstood. It did not occur to him that he had no right to expect that Carston should understand.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿泄了气。他并不痛快。人家骇人听闻地向他吐了心腹之言，而他竟没有听懂。先前对他们的厌恶又回来了，还夹着一种不自在的敬意。圣所圣杯不是随便登谁的门的。这少年是个年轻的东西，正告诉他，他多么恨贫穷、恨梦。好一个势利眼。张口闭口，全是那些干不成的事。菲利克斯想起罗斯早就给他们打过招呼，说他们这伙人注定要被人误解。他却没有想到，自己并无权利指望卡斯顿非懂不可。

## IX / 九、荒原迷途

===Original===
At a choice of tracks apparently parallel and similar, Picus had led them to the left. He had trooped them through a wood and sweated them over a crest, to drop them again in a sunk road, made for running contraband a century before. He was asking: “Where are the others?”

===Chinese===
几条小径看上去平行而相似，一时无从挑拣，皮库斯（Picus）却领着大家往左去了。他领着这队人穿过一片树林，又让他们汗涔涔地翻过一道山脊，再把他们重新丢进一条洼路——一条百年前走私客跑出来的路。他问：“其他人呢？”

===Original===
“I want my mead,” said Felix. “The farm’s ahead on the water’s edge, where the heath runs out in a point.”

===Chinese===
“我要喝我的蜂蜜酒（mead），”菲利克斯（Felix）说。“农庄就在前头水边上，荒原伸出个尖角的地方。”

===Original===
Sunk roads are filled with loose sand. They go from nowhere to nowhere now. They trap the sun and keep out air. Their banks curl over in a fringe of heather wire. Adders bask on their striped sides. From this one Starn was invisible, and the eventual blue water bright as dew.

===Chinese===
洼路里积满浮沙。如今它们从无处通向无处。它们把太阳困在里面，把风挡在外面。路坡的边沿蜷起一圈石楠铁丝似的流苏。蝰蛇在条纹斑驳的坡壁上晒太阳。从这一条里望不见斯塔恩（Starn），也望不见尽头那片蓝色水面，亮得如同露水。

===Original===
Carston plodded beside Clarence, his feet chafing with sand. They left the wood and crossed another ridge, and saw on their right a creek of yellow grass running inland behind them. They walked another mile till they came to the base of the point, and saw cottages under a crest of dark land. Felix cried out: “We were wrong. Look over there!” They looked across the creek, now filled with water, and only bordered with its grass. Two miles on the farther side, on a patch of green land reclaimed from the heath, was a noble group of trees round a white farm.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿（Carston）挨着克拉伦斯（Clarence）一步步苦走，双脚叫沙子磨得生疼。他们出了树林，又翻过一道山脊，看见右边一条生满黄草的水湾，在他们身后折向内陆。又走了一英里，到了那尖角的根上，望见一道黑土地的坡顶下有几座村舍。菲利克斯喊了起来：“咱们走错了。看那边！”他们朝水湾对岸望去——湾里这会儿已涨满了水，只剩两岸镶着那条黄草边。对岸两英里开外，一片从荒原里垦出来的绿地上，一簇气派的树木环着一座白色的农庄。

===Original===
“We should have gone there,” said Felix﻿—“this comes of Picus’s swank about knowing the heath. This leads only to a place we called Misery, because they starved their dogs.”

===Chinese===
“咱们本该往那边去，”菲利克斯说——“这都是皮库斯吹嘘自己认得荒原的下场。这条路只通到一个我们管它叫‘苦命庄’的地方，因为那儿的人把自家的狗都饿得半死。”

===Original===
“I saw no tracks in the sand,” said Carston.

===Chinese===
“我在沙地上可没看见什么岔道，”卡斯顿说。

===Original===
“I supposed you knew the way.” He heard them screaming like gulls over dead fish and would have preferred the birds’ company. Abusing each other for what they would forgive in two minutes. Consider rather a joke. Forget. They none of them saw that Picus had done it on purpose. Then Carston admitted that he might not have thought of it himself if the night before Picus had not slept with the woman he wanted. Why had he done it? He had separated himself from the woman, to mislead them.

===Chinese===
“我还以为你认得路呢。”他听着他们吵，像一群围着死鱼尖叫的海鸥，倒宁愿有那群鸟做伴。为着两分钟后就会彼此原谅的事，互相臭骂。不如当它个笑话。忘了它。他们谁都没看出来，皮库斯是故意干的。接着卡斯顿承认，倘若头天夜里皮库斯没有跟他想要的那个女人睡，他自己未必想得到这一层。他为什么要这么干？他把自己从那女人身边分开，为的是误导他们。

===Original===
They were lurching back through the sand the way they had come.
