“Now you see why that cup upset Felix? If it is anything, it is only a Celtic mass-cup. And that, perhaps, is not certain. I don’t think you do see. As Felix would say: ‘I don’t blame you.’ But an American poet said: ‘*Memory, you have the key.*’ ”

===Chinese===
“这下你明白那只杯为什么搅得菲利克斯不安了吧？它就算是什么，也不过是一只凯尔特（Celtic）弥撒杯。而这个，兴许也未必作准。我看你其实还不明白。照菲利克斯的说法：‘我不怪你。’不过有位美国诗人说过：‘*Memory, you have the key.*（记忆，你握着钥匙。）’”

===Original===
“I have no memories,” said Carston.

===Chinese===
“我没有记忆。”卡斯顿说。

===Original===
“We are all wishing we hadn’t; because memory produces imagination, and imagination is a state by itself. Memory was the Muses’ mother, and the muses are nine names of the imagination. I told you you’d see some fun. Now I must go over to the coastguards and order a car. We want to take you somewhere tomorrow. See the thunder clouds banking up? I must get back before the rain.”

===Chinese===
“我们都巴不得自己没有呢；因为记忆生出想象，而想象自成一界。记忆是缪斯（Muses）的母亲，缪斯又是想象的九个名字。我说过你会看到些乐子的。现在我得去海岸警卫队那边订一辆车。我们明天要带你到一个地方去。看见雷雨云正一堆堆垒起来吗？我得赶在雨前回去。”

===Original===
Carston thought: Getting rid of me. In an instant she would be off like a hare. He said:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿心想：这是要甩开我。转眼她就会像只野兔似的窜没影。他说：

===Original===
“Stay a minute. Maybe it’s because I have no memories, but I don’t see where the fun comes in.”

===Chinese===
“再待一会儿。也许正因为我没有记忆，我看不出乐子打哪儿来。”

===Original===
“Don’t you call it fun to watch how violently, strangely and in character people will behave? Watch Ross, watch Clarence. Watch me.” He was watching her. Green, pointed feet in plaited shoes, bare arms, pointed breasts under a dress full of air. *Blow away the morning dew.* That was remembering something. Like open fir-cones dipped in fire and cream, the thunderclouds were piling up the sky. Mounting the hills, a wing of them rising out of the sea. Inshore, a breath of wind clashed the pine needles.

===Chinese===
“看人们行事会那样暴烈、那样古怪、又那样各随其性——你不把这个叫乐子吗？看罗斯，看克拉伦斯。看我。”他正看着她。绿色的、尖尖的双足，穿一双编带的鞋，胳膊裸着，一条鼓满空气的裙子底下，是尖尖的胸。*吹散晨露吧。*这是记起了什么。像一颗颗张开的冷杉球果蘸了火与乳脂，雷雨云正往天上堆积。涌上山岗，其中一翼从海里升起。近岸处，一丝风把松针刮得铮铮作响。

===Original===
Another memory.

===Chinese===
又一段记忆。

===Original===
*Love only me.*

===Chinese===
*只爱我一人。*

===Original===
*Donna Lombarda.*

===Chinese===
*《伦巴第贵妇》（Donna Lombarda）。*

===Original===
*Love only me.*

===Chinese===
*只爱我一人。*

===Original===
*Love only me.* Because the tune was what the needles brushed out, and the words the wish that made his body ache.

===Chinese===
*只爱我一人。*因为那调子正是松针刮出来的，而那词句，正是那个让他浑身发疼的愿望。

===Original===
“Can I come with you?”

===Chinese===
“我能跟你一块儿去吗？”

===Original===
She looked at him candidly. She wanted to be alone. “I must go to the farm as well; I do the housekeeping at this time. It’s a hot flat walk. The others have gone down to the rocks to fetch a driftwood log. If you liked to help them to get it up. It’s bleached white, and when it burns it will go up in blue and green sparks.” He saw that there was something pathetic in the way they made a game of their poverty.

===Chinese===
她坦率地望了望他。她想要独自待着。“我还得去农场走一趟；这个时节的家务都归我管。那是一段又晒又平的路。其余的人下礁石那边拖一根浮木去了。你要是愿意，就去帮他们把它弄上来。那木头漂得雪白，烧起来会蹿出蓝的绿的火星。”他看出来了，他们把自己的穷困当游戏来打趣，那里面有点叫人心酸的东西。

===Original===
“I’ll go to them,” he said, “but you haven’t explained what the American poet meant when he said that memory had the key?” She had moved away from him.

===Chinese===
“我去找他们，”他说，“可你还没解释，那位美国诗人说记忆握着钥匙，是什么意思？”她已经从他身边走开了。

===Original===
“He said:

> Mount.﻿ ﻿…
 

 Put your shoes at the door,
 

 Sleep, prepare for life.

And called it: *The last twist of the knife.* Adieu.”

===Chinese===
“他说：

> 攀登吧。……
 

 把鞋放在门口，
 

 睡吧，为人生作好准备。

还给它起了个名字：*刀子的最后一拧。*再会。”

---

===Original===
The log, as he expected, was large and most unwilling to be moved; the cliff-path more a gesture in broken clay than an ascent.

===Chinese===
那根浮木正如他所料，又大又死活不肯挪窝；崖上的小径与其说是道登坡，不如说是在碎泥里比划出的一个手势。

===Original===
He saw her in his mind, dew blowing away over burnt, empty grass towards a formidable other world, its edges drawn in fire, the thunderclouds now halfway across the sky.

===Chinese===
他在心里看见她，晨露在烧焦的、空荡荡的草地上被吹散，向着另一个可畏的世界而去，那世界的边缘以火勾出，雷雨云此刻已横过半边天。

===Original===
Before dinner, he remembered the library, the middle room of the house. Alone there, he looked for something, not Tennyson, to enlighten him. He found a book, and sat in a window with it. Presently he noticed the entrance, one after the other, of Clarence, Felix, and Ross, and that they all went, reticently but eventually, to the same gap in the shelves.

===Chinese===
晚饭前，他想起了书房——宅子居中的那间。独自待在那里，他找一本能给他开窍的书，不要丁尼生。他找到了一本，拿着在窗边坐下。不多时，他留意到克拉伦斯、菲利克斯和罗斯一个接一个走进来，而且一个个都缄默地、却终究走向了书架上同一道豁口。

## VII / 七、风暴夜曲

===Original===
They dined without Picus or Scylla. He saw Clarence, uneasy, and heard that Picus had gone out alone because the doctor had told him to take walks. The earth was now closed in a hot, purple air-ball, the lightning flicking on and off. Without any regard for the weather, Ross arranged their chairs in the verandah while the storm banged about. Carston was silent. He was not accustomed to invite the lightning to visit him under trees. And Clarence seemed fatuous to him when he turned on the gramophone to play against the sky. They were not disturbed about Scylla, who might be out walking alone in the livid night. Apparently the farmer had an old father who gave her beer and told her ghost stories. It was Clarence who swung up and down, turning disks, and saying teasings that were brittle and raw, in his rich, sad voice, tortured and made petulant by the uproar through which his friend’s feet could not be heard coming through the wood.

===Chinese===
晚饭席上没有皮库斯（Picus），也没有斯库拉（Scylla）。他见到了心神不宁的克拉伦斯（Clarence），又听说皮库斯独自出了门，因为医生嘱咐他多散步。此刻大地被封在一只热烘烘的紫色气球里，电光一明一灭。罗斯（Ross）全不理会天气，只管在游廊里替大家摆椅子，任风暴在外头乒乒乓乓地闹。卡斯顿（Carston）沉默着。他可不习惯把闪电请到树底下来拜访自己。待到克拉伦斯打开留声机、放唱片去同天空对垒，他简直觉得此人愚蠢透顶。他们谁也不为斯库拉担心，她也许正独自走在那铁青色的夜里。据说那农夫有位老父亲，请她喝啤酒，讲鬼故事给她听。偏是克拉伦斯在游廊里来回晃荡，一张张换着唱片，嘴里丢出些又脆又生的打趣话——用他那副丰润而哀愁的嗓音——被那场听不出朋友脚步穿林而来的喧闹折磨着，弄得焦躁任性。

===Original===
Carston thought that it was like the place to leap up from its equivocal quiet into an orgy of cracking and banging. He wanted to go and meet Scylla. To see her safely home. Why were they so careless of their women? She had told him that love had left them. Had courtesy? She might come a hundred ways. It was the same as on the night when the men had been lost. They had come back safe from adventure. He wanted her to enter with him. He was an American gentleman in an uneasy place. Yes, he would go at once and fetch his young hostess. A proper thing to do. He felt at ease for the first time.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿心想，这地方正是这样的脾性：从它暧昧的安静里一跃而起，跳进一场噼里啪啦的狂欢。他想去迎斯库拉。送她平平安安进家门。他们对自己的女人怎么这样漫不经心？她对他说过，爱已经离开了他们。那么礼数呢？她可能从一百条路上回来。这同男人们走失的那晚一模一样。他们不是都从冒险里平平安安回来了么。他要她同他一道进门。他是一位身处不安之地的美国绅士。对，他这就去把年轻的女主人接回来。一件合乎体统的事。他头一回觉得自在了。

===Original===
Ross advised not: “She knows her way. You’d never find it. It will rain in a minute. You’ll see tomorrow morning the freshest earth there is.”

===Chinese===
罗斯劝他别去：“她认得自己的路。你休想找得着。雨马上就到。明儿早晨，你自会看见世上顶新鲜的土地。”

===Original===
> “Prepare for life,
 

 The last twist of the knife.”

===Chinese===
> “为人生做好准备，
 

 刀子的最后一拧。”

===Original===
For the freshest earth there is. *Phat* went a raindrop on a flag, and a double uproar began. For an hour it rained, through sheet lightning, and thunder like a departing train, the hills calling to one another. The gutters of the roof rushed and sang and leaked, single notes from which the ear eventually picked out a tune. Syncopation, magic, nature imitating Mozart? Carston came to hear it as an overture, for some private earth-life, mercifully and tiresomely apart from his.

===Chinese===
就为着世上顶新鲜的土地。*啪嗒*，一滴雨砸在石板上，两重喧闹一齐开了场。雨下了整整一个钟头，中间夹着片状闪电，雷声像一列驶远的火车，群山彼此呼喊应答。屋顶的檐槽奔流、歌唱、漏水，从那一颗颗单音里，耳朵终于拣出了一支曲子。切分音，魔法，大自然在模仿莫扎特（Mozart）？卡斯顿渐渐把它听成了一支序曲——献给某一场私下的大地生活，仁慈地、也叫人腻烦地，同他自己的那一场不相干。

===Original===
Things going on singing, not to him. Escaping also, not finishing, or finishing somewhere else. Beginning again, to enchant him with fragments. He admitted that he was enchanted﻿—When would Scylla wind up the charm by coming through the wood?

===Chinese===
万物只管歌唱，不是唱给他听的。也总在躲开他，不收尾，或是在别处收了尾。又重新起头，拿残篇断简蛊惑他。他承认自己着了魔——斯库拉几时才穿林而来，替这道魔咒收束？

===Original===
The storm tuned up again, the rain striking in rods, filling the air with fine spray. The others were enjoying it, the first row of the stalls for a nature-play. Discussing other spectacles. Then he saw Scylla and Picus run out of the wood and across the lawn, laughing, wet as dogs. He heard Clarence order Picus upstairs to change, to be ignored, while Scylla squeezed out her hair.

===Chinese===
风暴重新调准了弦，雨一杆一杆砸下来，把空气里填满细沫。其余的人正看得高兴，等于坐着池座第一排，看一出大自然戏，一边还议论着别的奇观。这时他看见斯库拉和皮库斯笑着跑出树林，跑过草坪，湿得像两条狗。他听见克拉伦斯喝令皮库斯上楼换衣裳，只落得没人理会，斯库拉正拧着头发里的水。

===Original===
“We ran back together. It was too good to miss. Tell me, Carston, does the lightning get you when you’re under a tree, or when you’re not? We tried both.”

===Chinese===
“我们是一路跑回来的。这场好戏岂能错过。告诉我，卡斯顿，闪电是躲在树底下挨得着呢，还是不躲挨得着？两种我们都试过了。”

===Original===
That was all there was to it. But how had they met? All prearranged he supposed. No, Picus had done it. Loped off another way to meet her at the farm. She was saying: “You’re lucky that you didn’t come with me. Admit you’d have hated it?” Was that flirting with him? He asked her what she meant. “Storms aren’t in your schedule.” So wet they both seemed naked. They all went in. He made hot grog for her over the wood fire, the acid smoke bringing water to his eyes. They looked like real tears to Ross, who wondered. Scylla came down in blue, her hair tied up in a gold cloth, unable to stop laughing. Clarence followed her. Ross said: “Got Picus to bed?”

===Chinese===
事情的全部就这么多。可他们是怎样碰上的呢？他猜一切都是预先安排。不对，是皮库斯干的。抄另一条路溜出去，到农庄上同她会合。她正说着：“你没跟我去真是运气。承认吧，你准会嫌死了？”这算是在同他调情么？他问她这话什么意思。“风暴可不在你的日程表上。”两个人湿得都像光着身子。大家进了屋。他在柴火上替她调热格罗格（grog）酒，酸烟熏得他两眼出水。那副样子落在罗斯眼里竟像真的眼泪，害他纳闷。斯库拉穿一身蓝下楼来，头发用一块金布裹起，笑个不住。克拉伦斯跟在她后面。罗斯说：“皮库斯打发上床了？”

===Original===
“No: insists on shaving, and to spite me stands about in his skin.” There was more behind what he said than self-pity, yet Carston felt that Clarence had better have hit them in his exasperation than have pitied himself. Why, he wondered? Scylla had said that goodwill had left the earth, but he had noticed that they were compassionate. Perhaps it was that they knew pity’s value and feared a sudden demand. At the same time he had no sympathy for Clarence, and they had, who were looking askance at him as though he had said a tactless obscenity. Scylla was saying:

===Chinese===
“没有：他非要刮脸不可，还存心气我，光着身子满屋子晃。”他这话里除了自怜还有别的意思，可卡斯顿仍觉得，克拉伦斯恼火起来，倒不如揍他们一顿，也别那样可怜自己。为什么呢，他纳闷？斯库拉说过，善意已经离开了这个世界，他却注意到他们个个心肠软。也许正因为他们知道怜悯的价钱，才怕有人突然来讨。与此同时，他对克拉伦斯没有一丝同情，他们倒有——他们正斜眼瞟着他，仿佛他说了一句不知轻重的脏话。斯库拉正说：

===Original===
“Warm inside and out. Carston, you’re sound on grog.”

===Chinese===
“里外都暖了。卡斯顿，你调格罗格酒的手艺靠得住。”

===Original===
Picus came down, flushed and transparent, and asked him for some. He found that he could not say “Help yourself,” forced to wait on him.

===Chinese===
皮库斯下楼来，红着脸，透着亮，也向他讨了一杯。他发现自己说不出“请自便”，只得动手伺候他。

===Original===
“Let’s dance,” said Scylla; and they danced together, the six of them, but Picus infinitely the best. One of the little things he could do, but not one with Scylla, who moved about with her brother, limbs of the same tree.

===Chinese===
“跳舞吧。”斯库拉说。于是他们一起跳，六个人，只是皮库斯跳得不知好出多少。那是他会的小小把戏之一，只是同斯库拉配不成一对——她只同她弟弟转来转去，同一棵树上的两条枝子。

===Original===
There were only five glasses when they all wanted drinks﻿—Picus came over with the cup for Clarence to fill with whiskey and soda. “I don’t mind using the ashtray,” he said; and Carston heard through the jazz and the slackening rain a voice which might have been a woman’s or a man’s: “He doesn’t mind using the cup of the Sanc-Grail for whiskey and soda,” and another voice, which might have been a man’s or a woman’s: “He doesn’t mind using for a whisky and soda, the cup we use for an ashtray, the cup of the Sanc-Grail.”

===Chinese===
等到人人都想喝一杯，杯子却只有五只——皮库斯拿了那只杯过来，让克拉伦斯给他兑威士忌苏打。“我不嫌它当过烟灰缸。”他说。于是卡斯顿隔着爵士乐和小下去的雨声，听见一个也许属于女人也许属于男人的声音：“他不嫌把圣所圣杯（Sanc-Grail）的那只杯拿来兑威士忌苏打。”又听见另一个也许属于男人也许属于女人的声音：“他不嫌拿咱们当烟灰缸使的那只杯来兑威士忌苏打，圣所圣杯的那只杯。”

===Original===
The last of the lightning winked at them, the rain turned to a sweet shower, an afterthought.

===Chinese===
最后一线闪电朝他们眨了眨眼，雨转成一阵甜软的阵雨，像个事后添上的念头。

===Original===
*What’ll I do, what’ll I do?* the gramophone was saying: *What’ll I do, what’ll I do?* Make love to Scylla, thought Carston. Hadn’t they ever thought of that? Show then that they had among them a living cup. He remembered the new records he had brought them from London, and went upstairs to fetch them. Outside Picus’s door he remembered. They were making a noise downstairs. He could look in. More Scylla. A whip-up for senses which were, perhaps, older than theirs. Not refreshed﻿—he thought of it with a sneer﻿—by memories and the past. They should create his memories for him.

===Chinese===
*我怎么办，我怎么办？*留声机念叨着：*我怎么办，我怎么办？*去向斯库拉求爱呀，卡斯顿想。他们就从来没一个想到过这个？那就让他们瞧瞧，他们中间有一只活着的杯。他记起从伦敦给他们捎来的新唱片，上楼去取。走到皮库斯门外他才想起来。楼下他们正闹得欢。他可以进去瞧一眼。更多的斯库拉。给感官来一记策鞭——那些感官，也许比他们的都古老。可不是靠记忆和过去滋补的——他想起这一点，不由冷笑——该由他们来替他制造记忆。

===Original===
He fetched the records, and, a little elated by drink, opened the door of Picus’s room. The draught from the window made his candle stream. He saved it and looked. There was no statuette. Even the broken pieces had been cleared away. His light under control, he looked round. Clothes in exquisite order, chaste, ivory dressing things in rows. Scent bottles with a silver strainer, a hollowed bunch of grapes. Nothing to read. Like Ross there. The other went about weighted with books. Something to read. Somebody’s book on early church vessels. So Picus had a rationalist mind? Not much read. Time to go.

===Chinese===
他取了唱片，带着几分酒意，推开皮库斯房间的门。窗口的穿堂风把他的烛火吹淌成一条。他救住火苗，四下里看。蜡像不在了。连碎片也已收拾干净。他把光压低，环顾四周。衣裳放得精致齐整，一排排素净的象牙色梳妆用具。香水瓶配一只银滤网，做成一串镂空葡萄的样子。没有可读的书。跟罗斯那儿一个样。倒是另一位，走来走去身上都坠着书。总得有点什么读读。某人那部论早期教会圣器的书。这么说皮库斯有理性主义的头脑？读得不深。该走了。

===Original===
Below, he was greeted with cheers.

===Chinese===
楼下，一阵欢呼迎接他。

===Original===
Airs went to his head.

===Chinese===
一支支曲子直往他脑袋里灌。

===Original===
> Waiting for the moon to rise and show me the way
 

 To get you to say
 

 I love you.

===Chinese===
> 等着月亮升起，为我指路
 

 好教你说出
 

 我爱你。

===Original===
“Will there be a moon, soon?” he asked Ross﻿—“after the storm, I mean?”

===Chinese===
“快有月亮了么？”他问罗斯——“我是说，风暴过后？”

===Original===
“Sorry; she’s over. To do her tricks, I mean. Aldebaran’s very bright just now.”

===Chinese===
“抱歉；她完了。我是说，变完她的戏法了。毕宿五（Aldebaran）这会儿亮得很。”

===Original===
Damn the stars. *I know starlight.* And the penalty. Leave the stars to them. Carston turned a disk: “I think you’ll like this. Not come to London yet.”

===Chinese===
该死的星星。*星光我领教过。*还有它的代价。星星留给他们的份。卡斯顿翻过一张唱片：“我想你们会喜欢这张。伦敦还没传到呢。”

===Original===
They did. Incarnated him responsible for *O Lady be good*, as for everything else in America. Scylla, dancing with him, smiled as he sang *I’ve been so awfully misunderstood*, with candour, with friendship, with something spilt over from a reserve of joy. He derided the men because not one of them knew what she was, because an American would discover a treasure worth a hundred Sanc-Grails. There was Picus dancing about like a marsh-bird courting, with an old cup on his head. Up and down and sideways, and never a drop spilt. Tilted his head sideways and caught it as it fell, and it was empty all the time. “Now I call that cheating.” In a moment it was back again and full, and never a drop was spilt.

===Chinese===
他们果然喜欢。径直把他当作*《哦，女士，请待我好》*（*O Lady be good*）的化身兼担保人，一如美国的万事万物也都由他担保。斯库拉同他跳着舞，听他唱到*我一直被人误解得这么惨*，笑了——笑得坦白，笑得友好，还带着一点从欢愉的蓄水池里漫出来的东西。他打心里瞧不起那几个男人，因为没有一个知道她是什么，而一个美国人准会发现一件抵得上一百只圣所圣杯的宝藏。那边皮库斯正像一只求偶的沼泽鸟似的蹦跶，头顶一只旧杯。上上下下，左左右右，一滴不洒。脑袋一侧，杯子坠落时正好接住——而它其实一直是空的。“这我可要叫声作弊了。”转眼间杯子又回到头顶，还满着，照样一滴不洒。

===Original===
Then Carston showed them the Charleston, and tumbled with them up to bed, shaking hands at doors, easy at last, and full of goodwill.

===Chinese===
随后卡斯顿教他们跳查尔斯顿（Charleston）舞，又同他们一路闹上楼去睡，在各人门口一一握手，终于松快下来，满怀善意。

===Original===
The air that filled his room was moist and strong, preparation for the freshest earth there is. The elation went out of him and left content. The visit had given him wonder. That was good, because one got brittle and lonely travelling round, and quick to mistrust. How simple it had been to win on these lordly young men. Love their women. Their place was his now. And the wood. It stood like something punished under the rain. He blew out his candle, and lay down in bed.

===Chinese===
满屋子的空气又湿又有劲，正为世上顶新鲜的土地做着准备。那阵兴奋从他身上退了出去，留下的是满足。这一趟做客给了他惊奇。这很好，因为一个人四处游历，容易变脆，容易孤单，也容易动辄起疑。要赢下这些倨傲的年轻人，原来这么容易。去爱他们的女人就是了。他们的地方如今是他的了。还有那片树林。它立在雨里，像一件受了责罚的东西。他吹熄蜡烛，躺上了床。

===Original===
A minute later the tail of the lightning winked. The rain quickened. The door next his opened. The gutter outside began to run fast again. Through the finale of the storm, he heard a gull crying. Then, outside his door he heard a whistle like a glass flute. How loud, how long he could not judge, startled by it, teased by it. It was outside the door where Scylla slept. All he could do was repeat the words of the call, as it poured out, with grace notes and repeats:

===Chinese===
一分钟后，闪电的尾巴眨了一下。雨急起来。他隔壁那扇门开了。外头的檐槽又奔流起来。隔着风暴的终曲，他听见一声鸥叫。接着，他听见自己门外有一声口哨，像一支玻璃笛。多响，多长，他判断不出，只叫它惊着了，撩拨着了。那哨声就在斯库拉睡的那扇门外。他能做的，只有随着它倾泻而出的一串装饰音和反复，把曲中的词复述一遍：

===Original===
> Oh, sweet and lovely lady be good,
 

 Oh, lady, be good to me.
 

 I’ve been so awfully misunderstood,
 

 So, lady, be good to me.
 

 Oh, lady, please have pity,
 

 I’m all alone in this great city,
 

 I’m just a lonely babe in the wood,
 

 So, lady, be good to me.

===Chinese===
> 哦，甜美可爱的女士，待我好些，
 

 哦，女士，请待我好。
 

 我一直被人误解得这么惨，
 

 所以，女士，请待我好。
 

 哦，女士，行行好，
 

 我孤零零在这偌大的城里，
 

 我只是林中一个孤单的弃儿，
 

 所以，女士，请待我好。

===Original===
Scylla’s door opened, neither noisily nor stealthily. Carston was out of bed, his ear to a split panel. He heard her laugh, her stage notion of an American accent. “I should worry.” Her door shut. He felt like a weight on his body, the three feet of stone between them. On the other side of that they were lying together, in the quiet of the wood. After a time, he went to the window to listen. But only the casement farthest from him was open, and there was no light. Shocked, almost whimpering, he went back to bed, falling, thanks to the strong air, very quickly asleep. Outside, the night cleared. Over the wood Orion hung up his belt and sword. In the pommel, Aldebaran shook; the star some time before Ross had offered to his attention.

===Chinese===
斯库拉的门开了，不响，也不鬼祟。卡斯顿已经下了床，耳朵贴在门板一道裂缝上。他听见她笑，听见她那套舞台味儿的美国腔。“我犯不上操心。”她的门关上了。隔开他们的那三英尺石头，像一副重担压在他身上。在那石头的另一边，他们正相拥躺着，在树林的静默里。过了一阵，他走到窗边去听。可只有离他最远的那扇窗开着，又没有一点光。他受了惊，几乎呜咽起来，回床上去，多亏这有劲的空气，很快便睡着了。外头，夜放晴了。树林上空，猎户座（Orion）挂起了他的腰带和佩剑。剑柄上，毕宿五颤动着——正是先前罗斯提请他留意的那颗星。

## VIII / 八、石楠私语

===Original===
The morning was a merciful bustle, with Ross’s promise come true of the freshest earth there is. The car arrived, and there seemed to be a controversy where they were to go. To pay a call or see some antiquity. Felix put his foot down on the antiquities.

===Chinese===
早晨是一场仁慈的忙乱，罗斯（Ross）的许诺应了验：世上顶新鲜的土地。汽车到了，去哪儿却似乎起了一场争执。是去拜客呢，还是去看什么古迹。菲利克斯（Felix）对古迹斩钉截铁地投了反对票。

===Original===
Carston saw Scylla, preoccupied, perfectly and hideously gay.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿（Carston）见到了斯库拉（Scylla），她心不在焉，快活得淋漓尽致，快活得吓人。

===Original===
They took him to Starn and showed it him: as if it was a live thing; and did not notice that he resented its life, and was making attempts to kill it. Principally he remembered it because it was half full of people from the world outside. Not peasants, people in vulgar clothes, on motorcycles, in Ford cars, come to stare because it was summer, whom his party treated as if they were a disease.

===Chinese===
他们带他去斯塔恩（Starn），把它指给他看：像出示一件活物；却没有察觉他忌恨它的活气，正一门心思想法子弄死它。他记得它，主要因为它半街塞满了外面世界来的人。不是庄稼人，是些衣着俗艳的人，骑摩托车的，坐福特车的，因为入了夏，跑来瞪眼看风景——他这一伙人待他们，像待一场瘟疫。

===Original===
After lunch the object of the expedition leaked out. He was told that there was a farm, some way off, where mead was made and could be bought. The price of whisky and drinkable wine had turned their thoughts to it. There was no road to the farm. About the distance they were delicately indefinite. They spoke about a track across a place they called the Heath.

===Chinese===
午饭之后，此行的目的泄露了出来。他被告知，几里外有一座农庄，酿蜂蜜酒（mead），也出售。威士忌和还喝得下口的酒的价钱，把他们的念头引到了那边。去农庄没有路。至于路程远近，他们文雅地含糊其辞。他们说起一条小径，穿过一片他们唤作“荒原”的地方。

===Original===
Carston had seen the Heath, had crawled along it for miles in the train. It had seemed purple and endless, and he suspected full of traps. The other side of these people’s world of hills and the sea. Their idea was that it was time for Carston to visit it, covered with a gauze of every variety of heather, the sweet blood-bright burning crop out of which the honey wine was made.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿见过那片荒原，坐火车沿着它爬行了好几英里。它看上去紫色无边，而且他疑心它布满陷阱。在这些人的山与海的世界的另一侧。他们的意思是，卡斯顿该去见识见识它了——它罩着一层千品万类的石楠轻纱，那甜的、血一般亮的、燃烧似的收成，蜂蜜酒正是从它酿出来的。

===Original===
They rested a little and set out.

===Chinese===
他们歇了一会儿，便动身了。

===Original===
Ross caught up Scylla, walking ahead, picking out the track. “A word with you,” he said, “don’t let anyone know.”

===Chinese===
罗斯赶上走在前头辨路的斯库拉。“跟你说句话，”他说，“别让任何人知道。”

===Original===
“Why not?”

===Chinese===
“为什么不能？”

===Original===
“Because of the American, because of Felix, because of Clarence.”

===Chinese===
“因为那个美国人，因为菲利克斯，因为克拉伦斯（Clarence）。”

===Original===
“Why shouldn’t they know that Picus and I have an amourette and have magic between us?”

===Chinese===
“皮库斯（Picus）同我有一段小情缘（amourette），彼此之间有魔法，为什么就不该让他们知道？”

===Original===
“There’s something wrong today with Carston.”

===Chinese===
“卡斯顿今天有点不对劲。”

===Original===
“At worst he’ll leave and blast my reputation for a bit. What of that? And, anyhow, how did you find out, a floor up with Felix and Clarence?”

===Chinese===
“往坏里说，他一走了之，把我名声糟蹋一阵子。那又怎样？再说，你到底是怎么发觉的？你不是同菲利克斯和克拉伦斯住上一层？”

===Original===
“Felix and Clarence snoring. Carston quiet, Picus whistling.”

===Chinese===
“菲利克斯和克拉伦斯打呼噜。卡斯顿没动静，皮库斯吹口哨。”

===Original===
“Ross, why have that tall bird and I become lovers? I want to know that. I think it is the kind of thing we shall find out about when it’s over, and wonder at.” Ross said:

===Chinese===
“罗斯，那只高个子鸟儿和我，怎么就成了情人？我倒想知道这个。依我看，这种事非得等它过去了，咱们才会弄明白，才会为之惊叹。”罗斯说：

===Original===
“There is trouble about. The kind that comes with brightness. Can you see that?”

===Chinese===
“四下里有麻烦在近身了。随着光亮一道来的那一种。你看得出么？”

===Original===
“I can,” she said. “Do you mean that Picus is up to no good? I rather agree.”

===Chinese===
“看得出，”她说。“你是说皮库斯憋着坏？我颇为同意。”

===Original===
“The first rule,” said Ross, “is that Picus is never up to any good.”

===Chinese===
“头一条法则，”罗斯说，“皮库斯从来没什么好心眼儿。”

===Original===
“Allow me a little fantasy about him.”

===Chinese===
“那也容我对他存一点幻想吧。”

===Original===
“Remember that I told you.”

===Chinese===
“记住，是我提醒过你。”

===Original===
“You are being one of the enemies of the rose. Why should you? You always do what you like. Leave that to Clarence.”

===Chinese===
“你这会儿正充当玫瑰的敌人之一。何必呢？你自己向来想干什么就干什么。那一行留给克拉伦斯吧。”

===Original===
“I’m telling you to be careful.”

===Chinese===
“我是提醒你当心。”

===Original===
“Are we never to have any peace, only adventure and pain? And you, Ross, have a sacred peace.”

===Chinese===
“难道我们就永远得不到安宁，只有冒险和苦痛？而你，罗斯，却有一份神圣的安宁。”

===Original===
“So have you. It’s the others. That’s why they had better not know.”

===Chinese===
“你也有。是旁人没份。所以才顶好别让他们知道。”

===Original===
“Perhaps not Felix. Brothers will be brothers.”

===Chinese===
“菲利克斯也许除外。兄弟到底是兄弟。”

===Original===
“Carston’s bored. Today he’s upset. Satan’s looking for a job for him. I think you were to have been the job.”

===Chinese===
“卡斯顿闷得发慌。今天他又心绪不宁。撒旦正在替他找一件差事。我看你本来就该是那件差事。”

===Original===
“First I’ve heard of it.”

===Chinese===
“这话我头一回听说。”

===Original===
“We didn’t tell him how long he was to stay.”

===Chinese===
“我们没告诉他，他这趟该待多久。”

===Original===
“You mean that our ‘come down and see us’ is going to add an episode to what Felix calls the family horror?”

===Chinese===
“你是说，咱们那句‘下来看看我们吧’，就要给菲利克斯嘴里的家庭恐怖再添上一段？”

===Original===
“I mean that anything that’s going to happen that shouldn’t will find him useful to happen through. Also, a thing you will be too vain to see. This is a move against Clarence by your fancy-boy.”

===Chinese===
“我是说，凡是那种不该发生而偏要发生的事，都会发觉他正好合用，尽可以借着他发生。还有一件事，你太自负，看不见。这是你的小情郎冲着克拉伦斯走的一步棋。”

===Original===
She looked across the purple land to where it ended in the waters of an estuary, more transparent than the sky. “Remember what has to be remembered, too﻿—that Picus and I are young and handsome, rather in love. That he is full of fun and dancing, and bird-calls. Like I am. These things count as well.”

===Chinese===
她望过这片紫色的土地，望到尽头处一片河湾的水，比天空还透明。“该记住的你也记住——皮库斯和我年轻貌美，彼此相当倾心。他满身是玩笑、舞蹈和鸟鸣。同我一样。这些也一并作数。”

===Original===
“You’ll see.”

===Chinese===
“往后你自会看见。”

===Original===
“You are not counting on me?”

===Chinese===
“你不是在指望我吧？”

===Original===
“If you can keep things steady, you’d better.”

===Chinese===
“你要是稳得住局面，最好还是稳住。”

===Original===
“Perhaps I’m tired of keeping things steady for you. This is my pleasure and my game. And Clarence is unreasonable. Think of it another way, Ross: that Picus is giving us an excuse for the sacred game.”

===Chinese===
“也许我替你稳局面稳得累了。这是我的乐趣，我的游戏。再说克拉伦斯不可理喻。换个想法吧，罗斯：皮库斯这是在递给咱们一个由头，好去玩那场神圣的游戏。”

===Original===
He answered sullenly: “Americans are bad players. At bridge or gardening, or life; especially when it is life as the sacred game. And it isn’t his game here, anyhow.”

===Chinese===
他悻悻地答道：“美国人是拙劣的玩家。打桥牌也好，侍弄园子也好，过日子也好；尤其当日子被当作那场神圣的游戏来过的时候。再说，这儿的局反正也轮不到他。”

===Original===
Scylla said: “I’ll tell you something. Picus doesn’t want Clarence to know. He’s afraid of that.”

===Chinese===
斯库拉说：“告诉你一件事。皮库斯不愿意让克拉伦斯知道。他怕这个。”

===Original===
“So he should be. Where would he be without him?”

===Chinese===
“他也该怕。没有他，他还能在哪儿？”

===Original===
“Dead, perhaps. That’s why I’ll do my best to be decent. And now, I want you to tell me what you think about the cup?” He turned away and beat the heather with his stick.

===Chinese===
“兴许死了。所以我才要尽力把体面做足。现在，我要你告诉我，你对那只杯怎么看？”他扭过脸去，拿手杖抽打石楠。

===Original===
“It’s too late, Ross, to be petulant, because you know too much or too little. When you said there was trouble about like brightness, you spoke of things which are *nefas*, and you’ve got to go on. Remember Freud.”

===Chinese===
“罗斯，这时候使性子已经太迟了，因为你懂得的不是太多，就是太少。你说麻烦跟着光亮一道来，你说的就是*nefas*（不祥渎神）之事，而你既然开了口，就只得一路讲下去。想着点弗洛伊德（Freud）。”

===Original===
“We aren’t worthy,” he cried.

===Chinese===
“我们配不上。”他喊道。

===Original===
“Worthy? What’s worthy? Was anyone? And you’ve forgotten Gawaine, the knight of the world and of courtesy.”

===Chinese===
“配？什么叫配？又有谁配过？再说你忘了高文（Gawaine）——尘世与礼数的骑士。”

===Original===
“I didn’t know he came into it.”

===Chinese===
“我不知道这里面还有他。”

===Original===
“That comes of never opening a book.”

===Chinese===
“这就是从不翻书的下场。”

===Original===
“I detest women.”

===Chinese===
“我厌恶女人。”

===Original===
“Never mind detesting me, which is what you mean. If that cup is anything at all, if it was once an old cup of the sacrament people called ‘big magic,’ if it’s anything or nothing, we can’t hurt it, and it can’t hurt us. We have our courage and our imagination. We have to be as subtle as our memories. That’s all. And but one thing: Picus has given the cup to me.”

===Chinese===
“厌恶我不必了，你指的是这个。那只杯就算真是个什么，就算它从前当真是弥撒圣事的一只旧杯，让人唤作‘大魔法’，就算它什么都是、或者什么都不是——我们伤不了它，它也伤不了我们。我们有我们的勇气和我们的想象。我们只须练得同我们的记忆一样精细。就这么些。只除了一件事：皮库斯已把那只杯给了我。”

===Original===
“Considering your relations, I suppose he had to.”

===Chinese===
“就凭你们俩的关系，我想他不能不给。”

===Original===
“Then forward, damsel of the Sanc-Grail.”

===Chinese===
“那就请上路吧，圣所圣杯（Sanc-Grail）的少女。”

===Original===
“How dare you!” said Ross, “how dare you!”

===Chinese===
“你放肆！”罗斯说，“你放肆！”

===Original===
She looked at him stoically, “I thought better of you, Ross. Thought there was something hard and great in you. I’m tired of being disappointed. Hear the words of the lover of a bird. He is light and winged and holy. And I mean by holy all there can be in the word; I mean, taboo. You are heavy, wingless, and sacred. And you are a very sensual man. You should understand.”

===Chinese===
她隐忍地望着他。“罗斯，我原先把你想得更好。想着你身上有一种坚硬而伟大的东西。我失望得累了。且听一听一只鸟的情人的话。他轻盈，有翼，而且圣洁。我说圣洁，是把这个词所能有的意思全算上；我是说，禁忌。你沉重，无翼，而且神圣。而你又是个十分肉感的人。你本该明白。”

===Original===
“I understand that you’re up in risky air, because you’ve got off with the worst of the lot of us.”

===Chinese===
“我懂得的是，你已经蹿到危险的空气里去了，因为你同我们这伙人里最坏的一个勾搭上了。”

===Original===
“I can fly.” She waited a moment and then spoke cheerfully: “The first thing I understand is that you and I are being unpleasant to each other. The difficulty in this business will be to see the obvious. If Picus is up to his tricks, he’s won the first round. And I’ve tried to pretend that Picus means less than he does to me. To please you, and because it sounds chic. I take that back. And we shall all see.”

===Chinese===
“我飞得起来。”她顿了片刻，随即快活地说：“我明白的头一件事，是你我此刻正彼此不痛快。这桩事里的难处，将在于看清那明摆着的东西。要是皮库斯在耍他的花招，那他已经赢了第一回合。而我一直装作皮库斯在我心里没有他实际的分量重——为的是讨你欢心，也因为那样说听着潇洒。这话我收回。往后咱们大家走着瞧。”

===Original===
Ross stood still, his face wrinkled like a pony, sniffing.

===Chinese===
罗斯站住了，脸皱得像匹小马，抽着鼻子。

===Original===
“Where are they?” he said. They looked back over magenta risings, yellow sand-holes, black bunches of trees. Quite different from the ravishing gauze seen from Starn on its hill. They waited, but neither one body nor four could be seen moving out of a pocket or over a ridge of the huge, broken honeycomb. Scylla said:

===Chinese===
“他们在哪儿？”他说。两人回头望过去：品红色的隆起、黄色的沙坑、一丛丛黑压压的树。同从山上的斯塔恩望见的那幅醉人轻纱，全然是两回事。他们等着，可无论一个身影还是四个身影，都不见从哪一处凹兜里挪出来，也不见翻过这张巨大而残破的蜂窝的哪一道脊。斯库拉说：

===Original===
“Has Carston got lost?”

===Chinese===
“卡斯顿迷路了？”

===Original===
Ross did not suppress a laugh. “He’s got three of us with him.”

===Chinese===
罗斯没忍住笑。“他身边带着咱们三个呢。”

===Original===
“We’re not heath-people.”

===Chinese===
“咱们可不是荒原人。”

===Original===
“Picus knows it. Spends days prowling about here.” That subject, delicately picked up, was dropped. “Let’s get on.”

===Chinese===
“皮库斯认得这地方。一逛就是几天，在这片转悠。”这话题被轻轻地拾起，又被轻轻放下。“走吧。”

===Original===
“No, let’s wait.”

===Chinese===
“不，等等他们。”

===Original===
“Why should we?”

===Chinese===
“为什么要等？”

===Original===
“Because of the mead. Think of us, sweating back with a dozen between us.”

===Chinese===
“为了蜂蜜酒。想想咱们回程的模样：两个人当中抬着一打，一路淌汗。”

===Original===
*Bing.* A large black bee slapped Ross’s cheek and swung out along the ribbon of sand-path across which the heather stalks whipped their ankles.

===Chinese===
*啪。*一只肥大的黑蜂扇上罗斯的脸颊，又荡出去，沿着那条缎带似的沙径——径旁的石楠茎条正抽打着他们的脚踝。

===Original===
“Follow the bee,” he said, “it won’t be the last walk we shall take together.”

===Chinese===
“跟着蜜蜂走，”他说，“这不会是咱们最后一次同行。”

===Original===
“Good!” she said﻿—“at worst they will only be lost. Who minds being lost when there is so much to see? They can steer by Starn. We’ll collect what we can, and get home with some martyrdom in hand.”

===Chinese===
“好！”她说——“往坏里说他们也不过迷个路。有这么多可看的，谁还怕迷路？他们可以对着斯塔恩定方向。咱们能收多少收多少，回家时手里好歹也挣下了一场受难。”

===Original===
For the last time she looked back over the blazing plain from which an army might pop out. Ross did not look back. He stood with his head flung up, his mouth stretching into its wild-animal smile. With violent, silent amusement, he said:

===Chinese===
她最后一次回头，望过那片灼灼燃烧的平原——说不定会从那里头突然杀出一支大军来。罗斯没有回头。他站着，头颅高高扬起，嘴角咧开他那副野兽的笑。带着一阵无声而猛烈的开怀，他说：

===Original===
“It’s beginning.”

===Chinese===
“开始了。”

## When We Were Very Young / 当我们还很小的时候

===Original===
While Felix was whirling down from Montmartre in a taxi with the boy friend and the Russian, all of them drunk. Different drunks. The boy friend roaring gay, the world forgiven because of midsummer Paris, crossed with his original Irish love of a spree. He beamed, an arm round Felix, an arm round the Russian whom he usually detested.

===Chinese===
那时菲利克斯正和那位小男朋友、那个俄国人一道，坐在出租车里从蒙马特一路盘旋而下，三个人都醉了。各有各的醉法。小男朋友醉得放声欢笑——因为仲夏的巴黎，整个世界都得了原谅——这股欢劲又掺上他爱尔兰人天生的爱闹腾。他满面生辉，一条胳膊搂着菲利克斯，一条胳膊搂着他平日里厌恶的那个俄国人。

===Original===
“I’ve got a new crab.”

===Chinese===
“我弄到一只新螃蟹。”

===Original===
“Let’s go and christen it.”

===Chinese===
“咱们去给它行洗礼吧。”

===Original===
“What shall we call it?”

===Chinese===
“给它起个什么名字？”

===Original===
“Aloysius.”

===Chinese===
“阿洛伊修斯（Aloysius）。”

===Original===
“*Où allons-nous pour le baptême?*” Felix suddenly pitched sideways, his forehead on the Russian’s knees.

===Chinese===
“咱们上哪儿去行洗礼呢？”（*Où allons-nous pour le baptême?*）菲利克斯突然朝旁边一栽，额头抵在俄国人的膝上。

===Original===
“I speak it a bit, Boris,” he said. It was true. The American felt too jolly to be angry even at the unknown tongue. And Felix knew that he had found what he had come for, this slip out of Russia, a burning black pillar of congenial romance. Birth and ruin and exile, and a name not like green hills, but a wild, snow-crested tree. He would take Boris away. He would go back with Boris into Russia. He would take Boris home. He had found what he had come to find. Not the other one, who, after all, wasn’t a gentleman. An awful, delicious fear that Boris could read his mind, had heard his last thought and was amused by its stupidity. While the American sobered up to be surprised at Felix, all his high and mighty airs melted before that notorious lost wolf-cub. God only knew what sort of a not bad sort originally, but finished by having to live on its wits. And Boris was a trifle embarrassed, observant, indifferent, and thoroughly enjoying himself. An evening after his own heart.

===Chinese===
“我会说一点，鲍里斯。”他说。这是真的。那美国人太快活了，连听不懂的外国话也恼不起来。而菲利克斯知道，他此来要找的东西找着了：这截从俄国剪下的枝条，一根气味相投的传奇黑火柱。出生、毁灭与流亡，还有一个不像青山、倒像一棵顶着雪冠的野树的名字。他要把鲍里斯带走。他要同鲍里斯一道回俄国去。他要带鲍里斯回家。他此来要找的，找着了。不是另外那一个——那人到底算不得绅士。一种可怕又美妙的恐惧：怕鲍里斯读得出他的心思，已经听见了他的最后一个念头，正为它的愚蠢暗自发笑。与此同时，那美国人酒意渐醒，开始对菲利克斯感到惊讶，他那副高高在上的架子，全化在那只名声在外的走失小狼崽面前。只有上帝知道，这原本是个什么样不坏的种，到头来却落得全靠心机过活。而鲍里斯有一点局促，看在眼里，淡漠处之，又玩得十二分尽兴。一个正中他下怀的夜晚。

===Original===
They were on the shore of the Place de la Concorde, this time an empty sea. The taxi raced across its grey glass, over the arc of a bridge, and began to thread old Paris like a furious shuttle.

===Chinese===
他们正跑在协和广场的岸边——这一回，是一片空空荡荡的海。出租车驰过它灰色的玻璃，越过一道桥的弧线，开始像一支狂怒的梭子，穿引老巴黎。

===Original===
“*O bel! O gai!*” whispered Felix, upright now between them, his dark blue eyes turned stars. Both admired him a little, nervously. Both profoundly hoped he’d enough money on him, the Russian because he had none, the American because he had no intention of spending any he had. And Felix saw Europe folk-wandering, and how out of the movements of the peoples he had found his companion, young and wicked, and in need and kind. Like a bow bent and relaxed, and strung with fresh arrows, his desires took purpose. At a ghastly little mixed bordel, he walked in like a prince come home.

===Chinese===
“哦美！哦欢！”（*O bel! O gai!*）菲利克斯低声念道，这会儿他已在两人中间坐直了，深蓝色的眼睛变成了星星。两人都对他有点倾倒，带着不安。两人都深切盼着他身上带的钱够使：俄国人是因为自己分文皆无，美国人是因为自己压根不打算破费分文。而菲利克斯看见欧洲正在部族游徙，看见自己如何从诸民族的迁徙里找到了他的同伴——年轻，邪性，穷困，而心善。他的种种欲望，像一张弯了又弛下的弓，重新束上簇新的箭，有了准头。在一家乌烟瘴气、人色混杂的小妓院，他抬脚走了进去，像一位王子归家。

## X / 十、满屋寻杯

===Original===
The next morning the sky was white round a blue zenith. Carston came down, not pleased, because through every discomfort of soul he was feeling well, his body content with itself, a steady animal health. He should have been all of a tremble, and he was hoping that there would be fish for breakfast, lots of fish. There was fish, and after it the toast came up, hot and hot. He remembered again to make opportunity serve him. Health would give him power. Also he would desire Scylla more. Desiring her more, and not wholly as panache he might get her. Picus was bound to let her down. He was saying: “I’m sorry I lost my temper. It was trying to lose you three like that. You know how one is.” Scylla said:

===Chinese===
第二天早晨，蓝色天顶的四周罩着一圈白。卡斯顿（Carston）走下楼来，心里并不痛快，因为透过灵魂的种种不适，他只觉身子舒泰，这具肉体对自己颇为满意，一种沉稳的兽性健康。他本该浑身发颤才对，可他一心盼着早餐有鱼，要有很多鱼。果然有鱼，鱼过后吐司端了上来，滚烫滚烫。他又记起要让机会为己所用。健康会给他力量。还会让他更渴望斯库拉（Scylla）。越是渴望她——而这渴望又不全是为了摆阔——他便越可能得到她。皮库斯（Picus）注定要让她失望。只听他说道：“对不起，我发了脾气。像那样把你们三个弄丢了，实在熬人。你知道人有时候就是那样。”斯库拉说：

===Original===
“Served you right, when you’d lost them.”

===Chinese===
“谁叫你把人丢了，活该。”

===Original===
“How did you do it?” said Ross.

===Chinese===
“你是怎么弄丢的？”罗斯（Ross）说。

===Original===
There was no answer. Picus was giving the impression that he was about to flirt his tail and vanish. Carston was irritated again. There came droning into his mind an ugly sentence, a haunting from barren New England: *Thou shalt not suffer a witch to live.* He was hearing Picus say: “How’s the cup this morning?” and saw Scylla get up for it.

===Chinese===
没有人回答。皮库斯一副就要轻摆尾巴、倏然隐去的样子。卡斯顿又恼了。一句难听的话嗡嗡钻进他的脑海，一个来自贫瘠的新英格兰（New England）、缠人不散的鬼魂：*行邪术的女人，不可容她存活。*他听见皮库斯说：“今早那只杯怎么样了？”又看见斯库拉起身去取。

===Original===
It was not on the card-table, folded over in a half-moon. It was not on the chimneypiece, or on the sideboard among the candlesticks, whose silver had worn down to the copper and polished rose.

===Chinese===
它不在牌桌上——那桌子折拢着，合成一弯半月。它不在壁炉台上，也不在餐具柜的烛台中间——那些烛台的银层磨薄了，露出铜色，又擦出玫瑰般的光。

===Original===
They passed the morning entering and leaving rooms where the cup was not. Not in the kitchen, the lavatory, or the library. In twos and threes and singly, getting more silent as they passed each other. Up and down the curved stairs whose banister was a rope run through rings. Carston saw a good deal in that journey. Scylla’s room which had its objects of luxury, where the first time he stared at the bed, he suffered and desired to throw himself on to it. In and out of the room several times, he became indifferent, content with repeating to himself: “I shall sleep there.” Picus’s room, noncommittal, in exquisite order like a manly woman’s and Clarence’s, full of frivolities. Then he remembered something he had read about an Emperor’s collection of hats and wigs which sometimes solace the leisure of a military man. There were no wigs and no hats. But no memory in that place ever had a straight point. Only his room was a sad, gay, desperate display of something like toys. He pitied, without humour, having no humour; slightly wanting for himself a glass orange, each of whose fingers came out and held a different kind of scent.

===Chinese===
他们的整个上午，就耗在一间间没有那只杯的房间里进进出出。厨房里没有，盥洗间里没有，书房里也没有。两人一伙、三人一帮，也有单枪匹马的，彼此错身而过时，越来越沉默。上上下下那道弯弯的楼梯，扶手是一根绳子，穿在一圈圈环里。这一路卡斯顿看清了不少东西。斯库拉的房间摆着她那些奢侈的小物件，头一回盯着那张床看时，他受着煎熬，恨不得一头扑上去。进进出出几趟之后，他淡漠了，满足于对自己反复念叨：“我要在那儿睡。”皮库斯的房间看不出名堂，收拾得精致整洁，像个男人气十足的女人的房间，也像克拉伦斯（Clarence）的，满是轻巧的小玩意儿。接着他想起读到过的一段话，说某位皇帝收藏帽子与假发，那些东西有时能慰藉一位军人的闲暇。这里没有假发，也没有帽子。可在那个地方，没有一段记忆是直来直去的。唯有克拉伦斯的房间，是一场又悲哀、又欢快、又不管不顾的陈列，陈列着些近乎玩具的东西。他心生怜悯，并无半分幽默——他本就没有幽默；只微微动念，想把那只玻璃橘子据为己有——那橘子的每一瓣都抽得出来，各各盛着一种不同的香气。

===Original===
All their things for art or sport were cared for like tools or choice animals. In Felix’s room there were leopard skins; in Ross’s a Buddha in a red lacquer shrine held a crystal, a reflection for their mutual contempt.

===Chinese===
他们那些用于艺术或运动的家什，都被照料得像工具，或像精选的牲畜。菲利克斯（Felix）的房间里有豹皮；罗斯的房里，红漆神龛中一尊佛陀（Buddha）托着一颗水晶，正好给他们彼此的轻蔑当映照。

===Original===
And the beds were humble and the linen darned, the bare floors solid like glass rocks. He could feel the weight of the blue slate roof, cooling and darkening the rooms, holding the house to the earth, while they searched it like serious children for a thing which could not walk. In and out and up and down a house much larger than it seemed, cooler than the wood’s heart.

===Chinese===
床铺都简朴，亚麻织物打着补丁，裸露的地板坚实得像玻璃岩石。他能感觉到蓝板岩屋顶的分量，让房间阴凉、幽暗，把这座宅子按在大地上，而他们像一群神情严肃的孩子，在宅子里搜寻一件不会走路的东西。进进出出、上上下下，这座宅子比看上去大得多，也比树林的心脏更凉。

===Original===
Dazed Carston played too, arrived at the long attic, roaring dark, directly under the roof. The roar was from bees who hived under the slates, and the smell their fresh honey and the black clots, old combs. There was Felix opening a Victorian work-box made out of an elephant’s foot. A place where it was utterly impossible for the cup to be. No more impossible than that it should have been overlooked anywhere else. Short of idiocy, a miracle, or a trick, the thing was off the map. A conclusion they had reached by lunchtime, after a morning’s exercise indoors.

===Chinese===
昏昏然的卡斯顿也跟着玩，来到了长长的阁楼——一片轰响的黑暗，紧贴屋顶底下。轰响来自在板岩下筑巢的蜂群，气味则是它们的新蜜，还有一团团黑色的旧巢脾。菲利克斯正在那儿开一只维多利亚式的针线盒，用一只象脚做的。一个那杯绝对不可能在的地方。可这也不比它在别处被统统漏看更不可能。除非是犯傻、出了奇迹、或是有人捣鬼，这东西已然出了版图。这是他们到午饭时分得出的结论——在室内运动了一上午之后。

===Original===
It was a quiet meal. Two things had been lost. Picus and a cup. Picus had found himself. As for the cup, they had reached the time before the time for real consideration, when the instinct is to find something else to do. Carston, no more than the others, was quite ready to say that, since six pairs of eyes had failed to see something, then that thing must have been hidden, and hidden well. Instead he was made unhappy, because he heard Picus say to Scylla: “We don’t want to bathe today, do we? Come over to Gault Cliff and I’ll show you something﻿—”

===Chinese===
这顿饭吃得很静。丢了两样东西。皮库斯，和一只杯。皮库斯已经找到了他自己。至于那只杯，他们到了真正细想之前的那个时辰——在那样的时辰，本能催着人赶紧去找点别的什么事做。卡斯顿并不比别人更情愿说出：既然六双眼睛都没能看见一样东西，那它必是被人藏了，而且藏得很好。他不肯说，反而闷闷不乐起来，因为他听见皮库斯对斯库拉说：“咱们今天不想去洗海澡了，对吧？到高尔特崖（Gault Cliff）来，我给你看样东西——”

===Original===
“Birds?” He nodded once. “I’m coming.”

===Chinese===
“鸟儿？”他点了一下头。“我这就来。”

===Original===
They were in the library, the sacred neutral zone for arrangements that had no reference to their life as a group. An arrangement Carston could not be expected to have understood. It was a very dark room. From a window seat he saw their bodies straining to be away. And hated it. Like a man put in a bag and shaken up, instincts were stirring in him, like muscles unused in years, and sore and strengthening.

===Chinese===
他们是在书房里，那是个神圣的中立地带，专门用来谈那些与他们共同生活无关的约定。这样一层默契，本不能指望卡斯顿懂得。那是个很暗的房间。他从窗座上望着他们的身体绷紧了要往远处去。他恨这个。像被人装进一只口袋抖了一通，他体内的本能苏醒过来，像多年不用的肌肉，酸痛，而且越练越硬。

===Original===
The American appears to the English everything that is implied by saying oversensitive, touchy, or abnormally quick to take offence. Our reaction is usually bewilderment, grief that our intentions have been misunderstood. Followed by a desire to give them something to cry about.

===Chinese===
在英国人眼里，美国人就是“过分敏感”“碰不得”“动辄觉得受辱”这些字眼所暗含的一切。我们的反应通常是发懵，是伤心——自己的一片用意竟被误解了。继而生出一股冲动，想让他们有点真正值得哭的事。

===Original===
Carston thought they had seen him, could not have understood that no one was seen in that room. And they had not seen him. If they had, they would have supposed that he was there not to be seen. Scylla was sitting on the top of the library steps, made like everything in the house before the days of cheap furniture, shabby and characteristic as an old dog. She said to Picus:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿以为他们看见他了；他却不可能明白，在那间房里，根本看不见任何人。而他们确实没有看见他。倘若看见了，他们也会认定，他待在那儿正是为了不被人看见。斯库拉坐在书房梯凳的顶上，那梯凳同这座宅子里的一切一样，都造于廉价家具的年代之前，破旧而有个性，像一条老狗。她对皮库斯说：

===Original===
“You idle baggage, what have you found on the hills?”

===Chinese===
“你这懒东西，在山丘上找到什么了？”

===Original===
“Come and see!”

===Chinese===
“来看看！”

===Original===
“Let me down!” He put his hands under her armpits and let her down, so gently that Carston did not hear her feet touch the floor. He saw their colours; her white; Picus’s blue and grey. He saw their beauty, their own, and the beauty of their passion. And another thing; that the man’s right hand ought not to belong to his body; that it was red and thick and swollen at the joints. He remembered the delicate adaptability of Ross’s, Felix’s, Clarence’s hands. This gave him a key. To a very old feeling about sin and fleshy lust. A refinement on sensuality, he knew, but an excuse for rage. And a warm feeling that what would relieve him would be right. Right for him to possess Scylla, if he had first rescued her. That would give him the claim on her body. Rescue her from what? From passion for a tall delicate man with coarse hands. Also because he had glory, a kind of lost god. From what was the glory? From the devil. How did one shame the devil? By taking the honour out of his sufferings. *Thou shalt not suffer a witch to live.* And the reward would be Scylla.

===Chinese===
“扶我下来！”他把双手插到她的腋下，把她放了下来，轻得卡斯顿没听见她的脚碰着地板。他看见他们的颜色：她的白，皮库斯的蓝和灰。他看见他们的美——他们自身的美，以及他们情欲的美。还有一桩：那只男人的右手不该属于他的身体；它红、粗，关节处肿着。他想起罗斯的手、菲利克斯的手、克拉伦斯的手，那样精巧柔韧。这给了他一把钥匙，打开一种关于罪与肉欲的极古老的情感。他知道，这不过是声色之欲的精致变体，却正好给怒火作借口。还有一种暖烘烘的感觉：凡是能让他松快的事，便是对的。他先救出她，再去占有她，便是正当。那将给他一份对她肉体的权利。从什么里救？从她对一个男人的情欲里——那男人高挑细巧，却生着一双粗手。也因为他有荣光，一种失落神祇式的荣光。荣光自何而来？自魔鬼。人怎样叫魔鬼蒙羞？把他苦难中的荣誉抽走。*行邪术的女人，不可容她存活。*而报酬，就是斯库拉。

===Original===
What would he give her? Respect, for he did not respect her, or at least admire. Sincerity, loyalty, virility﻿—after what? He was sure Picus did not respect her, was not sincere with her, had no loyalty, and he didn’t know about the last. The fantasy in this sequence escaped him, because of the naivete of his cult for women. He had made a martyr of a person who was not a martyr, or to nothing which would have moved him. In the world there is a fifty-fifty deal of pleasure and grief. The excuse of that band was that they knew it, and that they had something else to occupy their attention, something that the wood knew.

===Chinese===
他能给她什么？敬重——可他并不敬重她，至少谈不上爱慕。真诚、忠贞、男子气——之后呢？他确信皮库斯不敬重她，对她不真诚，没有忠贞，至于最后一样，他不知道。这一串念头里的荒唐他察觉不到，因为他对女人的崇拜天真得很。他把一个人封成了殉道者，而那人并非殉道者，或者所殉之物根本动不了他。这个世界上，快乐与悲哀是对半的交易。那一伙人的可宽宥处，是他们明知这一点，却另有东西占着他们的心神——一样树林知道的东西。

===Original===
When they had gone, Carston went to the wall where a map hung, to look for a place called Gault Cliff. As well to know where they were going. He followed the coast with his thumb, and found it. About three miles away. There was a track they would probably take; and a way where no track was marked over God knew what to God knew where, which might get him there before them.

===Chinese===
他们走后，卡斯顿走到挂地图的墙边，去找一个叫高尔特崖的地方。反正先弄清他们要去哪儿也好。他用拇指沿着海岸一路摸过去，找到了。大约三英里外。有一条他们多半会走的小径；还有一条路，图上没有标注，天晓得越过什么、通到天晓得哪里，却也许能让他赶在他们前头到。

===Original===
The others seemed to expect him to want to be left very much alone. Praise their country and give them the slip? Or take Felix down to the sea and make him talk?

===Chinese===
其余的人似乎料定他巴不得独自待着。那么——把他们的乡野夸赞一番，再趁隙溜走？还是带菲利克斯下海边去，套他的话？

===Original===
He decided to do that.

===Chinese===
他决定这么办。

## XI / 十一、崖下秘林

===Original===
Picus and Scylla went out of the house along the green road, walking separately. They sprang up the hill, the air from the sea fortifying them, against the midday, against the desire to anticipate in thought a distance their muscles must cover.

===Chinese===
皮库斯（Picus）和斯库拉（Scylla）出了宅子，沿着绿径走去，两人分开走。他们一路轻快地跃上山坡，海上来的空气给他们添着力气，对抗正午，也对抗那股欲望——想用思绪抢先赶完一段路，而那段路本该由筋骨一步步量过去。

===Original===
They came to the coast where Ross had sat and painted, his back to the house. “This way,” said Picus, and turned through a gate into a tunnel of hawthorns where a bird had flown out before Ross. There Scylla paused, but he took no notice, and she fell behind him, following his stride.

===Chinese===
他们来到海边，罗斯（Ross）先前坐在这儿画过画，背朝着宅子。“这边。”皮库斯说着，转身穿过一道门，钻进一条山楂树的隧道，先前曾有一只鸟从罗斯面前飞出来。斯库拉在那里迟疑了一下，他却毫无觉察，她便落到他身后，跟着他的步子走。

===Original===
The way became hard, no way at all, but climb and drop over unmortared stone walls, bound with bramble and thorn. They crossed them, without reference to each other, steady as hunting dogs. In time they came to the edge of Gault.

===Chinese===
路变得难走起来，根本没了路，只剩下爬上爬下，翻过一道道不施灰泥的石墙，墙身缠满荆棘和山刺。他们翻墙而过，彼此不相照应，稳得像一对猎犬。走了些时候，他们到了高尔特崖的边上。

===Original===
“Let’s go down!”

===Chinese===
“咱们下去吧！”

===Original===
“How?”

===Chinese===
“怎么下去？”

===Original===
“On the soft side, where the landslide was last winter. Can you do it?”

===Chinese===
“走软的那一面，去年冬天塌方的地方。你行吗？”

===Original===
“I suppose so.” She looked where a coast-path ran to the edge, straight out into midair.

===Chinese===
“大概行吧。”她望着那条通到崖边的海岸小径——它一直伸出去，直直地伸进了半空。

===Original===
Some time later they were in the unvisited wood under Gault, where the low trees grow to the edge of the sea. Where there is no bay, no beach, no landing-place, no way round. Taking advantage of a great fall of earth, Picus had created a way down.

===Chinese===
过了些时候，他们已经置身高尔特崖下那片无人到过的树林，低矮的树一直长到海边。这里没有湾，没有滩，没有登陆的地方，也没有绕行的路。皮库斯趁着一次大规模的塌方，开出了一条下来的道。

===Original===
From the immense crag water dripped, and ran out under the roots of a thorn over the tidemark of the open sea. They followed it up to where it ran over pebbles across a circle of grass enclosed by low trees. Greenwood within whispering distance of the unharvested sea. She said:

===Chinese===
巨大的崖壁上滴着水，水从一丛荆棘的根下淌出去，漫过外海的潮痕。他们溯水而上，走到它漫过卵石、横穿一圈草地的地方——一圈被低树围拢的草地。绿林近得能听见那片未收刈的大海的絮语。她说：

===Original===
“What have you found here?”

===Chinese===
“你在这儿发现了什么？”

===Original===
“What d’you mean?”

===Chinese===
“你指什么？”

===Original===
“The way down﻿—and this﻿—”

===Chinese===
“下去的路——还有这儿——”

===Original===
“Foxes, badgers, fifteen ways of looking at a finch. They don’t mind you here.”

===Chinese===
“狐狸呀，獾呀，还有看一只雀的十五种方式。在这儿，它们不把你当外人。”

===Original===
High over them the gulls squalled like sorrow driven up. At long intervals the water tapped the rocks like memory driven away. She knelt over the stream and washed dust and clay and a smear of blood from her bare arms and neck. Pulled a trail of thorns from her skirt. Threw away her hat.

===Chinese===
高高的头顶上，鸥鸟嘶叫，像被驱赶上天的哀愁。隔上好一阵，水滴叩一下岩石，像被驱赶远去的记忆。她跪在水边，洗去裸露的胳膊和脖颈上的尘土、泥灰和一抹血痕。从裙子上扯下一挂荆棘。把帽子一扔。

===Original===
“This is Bari,” she said, “the warm wood.”

===Chinese===
“这里是巴里（Bari），”她说，“温暖的树林。”

===Original===
“Which one was that?” said Picus, off his guard.

===Chinese===
“那是哪个巴里？”皮库斯问，一时没了防备。

===Original===
“Baldur’s wood,” she said, very carefully, not to make a mistake with him.

===Chinese===
“巴尔德尔（Baldur）的树林。”她说，字斟句酌，生怕在他身上出了差错。

===Original===
Gault Cliff hung over them, a terror to look up at, its seamed head raw in the light, but dark underneath and broken into bog and scree, interlude between the earth of pure stone, and the earth of wood and spring. No interval between the wood and the sea, it was that made the place incomparable. They lay on the grass on each side of the foot-wide brook, paddling their hands.

===Chinese===
高尔特崖悬在他们头顶，抬头看一眼都叫人胆寒：沟壑纵横的崖头在光里裸露着，底下却是暗的，碎成沼泽和碎石坡——那是纯然石头的地界与树木泉水之地之间的一段过场。树林与海之间没有间隔，正是这一点使此地无可比拟。他们躺在溪流两侧的草地上，那一尺宽的溪水中间，各自把手浸在水里拍打。

===Original===
“Not bad for a naked sword,” she said. He kissed her over it. “Hush!” A bird appeared. She cried out: “That’s a Great Black Woodpecker.”

===Chinese===
“拿它当一柄出鞘的剑，倒也不坏。”她说。他隔着它吻了她。“嘘！”一只鸟出现了。她叫出声来：“那是一只黑啄木鸟！”

===Original===
“I’ve been looking for it all my life,” he said. “*Chuck!*” said the bird at them and went off. They saw the scarlet ribbon on its head.

===Chinese===
“我找它找了一辈子。”他说。“*恰克！*”那鸟冲他们叫了一声，飞走了。他们看见了它头顶那条猩红的缎带。

===Original===
“What else is there here?”

===Chinese===
“这儿还有什么？”

===Original===
“There’s a badger that bit me, and a vixen I can almost nurse﻿—there’s the skeleton of a man﻿—”

===Chinese===
“有一只咬过我的獾，有一只雌狐，我差不多能把奶喂给它——还有一具人的骷髅——”

===Original===
“We won’t look there now﻿—”

===Chinese===
“咱们这会儿不去看那个——”

===Original===
“Not today, love.” She crossed her hands on her breast. He had given her all he had. This place. A fountain where saga and love were mixed. She looked up into the sky at Gault Cliff, where the mica glittered like sweat.

===Chinese===
“今天不去了，亲爱的。”她把双手交叠在胸前。他已把他的全部给了她。这个地方。一眼泉水，萨迦与爱情在那里相混。她仰头望向高尔特崖上的天空，崖上的云母闪闪发光，像汗珠。

===Original===
Again Picus said: “Hush!” She listened, loving him. The bird was back, the largest and rarest of the woodpeckers.

===Chinese===
皮库斯又说：“嘘！”她听着，心里爱着他。那只鸟回来了，是啄木鸟中最大、最稀有的一种。

===Original===
“I saw him when I was looking at the skeleton. In a tree.”

===Chinese===
“我看那具骷髅的时候见过他。在一棵树上。”

===Original===
She said: “He was a famous bird once, Picus Martius. He was Zeus.” He nodded, as if he was saying: “I thought as much.” But she had turned the corner where love sees. When she saw that she was lying by the same thing. That what she had said to confuse them prettily, to hide love by revealing, had been about this. Between the tree and the skeleton there had been the bird who had been god. He had seen it, who was called Picus the Woodpecker, who was a man, who was the same thing. Now she knew, who was her lover. And what was she now, the lover of a bird?

===Chinese===
她说：“他从前是只有名的鸟，皮库斯·马提乌斯（Picus Martius）。他就是宙斯（Zeus）。”他点点头，仿佛在说：“我料到就是这么回事。”可她已经拐过了爱开始看见的那道弯。她看见自己正躺在同一件东西旁边。她先前说的那些话——为了漂亮地搅乱他们、以揭示来遮掩爱意的话——说的原来就是这个。在那棵树与那具骷髅之间，一直有着那只做过神的鸟。看见它的是他，那被唤作啄木鸟皮库斯的人，他和它本是同一件东西。如今，身为恋人的她知道了。而她如今又算什么呢——一只鸟的恋人？

===Original===
*Even Leda found a blue egg.* She laughed. How long was this going to last? What was this? It was all right; worth whatever it hatched.

===Chinese===
*连勒达（Leda）也捡到过一只蓝色的蛋。*她笑了。这能持续多久？这算什么？不要紧；不管它孵出什么，都值。

===Original===
Only he must make love to her there.

===Chinese===
只是他必须就在这儿爱她。

===Original===
He had rolled up the sleeves of his blue sweater. He put out his arms, one marked with a badger-bite, across the stream and slung her over.

===Chinese===
他已卷起蓝毛衣的袖子。他伸出双臂——一条上带着獾咬的疤——隔着溪水把她一把抱了过去。

===Original===
It was late afternoon. They were lying again on either side of the stream. Gault over them, a little blacker with the sun turning behind it.

===Chinese===
已是傍晚。他们又躺在溪流两侧。高尔特崖悬在头顶，太阳转到崖背后去了，崖影黑了一层。

===Original===
“How did you come to find this place?”

===Chinese===
“你是怎么发现这地方的？”

===Original===
“Last spring, looking for young gulls. I looked down and thought if I found a lover﻿—”

===Chinese===
“去年春天，来找小鸥。我朝下一看，心想，要是我找到一个恋人——”

===Original===
“Any lover?”

===Chinese===
“随便什么恋人？”

===Original===
“I have only found one.”

===Chinese===
“我只找到了一个。”

===Original===
She kissed him over the ribbon of water. He said:

===Chinese===
她隔着那条水带吻了他。他说：

===Original===
“There’s a nest in every crack of the rock. It’s still pretty full.” That took away her fear of Gault. For the terrible crag was soft with birds, and where the birds ended the spring rose. None of the sequence without grace.

===Chinese===
“岩石的每道裂缝里都有鸟巢。眼下还满满的。”这句话卸去了她对高尔特崖的恐惧。因为那可怕的崖壁被鸟住软了，鸟到尽头的地方，泉水便涌起。这一连串环环相扣，没有一环缺了恩宠。

===Original===
They were coming out of the trance of love into a time which would have to be put up with until luck turned them birds again. He had given her his treasure. If he rose and strangled her, she had that to remember to him. There was trouble in his face, the old trouble, Picus’s grief. That was not named or rational or tamed or shared. Untractable, inexplicable, near to wickedness.

===Chinese===
他们正从爱的迷醉里走出来，走进一段只好将就的时光，直到运气再把他们变回鸟。他已把他的宝藏给了她。哪怕他起身掐死她，她也还有这个可以记他一记。他脸上有一种愁，是那桩旧愁，皮库斯的哀伤。那东西没有名字，不讲道理，驯不服，也分不了人。犟头犟脑，无从解释，离邪恶只差一步。

===Original===
The water divided them. She crossed it. He moved his hands as though he would be rid of them.

===Chinese===
溪水隔开了他们。她蹚了过去。他动了动双手，仿佛恨不得甩掉它们。

===Original===
“Show me the skeleton!” He shook his head.

===Chinese===
“带我看那骷髅去！”他摇了摇头。

===Original===
“Make the bird come back! I want to see him again.”

===Chinese===
“让那只鸟回来！我要再看看他。”

===Original===
“Woodpecker-Zeus,” she said, “leave your skeleton under the tree. Stop flirting with us. We know who you are. Eagle. Kingfisher. Swan. We have met you before. I am Leda. You know best who he is.”

===Chinese===
“啄木鸟宙斯，”她说，“把你的骷髅留在树下吧。别再跟我们调情了。我们知道你是谁。鹰。翠鸟。天鹅。我们从前进见过你。我是勒达。他是谁，你最清楚。”

===Original===
She waited, hoping for the best.

===Chinese===
她等着，往最好的地方盼。

===Original===
“There it is,” said Picus. She could not see it. The shadow of the cliff was moving towards them.

===Chinese===
“在那儿。”皮库斯说。她看不见。崖影正朝他们移过来。

===Original===
“In the thicket.” She clapped her hands, and the bird flew out.

===Chinese===
“在树丛里。”她拍拍手，那只鸟飞了出来。

===Original===
“There,” she said, and saw him suddenly pleased and changed.

===Chinese===
“瞧。”她说，随即看见他忽然高兴起来，像换了个人。

===Original===
Then he said:

===Chinese===
接着他说：

===Original===
“Wonder what that chap Carston’ll make of it?”

===Chinese===
“不知道卡斯顿（Carston）那家伙会怎么想？”

===Original===
“Make of what?”

===Chinese===
“怎么想什么？”

===Original===
They were looking at the other out of the corner of their eyes. Picus paddled one hand in the stream.

===Chinese===
两人都拿眼角瞟着对方。皮库斯把一只手浸在溪水里拍打。

===Original===
Scylla said:

===Chinese===
斯库拉说：

===Original===
“There is one thing which may have surprised him already. His room’s between ours.”

===Chinese===
“有一件事也许已经让他吃了一惊。他的房间就在我们俩的中间。”

===Original===
“Well, that ought to interest him.”

===Chinese===
“哈，那该让他来劲了。”

===Original===
“Only,” she said, “if he wanted me.”

===Chinese===
“那也得，”她说，“他想要我才行。”

===Original===
“He may be wanting you. Perhaps you’d better sleep with him. It would be better than his coming down here. Where nothing has been spoiled, love.”

===Chinese===
“他也许正想要你呢。没准你最好去陪他睡。总好过他闯到这儿来。这儿的什么都没糟蹋过，亲爱的。”

===Original===
“I see. *Mais comme tu taquines éternité.*”

===Chinese===
“我明白了。*可你多么会拿永恒打趣。*”

===Original===
She thought again: I have no business to be glad that Clarence does not know, nor ask if he will be taken here. I came first. This was an excuse, not only in honour, but in letting life alone.

===Chinese===
她又想：克拉伦斯（Clarence）不知道，我就为此高兴，这不对；也不该追问会不会带他到这里来。我先来的。这话是个托词——不仅是道义上的托词，也是任生活自行其是的托词。

===Original===
He got up and drew her on to her feet. He walked her along the grass between the thickets and boulders, so that her feet never touched a stone. Up the landslide she hardly felt the slant of the earth, held as if he were walking with a tree. At the top of the cliffs he gave her no time to look back. In their triumph they walked alone a little separate from each other.

===Chinese===
他起身把她拉了起来。他引着她沿树丛与巨石间的草地走，让她的脚始终碰不上一块石头。上塌方坡时，她几乎觉不出大地的倾斜——他扶着她，仿佛正同一棵树并肩走。到了崖顶，他不给她回头看的工夫。两人带着凯旋，各自单独走着，彼此稍稍隔开一点。

===Original===
At a gate he caught her up.

===Chinese===
到了一道门前，他赶上了她。

===Original===
“What y’ thinking about?” She saw his head on one side.

===Chinese===
“想什么呢？”她看见他歪着头。

===Original===
“Carston and the cup. That ought to get him going more than us.”

===Chinese===
“卡斯顿，还有那只杯。那该比咱们更让他来劲。”

===Original===
“Perhaps it will.”

===Chinese===
“也许吧。”

===Original===
“Picus, demon, where did you hide it?”

===Chinese===
“皮库斯，你这魔鬼，你把它藏哪儿了？”

===Original===
“Hush! love.”

===Chinese===
“嘘！亲爱的。”

## XII / 十二、杯来无端

===Original===
After an accident in the sea with a small octopus he would sooner have avoided, Carston returned to the house. Felix had not talked to him, said that it would be wiser not to talk, because there might be big magic about. Could not Carston feel it cooking up? Convinced that the boy was enjoying himself, he went up to his room. And what was there to do but think of those two, up somewhere high in air, kissing, or finding some strangeness in Nature and forgetting to kiss. He lay staring and fretting, until with slow alarm growing like a dream, he saw the lost cup, by itself, on the end of his mantelpiece. And earlier in the day, they had passed in and out of his room looking for it there.

===Chinese===
在海里同一只他巴不得躲开的小章鱼闹了一场事故之后，卡斯顿（Carston）回到了宅子。菲利克斯（Felix）没跟他说话，说还是不开口为妙，因为这一带说不定正有大魔法在酝酿。卡斯顿难道感觉不出它正在锅里咕嘟咕嘟地炖着吗？他认定那小子正自得其乐，便上楼回了自己房间。除了想那两个人，还有什么可做的呢——想他们正在高天的某处，亲吻，或者在自然里发现了什么怪东西，忘了亲吻。他躺着，干瞪眼，干焦心，直到一阵惊恐像梦一样慢慢长大——他看见那只失踪的杯，孤零零地，立在他壁炉台的尽头。而当天早些时候，他们还曾进出他的房间，在里头找过它。

===Original===
His first impulse was to run downstairs with it, crying. Crossing the room to take it, he slipped on the glassy boards, and the fall and anger from pain turned him. He did not want to touch it. There might be something about it after all. Working a splinter out of his hand, it occurred to him that they had put it there; that the morning had been a farce played for his benefit, a vile joke to make a fool of him. Those people who made love under his eyes, who had lost him on a moor. They had not let him into their lives. They would not believe his innocence. Under the shock of his fall, his imagination galloped reeling. He felt very lonely. He was very lonely. It did not cross his head that they would believe what he told them. Still less that it did not matter whether they believed him or not. Behind this, a dead fever reviving in the blood, was the literal fear of the cup, that it was uncanny, taboo. He passed a dreadful minute, staring at its impressive antiquity. His sensitive intelligence raced through a variety of panics, till the shock of his fall subsided and he began to arrange alternatives. To go down to tea with the cup and say: “I found it in my room. I don’t know how it got there.”

===Chinese===
他的第一个冲动，是捧着它奔下楼去，一边嚷嚷。穿过房间去取它时，他在亮得像玻璃的地板上滑了一跤，摔的这一下加上疼出来的怒火，让他变了主意。他不想碰它了。它身上说不定真有什么名堂。从手心里往外剔木刺的时候，他忽然想到：是他们把它放在那儿的；那个上午是一出专演给他一个人看的闹剧，一个捉弄他的恶毒玩笑，要把他变成傻瓜。那些人当着他的面做爱，又在荒原上把他弄丢。他们没让他走进他们的生活。他们不会相信他是清白的。摔跤的震荡之下，他的想象踉跄着狂奔。他觉得非常孤独。他确实非常孤独。他脑子里压根没转过这个念头：他们会相信他讲的话。更没转过：他们信不信他，其实无关紧要。在这底下，血液里一种死灭已久的热病复燃了，是对那只杯实实在在的恐惧——它邪门，是禁忌之物。他盯着它那慑人的古旧，挨过了可怕的一分钟。他那敏感的才智在一连串恐慌里疾驰，直到摔跤的震荡平息下去，他开始排列各种选择。端着杯下楼去喝茶，说：“我在我房间里找到了它。我不知道它怎么跑进去的。”

===Original===
To hide it in his baggage. In the house. To put it somewhere﻿—say, in Picus’s room. To destroy it.

===Chinese===
藏进他的行李。藏进宅子里。把它搁到哪儿去——比如，搁到皮库斯（Picus）的房间。把它毁掉。

===Original===
The first would make a fool of him, the second a thief, the third impossible, the fourth a trickster; the fifth might bring him to a bad end. This was what had come of his nosing round for power. Scylla would be coming in, burnt with kisses. Perhaps she had played off this trick on him. How many years had he been living in this Chinese box of tricks? If he could have believed in their belief of the possibility of a possible sanctity, gone down to them and said: “Here is something that may be precious,” he would have walked into their hearts. But that would not have served him, because he did not want their hearts. Did not want hearts. Wanted scalps.

===Chinese===
头一样会让他出丑，第二样会让他做贼，第三样根本办不到，第四样会让他做骗子；第五样兴许会送他去一个坏下场。这就是他四处嗅探权力的下场。斯库拉（Scylla）就要进来了，带着一身吻出来的灼痕。说不定这个把戏正是她捉弄他的。他住在这只层层设套的中国魔盒里，住了多少年了？倘若他能够信下他们所信的——那种「可能的圣性之可能」——下楼去对他们说：“这里有样东西，也许很珍贵”，他就走进他们心里去了。可那样对他没用，因为他不要他们的心。不要心。要的是头皮。

===Original===
On a final sweep of rage he went downstairs with a cup in his hands to Felix and Clarence and Ross. He said: “Here’s your cup. I should like to know which of you played this off on me. I should like to know who put it in my room.”

===Chinese===
最后一阵狂怒横扫过来，他双手捧着那只杯下楼，走到菲利克斯、克拉伦斯（Clarence）和罗斯（Ross）跟前。他说：“喏，你们的杯。我想知道你们哪一个对我玩了这一手。我想知道是谁把它放进我房间的。”

===Original===
“Oopsey daisy,” said Felix.

===Chinese===
“哎哟喂。”菲利克斯说。

===Original===
“If that’s your notion of hospitality, it doesn’t coincide with mine.”

===Chinese===
“要是这就是你们待客之道，那可跟我的对不上。”

===Original===
Clarence said: “If you don’t like us, what d’you come down here for?”

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯说：“你要是不喜欢我们，干嘛跑到这儿来？”

===Original===
“What we mean, is,” said Ross, “that we don’t understand why you should think such a thing.”

===Chinese===
“我们的意思是，”罗斯说，“我们不明白你怎么会想出这种事来。”

===Original===
“Are you trying it out on me that the thing got there by itself, and that none of you knew?” Felix said:

===Chinese===
“你们是想让我信这个吗——那东西自己跑过去的，你们谁都不知道？”菲利克斯说：

===Original===
“If we had known, why should we have spent a morning perspiring over it?”

===Chinese===
“我们要是知道，何苦搭上一整个上午，为它汗流浃背？”

===Original===
Carston cried at him:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿冲他嚷：

===Original===
“I’d not put it past you. The day I turn my back on you all will be the best I’ve spent. I can tell people then what I think of you.”

===Chinese===
“你们什么事都干得出来。哪天我背过身去不理你们，那天就是我过得最好的一天。到时候我可以告诉别人，我把你们看成什么。”

===Original===
Felix answered: “And we might as well tell the world that your thirst for antiquities led you to steal a family chalice. Nice kind of mind you’ve got. You know none of us put it in your room.” That was what he did not know. What he could not have done, others could do. There was a stupid, broken pause. Then he said, who had had time to think:

===Chinese===
菲利克斯回敬：“那我们也不妨告诉全世界：你对古物的渴念害你偷走了一只家传的圣杯。瞧你这份心思可真不赖。你知道我们谁都没把它放进你房间。”那恰恰是他不知道的。他自己干不出来的事，别人干得出来。一阵愚蠢的、破碎的沉默。然后，得了工夫细想的他开口道：

===Original===
“I suppose the alliance between Miss Taverner and Mr. Tracy explains it.”

===Chinese===
“我想，塔弗纳小姐和特雷西先生的联盟，把这一切都解释清楚了。”

===Original===
“What alliance explains what?”

===Chinese===
“什么联盟解释什么？”

===Original===
Carston looked at the brother; felt like a man pulling up blinds.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿看着那位当哥哥的；觉得自己像个正在哗啦拉起百叶窗的人。

===Original===
“Love made them mischievous, I suppose.”

===Chinese===
“我猜，是爱情让他们淘了气。”

===Original===
“What love?”

===Chinese===
“什么爱情？”

===Original===
Warm, sunburnt, they came in. They were in the room, leaning on their ashplants, serene, apart. After a silence, “What’s wrong?” said Scylla.

===Chinese===
两人进来了，浑身晒得暖暖的、黑黑的。他们立在屋里，拄着各自的白蜡木手杖，安详，隔开一点。沉默了片刻，“怎么了？”斯库拉说。

===Original===
“The cup’s turned up,” said Ross, “in Carston’s bedroom. Did either of you put it there?”

===Chinese===
“杯又露面了，”罗斯说，“在卡斯顿的卧室里。是你们俩谁放的吗？”

===Original===
Knowing Picus behind her, she laughed. Lovers’ jokes are sacred, pleasantries of a man who discovers the sea-wood, the rock soft with birds, the meeting of pure water and salt. Come down out of that to enchant and rule her equals.

===Chinese===
明知皮库斯就在她身后，她笑了。情人的玩笑是神圣的，那是一个男人发现了海畔树林、发现了被鸟住软的岩石、发现了淡水和咸水交汇之处之后的妙语。从那样的境界里降下来，降下来蛊惑、统治与她同等的人。

===Original===
“Count us out,” she said. “What’s biting you, Carston?”

===Chinese===
“别算上我们。”她说，“你哪根筋不对了，卡斯顿？”

===Original===
If she had shown a little decent concern it might have recalled him. But he went on:

===Chinese===
她若肯露出一分得体的关切，或许还能把他唤回来。可他只管往下说：

===Original===
“Then I suppose your friend did it. Not content with keeping me awake all night.”

===Chinese===
“那我猜是你那位朋友干的了。害我整宿睡不着还不算。”

===Original===
They stared at him. Clarence was practically invisible with frightful emotion.

===Chinese===
他们都瞪着他。克拉伦斯叫一种可怕的情绪逼得几乎看不见了人形。

===Original===
“Put what where?” said Picus, laughing.

===Chinese===
“放什么在哪儿？”皮库斯笑着说。

===Original===
“Four mysteries,” said Carston, “since I got here. First, you found that thing. Then Tracy vanished, after leading us a dance in an infernal prairie. Then the thing vanished. Then it’s found in my room. I’m waiting your explanations. I’ve gotten my own.”

===Chinese===
“四大疑案，”卡斯顿说，“打我到这儿起。第一，你们找到了那东西。跟着特雷西失踪了，还领着我们在一片地狱草原上兜了一场大圈子。跟着那东西失踪了。跟着它又在我房间里冒了出来。我等着你们的解释。我自己那套已经有了。”

===Original===
“Let’s hear ’em,” said Ross.

===Chinese===
“说来听听。”罗斯说。

===Original===
Scylla spoke: “It is my cup. My lover who gave it me. We who have enjoyed it. Carston can think what he likes. I did not put it in his room. It is he who will not play. If he wants to find out what has happened, he will find out. We will tell him when we know. Which we don’t at present. Don’t be a fool, man. No one has tried to trick you here.”

===Chinese===
斯库拉开口了：“这是我的杯。是我的恋人把它给了我。是我们享用过它。卡斯顿爱怎么想怎么想。我没有把它放进他房间。是他自己不肯入局。他要想弄明白出了什么事，他会弄明白的。我们一知道就会告诉他。眼下我们还不知道。别犯傻了，先生。这儿没有人存心捉弄你。”

===Original===
All fairly true, but Picus had done something. Just a little devilry. Her heart caught at a beat, she tasted something in her mouth, salt like pain. Pain so soon after. Other side of the halfpenny. She sneered and sat down, tapping the bright boards with her stick.

===Chinese===
句句差不多都是实话，可皮库斯确实做了一件事。就一点点小恶作剧。她的心在某一拍上噎了一下，嘴里尝到一样东西，咸的，像疼。这么快就疼。半个便士的另一面。她冷笑一声坐下，用手杖笃笃敲着光亮的地板。

===Original===
Carston felt disintegrating, sticky, a loser, afraid. Still standing, he stared out at the wood, at the ilex-limb, each leaf a white-fire flame. He became aware of all the noises of the wood, that it was cackling all the time, a frightful old long gossip about dirt and the dead ends of lies. His subtle brain raced on, took a glorious chance. He said:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿觉得自己正在瓦解，黏黏糊糊，是个输家，还害怕。他仍站着，凝望外面的树林，望那枝圣栎——每片叶子都是一朵白火的火苗。他开始听见树林里所有的声响，听见它一直在咯咯地笑，一场又老又长的可怕闲话，说的是污秽和谎言的死胡同。他那精微的脑子一路疾驰，抓住了一个绝妙的机会。他说：

===Original===
“I can tell you something then. Tracy has a book up in his room. On somebody’s collection of early church ornaments. He brought the cup down from London to work this off on you all. You remember how he stunted his ignorance? Just a little game to make you think something of yourselves and let you down. You may like being kidded. I don’t. I reckon I’ve done you a service﻿—”

===Chinese===
“那我倒可以告诉你们一件事。特雷西楼上的房间里有一本书。讲的是某人收藏的一批早期教会饰品。他把这只杯从伦敦带下来，就是要拿它把你们大家耍一道。你们还记得他怎么表演自己的无知吧？一个小小的把戏，先让你们自以为了不起，再把你们摔下来。你们爱被人哄着玩，我不爱。我看我算是帮了你们一个忙——”

===Original===
“Bright idea,” said Felix. “True, Picus?” He flew at them, with the menaces of a bird.

===Chinese===
“好主意。”菲利克斯说。“是真的吗，皮库斯？”他扑向他们，带着一只鸟的威吓。

===Original===
“What d’you mean? Scylla’s been talking. You are all a pack of old women intriguing against me. Making my life hell. Like Carston, I’m sick of your hospitality. Especially when it includes him.”

===Chinese===
“什么意思？准是斯库拉走了嘴。你们全是一伙跟我钩心斗角的长舌婆子。把我的日子弄成了地狱。跟卡斯顿一样，你们这份殷勤我受够了。尤其是这份殷勤里还捎带着他。”

===Original===
“*Our hospitality*,” said Felix. Quotation wasted on Picus, caught Carston.

===Chinese===
“*咱们的殷勤款待*。”菲利克斯说。这句引语砸给皮库斯是白砸，卡斯顿倒接了个正着。

===Original===
“Yes, a decent vendetta would be better than your poisoned fun.”

===Chinese===
“就是，一场体面的血仇都好过你们这种下了毒的取乐。”

===Original===
“We don’t seem to have cleaned up anything,” said Clarence.

===Chinese===
“咱们好像什么也没收拾利索。”克拉伦斯说。

===Original===
“Cleaned up,” said Picus, chattering at them. “Accusing me. Boring me. Interfering with me.” In this there was something that was not comic, in the dis‑ease he imparted.

===Chinese===
“收拾利索，”皮库斯冲他们喋喋个不停。“控告我。烦我。搅和我。”这里面有某种并不好笑的东西，就在他散布开来的那阵不安（dis-ease）里。

===Original===
“An aborted thunderstorm,” said Scylla. “I’m going up to change.”

===Chinese===
“一场没成形就流了的雷雨。”斯库拉说。“我上楼换衣裳去。”

===Original===
Five left, hating each other. Then Felix modestly, like the youngest: “I’ll go down the wood, and see about the dinner fish.” Four left. “Work to do,” said Ross. Three left. “Have you a time table?” said Carston. Clarence said: “Hadn’t you better stay till we know the truth?” Carston turned his back on him, and went out neatly through the library door. Two left.

===Chinese===
五个人散了，彼此怀恨。接着菲利克斯谦逊地、像最年幼的那个似的说：“我下树林去，看看晚饭的鱼。”剩下四个。“还有活儿要干。”罗斯说。剩下三个。“你们有列车时刻表吗？”卡斯顿说。克拉伦斯说：“不等弄清真相再走吗？”卡斯顿给他一个脊背，利利索索地从书房那道门出去了。剩下两个。

===Original===
“What is this about you and Scylla?”

===Chinese===
“你跟斯库拉是怎么回事？”

===Original===
“I suppose I am free to sleep with whom I like?”

===Chinese===
“我想跟谁睡，我有这份自由吧？”

===Original===
“Why her?”

===Chinese===
“为什么是她？”

===Original===
“Why not? You don’t want her.”

===Chinese===
“为什么不能是？你又不要她。”

===Original===
“God, no. But you might have told me.”

===Chinese===
“天哪，是不要。可你本可以告诉我一声。”

===Original===
“I thought I heard her tell you.”

===Chinese===
“我记得我听见她告诉过你。”

===Original===
“So you and Scylla are one voice then?”

===Chinese===
“这么说，你跟斯库拉是一个声音了？”

===Original===
Picus laughed again. “She shouldn’t have told.” Clarence smiled back at him faintly, as if he had to smile under pain, his own, anyone’s. And Picus chattered on, all of him dancing together, subtle, venomous, absurd.

===Chinese===
皮库斯又笑了。“她不该说出去。”克拉伦斯朝他淡淡一笑，仿佛疼着也得笑——自己的疼也好，谁的疼也好。皮库斯则喋喋不休，浑身上下一齐舞着，机巧，带毒，荒唐。

===Original===
Clarence listened, till the time came when he could listen no longer, and hid his face, the awful pain rising in him, drowning Picus’s presence. And he was thankful for it. Escape into infinite suffering, a deadly grey land, and he was thankful for it. Away from Picus forever. Not even to meet the true Picus, but to the country where there was no Picus. When that had gone away forever. That nerve dead. *Free among the dead.* He raced away on that black heath. Of course, the place where Picus had lost them the day before. But that country had been sapphire and purple, wild with bees. He was out there now in December.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯听着，听到再也听不下去的那一刻，把脸埋了起来——那可怕的疼痛在他体内升起，把皮库斯的存在淹没了。他为此感恩。逃进无边的受苦，一片死灰的国度，他为此感恩。从此永远躲开皮库斯。连真正的皮库斯也不必再见了，去的是那个没有皮库斯的国度。当那一切永远退尽。那根神经死了。*在死人中间，自由了。*他在那片黑色荒原上飞奔。当然，正是皮库斯前一天把他们弄丢的地方。可那片国土当时是蓝宝石色和紫色的，野蜂闹成一片。他此刻是在那里的十二月里。

===Original===
> The North cannot undo them.
 

 With a sleepy whistle through them.

===Chinese===
> 北风解不开他们，
 

 只一声困倦的口哨，穿过他们身心。

===Original===
True for trees, but what about the “gentle girl and boy”? He had hidden himself a long time in the pain.

===Chinese===
树木固然如此，可那“温柔的女孩和男孩”呢？他在那疼痛里躲藏了很久。

===Original===
When he took his hands away from his face, Picus had gone.

===Chinese===
他把手从脸上拿开时，皮库斯已经不在了。

## XIII / 十三、一日两爱

===Original===
Scylla went upstairs, and lying on her bed in her shift felt her elation and clean fatigue replaced by shabby weariness and fear.

===Chinese===
斯库拉（Scylla）上了楼，穿着衬裙往床上一躺，觉得那份兴奋和干净的疲乏，换成了寒碜的倦怠和恐惧。

===Original===
Picus had played that trick on Carston. Picus had spoiled her pride in him. Why had he played a spiteful joke? She had not begun to think it possible that he had arranged the story of the cup. Only the trick on Carston was ill-mannered, a little cruel. Also irrelevant. It had made the business seem empty, like the effect got at séances where the interesting, the decisive, the clear is always on the point of arrival, and invariably fades out before the point is reached.

===Chinese===
皮库斯（Picus）对卡斯顿（Carston）玩了那一手。皮库斯败坏了她为他感到的骄傲。他为什么要开一个恶意的玩笑？她还没来得及往那上面想——想到杯的那段故事可能就是他一手安排的。只是对卡斯顿的这一手失了分寸，有点狠。而且不相干。它把整桩事情衬得空落落的，像降神会上得到的那种效果：有趣的、决定性的、明晰的东西永远眼看就要到场，却总在到场之前消隐。

===Original===
Like the mass of Celtic art. Like, now she considered it, the whole Grail story, the saga story *par excellence* that has never come off, or found its form or its poet. Not like the Golden Fleece or Odysseus at Circe’s house. There was something in their lives spoiled and inconclusive like the Grail story. It would be her turn next for Picus to insult, as he had played a pointless joke on a foreign guest. A number of unpleasant emotions followed that thought, chiefly disturbed sexual vanity which sets the earth by the ears. Life was ice-bright, and disagreeable as flint. It was a maudlin dream. Chance couplings, little minds setting to partners. Victory of ants over the sphinx in flesh: over birds.

===Chinese===
像凯尔特艺术的大部分。像——如今她细想了——整个圣杯的故事，那篇*绝无仅有*的萨迦故事，从未圆满演成，从未找到它的形式或它的诗人。不像金羊毛，不像喀耳刻（Circe）家的奥德修斯（Odysseus）。他们的生活里有什么东西也是这样被败坏了、悬而不决，像圣杯的故事一样。下一个轮到她受皮库斯的羞辱，正像他对一位外国客人开了一个无谓的玩笑。这个念头引出一串不快的情绪，主要是被搅乱的性的虚荣——天下大乱，正是拜它所赐。生活亮得像冰，又硬得讨厌，像燧石。它是一场自伤自怜的梦。偶然的偶合，小小的心智各自配对起舞。蚂蚁对血肉斯芬克斯的胜利：对鸟的胜利。

---

===Original===
She watched the flies flashing across the window, a bee searching a flower head in ajar of mixed wild stalks Felix had put there. Then to detach herself she played an old game, that she was lying out on the wood’s roof: translating the stick and leaf that upheld her into herself: into sea: into sky. Sky back again into wood, flesh and sea.

===Chinese===
她望着苍蝇一道道掠过窗玻璃，一只蜜蜂在菲利克斯（Felix）插在那儿的一罐混扎的野花枝里搜寻一朵花头。接着，为把自己从自己身上拔出来，她玩起一个旧游戏：想象自己正躺在树林的顶上：把托着她的枝条和叶子翻译成她自己：翻译成海：翻译成天。再把天翻译回树林、血肉和海。

===Original===
It did not work, as it was meant to, to deliver her from herself, but it made her see Picus’s proceedings diabolic. Why so? Parody of a mystery. A mystery none of them believed. That reduced it to a bad taste. They did not quite disbelieve. Dangerous fooling then? Parodied also in her bed. Very cruel and so wrong.

===Chinese===
这游戏没有像它本来的用意那样奏效，把她从她自己那里解救出来，倒是让她看出了皮库斯那番作为的魔鬼底色。为什么这么说？是对一场奥秘的戏仿。一场他们谁也不信的奥秘。这就把它降成了趣味低劣。可他们又不全然不信。那么，是危险的胡闹？连她的床笫也遭了戏仿。非常残忍，大错特错。

===Original===
But under Gault Cliff there had been no parody. That she had to love Picus by, as much of a creation as any growth in nature. Or ritual, or rite produced by the imagination. As little symbolic as the result of any mystery propitiously performed.

===Chinese===
可在高尔特崖下，没有过任何戏仿。凭着那个，她才爱得下皮库斯——那同自然界里任何一株生长一样，是一件造化。或者说是一场仪轨，一次由想象生出的祭礼。同任何一场顺遂施行的奥秘的结果一样，毫不含什么象征。

===Original===
As she attended to what she was thinking, she laughed, her immense vitality racing back. Her entry had made his trick glorious. Dinner would be difficult. What had been wrong with Carston? She would go and talk to him. What about? She would propitiate Felix. How? They will all hate me. Without whom Picus would not have turned creator. Woman’s place indeed. Clarence wanted that job. He did the work, and I wear the crown. Not my fault. Chances of the sacred game.

===Chinese===
她留意到自己正在想的这些，笑了，那股巨大的活力奔驰着涌了回来。她那一进屋，把他的恶作剧衬得辉煌。晚饭不好办。卡斯顿到底是哪儿不对了？她要去找他谈谈。谈什么？她要安抚菲利克斯。怎么安抚？他们都会恨我。没有我，皮库斯成不了造物者。这可真是「女人的位置」了。克拉伦斯（Clarence）倒想要这份差事。活是他干的，王冠却由我来戴。不怪我。神圣游戏的机缘。

===Original===
Swept off into stadium of the game; which is the pleasure in actions for their own sake. Done for the love of playing. Done for the fun of it. Done for no pompous end.

===Chinese===
一头被卷进这游戏的竞技场；这游戏的乐趣，就在为行动而行动。为爱玩而做。为好玩而做。不为任何装腔作势的目的而做。

===Original===
That Felix was just a little nervous about.

===Chinese===
只这一样，让菲利克斯微微发怵。

===Original===
Played by Alexander, and young Cleopatra in a bundle at Caesar’s feet.

===Chinese===
亚历山大（Alexander）玩过它，年轻的克娄巴特拉（Cleopatra）裹成一团偎在恺撒（Caesar）脚边时也玩过它。

===Original===
Played by that demon Picus, when he had whistled up mystery with what was now undoubtedly a Victorian finger-bowl.

===Chinese===
那个魔鬼皮库斯也玩过——他用一只如今确凿无疑是维多利亚时代的洗指碗，吹哨召来了一场奥秘。

===Original===
Played by Malatesta having Isotta sculpted for the Madonna; and the man who broke the bank at Monte Carlo.

===Chinese===
马拉泰斯塔（Malatesta）玩过——他把伊索塔（Isotta）雕成了圣母；还有那个在蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）大破赌庄的人也玩过。

===Original===
Played by Chaucer who loved everything for what it was. A sword for being a sword, or a horse. And they for what they were, the “gentle girls and boys.”

===Chinese===
乔叟（Chaucer）玩过，他爱每一样东西，爱它的本然。剑之为剑，或者马之为马。他们也因本然而受爱，那些“温柔的女孩和男孩”。

===Original===
Good thinking, good eating. All things taking care of themselves. Each thing *accordynge to its kynde*.

===Chinese===
好好地想，好好地吃。万事各安其位。每样东西都*各从其类*（*accordynge to its kynde*）。

===Original===
Would there be a train for Carston to go away by? Good idea of Picus to say it would be better for him to be slept with than visit that wood.

===Chinese===
会有让卡斯顿走的那班火车吗？皮库斯那个主意出得好——说与其让他闯进那片树林，不如陪他睡。

===Original===
Was there anything to eat for dinner, anyhow? She jumped up and went out discreetly through the kitchen. In the scullery there was Felix, cleaning a basket of fish. Too much fish. Enough for half to go bad, and the rest infuriate Picus, who would say he had been given it to encourage his brain. Felix said:

===Chinese===
不管怎么说，晚饭可有东西吃？她一跃而起，蹑手蹑脚从厨房那头出去。洗碗间里是菲利克斯，正收拾一篮鱼。鱼太多了。多到足够放坏一半，再让另一半激怒皮库斯——他会说，给他这么多鱼，是给他补脑子用的。菲利克斯说：

===Original===
“I thought I might as well help. Nanna and Janet are at it all day. Nothing like getting in a stock. Had a good walk with Picus?”

===Chinese===
“我想我不妨搭把手。娜娜（Nanna）和珍妮特（Janet）整天都在忙这个。什么都比不上先囤下一批存货。跟皮库斯散步散得痛快吧？”

===Original===
The basket, full offish-shapes, was wet, black-ribboned inside, a shell sticking here and there, a live whelk walking up. Sea-smelling, almost living food, still running with the live sea. She took a knife and a fish, and cut down on the slab of dark blue slate used for cooling butter. Felix had covered it with scales and blood.

===Chinese===
那只篮子盛满了鱼的形体，湿漉漉的，内壁一道道黑缎带似的纹路，处处粘着贝壳，一只活蛾螺正往上爬。带着海腥的、几乎是活的食物，还流淌着活的海。她取过一把刀、一条鱼，在那块平日镇黄油的深蓝板岩案板上落刀剖开。菲利克斯已经在上面铺满了鳞片和血。

===Original===
“We’ll do it together,” she said.

===Chinese===
“咱们一起弄。”她说。

===Original===
“You’re not very good at it,” he said.

===Chinese===
“你干这个可不大在行。”他说。

===Original===
She hesitated, testing the contact with him.

===Chinese===
她迟疑了一下，试探着同他的这份接触。

===Original===
“We’ll do other things together, then.”

===Chinese===
“那就一起干点别的。”

===Original===
“Don’t we pretty well always?”﻿—His knife scraped down on a bone﻿—“I mean, it’s only half the time I don’t understand.”

===Chinese===
“咱们不差不多一直这样吗？”——他的刀顺着一根骨头刮下去——“我是说，我有一半时间闹不懂罢了。”

===Original===
She thought: So he went and gutted fish for me. She said: “What, my dearest dear, did you understand today?” He answered: “When you came in with Picus I saw your beauty. After Carston had been talking, and surprised me, rather. After the things which have happened lately. It was a kind of answer. A sudden opposite to what I was thinking. To what the world is usually, I suppose. You see, I would sooner have you or even Picus in the right. Only, I haven’t faith.”

===Chinese===
她想：原来他是为我跑去开膛剖肚收拾鱼来了。她说：“那么，我最亲最爱的，你今天懂了什么？”他答道：“你跟皮库斯一块儿进来的时候，我看见了你的美。是在卡斯顿说完那些话之后——说实话，他让我吃了一惊。是在近来发生的那些事之后。那算是一种回答。同我心里正想着的猛然相反。我想，也同这世界平日的样子猛然相反。你要知道，我宁愿是你对，甚至宁愿是皮库斯对。只是，我没有信心。”

===Original===
She thought: Try and have faith. No. Don’t try and have anything. Be with me.

===Chinese===
她想：试着去有信心吧。不。别试着去有无论什么。陪着我。

===Original===
And in answer, she told him about the wood. The bird, Picus. *O lady, be good.* Everything. That she could not have told the others. She heard a thought in his head: I shan’t be able to keep this up, but today I am my sister’s.

===Chinese===
