作为回答，她把那片树林讲给他听。那只鸟，皮库斯。*哦，女士，待我好些。*一切。那些她没法讲给别人听的东西。她听见他脑子里的一个念头：这我撑不长久的，可今天，我是属于我妹妹的。

===Original===
More love for her now, handed back through Carston’s spite; peace in the scullery with her flesh and blood. Fish blood and flesh on a stone between them. In one day, two kinds of perfect love. Life with Picus. Life with him. (He had understood love for Picus. Picus would not understand love for him.) Life without Picus? Life without him? She remade Antigone’s discovery that you can have more lovers and more children. Not another brother, once your people’s bearing days are gone.

===Chinese===
此刻给她的爱更多了，经卡斯顿的怨毒转手递了回来；洗碗间里，同她的骨肉至亲享着安宁。鱼的血和肉在一块石头上，横在他们中间。一天之内，两种圆满的爱。与皮库斯的生活。与他的生活。（他懂得了那份对皮库斯的爱。皮库斯却不会懂得那份对他的爱。）没有皮库斯的生活？没有他的生活？她重新发现了安提戈涅（Antigone）的发现：恋人可以更多，孩子也可以更多。兄弟却不能再有——一旦你的族人能生育的岁月一去不返。

===Original===
Life with the two gone. Life with Clarence, Carston, Ross? She thought she heard a voice saying: “You will soon be left alone with them. You will be without Felix. Because there is coming to you the opposite of what you’ve had. Must come to you. More than separation; avoidance, treachery. Equal to what you’ve had. At one point, life without them will mean that.”

===Chinese===
两个人都离去之后的生活。同克拉伦斯、卡斯顿、罗斯（Ross）一起的生活？她仿佛听见一个声音说：“你很快就要被单独留下，同他们在一起。你将失去菲利克斯。因为正在向你走来的，是你所得一切的反面。必得向你走来。比分离更甚：是躲避开，是背弃。与你所得的一切相等。到某一点上，没有他们的生活，指的就是那个。”

===Original===
“Not if I can help it!”

===Chinese===
“只要我拦得住，就不会！”

===Original===
Behind this somewhere was an immense discovery, a huge principle which made it immaterial if she could help it or not. She rested in the knowledge that it was there. Their nurse came in, and they thought at once of washing and going away to change. Mounting the stairs, their arms round each other’s shoulders, Carston saw them from his room, and was inexpressibly shocked, unable to understand how Scylla had persuaded her brother that her relations with Picus had been misunderstood.

===Chinese===
这话背后某个地方，有一个巨大的发现，一条庞大的原理，她拦得住拦不住，在它面前都成了无关紧要。她安然栖止于「它就在那里」的认知里。他们的老保姆进来了，两人立刻想到洗漱，各自回房更衣。上楼时两人互相搂着肩，卡斯顿从自己房里望见，受到难以言喻的震动，怎么也想不明白：斯库拉是怎么让她哥哥相信，她与皮库斯的关系只是一场误会。

## XIV / 十四、神圣出逃

===Original===
As Felix said, “pep” had been the *mot juste* for the way Carston behaved. When he had found that there was no train that night, he had walked across the valley. There he had discovered a coastguard, and, practically unaided, the system by which the station wireless picked up the lighthouse, and from there communicated by telegraph with Starn. From this, he produced a taxi and a lodging for the night. He walked back and packed vigorously, kneeling on the floor, his back to the window and the wood. When he had seen Scylla passing upstairs with her brother, he had shut his door, ceased to hear the silence of the house, heard instead the wood, a little restless, its branches changing places in a wind risen suddenly off the sea.

===Chinese===
正如菲利克斯（Felix）所说，“劲头”（pep）正是那个恰切的字眼（*mot juste*），形容卡斯顿（Carston）的做派再准不过。他查明当夜没有火车，便徒步穿过了山谷。在那儿他发现了一名海岸警卫队员，还几乎全凭一己之力摸清了那套系统——车站的无线电台先接收灯塔，再从那里用电报同斯塔恩（Starn）联络。凭着这一套，他变出了一辆出租车和一夜的宿处。他走回宅子，跪在地板上大张旗鼓地收拾行李，背对着窗户和树林。他看见斯库拉（Scylla）和她兄弟从楼上走过，便关上了自己的房门，不再去听宅子的寂静，改听那片树林——它有点躁动不安，枝条在一阵海上骤起的风里彼此换着位置。

===Original===
Odd that he would not see the place again; have no part with its men, or possess its woman. Never found out what had really happened.

===Chinese===
说也奇怪，他不会再见到这个地方了；不再与这里的男人相干，也占有不了这里的女人。真正发生过什么，永远弄不清了。

===Original===
He was still on the crest of the energy he had spent in denouncing them in a general sensation of burned boats. There had not risen yet doubt of himself, scrutiny, not of his motives, he knew better than to do that, but of the figure he had cut. Yet, his angry elation was like a fir-cone fire, needing baskets of brittle wood-shells. He had a fine story for his friends, something to think about. Scylla written off as a bad job, as a romance. It seemed equally impossible to say goodbye, to leave without saying it. Then the old nurse knocked, told him that his taxi was there, and that Mr. Felix, Miss Scylla, and Mr. Ross wished him a pleasant journey. He tipped her enormously, slipped across the verandah, fearing heads that were not watching. With jars and jerks, the taxi crept up the long hill.

===Chinese===
他还骑在自己那股劲头的浪尖上——那是痛斥他们一场所耗的劲头，浑身带着一种大而化之的破釜沉舟之感。对自身的怀疑还没有升起，还没有自审——审的倒不是动机，那个他还懂分寸不去碰，而是自己那副身段究竟成了什么样子。然而，他那怒气冲冲的兴奋，像一堆冷杉球果烧起的火，还得一筐筐脆裂的果壳来续。他给朋友们攒下了一个精彩的故事，一件够琢磨的事。斯库拉就此勾销，算一笔蚀本的账，算一段罗曼史。道别似乎不可能，不道别就走似乎同样不可能。这时老保姆敲了门，告诉他出租车到了，还说菲利克斯先生、斯库拉小姐和罗斯（Ross）先生祝他一路顺遂。他赏了她一大笔钱，溜过游廊，提防着一颗颗并没有张望的脑袋。出租车一颠一顿，爬上了长长的山坡。

===Original===
Divine escape. On the down-crest, the earth was a map of naked beauty he saw in the piece and understood. “I’ve been living inside a work of art”﻿—living what was meant to be looked at, not lived in; not to be chewed, swallowed, handled, kissed. He lay back, rocking over the grass track, almost satisfied with this. A piece of life, definitely over for him, with the stone age, and the Middle Ages, and﻿—A patch of purple gauze ahead, smoke of no earthly fire, now a patch of those tall, bee-shaken spikes they called foxgloves. As they passed it, he saw thin legs stuck out of it along the earth, a body backed against the flower wall. It was Picus out there, up there. Looking out at nothing; out to sea. Sitting on the top of the world.

===Chinese===
神圣的逃离。到了下坡的脊线，大地成了一幅裸露之美的地图，他看的是整幅，也尽皆懂得。“我原来一直住在一件艺术品里”——住的那样东西，本是生来给人看的，不是给人住的；不该拿来咀嚼、吞咽、抚弄、亲吻。他仰身靠回去，任车子在草径上颠簸，几乎为此感到满足。一段生活，对他算是断然收场了，连同石器时代，连同中世纪，连同——前方一片紫色的轻纱，一缕非人间之火的烟，再看，是一丛高高的、被蜜蜂摇得乱颤的花穗——他们管那叫毛地黄（foxgloves）。车经过时，他看见几条细长的腿从花丛里沿地面伸出来，一副身子抵着那面花墙。是皮库斯（Picus），在外面，在上面。望着一片空无；望着海。坐在世界的顶上。

## XIX / 十九、鲱鱼天

===Original===
It was perceptibly cooler, stale-cool, uneasy air-threads stirring in the straight wood drive. No sun since three o’clock, but a glaring grey gauze overhead. Outside the wood, below the little cliff, a small scoop of bay protected the fisherman’s boats. From the edge they could have stepped on to the roof of his hut, whose tarred shingles were frosted with salt. Set on a ledge out of storms’ reach, brambles padded it from the cliffs side, and it was reached down wooden balks, steep-set and built into the clay. It surprised Carston to see the egg-blue and peacock water changed to the colour of a gun-barrel. Little wind, but the sea was twitching, slapping against the rocks; the colours inland, neither light-veiled or shining, but off a new palette.

===Chinese===
天明显地凉了，一种闷坏的凉；笔直的林中车道上，一缕缕不安的气流在轻轻搅动。三点钟过后就没了太阳，头顶只罩着一层刺眼的灰纱。林子外面、小崖底下，一湾浅浅的凹湾护着渔人的船。从崖沿上，他们抬脚就能踏上他那棚屋的屋顶——屋面上焦油板瓦结了一层盐霜。棚屋安在一道风暴够不着的石台上，悬崖那一面有荆棘丛给它垫护着；人下去要走几条木跳，陡陡地嵌在黏土里。卡斯顿惊讶地看到，那蛋壳蓝、孔雀绿的海水换成了枪管的颜色。风不大，海却在抽搐，一下一下拍打着礁石；内陆的颜色，既不像蒙了层轻纱，也不见光亮，倒像才从一块新调色板上取下来的。

===Original===
Scylla was staring out to sea, and her head lifted in profile made her look at the sky, where it seemed as if some mathematical monster had risen out of the west. For where the sun was turning down-Channel, a ball glared, surrounded by ranks of rose bars, and out from these clouds radiated that reached over to the eastern heavens, across whose spokes strayed loose flakes dipped in every variety of flame, the triangles of empty sky stained all the greens between primrose and jade.

===Chinese===
斯库拉正凝望着外海。她抬起头，侧影一扬，目光便落到天上——那西天，仿佛升起了某种几何学怪物。就在太阳正沉向海峡下游的地方，一团光球灼灼刺目，四周环列着一排排玫瑰色的光杠；从这些云里辐散出一道道云，直铺到东天；辐条之间，游散着一片片蘸过百样火色的碎云，空当里一块块三角形的天，染遍了报春花色与翡翠绿之间的种种绿。

===Original===
“Herring sky,” she said.

===Chinese===
“鲱鱼天。”她说。

===Original===
“What does that mean?”

===Chinese===
“这是什么意思？”

===Original===
She laughed with a confident joy he understood the first time.

===Chinese===
她笑了，笑出一种笃定的欢喜，他头一回听见就懂了。

===Original===
“Southwest wind. Listen!” He heard his heart beating, a hair in his ear, and a trans-finite length away, the stirring that had made uneasy the sea.

===Chinese===
“西南风。听！”他听见自己的心在跳，耳朵里一根发丝的窸窣，还听见在超限遥远的远处，那阵搅得大海不宁的骚动。

===Original===
“A big storm,” she said, turning on him eyes full of an animal’s pleasure. Round the point where the day before he arrived they had played at Aphrodites, a boat came dipping back, the bowsprit dancing and dripping, where over the deep-travelling reef the sea had begun to coil under and over.

===Chinese===
“一场大风暴。”她说着，转过一双蓄满兽性欢愉的眼睛看他。绕过那个岬角——就在他抵达的前一天，他们曾在那儿扮过阿芙洛狄忒（Aphrodites）——一条船颠簸着回来了，船首斜桅又舞又滴水；在那道深水暗礁上，海水已经开始上下盘卷。

===Original===
“Harris is back in time. We’re in for it. No more fish.” A note in her brain about the fish problem. More too freshly killed meat. Picus and Felix on the subject. Then the only good poem a bad poet ever wrote, anticipating jazz:

===Chinese===
“哈里斯（Harris）回来得正是时候。我们要摊上一场了。再没有鱼啦。”她脑子里就鱼的难题记下一笔。还得添些现宰的鲜肉。这题目留给皮库斯和菲利克斯。接着，是那位坏诗人一生写下的唯一一首好诗，早早地预示了爵士乐：

===Original===
> “When descends on the Atlantic
 

 The gigantic
 

 Storm-wind of the equinox.”

===Chinese===
> “当那浩大的
 

 二分日风暴
 

 降临到大西洋之上。”

===Original===
He took her hands and they rocked, saying with her:

===Chinese===
他拉起她的手，两人摇着身子，跟着她一起念：

===Original===
> “From Bermuda’s reefs and edges
 

 Of sunken ledges
 

 On some far-off bright Azore
 

 From Bahama and the dashing
 

 Silver-flashing
 

 Surges of San Salvador.”

===Chinese===
> “从百慕大的礁脉与边沿，
 

 从沉没的礁台
 

 远方明媚的亚速尔，
 

 从巴哈马，和那奔腾
 

 银光飞闪
 

 圣萨尔瓦多的巨浪。”

===Original===
Fallen into nature with her. For the first time in his life. Good old Gulf Stream.

===Chinese===
同她一道坠进了自然。平生头一遭。好样的老墨西哥湾流（Gulf Stream）。

===Original===
Harris the fisherman had downed his mainsheet, flung out an anchor and was rushing a dinghy to shore.

===Chinese===
渔夫哈里斯已经落了主帆索，抛出一枚锚，正驾着小艇抢着往岸上赶。

===Original===
“Take your fish, Madame Taverner, it’s the last you’ll see. I’ve got my pots to get in.”

===Chinese===
“把鱼拿去吧，塔弗纳夫人，这是您能见着的最后一批了。我还得把龙虾笼收回来。”

===Original===
“We’ll help.”

===Chinese===
“我们来帮忙。”

===Original===
Two minutes later Carston was in a dinghy in a slightly resentful scoop of sea, and spent an ungrateful hour hauling up lobster-cages filled with kicking, pinching sea cardinals, ink blue; and the humanitarian protest came from Scylla when they were tossed into a cauldron bubbling on the stones, in an angle of the little secret cliff where England rose out of the harvested sea.

===Chinese===
两分钟后，卡斯顿已经坐在一条小艇里，漂在一小片微含怨气的湾水上，费了毫无谢意可言的一个钟头，拽起一笼笼又蹬又夹、一身墨蓝的海中红衣主教；而当这些龙虾被倒进一口在石上滚沸的大锅——锅安在小隐崖的一角，英格兰便是从那儿、从收割过的海里升起来的——发出人道主义抗议的，却是斯库拉。

===Original===
The wood was darkish, fearful and sad, until they saw the windows shut and already lit, a fire leaping up made out of the driftwood he had helped haul up. Blue sparks and white and green, wind shifting about outside in the trees, until with a scream the up-Channel gale was loosed and they became creatures couched under a stone that quivered in the uproar and mounted to bed with candles streaming.

===Chinese===
林子黑沉沉的，可怖而悲凉，直到他们望见宅子的窗都已关严、亮起了灯，一炉火正蹿着苗，烧的是他帮忙拖上岸的浮木。火花蓝的、白的、绿的，风在屋外的树间四处打转；随后，一声尖啸，上溯海峡的大风放了出来，他们便成了伏在一块石头底下的生灵，石头在喧嚣中震颤；他们端着火苗乱窜的蜡烛上了床。

===Original===
Carston lay in bed and heard above the thunder a gull repeating itself. “*Ai, ai*,” it said, up somewhere in the tumbling sky, a little noise laid delicately upon the universal roar of air. The house was strong, he thought, its stone thrilling but not a window that rattled, tapped by their climbing roses.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿躺在床上，听见雷声之上有一只鸥鸟在反复叫着同一声。“*唉，唉*（*Ai, ai*），”它说，在翻腾天空的高处某个地方，一点细巧的声响，轻轻覆在空气的普遍怒吼之上。宅子结实，他想——石头在发颤，却没有一扇窗格格作响，只有满墙攀上来的玫瑰在轻叩窗。

===Original===
There were pockets in the wind when he could hear the sea. A crash, then under-roar and scream of pebbles, the ravelled water dragged. A light-patch fell on his floor, a piece of the late moon racing apparently from a cloud whipped off her, and behind her a star or so, unhurried, observant and indifferent on a night when everything was out and about. He looked out to sea, surprised that it appeared no more than a bright silver lacquer, when water mountains should have been moving in.

===Chinese===
风里有一阵阵空当，那时他听得见海。轰然一响，接着是卵石的闷吼与尖叫，绞散的海水被拖了回去。一方光斑落在他地板上，一弯迟月，显然正从一朵被抽走的云后赶出来，身后跟着一两颗星，不慌不忙，冷眼看着，事不关己——在这样一个万物都出动了乱窜的夜里。他朝外海望去，惊讶它看上去不过是一层亮银的漆，尽管按说该有水的群山正推进来。

===Original===
Scylla alone wondered how the wood below Gault was bearing it, flooded with salt water, heaped with seaweed, the little stream choked with wrack.

===Chinese===
只有斯库拉独自挂念着，高尔特崖下那片树林怎么受得了——咸水漫了进去，海草堆了满地，小溪被冲上来的海藻噎得死死的。

===Original===
Dark again in Carston’s room. A rain-flaw drummed on the panes. Then with a shriek the wind sprang again. He could not hear the gull, but a few seconds later a crack and a long crash, knew it was a tree gone, and looking out in the next moon-interval, saw something altered in the outline of the wood. It disturbed him to think how hopeless the dawn would be, the “dew silky” quiet changed for grey air, spray-salted on the lips. Would the others enjoy it? Would it blow the nonsense out of them? They might be housebound for days. Oh, God! He was wondering how to prevent this when he fell asleep.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿房里又黑了下来。一阵斜雨敲着窗玻璃。随后风又尖啸着跳起来。他听不见那只鸥了，可几秒钟后，一声脆响，接着一声长长的轰塌——他知道是一棵树倒了；趁下一次月光间歇望出去，他看见树林的轮廓变了些样。他心烦地想，明天的黎明该多么无望：“露水如丝”的静谧换成了灰蒙蒙的天，唇上带着浪沫的咸味。旁人会觉得有意思吗？这场风会把他们身上的胡闹劲儿刮跑吗？他们说不定得困在屋里好几天。哦，上帝！他正琢磨着怎么防止这一层，就睡着了。

## XV / 十五、斯塔恩夜谈

===Original===
At Starn he was refreshed again with contacts from outside. There was an unusual number of tourists, two and two on the hillpaths, swarming the square. They did him so much good that he crossed to the station to meet a down-train that held more. Not many get out at evening, where there was nothing to do but stare. All he noticed in particular was an old gentleman.

===Chinese===
在斯塔恩（Starn），他又从与外界的接触里恢复了精神。游客多得反常，一对一对走在山径上，把广场挤得满满的。这些人让他受用极了，他索性踱到车站去，迎一列载着更多游客的下行车。傍晚下车的人不多——在这地方，除了干瞪眼无事可做。他特别留意的，只有一位老先生。

===Original===
He was beginning to enjoy the country. Enjoy Starn. Would have liked to know more, its history and contemporary life. He thought the old man with a red face, in grey flannels, a local landowner; thought him back to some dignified house, and was surprised later to see him dining at the inn.

===Chinese===
他开始喜欢上这片乡野了。喜欢上斯塔恩。本想多了解一些——它的历史，还有眼下的生活。他以为这位红脸膛、穿灰法兰绒的老者是位本地乡绅；以为他回某座体面宅邸去了，后来却吃了一惊——竟看见他在客栈里用晚餐。

===Original===
He was not known there, it seemed. There was a difficulty about a room for him. Carston felt that he should leave graciously, suggested that the old man should have his room, and he a bed anywhere. There was consultation, hesitation, acceptance, thanks. After an interval, they had settled down to coffee together.

===Chinese===
看样子，那儿没人认识他。给他腾房间出了点难处。卡斯顿（Carston）觉得此刻应当体面地告退，便提议老者住他这间房，他自己随便哪儿有张床就行。一番商量，一番迟疑，一番接受，一番道谢。过了一阵，两人已安顿下来，对坐共饮咖啡。

===Original===
It was easy to be charming to him. An obvious number of right things to say. In a flash that, too, had passed.

===Chinese===
讨他欢心很容易。现成的得体话有一大堆。一眨眼，这一套也过完了。

===Original===
“So you’ve been staying over at Gault House with the Taverners. Did you meet my son there﻿—Picus, I think they call him? My name’s Tracy.”

===Chinese===
“这么说，你一直住在高尔特宅（Gault House），和塔弗纳家（Taverners）的人做伴。你在那边见过我儿子吗——我记得他们管他叫皮库斯（Picus）？我姓特雷西（Tracy）。”

===Original===
Carston thought: Be cautious, be very cautious indeed. Don’t tell lies. The old man will be going there. His eyes are the colour of his flannels. What we call stone, and never is. Stone takes light.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿心想：要谨慎，千万要谨慎。别撒谎。老头儿是要往那边去的。他的眼睛，正是他那身法兰绒的颜色。我们管那叫石头色——其实从来不是。石头是会收光的。

===Original===
“Yes,” he said, “he is staying there with his friends.”

===Chinese===
“是的，”他说，“他正和朋友们一起住在那儿。”

===Original===
It became suddenly necessary to observe with every faculty he had. He had no idea why; not for distraction, not with reference to himself. The impression was that he was opposite someone very old﻿—not particularly in years, but in something built by centuries of experience, and now no longer in flower. The same could be said of his son and his son’s friends, only that they were in flower, and might not cease flowering once their bodies’ bloom was done. Centuries had gone to his construction. Carston was surprised at his attention to this, until he noticed that their setting outside the inn was the setting of a play.

===Chinese===
他忽然必须调动全部心力去观察。他说不清为什么；不为解闷，也不关他自己。得到的印象是：对面坐着的是一个极其苍老的人——苍老倒不在年岁上，而在某种由几个世纪的经验筑成的东西上，而那东西如今花期已过。他儿子和儿子的朋友们也可以这么说，只不过他们正在花期，而且等身体的盛放一过，也未必就停止开花。好几个世纪都花在了他的构造上。卡斯顿自己对这份专注也觉诧异，直到他看出：他们在客栈外的这片景象，正是一出戏的布景。

===Original===
Before he had wished for drama, and had not found his role. Now he was too much of a man to take himself off.

===Chinese===
从前他巴望过戏剧性，却没有找到自己的角色。如今他已是条十足的汉子，不至于就此抽身退场。

===Original===
The stage surpassed all romantic expectations, a town with towers, in hills high enough and low enough to set and display it equally. A fleece of stars over it, thick as the flocks on the downsides, turning, turning with the earth.

===Chinese===
这座舞台胜过一切浪漫的期待：一座有塔楼的小镇，四围的山不高不低，恰好把它安放得妥帖，又陈列得分明。镇子上空覆着一层繁星，厚得像山坡草场上的羊群，随大地转动，转动。

===Original===
“Do you like this country?” the old man said.

===Chinese===
“你喜欢这片乡野吗？”老者说。

===Original===
“I like it immensely. I am sorry to leave. I only came down for the weekend.”

===Chinese===
“喜欢极了。要走了，真遗憾。我本只下来度个周末。”

===Original===
“Still, you prolonged it. This is Friday.”

===Chinese===
“可你把假期延长了。今天是星期五。”

===Original===
Carston thought: A nice slip to start off with. I’d better be a bit more frank right away. “To tell you the truth, I’m a stranger, and I found things a bit difficult over there.”

===Chinese===
卡斯顿心想：一开场就露了个漂亮的破绽。我还是干脆多坦白些的好。“不瞒您说，我是外乡人，在那边觉得有些难处。”

===Original===
“Would it surprise you to hear that I’ve heard that said before?”

===Chinese===
“这话我从前也听人说过——你听了会奇怪吗？”

===Original===
“Not at all.”

===Chinese===
“一点也不。”

===Original===
They laughed together. Carston began to think backwards. The eighteenth century had produced this type, had set him in culture and conviction that Nature had appointed certain old men to approve and modestly direct her arrangements of air and fire. The Renaissance had kicked off that ball, now frozen into the marble and stucco that he was sure adorned his park somewhere. Behind that there was the matrix; the Middle Ages, feudalism, Christendom. Faith in a childishly planned universe as one thing. *The earth one great city of gods and men.* His history lessons were taking life at last.

===Chinese===
两人一同笑了。卡斯顿开始朝回想。是十八世纪造出了这一型人，把他安置进一种文化和信念里：相信造化委派了某些老人，去认可并谦逊地督导她对气与火的种种安排。那颗球是文艺复兴一脚开出的，如今冻进了大理石和灰泥——他敢肯定，那一类东西正陈设在庄园里的哪个角落。再往后是母体：中世纪、封建制、基督教世界。对一座按孩童心性规划出来的宇宙所抱的信仰，也算其中一项。*大地，神与人同栖的一座伟大城邦。*他的历史课总算活过来了。

===Original===
“I detest impertinence,” the old man said﻿—“I don’t think it will be necessary to go into that; but other people have found my son difficult. Was it anything to do with him?”

===Chinese===
“我最恨无礼放肆，”老者说，“我想这一层不必细究；不过别人也觉得我儿子难处。是为了他吗？”

===Original===
Carston thought: Picus again. In for some more. No good pretending. He said:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿心想：又是皮库斯。躲不过了。装是装不成啦。他说：

===Original===
“I reckon. Perhaps I didn’t catch on﻿—that he was fooling me.”

===Chinese===
“我想是的。也许我没回过味来——没看出他在耍我。”

===Original===
Like talking to an old stone idol; live stone idol; stone idol that walks: after something. I ate their bread, and I was rude to them. Be careful, be very careful, indeed.﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
像在对一尊古老的石偶像说话；活的石偶像；会走路的石偶像：在追逐着什么。我吃了他们的面包，却对他们出言无礼。当心，千万当心，真得当心。……

===Original===
“People, I hear, have left the house before, after what I could only wish was my son’s sense of humour had come into play.”

===Chinese===
“我听说，从前也有人离开过那座宅子——在我只肯认作我儿子的幽默感发作之后。”

===Original===
Carston saw that it was a question which was going to lure the other on. And he longed to tell someone something.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿看出，这问题是个饵，要钓他接着往下说。而他也正渴望向什么人讲点什么。

===Original===
“Who are down there now?”

===Chinese===
“眼下都有谁住在那边？”

===Original===
He gave the names. No harm in that.

===Chinese===
他报了名字。这没什么坏处。

===Original===
The old man meditated: “Ah! the heart of the band?”

===Chinese===
老者沉吟：“啊！那一伙的核心人物？”

===Original===
Why a band? More news.

===Chinese===
为什么说“一伙”？又多一条新闻。

===Original===
“My son has a bad habit. He is fond of other people’s property which may never be his. In this instance I am speaking for myself. He has a book of mine that I want. Also, it interests me to know why he should want it. And I may say that your quarrel interests me, whatever it was.”

===Chinese===
“我儿子有个坏习惯。他贪恋别人的财物——那些也许永远到不了他手里的东西。这一回，我是替自己说话。他拿了我一本书，我想要回来。再说，他为什么想要它，这层也让我感兴趣。还可以说一句：你们那场口角，不管因何而起，也让我感兴趣。”

===Original===
“Don’t call it a quarrel. I just didn’t like his way of going about things.”

===Chinese===
“别叫它口角。我只是不喜欢他行事的路数。”

===Original===
“And the rest of my family? We are all more or less related.”

===Chinese===
“那我家里其他人呢？我们多多少少都沾亲带故。”

===Original===
“I was to blame in part. Lost my temper and said more than I should, and they let me go.”

===Chinese===
“我也有一部分不是。发了脾气，说了过头的话，他们就放我走了。”

===Original===
“Very characteristic; of England, I mean. I am sorry. I suppose, by the way, you didn’t see about the house a book on early Church vessels? If you had, it would be easier for me to call my son to order.”

===Chinese===
“很有典型性——我是说，很有英格兰的典型性。我很遗憾。对了，我想问一句：你在那宅子里，没见到过一本论早期教会器皿的书吧？你要是见过，我要申诫儿子就更有话可说了。”

===Original===
Say no; say no; say no. A fool I shall look. He’s seen I hesitated. What in hell does it matter?

===Chinese===
说没有；说没有；说没有。不然我会像个傻子。他已经看出我犹豫了。这他妈的究竟有什么要紧？

===Original===
“I sort of remember a book like that in the library.”

===Chinese===
“我依稀记得，书房里是有那么一本书。”

===Original===
“Not in my son’s room?”

===Chinese===
“没在我儿子房里？”

===Original===
“No, I was never there.”

===Chinese===
“没有，我从没进过他的房间。”

===Original===
The old man did not seem pleasant; silent after he had been told a lie. Then he began to speak fast.

===Chinese===
老者显得不痛快；听了一句谎之后，沉默下来。随后他开始说得飞快。

===Original===
“As you have acknowledged a difficulty, I feel that I might as well tell you why I have come down﻿—at least to find out whether that book is there or not. Why should he want it? He’s quite illiterate. Only if he has it, I shall be on the track of what may be cropping up again. You know what I mean﻿—romantic ideas, now that we know they are lies, which are liable to fall into very silly and very evil practices. Excuses for perversions.”

===Chinese===
“既然你承认在那边待得不顺，我不妨告诉你我下来的缘由——至少是要查明那本书在不在。他要它做什么？他大字不识几个。可只要书在他手里，我就能循迹追下去，追那可能重新冒头的东西。你懂我的意思——浪漫的念头，如今我们已知是谎话，却动辄堕入极愚蠢、极邪恶的行径。为种种倒错张目的借口。”

===Original===
Carston thought: It’s coming out. The old man has a drink in him. *In vino veritas:* good old Montparnasse. Again his curiosity, he said:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿心想：要露底了。老头儿肚里有酒。*In vino veritas*（酒中见真言）：好一个老蒙帕纳斯（Montparnasse）。好奇心又占了上风，他说：

===Original===
“There was nothing like that down there.”

===Chinese===
“那边没有那样的东西。”

===Original===
The old man said: “My book’s gone﻿—and if he has taken that he may have taken something else. There’s his cousin, Scylla, there﻿—”

===Chinese===
老者说：“我的书不见了——他既然拿了那本，就还可能拿了别的。他那表妹斯库拉（Scylla）不就在那儿——”

===Original===
“She is beautiful﻿—”

===Chinese===
“她很美——”

===Original===
“I am glad to hear she is up to your new-world standards. But an affair with her mixed up with superstition and theft.”

===Chinese===
“听她够得上你们新世界的标准，我很高兴。可是同她纠缠的这桩事，掺和着迷信与偷窃。”

===Original===
“What is superstition over here?”

===Chinese===
“在你们这边，‘迷信’指什么？”

===Original===
“A disgusting relic of non-understood natural law.”

===Chinese===
“一种没弄懂的自然法则，留下的令人作呕的遗物。”

===Original===
“I’m at sea.”

===Chinese===
“我如坠五里雾中。”

===Original===
“Of course, you are, and I’m glad to hear it, and that you saw nothing objectionable. In spite of your little difficulty, whatever it was.”

===Chinese===
“你当然在雾里，我很高兴听你这么说——也高兴你没见到任何可指摘的东西。尽管你有过那么一点小小的不痛快，不管那是什么。”

===Original===
“Tell me,” said Carston, “what do you expect me to have seen?”

===Chinese===
“告诉我，”卡斯顿说，“您料想我会见到过什么？”

===Original===
The old man considered: “A strained, shall we say, morbid situation between my son and Scylla Taverner. Repetition in another key with Clarence Lake. Remember, the idea of the first comes from you. The latter, I have frequently observed with disgust. So long as there has been no mention of a cup﻿—”

===Chinese===
老者斟酌道：“一种紧张的、不妨说病态的局面，在我儿子和斯库拉·塔弗纳之间。换一个调子，在克拉伦斯·莱克（Clarence Lake）身上重演。记住，头一桩的说法出自你。后一桩，我可是屡屡冷眼看着，心里作呕。只消还没人提起过一只杯——”

===Original===
Cup. My God! And I’m half in mine. Carston heard a noise like bells he distinguished for the blood in his ears. Then there rang over Starn a variation on three notes, flood-tide pouring into the hill circle, passing out down the valleys, striking and hushed at once on the grass cloth of the hills.

===Chinese===
杯。我的天！而我自己也已半只身子泡在我的杯里了。卡斯顿听见一阵如铃的声响，他辨出那是自己耳中的血声。随后，三个音符的一组变奏鸣响在斯塔恩上空，像涨潮涌进群山的环抱，又顺着山谷流泻而出，一碰到群山铺开的草绿帷布，便霎时敲响又归于沉寂。

===Original===
“Don’t tell,” said Starn bells. “Don’t tell. Don’t tell!”

===Chinese===
“别讲出去，”斯塔恩的钟声说。“别讲。别讲！”

===Original===
He thought: I must tell something, I need to. There must be something I can tell. Not tell on them.

===Chinese===
他想：我必须讲点什么，我需要讲。总该有些东西是我能讲的。但不是告他们的状。

===Original===
The old man was talking with something in his voice of a stallion’s scream:

===Chinese===
老者正说着话，嗓音里带着一种东西，像种马的嘶鸣：

===Original===
“My son’s after Scylla Taverner with a piece out of my collection. As if I didn’t know where I got it, and all about it. And what put him up to it? And what’ll that neurotic hussy make of it? But if he did it, I’ve got a surprise for them. Its story’ll be the surprise, if he doesn’t mind being turned out with his fancy girl﻿—”

===Chinese===
“我儿子揣着我藏品里的一件东西，正去追斯库拉·塔弗纳。仿佛我不知道它的来历，不知道它的全部底细似的。是谁怂恿他干的？那个神经质的骚女人又会拿它搞出什么名堂？可他要是真干了，我有份惊喜等着他们。那东西的来历就是惊喜——只要他不嫌带着他那俏丽的小情人，一道被扫地出门——”

===Original===
Not tell on them.

===Chinese===
不告他们的状。

===Original===
Carston said: “It isn’t what you think, at all, Mr. Tracy. I’ve nothing against your son in general. I reckon now that I was jealous. You see, I’m in love with Miss Taverner, and his easy ways angered me. That’s why I left.”

===Chinese===
卡斯顿说：“事情根本不是您想的那样，特雷西先生。总的说来，我对令郎没有任何意见。我现在想，我是嫉妒了。您瞧，我爱上了塔弗纳小姐，而他那副熟不拘礼的随和劲儿惹恼了我。我走，是为这个。”

===Original===
At once he ceased to be an object of interest. But he was believed. Fooled the old man who was down there, up to less good than anyone else. The bells stopped. There was a feeling that the air had been emptied forever. A cow mooed. Life started again. He went on easily:

===Chinese===
他顿时不再是让人感兴趣的对象了。但他被相信了。骗过了这老头儿——老头儿自己不也下来了嘛，憋的坏水比谁都多。钟声停了。人只觉得空气被永远抽空了。一头牛哞了一声。生活重新开始。他不紧不慢地接着说：

===Original===
“Funny how a love you feel is hopeless spoils your judgment. Goodness knows I never noticed anything of what you suggest. I just couldn’t get inside their life, and I wanted to get into hers﻿—”

===Chinese===
“真怪，一场自觉无望的爱情，竟能这样败坏人的判断。老天知道，您影射的事我一件也没留心到。我只是进不到他们的生活里去，而我又想进到她的生活里去——”

===Original===
“There was that book in the library,” said the old man.

===Chinese===
“书房里确实有过那么一本书。”老者说。

===Original===
Inspiration came lighdy.

===Chinese===
灵感翩然而至。

===Original===
“You half talked me into that. Now I remember it had a bookplate in it, Felix Taverner’s.” If I fool him too much, he’ll go to bed. And I ought to warn them. Warn them of what? That the old man knows the cup’s gone. Certainly. Picus is the sort to take it out on Scylla. The Sanc-Grail theory’s bust anyhow. Tell ’em that. He listened to a theory of the rights of owners to their property which sounded exaggerated even in the mouth of an elderly English collector.

===Chinese===
“那话有一半是您引我说的。对了，我想起来了，那本书里贴着一张藏书票——菲利克斯·塔弗纳（Felix Taverner）的。”我若骗他太过，他就要起身去睡了。再说，我也该给他们递个信。递什么信？就说老头儿知道杯子丢了。一定得递。皮库斯是那号人，会拿斯库拉出气。圣杯（Sanc-Grail）之说反正已经砸了。就这么告诉他们。他听着一整套物主对其财产之权利的理论——那套理论即便出诸一位年迈的英国收藏家之口，也显得夸张。

===Original===
The old red lips moved unpleasantly in their thatch of dead-white hair:

===Chinese===
两片老而红润的嘴唇，在死白须发织成的茅草里讨厌地嚅动着：

===Original===
Prupperty: prupperty: prupperty.

===Chinese===
财——产：财——产：财——产。

===Original===
*The earth one great city of gods and men.*

===Chinese===
*大地，神与人同栖的一座伟大城邦。*

===Original===
He began to live again in moments of insight. They were exceedingly unlike the flashes by which they are generally described, more like obstructions removed, revealing a landscape that had always been there.

===Chinese===
他又开始在一次次洞见里活了过来。那些洞见，同人们惯常描写的那种灵光乍现极不一样，倒更像搬开了什么障碍物，露出一片本就一直在那儿的风景。

===Original===
The old man seemed to have come out of the Roman world. That was difficult to understand, except on a theory that times are grouped otherwise than in sequences. What had his kind been doing at the time of the Roman world? When they had been pouring out of Britain, who had been pouring in? The ancestors of the peasants Carston had seen; but it was not a question of ancestors. There had been a story then of a king, a *comitatus* called Arthur, whose business had been divided between chasing barbarians and looking for a cup. A kind of intermezzo in history, in a time called the Dark Ages, which had produced a story about starlight. Suns of centuries had succeeded it, while the story had lived obscurely in some second-rate literature, and more obscurely, and as an unknown quality, in the imaginations of men like Picus and Scylla, Felix, Clarence, Ross. A very bad old man was putting an unpleasant finger into that pie. Carston was sure of one thing, that he disliked him more than his son. The old man was studying him, with coldness fired by brandy.

===Chinese===
老者像是直接从罗马世界里走出来的人。这很难理解，除非依一种理论：时间的编组，并不按先后次序。罗马世界当令之时，他这一族人在做什么？当他们从布列颠倾泻而出，又是谁在涌入？是卡斯顿见过的那些庄稼人的祖先；可这又不是祖先的问题。那时有过一个王的故事，一支号称亚瑟（Arthur）的*comitatus*（扈从战团），其营生分作两半：驱逐蛮族，寻找一只杯。那算是历史中的一段幕间曲，落在一个号称黑暗时代的时期，产出了一个关于星光的故事。此后，一个个世纪的太阳轮番照临，而那故事晦暗地活在某些二流文字里，又更晦暗地、作为一种未知的质，活在皮库斯和斯库拉、菲利克斯、克拉伦斯、罗斯（Ross）这类人的想象里。如今，一个非常坏的老头儿，正把一根叫人不快的手指伸进这块馅饼。卡斯顿确信一件事：他厌恶这老头儿，胜过厌恶他儿子。老者正打量着他，白兰地把那份冷峻烧得发亮。

===Original===
“I can’t exactly promise to avenge your wrongs, Mr. Carston. But I assure you my son will regret it if he has tampered with my collection. If he has with him a small jade cup, quite ageless in appearance, and slightly ornamented, and if he has persuaded himself that it has some superstitious history, my visit may afford you some satisfaction.”

===Chinese===
“替你雪冤，我可不能确切应承，卡斯顿先生。但我向你保证：他若动了我的藏品，必会后懊。假使他身边带着一只小小的玉杯，看去全然无年代可考，略饰花纹；又假使他说服了自己，信它有什么迷信的来历——那么我这一趟，或许能让你出口气。”

===Original===
Carston thought: He is mad about property, and he hates his son. And his son’s lover, and youth and imagination, and all there is to love over there. He believes in something too. In the thing which he accuses in his son. Whatever that is. Something I can no more imagine in Picus than that Picus doesn’t wash. The devils believe backwards. I can’t grudge the man a trick or two with that behind him. Now I know the father, I can’t hate the son any more.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿心想：他对财产中了魔，他恨他儿子。恨他儿子的情人，恨青春，恨想象，恨那边一切值得爱的东西。他自己也信着点什么。信的正是他指控在他儿子身上的那桩事。不管那究竟是什么。那东西我没法想象会应在皮库斯身上，正如没法想象皮库斯竟会不洗澡。魔鬼也信——不过是倒着信。他背后既然站着那一套，我也不便怪他耍一两手花招。如今认得了父亲，我再也没法恨那儿子了。

===Original===
He noticed the bad moral that if he had stayed over there and behaved himself, he would not have had this interesting insight into his late hosts’ private lives. Another brandy went down. He wanted to go for the old man on their behalf, and excited with drink he needed to talk. So long as he did not say the word cup.

===Chinese===
他留意到一条糟糕的教训：倘若当初他留在那边、行为检点些，就绝不会对前东道主们的私生活获得这份有趣的洞见。又一杯白兰地下了肚。他想替那家人出头，去治一治老头儿，而且酒后亢奋，正需要说话。只消不说出“杯”这个字。

===Original===
“I assure you, sir, you won’t find anything a father would object to in that house.” He knows why I am saying this, because I’ve seen him.

===Chinese===
“我向您保证，先生，在那座宅子里，您不会找到任何当父亲的会反对的东西。”他知道我为什么说这话，因为我已见识过他这号人。

===Original===
“They strike me as people who have loved and suffered a great deal. That purifies.”

===Chinese===
“依我看，他们是一些爱得很深、也受苦很深的人。受苦能净化人。”

===Original===
“From what?”

===Chinese===
“从什么里净化？”

===Original===
“From being like what you say﻿—From only thinking of yourself.” It is not agreeable to be dismissed like a baby. He had to remember that the old man hadn’t seen through him.

===Chinese===
“从变成您说的那种人里——从只想着自己里。”被人当娃娃似的打发掉，可不是滋味。他得记着：老头儿并没有看穿他。

===Original===
“If you were to ask me, I should say that they were looking for something. Miss Taverner told me one day that what they wanted had been lost out of the world.”

===Chinese===
“您要是问我，我会说：他们在找一样东西。塔弗纳小姐有一天对我说过，他们想要的东西，已经从这世界上失落了。”

===Original===
“When and what?” said the old man.

===Chinese===
“什么时候？什么东西？”老者说。

===Original===
“I don’t quite get their dates. Might have been any time, the Middle Ages, or the day before yesterday﻿—a thing that’s been lost﻿—”

===Chinese===
“他们的年代我摸不太准。可能是任何时候——中世纪，也可能是前天——一样失落了的东西——”

===Original===
“There was only one thing lost of a symbolic value in the Middle Ages,” said the old man.

===Chinese===
“中世纪失落的、具有象征价值的东西，只有一件。”老者说。

===Original===
Then Carston’s cups influenced him to obvious caution﻿—then to dreams. He saw Picus making pretty things, Felix laughing, Ross painting, Clarence sleeping, Scylla running away into thunder over burnt grass: running in, in love, through a rain wall.

===Chinese===
随后，卡斯顿的几杯酒引他转入摆在明面上的谨慎——又引他坠入梦境。他看见皮库斯在制造漂亮物件，菲利克斯在笑，罗斯在作画，克拉伦斯在酣睡，斯库拉在焦草之上、雷声之中奔跑远去：又怀着爱，穿过一道雨墙奔了回来。

===Original===
The energy that had got him away must get him back, and damned early. He arranged to be called, arranged for a car, without question that the morning would find him in the same mind.

===Chinese===
当初把他拽开的那股劲头，现在必须把他拽回去，而且要快得要命。他安排了叫早，安排了汽车，毫不怀疑明早一觉醒来，自己心意依旧。

## XVI / 十六、毒杯来历

===Original===
Next morning Carston was by no means in the same mind, and hardly in the same body. It occurred to him, as he looked out on to a village square full of bellying sea fog, that sleeping above him was an impeccable old gentleman of considerable resource and command, tracing a son who had robbed him. At the same time that the old man would be making an early start, or might be watching him from his sleep. That in going back he would be taking sides in a peculiarly unpleasant family row. If there had been a train before midday, he would have taken it. Instead, he took exaggerated precautions, sure of only one thing that he did not want to meet the old man again: told the boots he was driving to a farther village to see the church: hoped he bribed the driver of the car to silence.

===Chinese===
第二天早晨，卡斯顿（Carston）的心境已全然不复昨夜，连身子也几乎换了另一副。他望着窗外一座涨满鼓囊囊海雾的村中广场，忽然想到：此刻睡在自己楼上的，是一位无懈可击的老先生，城府极深，号令惯熟，正追查着一个偷了他东西的儿子。同时想到：这老头儿眼见得要赶早动身；又或者，他正躺在睡梦里盯着自己，也未可知。还想到：这一回去，自己便是在一桩格外不堪的家庭内讧里偏袒一方了。倘若中午之前有一班火车，他早就搭上走了。火车既没有，他便把防备做得夸张其事，只确知一件事——他不愿再撞见那老头儿：他告诉擦靴侍者，说自己要坐车去更远的一座村子看教堂：但愿塞给汽车司机的那点钱，买得动他守口如瓶。

===Original===
Not until he was on the down-track again, by the foxglove patch, did any of last night’s elation return. He yielded to the idea that he was on a pilgrimage, dismissed the car, left his suitcase under a wall and walked the last mile. Over dew, through gauze and long sun-shafts down to the house.

===Chinese===
直到重新走上那条下坡小径，走到那丛毛地黄旁边，昨夜的一点兴奋才又回到他身上。他由着那个念头占了上风——他这是在朝圣——于是打发走汽车，把手提箱撂在一堵墙根下，步行完最后一英里。踏过露水，穿过薄纱似的雾霭和一道道长长的日光，一路下到宅子跟前。

===Original===
Seven-thirty o’clock. The old nurse in an older dressing-gown was waking up the kitchen life. He asked for Scylla, the easiest and the hardest person to see, and waited for her below. She came down, heavenly sleepy. He told her that he was sorry, and what had happened at Starn.

===Chinese===
七点半钟。老保姆裹着一件比她本人更老的晨衣，正唤醒厨房里的生气。他求见斯库拉（Scylla）——一个最容易、也最难见到的人——在楼下等她。她下来了，一副困倦得宛如天人的模样。他告诉她自己很抱歉，又把斯塔恩（Starn）发生的事说了。

===Original===
“That’s all right,” she said, “everyone gets worked at times. I only wish you had not sprung it on Clarence﻿—I’d like you to be sorry for that﻿—no, *not* because it was bad manners but because it was cruel and damned silly﻿—” That punished him, though he did not understand her aversion to cruelty, that kind. She went on﻿—“But a worse thing has happened, it may be for the best. It usually is﻿—” Suddenly, the complications of the story came over her, and he heard a sort of cry: “What is it all about﻿—I don’t like this,” and he saw that he meant to stay if he had to sleep out on the earth and gnaw grass to help her. Then she told him to go down and bathe before breakfast, while she told the rest, and gave his case to the old nurse.

===Chinese===
“不要紧，”她说，“谁都有沉不住气的时候。我只希望你当初别冷不丁冲克拉伦斯（Clarence）发作——我要你为这个抱歉——不，*不是*因为它失礼，而是因为它残忍，而且蠢得可以——”这话惩罚了他，尽管他并不理解她对残忍——对那一类残忍——的嫌恶。她接着说——“可是更糟的事发生了，没准倒是好事。通常是——”忽然，这故事的重重纠葛整个漫上她的心头，他听见她近乎一声哭喊：“这一切到底是为了什么——我不喜欢这样，”于是他明白了：只要能帮她，哪怕睡在露天的地上、啃草皮，他也打定主意留下。随后她叫他下去赶在早饭前洗个澡，由她去告诉其余的人，又把他的手提箱交给老保姆。

===Original===
“Take it up to Mr. Carston’s room. He did good work for us last night at Starn.”

===Chinese===
“提到卡斯顿先生房里去。他昨晚在斯塔恩为我们出了力。”

===Original===
A little breakfast with the Borgia’s. Poison anyhow, hurrying up over the hills. No woodpecker, no appetites. Clarence handing back with interest his insolence of the day before.

===Chinese===
同波尔贾家（Borgia）一道用了一点早餐。毒药反正已经在路上，正翻山越岭地赶来。没有啄木鸟，也没有胃口。克拉伦斯把他前一日的无礼，连本带利奉还了回来。

===Original===
Scylla said:

===Chinese===
斯库拉说：

===Original===
“Clarence, we must be practical. Go and find out what Picus has tried to do.” And Carston could have kicked the man when he assured her that it was now her business.

===Chinese===
“克拉伦斯，我们得讲实际。去看看皮库斯（Picus）都想法子做了些什么。”那人却一口咬定，这如今是她的事了，卡斯顿听了恨不得踹他一脚。

===Original===
“It’s not become that,” she answered, steadily﻿—“go you and manage him, as you have always done.”

===Chinese===
“事情还没到那一步，”她不动声色地答道——“还是你去管他吧，像你一向的那样。”

===Original===
No, Clarence would not. Waited to be entreated to have more fun in refusing.

===Chinese===
不，克拉伦斯不肯。他专等人来恳求，好在拒绝里多讨一份乐趣。

===Original===
“Are we going to lie, or aren’t we?” said Felix.

===Chinese===
“我们到底撒谎，还是不撒谎？”菲利克斯（Felix）说。

===Original===
“That depends on Picus.” It did.

===Chinese===
“那得看皮库斯。”也确实得看。

===Original===
“Whatever we do, he’ll do the opposite.” Felix got up and put the cup in a drawer.

===Chinese===
“我们做什么，他偏做什么的反面。”菲利克斯起身把杯子放进一个抽屉。

===Original===
“The book goes on the fire.”

===Chinese===
“书送上火里去。”

===Original===
It is not easy to burn a book. He was banging it down on the kitchen fire when Mr. Tracy walked into the house. Carston retreated backwards through the kitchen, where Felix pushed him into a cupboard, and kindly got him out again, and up the backstairs to the attic and left him alone with the bees. There he meditated on what was going on below, whether the bees would attack him, and what it would be like if they brought the old man up there.

===Chinese===
烧掉一本书并不容易。他正把书砰地砸向厨房炉火，特雷西先生（Mr. Tracy）恰好走进宅子。卡斯顿倒退着退出厨房，菲利克斯在那里把他塞进一个食橱，随即又好心把他弄出来，领上后楼梯进了阁楼，撂下他独自同蜜蜂做伴。他在那里思忖楼下正进行着的事，思忖蜜蜂会不会来攻他，又思忖倘若他们把老头儿也领上阁楼，那将是怎样一番光景。

===Original===
Below, Scylla thought: Keep things amiable: keep things casual: pay out what rope we have. She constrained Picus’s father to breakfast, because his son was unwell, and noticed how Clarence slipped away to warn him, now that the worst had come to the worst.

===Chinese===
楼下，斯库拉想着：维持和气：维持随常：把手里的绳一寸寸放出去。她硬留下皮库斯的父亲用早餐，说是他儿子身上欠安，一面留意到克拉伦斯怎样趁此刻溜了出去——如今最坏的事终于坏到了头——去给皮库斯报信。

===Original===
“It was nice of you to come,” she said﻿—“just as the place is at its loveliest.” Ross despised her for that, and Felix admired, while her spirit was falling away into pockets of pain like dropped heartbeats, because in everything Picus was a lie. Excepting under Gault Cliff, and they would never go there again. Never again. That brought up bubble upon bubble of agony each time they rose, with attention to the unpleasant details of his father’s visit. That sinister antique was saying:

===Chinese===
“您来得真客气，”她说——“正赶上这地方一派最美的时候。”罗斯（Ross）为这句话看不起她，菲利克斯却暗自佩服，而她的心神正塌陷下去，落进一个个疼痛的口袋，像一颗颗坠落的心跳，因为在一切事上，皮库斯都是一个谎。只有高尔特崖（Gault Cliff）底下除外，而他们再也不会去那里了。再也不会了。这些念头每回浮起，都顶起一泡又一泡的苦楚，还连带他父亲此番来访的种种不快细节。那件阴森的老古董正在说：

===Original===
“Call that old nurse of yours. I want to ask her a question.” And she did not dare to be anything but unspeakably civil, while he said:

===Chinese===
“把你家那位老保姆叫来。我有句话要问她。”而她只敢拿出无以复加的客气，听他说道：

===Original===
“Did you unpack for Mr. Tracy, and if so did you find a green bowl in his case, or a book that wasn’t a novel?”

===Chinese===
“你替特雷西先生开过箱没有？要是开过，在他箱子里见没见到一只绿碗，或者一本不是小说的书？”

===Original===
Trust Nanna. She almost put Mr. Picus’s father in his place. Felix’s business to have done that. Felix had gone out. Oh, God! to collect more fish?

===Chinese===
娜娜（Nanna）真靠得住。她差一点就把皮库斯先生的父亲顶了回去。这本该是菲利克斯的分内事。菲利克斯偏又出去了。哦，上帝！又捞鱼去了？

===Original===
Ross helped: “Picus is pretty unwell. Shall I take you up?”

===Chinese===
罗斯帮了腔：“皮库斯病得不轻。要不要我领您上去？”

===Original===
And she managed to say, coolly: “I don’t see why a book not a novel or even a cup should be out of order in anyone’s luggage. I could have asked Nanna that myself.”

===Chinese===
而她也设法淡淡地说：“我看不出一本不是小说的书、甚至一只杯子，搁在谁的行李里算得出格。这话我自己也问得了娜娜。”

===Original===
That bothered the old man’s exit. Ross went too, and she sat alone, wondering where Carston had got to. “He’s up with the bees, honey,” said her nurse. Tell the bees. Nanna did that when one of them died. Which of them was going to die first?

===Chinese===
这话把老头儿的退场搅乱了。罗斯也上去了，她独自坐着，纳闷卡斯顿究竟去了哪里。“他跟蜜蜂做伴呢，宝贝儿。”她的老保姆说。有话得告诉蜜蜂。娜娜向来这样，蜂群里死了哪只，就对它们说一声。他们中间，哪一个会先死呢？

===Original===
Picus had taken his father’s cup.

===Chinese===
皮库斯拿走了他父亲的杯子。

===Original===
Picus had stunted its origin.

===Chinese===
皮库斯把它的来历掐残了。

===Original===
Picus had had an idea, or why the book?

===Chinese===
皮库斯起过一个念头，不然要那本书做什么？

===Original===
Picus had run into small mystifications.

===Chinese===
皮库斯撞进了些小小的迷障。

===Original===
Picus had made love to her.

===Chinese===
皮库斯向她求过爱。

===Original===
Picus would not make love again, because they had been found out.

===Chinese===
皮库斯不会再求爱了，因为他们的事已经败露。

===Original===
Picus led Clarence a hard life.

===Chinese===
皮库斯叫克拉伦斯吃足了苦头。

===Original===
No one could go to Picus and say: “So much for your silly devilries. *Turn ye to me.*” And I even thought of marrying him because of his beauty. I did not catch the joy as it flew. Damn female instincts. Picus should not have pretended it was the cup of the Sanc-Grail. That will do in weaker minds and more violent imaginations than mine and Ross’s.

===Chinese===
谁也没法走到皮库斯面前说：“你那些愚蠢的胡闹，到此为止吧。*Turn ye to me*（转向我来）。”而我竟一度因为他那张脸，动过嫁给他的念头。欢乐飞过的时候，我没有接住它。该死的女性本能。皮库斯不该硬充它就是圣所圣杯（Sanc-Grail）的那只杯。那一套，对比我和罗斯更弱的头脑、更狂烈的想象才管用。

===Original===
Meanwhile, Carston had discovered a dormer in the attic roof, and saw her walking the lawns. He stuck his head out, powdered with the shells of dead bees, and called. She ran in and up to the attic door.

===Chinese===
这其间，卡斯顿在阁楼顶上寻见一扇老虎窗，望见她正在草坪上走来走去。他探出脑袋——扑了一头死蜂的碎壳——喊了她一声。她跑进屋，一路跑上阁楼门口。

===Original===
“Couldn’t you,” he whispered, “get him over to Tollerdown to look for himself? Get Clarence to take him. That will give us time.”

===Chinese===
“你能不能，”他低声道，“把他哄到托勒当（Tollerdown）去，让他自己找找看？叫克拉伦斯带他去。那能给我们争出点时间。”

===Original===
“Good,” she said, “I’ll go down and try it.” They both saw that the real need was to get rid of the old man. But as she opened Picus’s door, she heard:

===Chinese===
“好，”她说，“我这就下去试试。”两人都明白，眼下真正的急务是把老头儿打发走。可她推开皮库斯的房门时，听见：

===Original===
“Go over to Tollerdown to satisfy myself. Why? You’ve got it and you can keep it. Would you like to know its history? In India it was the poison-cup of a small rajah I knew. He was poisoned, all the same, drinking out of it. I saw him with a yard of froth bubble coming out of his mouth. Burnt up inside, I believe. I brought it away and gave it to a lady, who was frequently at Tambourne when you were at school. When she contracted tuberculosis she had a fancy for it as a spitting-cup. That is, so far as I know, any interest that attaches to the thing.”

===Chinese===
“到托勒当去，好叫我自己死心。为什么？东西在你手里，你就留着吧。愿不愿听听它的来历？在印度，它是我认识的一位小拉贾（rajah）的毒杯。饶是这样，他自己用它喝东西，照样中了毒。我见过他嘴里冒出一尺来长的泡沫。我看他是从里头烧坏了。我把它带了回来，送给一位太太——你上学那阵子，她在坦伯恩（Tambourne）常来常往。她染上肺结核之后，又喜好拿它当痰盂用。据我所知，这东西身上沾的趣味，也就这些了。”

===Original===
“Your mother drowned herself, didn’t she, Picus?” said Ross, with that impersonal interest in the event which was sometimes too strong an antiseptic, never a poison.

===Chinese===
“你母亲是自己投的水，对吧，皮库斯？”罗斯说，那副腔调对这桩事怀着一种置身事外的兴味——这兴味有时是太强的防腐剂，却从来不是毒药。

===Original===
“Yes,” said Clarence.

===Chinese===
“是的。”克拉伦斯说。

===Original===
“You see,” said the old man to his son﻿—“since that is your selection from my collection you may as well know your choice. You know now, and that your efforts to identify it as a mass-cup will hardly succeed.”

===Chinese===
“你瞧，”老者对儿子说——“既然那是你从我收藏里挑的货色，你不妨认清自己挑中的是什么。如今你知道了；也知道你想把它考订成一只弥撒杯（mass-cup），怕是很难成功。”

===Original===
“Picus,” said Felix, “it is up to you to tell us if you have this thing.”

===Chinese===
“皮库斯，”菲利克斯说，“这东西在不在你手里，该由你自己告诉大家。”

===Original===
“You fool,” said the old man, “I saw him take it, when he thought I was asleep before the fire.”

===Chinese===
“你这傻瓜，”老者说，“他拿它的时候我看见了——他当我还在炉火跟前睡着呢。”

===Original===
“What does it matter if he did, when we have none of us seen the thing?”

===Chinese===
“就算他拿了，又有什么要紧？我们哪一个都没见过那东西。”

===Original===
Picus raised his shoulders out of the sheets:

===Chinese===
皮库斯从被单里耸起肩膀：

===Original===
“Oh, cut that, Felix, when it’s where you put it, downstairs in the bureau drawer.” They noticed the father in the son. Then Scylla’s turn came﻿—“From the bridegroom to the bride. Hardly as propitious as one would like.”

===Chinese===
“哦，省省吧，菲利克斯，东西不就在你搁的地方——楼下书桌抽屉里。”他们在儿子身上认出了父亲。随后轮到了斯库拉——“新郎赠给新娘的。兆头嘛，可不如人愿那么吉利。”

===Original===
“That is superstitious,” said Ross﻿—“Scylla’s no bride for any son of yours, and the cup’s bitter history concerns no one but the dead.”

===Chinese===
“这是迷信，”罗斯说——“斯库拉做不得你家任何儿子的新娘，这杯子的苦史，也只与死人相干。”

===Original===
“Why did he pretend it was the cup of the Sanc-Grail?” said the old man.

===Chinese===
“他为什么要硬充它是圣所圣杯？”老者说。

===Original===
“How did you pretend he did?” said Ross.

===Chinese===
“那您又是怎么装作是他装的？”罗斯说。

===Original===
“A snip of an American called Carston told me last night at Starn. Another candidate for your rather secondhand beauties, Scylla﻿—”

===Chinese===
“昨晚在斯塔恩，有个叫卡斯顿的美国小子跟我说的。斯库拉，你那批相当二手的美男子，又添了一位候选——”

===Original===
“Felix, will you fetch him?” said Ross.

===Chinese===
“菲利克斯，去把他请来。”罗斯说。

===Original===
Upstairs, through the bee-roar, Carston heard the boy say:

===Chinese===
楼上，隔着蜜蜂的嗡轰，卡斯顿听见那少年说：

===Original===
“So you did give us away last night at Starn!”

===Chinese===
“这么说，昨晚在斯塔恩，你果真把我们卖了！”

===Original===
“I’m damned if I did. That’s his bluff.” He thought: I knew I’d have to go down. I’m in this. How life arranges itself without our tugging and kicking. “Give me a run-over what’s been said.”

===Chinese===
“我要是卖了就该死。那是他唬人。”他想：我就知道躲不过要下去。我已经在这局里头了。生活自有它的安排，全不用我们又拽又踢。“把说过的话，原原本本给我过一遍。”

===Original===
“He wants us to have it,” said Felix. “It was a rajah’s poison cup. Jade is supposed to show poison. Of course, it doesn’t, and the man died. I shouldn’t be surprised if old Tracy hadn’t a hand in it. He brought it back and gave it to a female tart. That was a bad story, because Picus’s mother pined about it, till they found her in the stream beneath old Tracy’s house. Picus was a kid at the time, and he adored her, and the old man had the woman to live with him at Tambourne till she died of T.B., and the cup was one of her belongings. Sort of thing which wouldn’t work out so badly today with divorces and fresh air. The old man’s loving it; spotted that Picus has given Scylla the cup.”

===Chinese===
“他巴不得我们收下它，”菲利克斯说。“那是某个拉贾的毒杯。据说玉能显出毒来。当然显不出来，那位王爷照样死了。要说老特雷西在里头没插一手，我才不信呢。他把它带回来，送了个骚货。那可是个糟糕的故事：皮库斯的母亲为此日见憔悴，直到人们在老特雷西宅子底下的溪水里找到她。皮库斯当时还是个娃娃，他崇拜她；老头儿让那女人住到坦伯恩，直到她痨病死了，这杯子就是她的遗物之一。这类事搁到今天，有离婚、又有新鲜空气，结果断不至于这么坏。老头儿这会儿正乐在其中；他已经看出，皮库斯把杯子给了斯库拉。”

===Original===
“Then why on earth the stunt about the spear and the well?”

===Chinese===
“那到底为什么要耍长矛和水井那一套花招？”

===Original===
“I don’t know﻿—He’s the old man’s son. Come down.”

===Chinese===
“我不知道——他毕竟是老头儿的儿子。下来吧。”

===Original===
Carston felt his position false again. Somehow he had given a clue to this hag-driven ancient: he was a little in alliance with him: he protested. Picus’s father said:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿又觉出自己的处境虚伪了。不知怎么，他竟递给了这个被妖梦驱赶着的老古董一条线索：他同老头儿结了几分盟：他抗议着。皮库斯的父亲说：

===Original===
“Quite enough, my dear boy, quite enough. You were obviously startled, and I had my theory of what startled you. I’m sure Scylla will forgive you in time, and I must be off now. I’ll leave you your treasure, but I should like my book on the mass-cups back. You see now that it will be quite useless to try and identify it from that.”

===Chinese===
“够啦，亲爱的孩子，够啦。你分明是受了惊，而吓着你的是什么，我自有我的一套理论。我敢说斯库拉日久总会原谅你。我这就要动身了。宝贝我给你们留下，只是我那本论弥撒杯的书，我想讨回去。你们如今该明白了：想凭它去考订这只杯子，是全然无用的。”

===Original===
“Ask Felix,” said Picus. No one knew whether to help him out or not. Carston thought of its boards smouldering on the kitchen fire, making it, as Nanna had pointed out, unfit for proper use.

===Chinese===
“问菲利克斯。”皮库斯说。谁也不知道该不该帮他解围。卡斯顿想起那两片书壳还在厨房炉火上闷燃，而且——正如娜娜指出的——害得那炉火不堪正经使用了。

===Original===
Scylla said, coolly: “I can’t part with the other half of my wedding-present.”

===Chinese===
斯库拉冷冷地说：“我这结婚礼物的另一半，不能割爱。”

===Original===
And this infuriated the old man. It was evident, even to their over-hurried perceptions, that he was more than insulting and exultant, he was in earnest. He began to frighten them. They could not decide whether to economise the truth or not. The old man seemed in need of exorcism. A bib. Altogether too gothic now.

===Chinese===
这话把老头儿激怒了。就连他们那班仓皇过甚的知觉也看得分明：他不止是出言侮辱、洋洋得意，他是认真的。他开始吓着他们了。他们定不下主意，真话要不要省着点说。老头儿看来需要一场驱邪。还得给他系块围嘴。如今整个儿未免太哥特了。

===Original===
Then Felix cried out: “I burnt the damned thing when Carston told me that you and Picus were playing us up.”

===Chinese===
于是菲利克斯喊了起来：“卡斯顿告诉我，说你和皮库斯在耍我们，我就把那该死的东西烧了。”

===Original===
The old man began to laugh. “That’ll do,” he repeated. And quite soon after he was gone, and they dragged out chairs and lay on the lawn at different angles, no one wishing to speak.

===Chinese===
老头儿笑了起来。“行了行了。”他一迭声地说。没过多久他就去了，他们拖出椅子，各自歪着角度躺在草坪上，谁也不想说话。

## XVII / 十七、幻影游荡

===Original===
Clarence figured it out. Picus had done this to get away from him, falsifying the devotion of years, flaunt a pretty cousin, marry a pretty cousin: because she had some money: because she was a bird and bee woman: because Carston was after her, must be after her, or he would not have come back from Starn. Or why should any man run back after such an exit, to help a woman, a mime, a baggage, a bag of excrement? There is a great difference between a sportsman, a painter, a man that feels the earth, between Ross and Scylla and his terrible green Sdi creature, and Clarence’s feeling for decoration best served by cities, a blasted heath no more than a site for his palace. He was on Carston and Felix’s side, never satisfied with the earth sacrifice the others munched, wanting *fausse route* with his friend, because his education was insufficient for his abilities. Not for the first time he did not try to correct himself, thought about his wounds and his wishes until they took phantom shape and he slipped off uneasily with his gun down the wood. Scylla made a face after him. Ross shook his head. Carston had an impulse to follow.

===Chinese===
克拉伦斯想明白了。皮库斯干出这桩事，就是为了摆脱他：把多年的深情篡改成谎言，炫示一个漂亮的表妹，娶一个漂亮的表妹：因为她有几个钱：因为她是个鸟儿蜜蜂一样的女人：因为卡斯顿在追她，必定在追她，不然他不会从斯塔恩回来。要不然，一个男人那样拂袖而去之后，为什么还要跑回来，去帮一个女人、一个做戏的、一件贱货、一袋粪土？一个运动家、一个画家、一个摸得到大地的人——罗斯、斯库拉，和他那条可怕的绿色斯迪（Sdi）怪物——跟克拉伦斯的趣味之间，隔着天大的差别：他的趣味在装饰，而装饰最好由城市来伺候；一片遭天谴的荒原，在他不过是替他的宫殿挑好的一块宅基。他站在卡斯顿和菲利克斯一边，从不满足于旁人嚼得起劲的那种大地祭品，一心想同他那位朋友去走*歧路*（*fausse route*），因为他的教养配不上他的才能。他没有设法纠正自己——这也不是头一回了——只管反刍自己的创伤和欲念，直到它们长出幻影的形状；随后他挟着枪，浑身不自在地溜下树林去了。斯库拉冲他的背影做了个鬼脸。罗斯摇了摇头。卡斯顿一时冲动，想跟上去。

===Original===
“Gone off to invent excuses for Picus,” said Felix, “for you to listen to﻿—It’s the occupation down here.”

===Chinese===
“跑掉去给皮库斯编借口了，”菲利克斯说，“好留给你们听——在这地方，这就是正经营生。”

===Original===
“Come and pose,” said Ross. “I want a model.”

===Chinese===
“过来摆姿势吧，”罗斯说。“我缺个模特。”

===Original===
Carston was alone with Scylla. He said:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿单独同斯库拉待在了一处。他说：

===Original===
“I think I’ve an excuse now to say ‘explain a bit.’ ” It was parching hot, gritty as if a storm of microscopic dust had filled up the holes in the leaves, in the grass-blades, in the skin.

===Chinese===
“我想这会儿我总算有借口说一句‘解释一下吧’了。”天气燥热灼人，满空气的砂砾，仿佛一场微尘的风暴填满了叶子里的孔、草叶里的孔、皮肤里的孔。

===Original===
“Reassure me, at least,” he said, “that this would have happened without me.”

===Chinese===
“至少宽宽我的心，”他说，“告诉我就算没有我，这一切也照样会发生。”

===Original===
“Of course. Much worse if it hadn’t been for you.” They stopped talking.

===Chinese===
“当然。要不是有你，还会糟得多。”两人住了口。

===Original===
“I tried to help you,” he said, “it is your turn.” Saw the effort she made, thought how easy these people were to spur.

===Chinese===
“我出过力要帮你，”他说，“这回轮到你了。”他瞧见她费了多大的劲，心想这些人多么一策就动。

===Original===
“Let’s go up and tackle Picus,” she said﻿—“there is one thing about staying in bed, it runs to earth.”

===Chinese===
“咱们上楼去会一会皮库斯，”她说——“赖在床上有一桩好处：等于自己入了穴，一找一个准。”

===Original===
“No,” said Carston, “you must excuse me. I’ve had about enough of that chap.”

===Chinese===
“不，”卡斯顿说，“恕我失陪。那位仁兄，我受得差不多了。”

===Original===
“So,” said Scylla, “for the moment, have I.”

===Chinese===
“说的是，”斯库拉道，“眼下我也受够了。”

===Original===
“Seems to me he played a mean trick on you all﻿—What I don’t see is why. Or why it should have got you.”

===Chinese===
“依我看，他是对你们大伙使了个损招——我不明白的是图什么。也不明白这事怎么偏偏就伤着了你。”

===Original===
The other side of the house Ross was seeing Felix flung in a chair, hearing the nervous sobbing his own cool voice could not control. Nor could he control in himself his aversion to speak or to help.

===Chinese===
在房子另一头，罗斯看着菲利克斯瘫进椅子里，听着那阵神经质的抽泣——自己冷冷的嗓音管束不住它。他心里那份嫌恶，自己也管束不住：既不想开口，也不想伸手。

===Original===
“What in hell do we come here for? I told Scylla to sell those shares and we’d have been at Biarritz.”

===Chinese===
“我们到底下这儿来干什么？我早叫斯库拉把那些股票卖掉，这会儿我们人已经在比亚里茨（Biarritz）了。”

===Original===
“It would have been the same at Biarritz.”

===Chinese===
“到了比亚里茨，还不是一样。”

===Original===
“You might be. I should be different there. You’re looking for something. I’m not. And I hope when you get it you’ll like it. Looking for the Sanc-Grail. It’s always the same story. The Golden Fleece or the philosopher’s stone, or perpetual motion, or Atlantis or the lost tribes or God. All ways of walking into the same trap. And Scylla gets into bed with old Tracy’s son.”

===Chinese===
“你也许一样。我在那儿就会是另一个人。你在找什么东西。我不找。等你到手了，我还盼你能喜欢它呢。找什么圣所圣杯。永远是同一套老故事。金羊毛啦，贤者之石啦，永动机啦，亚特兰蒂斯啦，失落的支派啦，要不就是上帝。条条路数，通的是同一口陷阱。而斯库拉呢，跟老特雷西的儿子上了床。”

===Original===
“That is not the point. What is it about a trap?”

===Chinese===
“那不是要紧的。陷阱又怎么说？”

===Original===
The boy got up and looked a little madly and very insolently at Ross, the blue eyes cold between lids red with weeping. Ross was surprised to find himself edging away, like a man who is to be shot at.

===Chinese===
那少年站起身来，有点发狂、又极其无礼地瞪着罗斯，哭红了的眼睑中间，一双蓝眼睛冷冰冰的。罗斯惊讶地发现自己正一点点往旁边蹭，像个等着挨枪子的人。

===Original===
“We’re through with the baby-brother business.”

===Chinese===
“当小弟弟的营生，我们收摊了。”

===Original===
Upstairs, Picus had finished shaving, his body worked on as delicately and scrupulously as a cat. Whistling to himself while Felix was sobbing, whistling back his power as their idol, like a god summoning an element or in confidence like prayer. He set his tie for the last time, shook himself, laid himself down on the window seat, and drew a ring with a pearl on to his atrociously powerful hand.

===Chinese===
楼上，皮库斯刮完了脸，一身皮肉侍弄得像猫一样精细讲究。菲利克斯在底下抽泣，他自顾自吹着口哨，把身为众人偶像的威能一声声吹回来，像一位神明召唤一种元素，又像祷告那样私下交心。他末了又扶正一回领带，抖擞了一下，往窗座上一躺，将一枚带珍珠的戒指套上那只力道骇人的手。

===Original===
And Clarence out on the high turf was not looking at the sea or the terrible crest of Gault suspended in the haze. Or at the small enamel floor he trod on, flower and leaf stars and bars and rings and crosses: or at a dozen rabbits hurrying: or at one hawk not hurrying, until he dropped faster than the eye and there would be one rabbit the less. He walked slowly, inside himself, petting his phantoms, especially a phantom of Picus, the body up at the house was behaving more and more unlike. He wondered also why Scylla had called him “medievalist,” because she said he assumed a form from inside and made things fit it, instead of compelling what is to do his construction for him. Hadn’t Picus invented a lying fancy to please her, to get off with her? Lying and lecherous his bird was, for a woman who had snapped him up for her body’s sake and her vanity. This went on until he saw the names he called her take body and walk to meet him out of the wood. Vanity, lechery, falsehood, and malice lolled along together across the grass, out of the trees. And because she called him medievalist, he saw them in archaic dress.

===Chinese===
而克拉伦斯在外头高处的草皮上，既没有看海，也没有看雾霭里悬着的高尔特崖那道可怖的顶脊。也没有看脚下那片珐琅般的小地面，花与叶缀成的星、条、环、十字：没有看十几只匆匆赶路的兔子：也没有看那只不慌不忙的鹰——直到它快过眼珠地一坠，兔子便从此少掉一只。他慢慢走着，人缩在自己心里，抚弄着他的幻影，尤其是皮库斯的一个幻影——楼上屋里那具肉身，行径却越来越不像了。他还想不通斯库拉干吗叫他“中世纪主义者”，因为她说过：他是先从心里取定一个形，再逼万事万物去凑合它，而不是强令实存替他把构造造出来。皮库斯难道不是编了个骗人的瞎话去讨她欢心，好把她弄到手？他那只鸟儿本就又撒谎又好色，图的是一个为了自己那副身子骨和虚荣、把他一把抢走的女人。这样想着想着，直到他看见自己加给她的那些名目生出肉身，从树林里走出来迎他。虚荣、淫欲、谎骗和恶意，懒洋洋结伴荡过草地，钻出树丛。又因为她叫他中世纪主义者，他看它们一身穿的都是古装。

===Original===
Scylla said to Carston on the lawn:

===Chinese===
草坪上，斯库拉对卡斯顿说：

===Original===
“So, you see, what sounded romantic excitement about the Sanc-Grail cup was real. And unfortunate?”

===Chinese===
“所以你瞧，当初关于圣所圣杯那杯子、听着像罗曼蒂克兴头的那些话，是真的。而且不幸，是不是？”

===Original===
Carston wondered, deplored and detested the European faculty for taking the skeleton out of the cupboard. Rattling it, airing it, lecturing on it. She was winding up a discourse without enquiry into his feelings. On what he supposed was the skeleton, the world skeleton. He heard:

===Chinese===
卡斯顿又是诧异、又是悲叹、又是憎恶这门欧洲本领——把柜子里的骷髅拖将出来。摇响它，晾晒它，拿它开讲。她正给一篇宏论收尾，全不过问他的感受。论题——据他猜想——正是那具骷髅，世界的那具骷髅。他听见：

===Original===
“If the materialist’s universe is true, not a working truth to make bridges with and things, we are a set of blind factors in a machine. And no passion has any validity and no imagination. They are just little tricks of the machine. It either is so, or it isn’t. If you hold that it isn’t, you corrupt your intellect by denying certain facts. If you stick to the facts as we have them, life is a horror and an insult. Nothing has any worth, but to tickle our sensations and oil the machine. There is no value in our passions and perceptions, or final differences between a life full of design and adventure and a life crawled out in a palace or a slum. The life of Plato or Buddha, apart from the kick of the illusion, was as futile as the lives of the daughters of Louis XV. Old talk, you say, and remember *In Memoriam*. But notice what is happening now people have become used to the idea. Any little boy in a Paris bar, who never heard of physics knows. Everyone gets the age’s temper. With results on their conduct﻿—‘Why be good any more’ they say, and the youngest ones not that. And it’s not intellectual beauty the culture-camp admire. It’s themselves for having such fine subconsciousnesses. Such an elegant sublimation of their infant interests. Watch the world with the skeleton acclimatised! Even when I was new we tried the bad to see if it might not be good. But the new lot aren’t interested. Don’t give a button for the good any more.

===Chinese===
“如果唯物主义者的宇宙是真的——不是拿来造桥派用场的那种好用之真——那我们便是一台机器里的一套瞎眼因子。一切激情皆无有效性，一切想象亦然。它们不过是机器的几手小把戏。要么如此，要么不然。你若咬定不然，便是否认某些事实，荼毒了自己的智力。你若坚守我们手头的这些事实，人生便是一场恐怖、一桩侮辱。万事皆无价值，除了撩拨我们的感官、给机器上油。我们的激情与知觉里没有价值；一座充满构想与冒险的人生，同一场在宫殿或贫民窟里爬完的人生，也没有了终极的差别。柏拉图或佛陀的一生，除去幻觉那一记兴奋，同路易十五的女儿们的一生一样无谓。你会说：老生常谈，还想得起《悼念集》（*In Memoriam*）。可是请注意，如今人们惯用了这个观念之后，正出着什么事。巴黎酒吧里随便哪个没听说过物理的小小子都懂得。人人都沾上时代的脾气。结果落在他们的品行上——他们说：‘干吗还要学好？’顶年轻的一辈，连这句都省了。文化营里的人景仰的又不是智性之美。是他们自己，景仰自己竟有这样出色的下意识。把他们婴儿期的兴致升华得何其雅致。瞧瞧这骷髅已服水土的世界吧！就连我还是新的时候，我们也拿恶试过，看它会不会其实是善。可新的一拨人不感兴趣了。对善，再不肯出一枚纽扣。”

===Original===
“And there is no evading it by any ‘service of humanity’ game. Unless you’re one of the people who get sensual kick out looking after things, why help humanity? Think of Wells’s Utopias. Birth-control, and peace and drains. And nothing left to do but report on the fauna of a further star. Our visiting-list extended to super-birds, or intellectually developed spiders. *Nothing* but physical adventure. Especially as we’ve picked up one priceless truth off the road, that every action brings with it its toxin and its antitoxin. If, instead of becoming cynical or scared, we started enquiry again from that﻿—”

===Chinese===
“而且，靠什么‘服务人类’的把戏也绕不开它。除非你是那号照管起东西来能照管出肉体快感的人，凭什么去帮人类？想想威尔斯（Wells）笔下的那些乌托邦吧。节育，和平，下水道。往后便没有事可做，只剩给更远一颗星球的动物志写报告。把拜客名单拉长，添上超级鸟类，或者智力发达的蜘蛛。*无非*是肉身的冒险罢了。何况我们还在路面上白捡了一条无价的真理：每一个行动，都随身带着它的毒素和它的抗毒素。倘若我们不去变愤世嫉俗、不去变惶恐，反倒从这一点上重新发问——”

===Original===
Felix shouted melodiously:

===Chinese===
菲利克斯朗声喊道，倒带着曲调：

===Original===
“I’m postwar. I’m just through getting clear of you. I admit you can scare me, but in reality you bore me. I don’t care any more. I may be a mass of inhibitions, but I’m out for myself﻿—”

===Chinese===
“我是战后人。我这才刚从你们手里脱开身。我承认你们吓唬得住我，可实际上你们叫我厌烦。我什么都不在乎了。我兴许是一团抑制，可我从此只为自己——”

===Original===
Through the grilling haze, Luxury, Malice, and Untruth strolled over the grass to Clarence. In Ross’s heart there twisted ache and dislike. Carston gave in to spiritual upset, while his body lay in a garden chair. Scylla ended:

===Chinese===
烤人的雾霭里，奢靡、恶意与虚谎荡过草地，朝克拉伦斯去了。罗斯心里绞着疼与厌。卡斯顿听任心境翻腾，身子却摊在花园椅上。斯库拉收了尾：

===Original===
“So even the memory of a great magic turned out to be a bird’s joke.”

===Chinese===
“于是，连一场大魔法的记忆，到头来也不过是一只鸟的玩笑。”

===Original===
Picus thought how he would appear downstairs and bewilder them again. Had enough of Scylla. Wished now he hadn’t given her that cup. Caught out he’d been by the old man, but that wasn’t over yet. Get right out and come in by another door. Make Clarence, Scylla, and the lot of ’em quite happy always. Play round his way and their way forever. *And I’ll give you leave to play till doomsday.* Not mother. Too late to do that. Sort of the old man’s prisoner. Just thinking of that made him feel ill and want Clarence. Felix reached his finale.

===Chinese===
皮库斯盘算着，下楼露面时该怎样再叫他们发一回懵。斯库拉，他受够了。如今倒懊悔把那只杯子给了她。他是叫老头儿抓了现行，可这事还没完。干脆整个人出去，再从另一扇门进来。叫克拉伦斯、斯库拉和他们那一伙人永远称心如意。照他的玩法加他们的玩法，一直玩下去。*我准你玩到世界末日。*别去找母亲。那已经太迟了。算是老头儿的囚徒了。光这么一想，他就觉得难受，就想要克拉伦斯。菲利克斯到了终曲。

===Original===
“You’ve confused me very successfully, and you can put up with what I’ve become.”

===Chinese===
“你们把我搅得够成功的，那你们就受着吧，受着我变成的这副样子。”

===Original===
Scylla saw that Carston had had enough, and felt stifled and alone. Clarence returned in agony up the doubling wood-path, not the straight. Ross withdrew into his picture, and Carston hung on tight to a thought: We’ve all got to get out of here for a bit.

===Chinese===
斯库拉看出卡斯顿受够了，自己也觉得气闷、孤单。克拉伦斯苦不堪言地顺着盘旋的林径往回走，没走笔直那条。罗斯退回他的画里去，卡斯顿则死死抓住一个念头：我们全都得离开这儿一阵子。

## XVIII / 十八、皮库斯交底

===Original===
“Tired,” said Scylla, changing her dress, and leaving that to stare out at the wood﻿—“Tired of your wretched beauty, your rearrangements of light on a leaf.”

===Chinese===
“腻了，”斯库拉（Scylla）说，一边换着衣裳，把换下的那件撂在那里，由它去瞪着外面的树林——“腻了你这恼人的美，腻了你把光在一片叶子上排来布去的把戏。”

===Original===
“Bored,” said Picus, who had gone to meet Clarence and missed him. And he meant sad. And Clarence, giving Nanna a hand skinning the rabbits he had shot, looked at his bloody hands: “I suppose I’ve got a broken heart and these wretched feelings come in through the holes.”

===Chinese===
“闷得慌，”皮库斯（Picus）说——他方才去找克拉伦斯（Clarence），没找着。他其实是说：难过。克拉伦斯正搭手帮娜娜（Nanna）剥他打来的几只兔子，看着自己那双血淋淋的手：“想是我这颗心已经碎了，这些难受的感觉，就是从那些碎洞里钻进来的。”

===Original===
“Alone,” said Felix, “when I’ve got away it’ll be the same.”

===Chinese===
“孤单，”菲利克斯（Felix）说，“等我脱开身，还不是一样。”

===Original===
Only Ross embraced his solitude, thought of the shape of each thing he drew, until the earth seemed one growing stillness, of innumerable separate tranquillities, forever moving, forever at rest.

===Chinese===
只有罗斯（Ross）拥抱自己的孤独，想着他画的每一样东西的形状，直到大地仿佛成了一片生长着的静止，由无数各各分开的安宁组成，永远动着，又永远息着。

===Original===
Unfortunately the members of the house-party were not behaving like that. An organic view of Felix, for instance? He damned the scene﻿—knew that he had handled it without imagination. Besides, the boy should be wearing his “youth’s gay livery” before a livelier audience than hills and the sea.

===Chinese===
只可惜这一宅子的宾客行事并非如此。比方说，对菲利克斯来一番有机的观照？他咒骂了这场面——心里明白，自己处理它时全无想象。再说，这孩子本该穿着他“青春的鲜亮号衣”，到比青山大海更活泼的观众面前去亮相。

===Original===
And there was more coming through than Picus’s wiles, life opening like the unfolding of a scene. An endless screen of coromandel lacquer, the design travelling with it, fold in, fold out. Enough for Ross to know that there was design and seize the detail, a man content with the tangible, piece by piece, to whom no single object was dumb. He thought of the brickness of a brick until he seemed aware of it throughout, not side after side or two or three, but each crumb of its body, and each crumb reduced to its molecular construction, until the brick ceased to be a cube and could as easily be reformed again. And the only prayer to which he condescended was that Scylla would keep her head since there were hysterics about; then left the studio and joined the party now slowly regathering on the lawn. All but Felix. Carston did not know how to greet Picus, not it seemed in any disgrace, and telling them a story about a parson’s wife.

===Chinese===
何况透过皮库斯的诡计，还有更多的东西透出来：生活正像一幕布景的展开那样打开。一架没有尽头的科罗曼德尔漆（coromandel）屏风，图样随屏而行，折进去，展出来。对罗斯来说，知道其中有图样、抓住细节，这就够了——一个安于有形之物的人，一件接一件地把握，没有一件东西对他是哑的。他想着一块砖之所以为砖的砖性，直到仿佛把它彻里彻外察觉了个透——不是一面接一面、一次两面三面地察觉，而是它躯体的每一粒碎屑，每一粒碎屑又化到它的分子构造里去，直到那块砖不再成其为一方立块，又能同样轻易地重新聚合成形。而他肯屈尊一祷的只有一件事：眼下这伙人里歇斯底里横行，愿斯库拉把持得住。随后他出了画室，加入草坪上正慢慢重新聚拢的那一伙。只少了菲利克斯。卡斯顿（Carston）不知道该怎么跟皮库斯打招呼——他似乎全无失势丢脸的模样，正在给他们讲一个牧师太太的故事。

===Original===
“So she knitted me a check sweater, and I had a pain. And I lay flat on the grass, and told a curate and another curate to play chess on my back. And I found a caterpillar and made them make love.”

===Chinese===
“于是她给我织了一件格子毛衣，我就疼了起来。我平平趴在草地上，吩咐一个副牧师和另一个副牧师在我背上下棋。我又寻到一条毛毛虫，叫他们恩爱起来。”

===Original===
“They don’t,” said Ross, “it’s the butterflies.”

===Chinese===
“它们不来这个，”罗斯说，“来这个的是蝴蝶。”

===Original===
“These did. And the first curate said check with the white bishop, and I stopped enjoying it, and hunched up and went in to get my tea, and there was a party, and I stopped thinking.”

===Chinese===
“这些就来了。后来第一个副牧师用白象叫了将，我玩兴尽了，蜷起身子进屋去喝茶，屋里正有一场聚会，我便不再思想了。”

===Original===
“When do you do your thinking?”

===Chinese===
“那你什么时候动脑子？”

===Original===
“Never.”

===Chinese===
“从来不动。”

===Original===
Scylla said: “I wish you had, before you let this morning’s business be sprung on us. What did you do it for?”

===Chinese===
斯库拉说：“但愿你动过——赶在由着今早那桩事冷不防砸到我们头上之前。你到底为什么要那么干？”

===Original===
They all waited for his answer. It came.

===Chinese===
大家都等着他的回答。回答来了。

===Original===
“Mind your own business.” Like a rude boy. And then:

===Chinese===
“少管闲事。”像个粗野的男孩。然后：

===Original===
“It was my mother who was driven to death.”

===Chinese===
“被逼死的，是我的母亲。”

===Original===
“That’s nothing to do with it,” said Ross.﻿—“Pawn your father’s collection. Throw it into the sea, but don’t﻿—”

===Chinese===
“那跟这事不相干，”罗斯说——“把你父亲的收藏当掉。扔进海里去好了，只是别——”

===Original===
“Don’t what?” said Picus.

===Chinese===
“别什么？”皮库斯说。

===Original===
Felix came out.

===Chinese===
菲利克斯出来了。

===Original===
“Here’s what’s left of your bloody book. Nanna took it off the fire.” Ross opened it with kind hands not afraid of char-black and turned the middle pages the fire had not curled up. He stared.

===Chinese===
“喏，你那本该死的书，剩下的都在这儿了。是娜娜从火上抢下来的。”罗斯伸出不怕炭黑的一双好心的手，把书打开，翻到炉火没燎卷的中段书页。他怔住了。

===Original===
“Look at this.” The book was open at a full-page photograph of the cup. Underneath was written﻿—Early English altar-vessel. From the collection of Christopher Tracy, Esq.

===Chinese===
“看这个。”书页正翻开在那只杯子的整页照片上。底下印着——早期英格兰祭坛圣器。录自克里斯托弗·特雷西先生（Christopher Tracy, Esq.）的收藏。

===Original===
While the cup was fetched from its drawer and passed from hand to hand, Carston appreciated Picus’s blissful look, untouched by relieved anxiety, not too elated or even too absorbed.

===Chinese===
杯子从抽屉里取了出来，在一双手又一双手里传递，卡斯顿趁这当口欣赏着皮库斯那副极乐的神情——不沾一点如释重负的惶惶之色，不显得过分得意，甚至也不显得过分专注。

===Original===
“Picus,” said Ross, “in common decency tell us what you know.”

===Chinese===
“皮库斯，”罗斯说，“凭一点起码的体面，把你知道的都告诉我们。”

===Original===
“Now that I’ve been asked, listen. I took the cup. Mother and I used to pretend with it. Not this time, but when I came back to Tollerdown last spring. The well was brimming and I took it out to get a drink, and it slipped through my fingers like a fish. It couldn’t be got out then, and I didn’t want Clarence to fuss. He didn’t know. Once it was gone, I wondered what it was, and I this time told my father that I hadn’t seen it lately, and something he said put me on to that book. So I left it to see what would happen. And Felix fished it out with Clarence’s spear. It may have come from India. The whore who killed my mother may have used it. It suited the old man to palm it off as a church vessel and to tell you it was a poison-cup. He’s lived in India long enough, and his best friend is an archdeacon. That’s all I know. Oh yes, I curled up behind an anthill on Hangar’s ridge Carston shied at. And I stuck the cup in his room to teach him that ants don’t bite, and give him something else to think about. Oh yes, I made love to Scylla because she is a darling, and usually I’m afraid of women. And﻿—”

===Chinese===
“既然有人问了，那就听着。杯子是我拿的。母亲和我从前常拿它玩假装的游戏。不是这一回——是去年春天我回托勒当（Tollerdown）的时候。井水涨得满溢，我把它带出来想舀一口喝，它却像条鱼似的从我指缝里滑了下去。那会儿捞不上来了，我又不想让克拉伦斯大惊小怪。他不知道。东西一丢，我倒纳闷它究竟是什么，这一回我告诉我父亲说近来没见着它，他说的哪句话点拨了我，叫我去翻那本书。于是我把它留在井里，看会有什么事发生。结果菲利克斯用克拉伦斯的长矛把它捞了出来。它兴许真来自印度。害死我母亲的那个婊子兴许用过它。老头儿乐得把它冒充成一件教会圣器，再告诉你们它是只毒杯。他在印度住得年头够久，他顶要好的朋友又是一位会吏长。我知道的就这些。哦对，我蜷在哈恩加山脊（Hangar’s ridge）上一座蚁丘的背后——卡斯顿就是在那道山脊上惊乍的。我把杯子塞进他房里，好教他明白蚂蚁不咬人，也好给他点别的可想。哦对，我向斯库拉求了爱，因为她招人疼，而我平素是怕女人的。还有——”

===Original===
“Hold up﻿—” said Ross.

===Chinese===
“打住——”罗斯说。

===Original===
It was a great blessing that the old man had done the lying. Put untruth away in a far corner. Far corners are more difficult to get at. But what they needed then was Picus’s brightness restored.

===Chinese===
幸而撒谎的活儿是老头儿包了的，实在是莫大的福气。把那套不实之词搁进了一个远角落。远角落更难够得着。可他们那时需要的，是把皮库斯那份明亮恢复回来。

===Original===
Carston said: “It seems to me that your father’s story was a lie because he wanted the book back.”

===Chinese===
卡斯顿说：“依我看，你父亲那套故事是谎话，因为他想要回那本书。”

===Original===
“Trust Nanna,” said Scylla, blissfully, “she wouldn’t have the kitchen fire put out.”

===Chinese===
“娜娜靠得住，”斯库拉极乐似的说，“她才不肯让厨房的火熄掉。”

===Original===
“The kitchen fire, mark you,” said Felix, suddenly interested again.

===Chinese===
“厨房的火，你们记住，”菲利克斯说，忽然又来了兴致。

===Original===
Hestia is an old goddess﻿—I think she had a name written under her altar not even the Romans might know. And in her case, their lives, the sap of their bodies was nourished at Nanna’s fire. There the sea bubbled in butter, the meat dripped its red juice, birds split into white shreds. Round it the lettuces sparkled, the roots under the wood boiled, old herbs scented the place, wine dripped in like dew, and Nanna was perhaps the only person unequivocally loved.

===Chinese===
赫斯提亚（Hestia）是位古老的女神——我想她的祭坛底下写着一个名字，连罗马人也不容知晓。而在她的名下，他们的生命、他们身体的浆液，都靠娜娜的炉火滋养。在那炉火上，海物在黄油里翻泡，肉滴下红红的汁水，禽鸟绽开成白色细丝。炉火四周，生菜晶晶发亮，柴火底下的根茎煮得翻滚，老香草熏香了这一方地方，酒像露水一样滴进来，而娜娜，兴许是唯一一个被爱得毫不含糊的人。

===Original===
The wailing that went up round her: “Nanna, I’m hungry.” “Nanna, I’ve got a cold.” “Nanna, I’ve blistered my heel.” “Nanna, where are the buttons on my white waistcoat?” Nanna who liked cigarettes and silk stockings and no device for saving labour, her hair tied up like an old gipsy, and her tongue free. She had got them round this corner. All the same, a lie is harder to run to earth three counties away.

===Chinese===
围着她升起来的哀告：“娜娜，我饿了。”“娜娜，我着凉了。”“娜娜，我脚后跟磨起泡了。”“娜娜，我白背心上的扣子哪儿去了？”娜娜——爱香烟，爱丝袜，不爱任何省力的巧器，头发扎起来像个老吉卜赛婆子，舌头百无禁忌。是她带着他们拐过了这个角。话虽如此，要把一句谎话追到三个郡外的老巢，终究更难。

===Original===
“We are left where we began,” said Felix, “with the Thoroughly Rum.”

===Chinese===
“我们又落回了起步的地方，”菲利克斯说，“守着那件‘彻头彻尾的邪门货’。”

===Original===
Also, as Carston noticed, with the thoroughly boring. The adventure of the cup had happened. It had been complicated, violent, inconclusive. Now it would be too much trouble to take trouble for more trouble. For a new series of untruths or “stubborn, irreductible” facts. Not much chance of them, and difficult to get. Three counties away. As though they had been in a room together, and something had passed through that had left too raw traces for all its invisibility, had left them alone with private griefs and memories quickened. Which, if it came again, would enter by a different door. Picus, stirred by the story of his mother, victim of Victorian social stupidities in an age less agreeable and more remote than that which produced mass-cups, and complicated biographies of public characters like Huon of Bordeaux, son of Julius Caesar, and Morgan le Fay.

===Chinese===
还有——正如卡斯顿注意到的——守着那彻头彻尾的乏味。杯子的奇遇已经发生过了。它错综、暴烈、没有结局。如今再劳神费力去自讨更多的麻烦，未免太麻烦了。为的是一套新的谎话，或一堆“顽固、无从化约”的事实。它们指望不大，又难弄到手。远在三个郡之外。就仿佛他们曾同处一室，有什么东西穿堂而过——尽管无形，却留下了太生疼的痕迹——把他们撇下，各自守着私痛与被催活的旧忆。而那东西若是再来，将从另一扇门进来。皮库斯则被他母亲的故事搅动了心——他的母亲，是维多利亚式社会愚行的牺牲品；那个时代，比起既造出弥撒杯（mass-cup）、又给波尔多的于昂（Huon of Bordeaux）——尤利乌斯·恺撒（Julius Caesar）之子——和摩根勒菲（Morgan le Fay）这类公众人物编排复杂传记的时代来，更不宽厚，也更邈远。

===Original===
They were tired of it, as he saw, till another door opened. This, when his own interest for the first time was really aroused. The business was more or less out in the open. News. A story. He wanted to find out exactly what Mr. Tracy had been up to. If they were prepared to leave it alone until something else happened, he was not; anticipating a dateless, glorious moment when he would appear before them with the story complete. Hand them the finished psychology of old Mr. Tracy. A new philosophy: a fortune: the cup of the Sanc-Grail. No intention of leaving now.

===Chinese===
他们都腻了这件事，他看得出——直到另一扇门打开。这一开，他自己的兴味头一回真正被撩了起来。这桩事的底细，已大体摆上了明面。新闻。故事。他想弄清楚特雷西先生（Mr. Tracy）究竟搞的什么名堂。他们尽可以把它撂下不管，等下一桩事自行发生；他可不——他预期着某个不知日期的光辉时刻，届时他将带着完完整整的故事出现在他们面前。把老特雷西先生一份做成了的心理剖析，交到他们手上。一套新哲学：一笔财富：圣所圣杯（Sanc-Grail）的那只杯。此刻，全无离开的打算。

===Original===
Then a sound that was almost “service” rose in his thought. Not public or personal or progressive, or in relation to the “hard-eyed men of the Y.M.C.A.” Not even for results; there might be no results. As the conception grew, fortifying like a cup of wine *à point*, he saw an approach to Scylla, without reference to possession or to her reaction. What she did not have with these unemployed condottieri her peers. What he must do for her, and she was no spoilt American bitch.

===Chinese===
接着，一个近乎“侍奉”的声音在他心里升起。不是为公、为私、为进步，也同“基督教青年会（Y.M.C.A.）那班目光冷硬的汉子”不相干。甚至不为结果；可能根本没有结果。这念头越长越大，像一杯恰到好处（*à point*）的酒那样给他添力，他看见了一条走向斯库拉的路——不涉占有，也不问她的反应。那是她同这班失业的雇佣兵头子（condottieri）——她的同辈——之间所没有的。那是他必须为她做的事，而她可不是什么被惯坏的美国贱货。

===Original===
Immediately, he stepped into another world, their world and his own. At its largest, airiest and freest. He had never been there before. He had always been there. He would always be there, never the same apprehensive, gifted, rootless man. Reckoning without his hosts, he urged another slice of cake on her, and suggested that it might be his turn to fetch the evening fish.

===Chinese===
顷刻之间，他一步跨进了另一个世界——他们的世界，也是他自己的世界。在它最宏大、最空阔、最自由的境地。他从没到过那里。他又一直在那里。他将永远在那里，再不是从前那个忐忑不安、有天分而无根柢的人。这一着全然没把东道主们算计在内，他劝她又吃了一片蛋糕，还提议这回该轮到他去取晚间的鱼了。

===Original===
“We’ll go together,” she said. “A walk will do me good.”

===Chinese===
“我们一起去，”她说。“走一走对我有好处。”

## XX / 二十、死亡家宴

===Original===
Breakfast reassured him how far they minded the weather. They had been out and there was news, a tramp aground on Tunbarrow Ledges, and twenty-three drowned men laid out on tables in the parish room.

===Chinese===
早饭让他放了心，看明白了他们把这场天气放在心上有几分。他们出去过一趟，带回了消息：一艘流浪货船在坦巴罗礁（Tunbarrow Ledges）搁了浅，二十三个溺死的人陈放在教区活动室的桌子上。

===Original===
“A Danish boat,” said Scylla. “Does anyone here speak it?”

===Chinese===
“一条丹麦船。”斯库拉说。“这儿有谁会说丹麦话？”

===Original===
Carston did, but did not see that it was his duty to say so in order to assist what was left of the crew, who must have a consul somewhere. He bore it when Scylla took him out down the wood, quiet under a colonnade, until they came out and staggered against a wall of air.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿会，可他看不出这有什么义务非说不可，好去搭救船员里剩下的那几个人——他们总归在什么地方有位领事。斯库拉领他出门、顺林中车道往下走时，他都忍着没作声；林子静悄悄的，像行在一排廊柱底下，直到出了林子，两个人一晃，撞上了一堵风墙。

===Original===
The little bay disappointed him, packed with dull drift that choked the waves, a pocket for the storm’s mess, and on the headland they found the sea racing, but not with the explosion he had imagined up the cliffs side.

===Chinese===
小湾叫他失望：塞满了黯淡的漂流杂物，噎得浪都透不过气，成了收存风暴秽物的一只口袋；到了岬角上，他们看见大海在狂奔，却没有他在崖壁那头想象出的那股爆发的声势。

===Original===
“Tide’s out.” So that formless bright patch of moon was still pulling the sea about, holding it off the land. Then she led him carefully to the edge, and he saw a hole in the clay, blue, raw and dripping from a wave’s mouth.

===Chinese===
“潮退了。”这么说，月亮那块无定形的亮斑还在牵动大海，把它从陆地上引开去。随后她小心地把他领到崖沿，他看见黏土里有一个洞，蓝莹莹、新豁豁的，还从一张浪嘴里滴着水。

===Original===
“That came out last night.”

===Chinese===
“这是昨晚冲出来的。”

===Original===
Still the sky travelled, torn cloud and blue enough for trousers, rain-flaws, and air ribbons. The wildness enervated him. The excitement was cerebral, all spectacle, a whip-up for the eyes and the salt-refreshed palate, the ears cut off from common sounds.

===Chinese===
天仍在奔行，碎云，蓝得够裁一条裤子的天，一阵阵雨痕，一条条气流带。这番野态使他瘫软。兴奋只在大脑里，全然是景观，是朝眼睛和被盐味激爽的口腔抽起的一鞭，耳朵则与寻常声响隔断了。

===Original===
At lunch words crossed his wind-filled head like the gull’s cry in the night. The well at the cottage on Tollerdown would be filling; floating corpses could be skimmed off: the vicar at Tunbarrow was reliable with the shipwrecked: Felix had gone over there.

===Chinese===
午饭时，话语穿行在他灌满风的脑袋里，像夜里那只鸥的啼叫。托勒当小屋的那口井该在涨水了；漂起来的尸首可以撇出去：坦巴罗的牧师对付海难的人靠得住：菲利克斯已经过去了。

===Original===
While they relaxed over coffee, the boy came in, bareheaded, strapped into oilskins, pale, his cheeks burning with two red circles exercise would not account for.

===Chinese===
大家喝着咖啡歇息时，那少年进来了，光着头，紧束在油布雨衣里，脸色苍白，两颊烧着两团红晕——那可不是跑动跑得出来的。

===Original===
“Eaten?” said his sister.

===Chinese===
“吃了没？”他姐姐说。

===Original===
“I don’t want to. I’ve seen them.”

===Chinese===
“不想吃。我看见他们了。”

===Original===
“Seen what?”

===Chinese===
“看见什么了？”

===Original===
“Twenty-three dead men.” They all reacted to the young voice horror and drama made unsteady.

===Chinese===
“二十三个死人。”人人都对那嗓音起了反应——一副被恐怖和做戏搅得发颤的少年嗓音。

===Original===
“Singularly drowned with their wounds showing﻿—where the fish gnawed﻿—”

===Chinese===
“溺得离奇，伤口都露着——鱼啃过的地方——”

===Original===
“Haven’t had time to be gnawed,” said Ross. “Don’t overdo it.”

===Chinese===
“还来不及叫鱼啃呢。”罗斯说。“别过头了。”

===Original===
“I saw them till they had no significance whatever, because I saw death. I suppose you admit they’re dead?”

===Chinese===
“我看着他们，一直看到他们毫无意义为止，因为我看见的是死。我谅你们也承认他们是死了的吧？”

===Original===
“A death,” said Ross, coldly, “you court yourself in the cutter year in, year out. We court. What about it?”

===Chinese===
“死嘛，”罗斯冷冷地说，“你自己年复一年驾着独桅快艇去招的。我们都在招。又怎样？”

===Original===
Felix swallowed, and stared right and left.

===Chinese===
菲利克斯咽了口唾沫，两眼左右直瞪。

===Original===
“Death’s family party,” he said. “I’ve seen it. Getting nearer home. Don’t you know you’re in league with that sort of thing? And that your shifts for getting away are hopeless﻿—”

===Chinese===
“死亡的家宴。”他说。“我见着了。离咱家越来越近了。你们不知道你们跟这种东西串通一气吗？不知道你们那些脱身的伎俩全都没指望吗——”

===Original===
“What shifts?” said his sister. “Ease up, boy, stop running round in circles. I didn’t drown them.”

===Chinese===
“什么伎俩？”他姐姐说。“缓一缓，孩子，别兜着圈子跑了。又不是我淹死他们的。”

===Original===
He addressed himself to her:

===Chinese===
他冲着她说：

===Original===
“Your love affairs﻿—what are they worth? and your famous strength that supports us? I know you’re a strong woman, with your stunt of opening doors every sane person knows are better shut. I’m your brother and you’ll not take me in. Twenty-three bodies, twenty-three pictures of death have taught me the worth of your tricks. And I don’t flatter myself I shall do anything on my own. You’ve sucked me too dry for that﻿—”

===Chinese===
“你的那些风流事——值几个钱？还有你那撑持我们大家的、出了名的力气？我知道你是个强女人，惯会来那一手：去推开每个神志清明的人都知道不如关上的门。我是你弟弟，你哄不了我。二十三具尸体，二十三幅死的图景，教会了我你那些花招的价值。我也不自抬身价，以为自己单枪匹马还能干成什么。你把我吸吮得太干了——”

===Original===
Carston saw her swing the crystal slung from her neck he knew the boy had given her.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿看见她晃了晃挂在脖子上的一块水晶——他知道那是这少年送她的。

===Original===
“Dearie,” said Picus, “let your back hair down, and be yourself.”

===Chinese===
“小亲亲，”皮库斯说，“把你后面的头发放下来，做回你自己吧。”

===Original===
“Go away,” said Ross.

===Chinese===
“走开。”罗斯说。

===Original===
“Go and look for what you want where you think you’ll find it,” she said. Temperately, ineffectually, the reserve showing how she loved him.

===Chinese===
“去找你想要的东西吧，去你以为找得到的地方。”她说。语气平和，全无用处；那份克制，正显出她爱他有多深。

===Original===
Carston wanted to kick him. Clarence yawned. The boy took no notice. Carston thought: Ways of clearing the house. A full well at Tollerdown, and Biarritz the brighter by one cub. Whose adieux were being made separately.

===Chinese===
卡斯顿真想踹他一脚。克拉伦斯打了个哈欠。少年毫不理会。卡斯顿心想：清空这座宅子的种种法子。托勒当一口灌满的井，比亚里茨添亮的一只狼崽子。而道别，正在分头进行着。

===Original===
“And Clarence can nurse his fancy heartbreak and Picus his second-rate chic. And Ross make his appetites serve his art, or whichever way round he does it. And Carston get kick out of being taken in by our fake aristocracy. And Nanna slave and tell you how wonderful you are. I’m going where there won’t be any more fairy-stories, and my complexes can rot me or﻿—”

===Chinese===
“还有克拉伦斯，可以抱着他那想象出来的心碎好生将养；皮库斯抱他那二流的风雅；罗斯拿他的胃口伺候他的艺术，或者随他哪一头是哪一头；卡斯顿呢，被我们这冒牌贵族做派一哄，就乐在其中；娜娜做牛做马，还得夸你多么了不起。我要去一个再没有童话故事的地方，让我的情结烂死我好了，要么——”

===Original===
“All right,” said his sister, “we’ll try not to overwork Nanna, or impose on Carston too much.”

===Chinese===
“好吧，”他姐姐说，“我们尽量不把娜娜使唤过头，也不过分欺负卡斯顿。”

===Original===
Well, well, thought the latter: the new type of child: Biarritz, bars. *What every little boy in a bar knows.* And how far had her love got Scylla? His newfound confidence working easily in him, he smiled at Felix.

===Chinese===
唉，唉，卡斯顿心里想：新式孩子：比亚里茨，酒吧。*每个酒吧里的小男孩都知道的事。*至于她的爱，又把斯库拉带到了多远？新到手的自信在他身上运转自如，他朝菲利克斯笑了笑。

===Original===
“ ‘Portrait of the artist as young man,’ ” he said. “Good luck.”

===Chinese===
“《一个青年艺术家的画像》，”他说。“祝你好运。”

===Original===
But the boy answered:

===Chinese===
可那少年答了话：

===Original===
“D’you fancy my sister so much that you’ve learned her tricks? She is keeping them for someone else than me, that’s all.”

===Chinese===
“你就这么看上我姐姐，连她的花招都学会了？她那些花招是留着给除我之外的人的，就这么回事。”

===Original===
She wondered as she left the room, and for once ordered Nanna to iron his linen immediately, if his version of the truth was refreshing him, as any contact should. And, pitifully, how long it would last. And anxiously, what he would do. And, maliciously until she felt better what sort of a fool he would make of himself, what gaping mouth would snap him up.

===Chinese===
她离开房间时一路想着——还破天荒吩咐娜娜立刻去熨他的衬衣——不知他那套版本的真话，是否正给他提神，一如任何接触所应做到的。又怜悯地想：这能撑多久。又忧心地想：他会做出什么来。又恶意地想——想到自己心里好过些为止——他会把自己弄成个什么样的傻瓜，哪一张豁开的大口会一口把他吞掉。

===Original===
So he lost her until he should come to look for her, Grail vanished, girl and all.

===Chinese===
于是他失掉了她，直到该由他去寻她之时——圣杯没了踪影，姑娘也一并没了。

===Original===
Incidentally, he settled which of the rest should go or stay. Next day, contrary to custom, the wind fell, and a torrent of soft mist packed in rain brimmed the land, refilled ceaselessly off the falling sea as it passed in over the hills. They could hear the water slowly thundering and not much else but their rather distressed voices. Carston alone had the serenity of plans. After he had persuaded Scylla to go up to London, Clarence said that he would go over to the cottage or the dust and damp would get in and annoy his young man. Picus had gone already, flitted off, the raining fog hiding him for a time. Carston meant the same landscape to swallow him on the trail of old Mr. Tracy. He asked Ross for his plans.

===Chinese===
顺便说一句，其余人谁走谁留，他也定下了。第二天，风竟一反常态地停了，一股夹满雨的软雾洪流漫过大地，翻过山丘往里灌，又从退落的海上不住地回填。他们听得见水在缓缓轰响，此外便只有他们几个颇为烦乱的人声。唯独卡斯顿怀着一副计划的安详。等他劝动斯库拉上伦敦去之后，克拉伦斯说他要过去照看小屋，不然灰尘潮气钻进去，会惹恼他那位年轻的男伴。皮库斯已经走了，翩然而逝，落雨的浓雾把他掩了一阵。卡斯顿打定主意，也让同一片风景吞掉自己，循着老特雷西先生的踪迹去。他问罗斯有什么打算。

===Original===
“Stay here and get on with things. I’ll wait till you come back.”

===Chinese===
“待在这儿接着做事。等你们回来。”
